However, an often-overlooked issue in real-world behavioral datasets is that the recorded data may be missing observations that were available to the original decision-maker. In use-inspired settings such as healthcare, this can make expert actions appear suboptimal, even when they were near-optimal given the information available at the time. As a result, the rewards learned by standard IRL may be misleading. In this paper, we identify the minimal perturbations to the recorded observations needed for the expert's actions to appear optimal. We develop a practical algorithm for this problem and demonstrate its utility for quantifying the possible extent of missing observations in behavioral datasets through extensive experiments on synthetic navigation tasks, a cancer treatment simulator, and ICU treatment data.

</details>


### [52] [Discrete Stochastic Localization for Non-autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12836)
*Yunshu Wu,Jiayi Cheng,Longxuan Yu,Partha Thakuria,Rob Brekelmans,Evangelos E. Papalexakis,Greg Ver Steeg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DSL框架，通过单位球token嵌入实现SNR不变的贝叶斯最优去噪，单一网络支持多种采样路径，在MDLM基础上微调后显著提升离散序列生成质量，并支持混合采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续扩散模型在离散序列生成任务上通常落后于掩码离散扩散模型。作者认为瓶颈不在于连续性本身，而在于表示方式——传统去噪过程依赖于时间步索引的噪声机制，这限制了模型的灵活性和性能。

Method: 提出离散随机定位(DSL)框架，采用单位球面token嵌入，其贝叶斯最优去噪器对定位信道下的名义信噪比(SNR)具有不变性。单一训练网络支持完整的per-token SNR路径族，其中端点掩码扩散路径作为特例。通过微调预训练的MDLM检查点实现。

Result: 在OpenWebText数据集上，DSL在所有步数预算(T=128到T=1024)下显著提升分布保真度(MAUVE指标)。同一检查点支持随机顺序自回归采样，以及仅用T=48步的连续-离散混合采样器，无需蒸馏或重新训练。

Conclusion: DSL证明了连续状态框架通过恰当的表示设计可以超越现有离散扩散模型，实现了模型灵活性、生成质量和采样效率的统一，为非自回归生成提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Continuous diffusion is a natural framework for non-autoregressive generation but has generally lagged behind masked discrete diffusion models (MDMs) on discrete sequence generation. We argue that the bottleneck is not continuity itself, but a representation in which denoising depends on timestep-indexed noise regimes. We introduce \emph{Discrete Stochastic Localization} (DSL), a continuous-state framework with unit-sphere token embeddings whose Bayes-optimal denoiser is invariant to the nominal signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) under the localization channel. One trained network then supports an entire family of per-token SNR paths, with endpoint masked-diffusion paths as a special case. Fine-tuning a pretrained MDLM checkpoint with DSL substantially improves distributional faithfulness (MAUVE) on OpenWebText across all step budgets from $T{=}128$ to $T{=}1024$, and the same checkpoint supports random-order autoregressive sampling, as well as a hybrid continuous-then-discrete sampler using as few as T=48 total steps -- without distillation or retraining.

</details>


### [53] [Bayesian Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12843)
*Kaiyang Li,Shaobo Han,Qing Su,Shihao Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出贝叶斯模型合并（BMM）框架，通过双层优化和锚模型先验解决现有方法忽视强先验和缺乏全局超参数优化的问题，在多任务合并中显著优于现有基线，ViT-L/14上8任务合并性能接近专家模型平均水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法存在两个关键局限：(1)忽视强锚模型的归纳偏置，从零开始估计合并权重；(2)在网络不同模块间使用共享超参数设置，缺乏全局优化策略。

Method: 提出双层优化框架BMM：内层将模型合并建模为基于锚模型强先验的激活贝叶斯回归，得到高效闭式解；外层利用贝叶斯优化在小验证集上全局搜索模块特定超参数。此外，发现激活统计量与任务向量关键对齐，推导出无需数据的BMM变体。

Result: 在视觉领域最多20任务合并和语言领域5任务合并的广泛基准测试中，BMM持续优于所有即插即用锚基线（TA、WUDI-Merging、TSV）。特别在ViT-L/14基准的8任务合并中，单个合并模型达到95.1分，接近八个任务特定专家的平均性能95.8分。

Conclusion: BMM通过引入锚模型先验和全局超参数优化，为模型合并提供了一种高效且性能优越的解决方案，无需联合重训练即可实现接近专家模型性能的多任务合并，证明了贝叶斯框架在模型合并领域的有效性。

Abstract: Model merging aims to combine multiple task-specific expert models into a single model without joint retraining, offering a practical alternative to multi-task learning when data access or computational budget is limited. Existing methods, however, face two key limitations: (1) they overlook the valuable inductive bias of strong anchor models and estimate the merged weights from scratch, and (2) they rely on a shared hyperparameter setting across different modules of the network, lacking a global optimization strategy. This paper introduces Bayesian Model Merging (BMM), a plug-and-play bi-level optimization framework, where the inner level formulates the model merging as an activation-based Bayesian regression under a strong prior induced by an anchor model, yielding an efficient closed-form solution; and the outer level leverages a Bayesian optimization procedure to search module-specific hyperparameters globally based on a small validation set. Furthermore, we reveal a key alignment between activation statistics and task vectors, enabling us to derive a data-free variant of BMM that estimates the Gram matrix for regression without any auxiliary data. Across extensive benchmarks, including up to 20-task merging in vision and 5-task merging in language, BMM consistently outperforms all plug-and-play anchor baselines (e.g., TA, WUDI-Merging, and TSV). In particular, on the ViT-L/14 benchmark for 8-task merging, a single merged model reaches 95.1, closely matching the average performance of eight task-specific experts (95.8).

</details>


### [54] [Multitask Multimodal Fusion with Tabular Foundation Models for Peak and Durability Prediction of Pertussis Booster Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12852)
*Divya Sitani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多任务对比多模态融合架构，用于同时预测百日咳加强疫苗接种后的抗体峰值和长期持久性，解决了两个阶段生物学机制分离、样本小、模态缺失等挑战，在两项任务上均显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 百日咳加强疫苗接种后的免疫反应在峰值大小和长期持久性方面存在显著的个体差异，这两个阶段由部分不同的生物区室调控：峰值反映急性B细胞激活和抗体分泌，而持久性反映长期体液免疫记忆的建立。现有计算模型大多仅针对其中一个阶段，无法完整预测"增强-衰退"的完整轨迹。联合预测这两个终点具有挑战性，因为它们在生物学上是分离的而非冗余的，同时面临样本量小、模态异质性强且存在结构性缺失、以及两个任务依赖不同的测量时间窗口等问题。

Method: 提出了一种多任务对比多模态融合架构，包含以下关键组件：(1)每个模态使用冻结的TabPFN-v2编码器；(2)双标签监督对比损失，当两个受试者在任务1或任务2标签上一致时将其视为正样本对；(3)根据经验缺失率校准的模态dropout；(4)缺失掩码注意力融合机制。该方法应用于CMI-PB百日咳加强数据集的精选子集（158名受试者，四种模态，44.9%存在至少一种模态缺失）。

Result: 在测试集上，模型对峰值响应预测的AUROC为0.797（95% CI [0.621, 0.948]），对持久性预测的AUROC为0.755（95% CI [0.519, 0.945]），在联合标签置换检验中均显著（N=1000，p值分别为0.002和0.045）。与逻辑回归、XGBoost和MLP基线模型（在原始特征和TabPFN嵌入上）相比，所提出的模型是唯一一个在两项任务上95%置信区间同时高于随机水平的模型。各模态贡献分析显示：峰值预测主要由细胞因子信号驱动，而持久性预测主要由基线抗体特征驱动，这与潜在免疫学机制一致。

Conclusion: 该多任务对比多模态融合架构成功解决了百日咳疫苗免疫反应预测中的关键挑战，能够同时准确预测峰值响应和长期持久性。模型不仅显著优于现有基线方法，而且其学习到的模态贡献模式与已知的免疫学机制相吻合，为疫苗免疫反应的个性化预测提供了有效工具，具有重要的临床应用价值。

Abstract: Pertussis booster vaccination produces immune responses that vary widely across individuals in both peak magnitude and long-term durability. These two phases are governed by partly distinct biological compartments:peak reflects acute B-cell activation and antibody secretion, while durability reflects the establishment of long-term humoral memory. Yet most computational models target only one, missing the full boost-and-wane trajectory. Jointly predicting both is non-trivial because the two endpoints are biologically dissociated rather than redundant; samples are small, modalities are heterogeneous with structured missingness, and the two tasks rely on different measurement windows. We propose a multi-task contrastive multimodal fusion architecture combining frozen TabPFN-v2 per-modality encoders, a dual-label supervised contrastive loss that treats two subjects as a positive pair if they agree on the Task 1 label or the Task 2 label, modality dropout calibrated to empirical missingness, and missingness-masked attention fusion. Applied to a curated subset of the CMI-PB pertussis booster dataset (n = 158 subjects, four modalities, 44.9% with at least one modality missing; Spearman r = -0.58 between peak and durability, n = 96), the model achieves test AUROC 0.797 (95% CI [0.621, 0.948]) for peak response and 0.755 (95% CI [0.519, 0.945]) for durability, with both significant under joint label permutation (N = 1000; p = 0.002 and p = 0.045). Across logistic regression, XGBoost, and MLP baselines on raw features and on TabPFN embeddings, the proposed model is the only one whose 95% CIs lie above chance on both tasks simultaneously. Per-modality contribution analyses recover task-specific modality contributions consistent with the underlying immunology: peak prediction is carried by cytokine signatures, while durability is carried by baseline antibody features.

</details>


### [55] [SMA: Submodular Modality Aligner For Data Efficient Multimodal Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12872)
*Truong Pham,Anay Majee,Rishabh Iyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子模态互信息的组合对齐范式（SMA），通过集合级建模利用数据的多个增强和描述，在仅使用数万样本的低数据场景下实现了优异的多模态泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态基础模型依赖大规模配对数据，且采用实例级对齐方式，仅最大化单个图文对的关联，忽略模态间的几何结构，导致模态鸿沟问题。这在低数据和罕见场景中限制了模型应用，亟需数据高效的多模态学习方法。

Method: 提出组合式多模态对齐范式，引入子模态对齐器（SMA）。将实体的多个增强和描述视为集合，利用子模态互信息（SMI）构建目标函数，联合最大化模态间互信息并减少跨模态差异，从而有效利用多个正关联。

Result: 在CLIP基准的14个零样本分类和检索任务上进行评估，SMA在低数据场景下取得一致提升。仅使用数万样本即可实现强大的多模态泛化能力，比标准方法所需的样本量减少多个数量级。

Conclusion: 基于集合的建模方式和子模态目标对于数据高效的多模态学习至关重要，能够显著降低对大规模配对数据的依赖，为低资源场景下的多模态学习提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Despite the recent success of Multimodal Foundation Models (FMs), their reliance on massive paired datasets limits their applicability in low-data and rare-scenario settings where aligned data is scarce and expensive. A key bottleneck is the adoption of an instance-level formulation, which learns alignment by maximizing correlation between individual image-text pairs while neglecting the underlying geometric structure across modalities resulting in a modality gap across input modalities. In this paper, we propose a combinatorial paradigm for multimodal alignment that moves beyond pairwise learning and introduce the \emph{Submodular Modality Aligner (SMA)}, which treats multiple augmentations and descriptions of an entity as a set, leveraging multiple descriptions of the data to capture richer cross-modal structure. We instantiate SMA using a principled objective based on Submodular Mutual Information (SMI), which jointly maximizes inter-modality mutual information while reducing cross-modal divergence. This formulation enables the model to effectively utilize multiple positive associations and extract significantly more information from limited data. We evaluate SMA on 14 zero-shot classification and retrieval tasks from the CLIP benchmark and demonstrate consistent gains in the low-data regime. Notably, SMA achieves strong multimodal generalization using only tens of thousands of samples. This is orders of magnitude fewer than standard approaches. Our results highlight the importance of set-based formulations and submodular objectives for data-efficient multimodal learning.

</details>


### [56] [Descriptive Collision in Sparse Autoencoder Auto-Interpretability: When One Explanation Describes Many Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12874)
*Jordan F. McCann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现了稀疏自编码器（SAE）自动可解释性流程中的“描述性冲突”问题：多个不同的SAE特征会被赋予相同的自然语言解释，导致特征无法被有效区分。研究提出了两种新的评估指标来惩罚无法区分特征的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的SAE自动可解释性批评主要集中在多义性（一个特征有多种含义）或解释是否预测激活值上。作者识别了一个互补且结构独特的问题：描述性冲突，即多个不同的SAE特征接受相同的解释，这影响了特征解释的有效性和区分度。

Method: 重新分析了最大的公开人类标注SAE特征数据集（包含722个标注特征，跨越Gemma 2 2B和Pythia 70M）。信息论分析显示平均标注仅解析了70%的特征身份。作者形式化了一个称为“区分度”的属性，证明了当前检测式自动可解释性评分对冲突具有不变性，并提出了两个互补的修正指标：冲突调整检测评分和区分度评分。

Result: 平均标注字符串在3.07个特征中被重用；82.1%的特征至少与一个其他特征共享标注；最常见的标注字符串（"复数名词"）标记了跨越18层和四个模型组件的101个不同特征。忽略冲突问题会使报告的特征可解释性膨胀，膨胀量约等于识别一个特征所需信息量的三分之一。

Conclusion: 描述性冲突问题是独立于先前识别的自动可解释性失效模式之外的问题，且具有叠加效应。该问题在现有评估框架中被忽视，导致对特征可解释性的高估。新提出的指标显式惩罚无法区分特征的解释，为解决这一问题提供了方法。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are now standard tools for decomposing language model activations into interpretable features, and automated interpretability pipelines routinely assign each feature a short natural-language explanation. Existing critiques of this practice focus on polysemanticity -- one feature with many meanings -- or on whether explanations predict activations. We identify a complementary, structurally distinct problem we call descriptive collision: many distinct SAE features admit the same explanation. Reanalyzing the largest publicly-available dataset of human-annotated SAE features (Marks et al., 2025), comprising 722 annotated features across Gemma 2 2B and Pythia 70M, we find that the mean annotation string is reused across 3.07 features; 82.1% of features share their annotation with at least one other feature; and the single most common annotation string ("plural nouns") labels 101 distinct features spanning 18 layers and four model components. Information-theoretically, the average annotation resolves only 70% of feature identity. We formalize a property called discrimination, prove that current detection-style auto-interpretability scoring is invariant to collision, and propose two complementary corrective metrics -- collision-adjusted detection and discrimination scoring -- that explicitly penalize explanations that fail to distinguish a feature from its neighbors. The collision problem is independent of, and additive with, previously identified failure modes of auto-interpretability; ignoring it inflates reported feature interpretability by a quantity equal to roughly one-third of the bits required to identify a feature.

</details>


### [57] [Certified Robustness under Heterogeneous Perturbations via Hybrid Randomized Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12876)
*Blaise Delattre,Hengyu Wu,Paul Caillon,Wei Yang Bryan Lim,Yang Cao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对混合离散-连续输入（如文本-图像）的统一随机平滑框架，解决了多模态模型在联合对抗扰动下的鲁棒性认证空白问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机平滑鲁棒性认证方法仅能处理单一模态（仅连续或仅离散输入），无法适用于多模态模型。在多模态场景中，决策依赖跨模态语义，攻击者可对异构输入进行联合扰动，导致单模态认证失效，亟需一种能处理混合输入的统一认证方法。

Method: 提出一个基于Neyman-Pearson联合最坏情况问题解析公式的统一随机平滑框架。通过分析由因子化离散和连续噪声诱导的联合似然排序，推导出一个闭式的一维认证证书，在理论上严格推广了高斯（仅图像）和离散（仅文本）随机平滑方法。

Result: 成功推导出针对混合离散-连续输入的闭式鲁棒性证书。在多模态安全过滤任务上验证了该框架的有效性，据作者所知，这是首个针对交互式文本-图像安全过滤中联合离散token和连续图像扰动的模型无关Neyman-Pearson证书。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了多模态鲁棒性认证的理论空白，将随机平滑技术从单模态推广至混合模态场景，为多模态模型抵御联合对抗攻击提供了坚实的理论基础与实用工具。

Abstract: Randomized smoothing provides strong, model-agnostic robustness certificates, but existing guarantees are limited to single modalities, treating continuous and discrete inputs in isolation. This limitation becomes critical in multimodal models, where decisions depend on cross-modal semantics and adversaries can jointly perturb heterogeneous inputs, rendering unimodal certificates insufficient. We introduce a unified randomized smoothing framework for mixed discrete--continuous inputs based on an analytically tractable Neyman--Pearson formulation of the joint worst-case problem. By analyzing the joint likelihood ordering induced by factorized discrete and continuous noise, our approach yields a closed-form, one-dimensional certificate that strictly generalizes both Gaussian (image-only) and discrete (text-only) randomized smoothing. We validate the framework on multimodal safety filtering, providing, to our knowledge, the first model-agnostic Neyman--Pearson certificate for joint discrete-token and continuous-image perturbations in interaction-dependent text--image safety filtering.

</details>


### [58] [ASAP: Amortized Doubly-Stochastic Attention via Sliced Dual Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12879)
*Huy Tran,Max Milkert,David Hyde*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ASAP方法，通过“训练-编译”策略，先用Sinkhorn训练双随机注意力层，再用固定切片对偶算子替换推理时的迭代循环，在保持性能的同时实现5.3倍推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双随机注意力是行softmax注意力的有效替代方案，但现有方法存在权衡：Sinkhorn缩放在每次推理时需重复矩阵缩放迭代，计算开销大；切片传输注意力虽移除了在线迭代，但其软排序近似需为每个切片实体化稠密张量，训练资源消耗远高于Sinkhorn。本文旨在解决这一训练效率与推理效率之间的矛盾。

Method: 提出ASAP（通过切片对偶投影的摊销双随机注意力）：采用“训练-编译”范式。(1)训练阶段：使用高效的Sinkhorn算法训练双随机注意力层；(2)编译/推理阶段：用一个固定的切片对偶算子替换迭代缩放循环。具体而言，学习一个轻量级参数化映射，从精确的一维Kantorovich势推断Sinkhorn查询侧对偶变量，然后通过双侧熵c-变换重建注意力计划。

Result: 在语言和视觉基准测试中，ASAP保持了廉价的训练设置，并与近期基线模型表现相当。在主要冻结层基准测试中，ASAP比训练好的Sinkhorn教师模型快5.3倍，同时准确率相当；在下游替换任务中，ASAP无需重新训练即可恢复教师模型的大部分性能。

Conclusion: ASAP成功地将切片传输思想融入双随机注意力机制，通过解耦训练和推理过程，实现了训练资源消耗低与推理速度快、性能保持三者之间的良好平衡。该方法为构建高效Transformer变体提供了一条切实可行的路径。

Abstract: Doubly-stochastic attention has emerged as a transport-based alternative to row-softmax attention, with recent Transformer variants using it to reduce attention sinks and rank collapse while improving performance. In this family, the standard approach is Sinkhorn scaling, which trains more efficiently but still repeats matrix scaling in every inference forward pass. Sliced-transport attention removes the online iteration, but its soft sorting approximation materializes dense tensors for each slice, requiring substantially more training resources than Sinkhorn attention. We introduce ASAP: Amortized Doubly-Stochastic Attention via Sliced Dual Projection, a train-then-compile method that trains the doubly-stochastic layer with Sinkhorn, then replaces the iterative scaling loop at inference with a fixed sliced-dual operator. It learns a lightweight parametric map from exact one-dimensional Kantorovich potentials to the Sinkhorn query-side dual, then reconstructs the attention plan with a two-sided entropic c-transform. Across language and vision benchmarks, ASAP keeps the cheaper training setup and remains highly competitive with recent baselines. In the main frozen-layer benchmark, ASAP is 5.3 faster than the trained Sinkhorn teacher while matching its accuracy; in downstream replacements, ASAP recovers most of the teacher performance without any retraining.

</details>


### [59] [RISED: A Pre-Deployment Safety Evaluation Framework for Clinical AI Decision-Support Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12895)
*Rohith Reddy Bellibatlu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RISED框架，通过五个维度的预部署评估（可靠性、包容性、敏感性、公平性、可部署性）检测传统聚合准确度指标无法发现的临床AI部署风险，并在合成队列和三个真实世界队列上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前临床AI决策支持系统的评估主要依赖聚合准确度指标，但这些指标无法检测部署阶段的输入可靠性、子群公平性、阈值敏感性和操作可行性等失败问题，存在部署风险盲区。

Method: 提出RISED五维度预部署评估框架（可靠性、包容性、敏感性、公平性、可部署性），每个维度通过形式化子标准、预设通过/失败阈值、偏差校正加速(BCa)bootstrap 95%置信区间，并结合Holm-Bonferroni族错误校正进行操作化。公平性维度重构为代理依赖诊断，需在绑定门控前获取独立于结果的需求度量。

Result: 在合成队列和三个跨越35年临床数据年份的真实世界队列（从1980年代心脏病学数据集到2024年全国代表性健康调查）上验证，发现满足传统高判别基准的分类器可能同时无法通过输入编码稳定性和阈值偏移敏感性检查，且子群AUC奇偶性统计结论不明确，不同队列的失败维度各异，初步证明了构念效度。

Conclusion: RISED框架能够揭示传统聚合评估无法检测的部署风险，为临床AI报告标准提供定量裁决，建立了从计算机模型验证到无声临床试验评估之间的原则性网关，并已作为开源Python包发布。

Abstract: Aggregate accuracy metrics dominate the evaluation of clinical AI decision-support systems but do not detect deployment-phase failures of input reliability, subgroup equity, threshold sensitivity, or operational feasibility. We propose the RISED Framework: a five-dimension pre-deployment evaluation covering Reliability, Inclusivity, Sensitivity, Equity, and Deployability, in which each dimension is operationalized through formal sub-criteria, pre-specified pass/fail thresholds, and bias-corrected accelerated (BCa) bootstrap 95% confidence intervals combined under a Holm-Bonferroni family-wise error correction. A central demonstration is that a classifier satisfying conventional high-discrimination benchmarks can simultaneously fail input-encoding stability and threshold-shift sensitivity checks, while subgroup AUC parity remains statistically inconclusive, pointing to deployment risks that aggregate evaluation alone cannot detect. We validate this differential pass/fail pattern on a synthetic cohort and three publicly available real-world cohorts spanning 35 years of clinical data vintage, from a 1980s cardiology dataset to a 2024 nationally representative health survey, where failing dimensions differ across cohorts, providing preliminary evidence of construct validity. The Equity dimension is reframed as a proxy-dependence diagnostic rather than a stand-alone gate: any need-based fairness verdict computed against a utilization-derived proxy carries a construct-validity problem the framework surfaces explicitly, triggering a procurement requirement for an outcome-independent need measure before the gate is binding. RISED is released as an open-source Python package that supplies the quantitative verdicts existing clinical AI reporting standards require, providing a principled gateway between in-silico model validation and silent-trial clinical evaluation.

</details>


### [60] [VIP-COP: Context Optimization for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12904)
*Yilong Chen,Xueying Ding,Leman Akoglu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: VIP-COP是一种针对表格基础模型的测试时上下文优化方法，通过显式选择机制筛选高价值的训练样本和特征，突破上下文长度限制，在几分钟内显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型受限于上下文长度限制，只能应用于中等规模数据，当推理时数据超过预训练规模分布时性能下降。现有方法如软提示优化需要模型梯度、产生抽象潜在token且缺乏显式信号区分能力。

Method: 提出VIP-COP（Value of Importance for Prediction - Context OPtimization），基于在线KernelSHAP回归进行迭代优化，包含价值引导的上下文采样和多保真度剪枝。该方法显式筛选"非常重要的预测器"（样本和特征），抑制噪声并隔离有影响力的数据，支持数据增强场景下优先选择高价值样本和特征。

Result: 在大规模高维测试平台、数据增强和数据噪声设置下，VIP-COP持续优于启发式和优化基线方法，建立了TFMs测试时上下文精炼的新最先进水平。该方法快速（几分钟内提升性能）、预算感知且可随时中断、兼容黑盒模型（无需访问模型内部）、可解释性强且对噪声鲁棒。

Conclusion: VIP-COP通过显式选择机制有效解决了表格基础模型的上下文长度限制问题，相比需要模型梯度的软提示优化方法，具有黑盒兼容、可解释、鲁棒等优势，为TFMs的实际应用提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models (TFMs) have emerged as a powerful paradigm for in-context learning on structured data, enabling direct prediction on new tabular tasks without task-specific training. However, their effectiveness is constrained by context length limits, restricting application to medium-scale data and degrading performance when inference-time data exceed pretraining size distributions. Our work introduces VIP-COP, estimating the Value of Importance for Prediction of training examples and features for hard Context OPtimization for TFMs. Its explicit selection mechanism suppresses noise and isolates influential data, enabling the model to also benefit from data augmentation by prioritizing high-value augmented samples and features. VIP-COP is (i) fast, boosting performance often within minutes of optimization, based on an online KernelSHAP-based regression with iterative refinement, value-guided context sampling, and multi-fidelity pruning; (ii) budget-aware and any-time, improving with additional test-time compute unlike heuristics that produce fixed contexts; (iii) model-aware yet fully black-box, requiring no access to model internals, making it compatible with both proprietary and open-source TFMs; (iv) interpretable, identifying discrete ``Very Important Predictors'' (samples and features) that maximize signal-to-noise, which makes it (v) robust, isolating high-value data from noise. In contrast, soft-prompt optimization requires model gradients, produces abstract latent tokens, and lacks explicit signal discrimination. Extensive experiments show that VIP-COP consistently outperforms heuristic and optimized baselines across large-scale high-dimensional testbeds, including data augmentation and data-noise settings, establishing a new state of the art in test-time context refinement for TFMs.

</details>


### [61] [Data Difficulty and the Generalization--Extrapolation Tradeoff in LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12906)
*Siyuan Liu,Tinghong Chen,Xinghan Li,Yifei Wang,Jingzhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现SFT中不存在普遍最优的数据难度，最佳难度取决于数据规模：随着数据预算增加，最优难度会向更难的数据偏移。这一现象由分布内泛化差距与外推差距的相互作用机制解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于启发式方法（如困惑度、难度、长度）的数据选择研究结果往往不一致或依赖于具体情境，缺乏对数据难度在微调中作用的系统性理解。研究旨在揭示数据难度与数据规模如何共同影响SFT的泛化与外推权衡。

Method: 采用实证与理论相结合的方法：1) 系统研究数据难度在微调中的作用；2) 通过受控合成实验揭示底层机制；3) 使用PAC-Bayesian泛化界进行理论分析，验证分布内泛化差距与外推差距的相互作用机制。

Result: 发现固定数据预算下存在最优数据难度，且该最优难度随数据预算增加而向更难数据偏移。识别出简单底层机制：分布内泛化差距与外推差距的相互作用。理论分析支持该机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了数据规模与难度如何共同影响SFT中泛化与外推的权衡，为特定模型和数据条件下的基于难度的数据选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Data selection during supervised fine-tuning (SFT) can critically change the behavior of large language models (LLMs). Although existing work has studied the effect of selecting data based on heuristics such as perplexity, difficulty, or length, the reported findings are often inconsistent or context-dependent. In this work, we systematically study the role of data difficulty in fine-tuning from both empirical and theoretical perspectives, and find that there is no universally optimal difficulty level; rather, its effectiveness depends on the dataset size. We show that for a fixed data budget, there exists an optimal data difficulty for SFT, and that this optimal difficulty shifts toward harder data as the data budget increases. To explain this phenomenon, we conduct controlled synthetic experiments that reveal a simple underlying mechanism: the interplay between the (in-distribution) generalization gap and the extrapolation gap. We further support this mechanism through a theoretical analysis using PAC-Bayesian generalization bounds. Overall, our results clarify how data size and difficulty jointly affect the trade-off between generalization and extrapolation in SFT, providing guidance for difficulty-based data selection under certain model and data conditions.

</details>


### [62] [Revisiting DAgger in the Era of LLM-Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12913)
*Changhao Li,Rushi Qiang,Jiawei Huang,Chenxiao Gao,Chao Zhang,Niao He,Bo Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将DAgger算法应用于长视野多轮交互的LM智能体训练，在SWE-bench Verified上显著超越强基线，4B模型性能甚至超过已发表的8B系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野LM智能体在多轮交互中容易因早期错误导致轨迹偏离。现有方法存在互补性缺陷：监督微调虽提供密集监督但受协变量偏移困扰（训练于离策略教师轨迹）；强化学习虽避免该问题（从在线策略学习）但仅有稀疏的结果反馈。

Method: 重新审视并适配DAgger算法用于多轮LM智能体：(1) 通过学生与教师策略的轮次级插值收集轨迹，使模型暴露于部署时可能遇到的真实状态；(2) 使用教师提供的监督标签在收集的轨迹上训练学生模型，获得丰富的学习反馈。

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，DAgger训练相比最强后训练基线提升+3.9分(4B)和+3.6分(8B)。4B智能体达到27.3%超越代表性8B系统，8B智能体达29.8%超越SWE-Gym-32B。在SWE-Gym保留集上也取得一致增益。

Conclusion: DAgger有效结合了监督学习的密集反馈和在线策略学习的协变量偏移规避优势，为现代长视野LM智能体训练提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon LM agents learn from multi-turn interaction, where a single early mistake can alter the subsequent state distribution and derail the whole trajectory. Existing recipes fall short in complementary ways: supervised fine-tuning provides dense teacher supervision but suffers from covariate shift because it is trained on off-policy teacher trajectories; while reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards avoids this off-policy mismatch by learning from on-policy rollouts but with only sparse outcome feedback. We address this dilemma by revisiting Dataset Aggregation (DAgger) for multi-turn LM agents: the algorithm collects trajectories through a turn-level interpolation of student and teacher policies, and the student is then trained on these trajectories using supervised labels provided by the teacher. By directly interacting with environments, we expose the model to realistic states likely to be encountered during deployment, thereby effectively mitigating covariate shift. Besides, since the student is learned by mimicking the teacher's behavior, it receives rich feedback during learning. To demonstrate DAgger enjoys the benefits of both worlds, we tested the algorithm to train a software-engineering agent with 4B- and 8B-scale student models. On SWE-bench Verified, our DAgger-style training improves over the strongest post-training baseline by +3.9 points at 4B and +3.6 points at 8B. The resulting 4B agent reaches 27.3%, outperforming representative published 8B SWE-agent systems, while the 8B agent achieves 29.8%, surpassing SWE-Gym-32B and coming within 5 points of stronger 32B-scale agents. Together with consistent gains on the held-out SWE-Gym split, these results suggest the effectiveness of DAgger for modern long-horizon LM agents.

</details>


### [63] [IV-ICL: Bounding Causal Effects with Instrumental Variables via In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12924)
*Vahid Balazadeh,Hamidreza Kamkari,Medha Barath,Ricardo Silva,Rahul G. Krishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了IV-ICL方法，这是一种基于摊销贝叶斯上下文学习的方法，用于在工具变量设置下直接学习因果效应的边缘后验分布，并通过分位数推导边界，避免了传统方法需要闭式边界估计量和高计算成本的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工具变量(IV)设置中，由于未观察到的混淆因素导致点识别不可能，现有的部分识别方法面临方法学瓶颈：需要闭式边界估计量（如二元IV中的Balke-Pearl方程），且需要针对每个估计量手动设计精确的估计器。直接贝叶斯推断虽然可以绕过这些挑战，但通常计算量大，且存在先验敏感性高或后验分布欠分散的问题。

Method: 引入IV-ICL（摊销贝叶斯上下文学习方法），该方法直接学习因果效应的边缘后验分布，并将边界作为其分位数推导出来。与优化排他性KL散度的标准变分推断不同，摊销贝叶斯推断最小化期望包含性KL，这是一个质量覆盖目标。此外，还提出了一种将随机对照试验转换为IV基准的程序，该程序可证明保留了基本事实因果效应。

Result: 在合成和半合成IV基准上的评估表明，IV-ICL生成的区间比高效的半参数、贝叶斯和插件基线更可靠、更有效，且推理时间降低了20-500倍。实验观察到优化包含性KL可以恢复不同数据生成过程中的整个识别集，而相同贝叶斯公式的排他性KL（如变分推断）会坍缩到单一模式，无法覆盖识别集。

Conclusion: IV-ICL方法成功克服了现有IV部分识别方法的局限性，通过优化包含性KL目标实现了更好的质量覆盖特性，提供了更可靠有效的因果效应区间估计。同时提出的RCT到IV基准转换程序为部分识别方法提供了更现实、可验证的评估框架。

Abstract: The instrumental-variables (IV) setting is standard for partial identification of causal effects when unobserved confounding makes point identification impossible. Existing approaches face methodological bottlenecks: closed-form bound estimands are required -- e.g., Balke-Pearl equations in binary IV -- and even when available, designing accurate estimators requires manual effort tailored to each estimand. While direct Bayesian inference of the causal effects, instead of the bounds, circumvents these challenges, it is often computationally intensive and suffers from high prior sensitivity or under-dispersed posteriors. As a remedy, we introduce IV-ICL, an amortized Bayesian in-context learning method that learns the marginal posterior distribution of the causal effects directly and derives bounds as its quantiles. Unlike standard variational inference that optimizes exclusive KL divergence, amortized Bayesian inference minimizes the expected inclusive KL, a mass-covering objective. We empirically observe that optimizing inclusive KL can recover the entire identified set across diverse data-generating processes, while exclusive-KL (e.g. with variational inference) of the same Bayesian formulation collapses onto a single mode and fails to cover the identified set. We evaluate IV-ICL on synthetic and semi-synthetic IV benchmarks and show it produces intervals that are more reliably valid and more informative compared to efficient semi-parametric, Bayesian, and plug-in baselines, at 20-500x lower inference time. Beyond methodology, we propose a procedure to convert randomized controlled trials into IV benchmarks with provably preserved ground-truth causal effects that enables a more realistic evaluation of partial-identification methods.

</details>


### [64] [The Efficiency Gap in Byte Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12928)
*Celine Lee,Jing Nathan Yan,Chen Liang,Jiaxin Shi,Yin Zhang,Jeremiah Liu,Pengcheng Yin,Fernando Pereira,Ed Chi,Derek Cheng,Alexander M. Rush,Ruoxi Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究通过计算匹配的扩展性分析，发现字节级建模在掩码扩散模型（MDM）中的性能惩罚比自回归模型（AR）更严重，原因在于MDM目标破坏了局部连续性，导致难以从原始字节中高效解析语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型主要依赖子词分词和自回归排序，这些设计选择内置了先验。字节级建模和掩码扩散建模作为替代范式挑战了传统方法，但其结合去除了结构先验，带来了显著的计算成本。本研究旨在调查这一成本及其在不同建模范式中的表现。

Method: 采用计算匹配的扩展性研究方法，对比字节级建模在自回归（AR）和掩码扩散模型（MDM）中的表现。通过受控排列实验分析性能差异，提出"上下文脆弱性"假设解释观察到的差异。

Result: 研究发现字节建模的性能惩罚不均匀：随着规模扩大，字节建模在MDM中的扩展开销比AR更严重。这是因为AR的稳定因果历史允许模型自然重新发现子词模式，而MDM目标破坏了从原始字节高效解析语义所需的局部连续性。

Conclusion: 未来的模态无关设计必须在字节机制中引入替代性结构偏置，以维持可行的扩展轨迹，才能在不依赖传统结构先验的情况下实现有效扩展。

Abstract: Modern language models have historically relied on two dominant design choices: subword tokenization and autoregressive (AR) ordering. These design decisions bake in priors that dictate a model's learning. Recently, two alternative paradigms have challenged this: byte-level modeling, which bypasses static statistically-derived token vocabularies, and masked diffusion modeling (MDM), which conducts parallel, non-sequential generation. Their intersection represents a fully end-to-end modality-agnostic generative prototype; however, removing these structural priors incurs a significant computational cost. In this work, we investigate this cost through a compute-matched scaling study. Our results reveal that the performance penalty of byte modeling is not uniform; across scale, the scaling overhead of byte modeling is worse for MDM than for AR. We hypothesize that this disparity stems from context fragility: while AR's stable causal history allows models to naturally rediscover subword patterns, the MDM objective destroys the local contiguity required to efficiently resolve semantics from raw bytes. Our findings from controlled permutation experiments suggest that future modality-agnostic designs must incorporate alternative structural biases to maintain viable scaling trajectories in the byte regime.

</details>


### [65] [The Expressivity Boundary of Probabilistic Circuits: A Comparison with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12940)
*Zhiyu Zhao,Xuejie Liu,Muhan Zhang,Anji Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从统一的自回归视角揭示了PCs落后于LLMs的两大瓶颈：输出瓶颈可通过logit空间参数化缓解；上下文编码瓶颈源于结构化可分解PCs的固定路由结构限制，并证明了可分解PCs比结构化可分解PCs具有更强的表达能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率电路（PCs）作为支持精确高效概率推理的深度生成模型，在自回归语言建模任务上仍显著落后于基于Transformer的大语言模型（LLMs），这暗示了二者之间存在重要的表达能力差距。为了理解并缩小这一差距，作者在统一的自回归框架下对PCs和LLMs进行了深入比较分析。

Method: 作者在统一的自回归表述下对比分析了PCs和LLMs。首先，识别出输出瓶颈：PCs在概率空间使用凸组合参数化预测，难以表示语言中典型的尖锐分布；采用logit空间参数化可显著缩小该差距。其次，研究上下文编码瓶颈：理论上证明结构化可分解PCs在vtree对齐的划分上可以匹配Transformer的分离秩，但通过理论和实验表明这种能力局限于与固定路由结构对齐的划分，当数据呈现异构依赖拓扑时会导致严重退化。进一步从理论上证明可分解PCs比结构化可分解PCs具有严格更强的表达能力。

Result: 研究发现两个核心瓶颈：（1）输出瓶颈：采用logit空间参数化相比概率空间的凸组合能更好地表示语言的尖锐分布；（2）上下文编码瓶颈：结构化可分解PCs的表达能力受限于其固定路由结构，仅在vtree对齐的划分上有效，面对异构依赖拓扑时性能严重下降；（3）理论证明可分解PCs比结构化可分解PCs具有严格更强的表达能力，但其有效优化仍是一个开放挑战。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和实证角度系统揭示了PCs在语言建模中落后于LLMs的根本原因，指出输出表示和上下文编码两个关键瓶颈，并为未来改进PCs提供了明确方向：logit空间参数化和更灵活的结构设计，同时指出了优化更强大的可分解PCs是重要的开放研究问题。

Abstract: Probabilistic Circuits (PCs) are deep generative models that support exact and efficient probabilistic inference. Yet in autoregressive language modeling, PCs still lag behind Transformer-based large language models (LLMs), suggesting an important expressivity gap. In this work, we compare PCs and LLMs under a unified autoregressive formulation. First, an output bottleneck: PCs parameterize predictions as convex combinations in probability space, which struggles to represent the sharp distributions typical of language; adopting a logit-space parameterization substantially narrows this gap. Second, a context-encoding bottleneck: we prove that structured-decomposable PCs can match Transformer separation rank on vtree-aligned partitions, but show, both theoretically and empirically, that this capacity is limited to partitions aligned with the fixed routing structure, leading to severe degradation when the data exhibits heterogeneous dependency topologies. We further prove that decomposable PCs are strictly more expressive than structured-decomposable ones, though effectively optimizing them remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [66] [Reinforced Collaboration in Multi-Agent Flow Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12943)
*Zheng Wang,Yuang Liu,Yangkai Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MANGO框架，通过流网络、强化学习和文本梯度联合优化多智能体系统的协作路径与行为，有效缓解了错误传播问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体LLM系统容易因工作流设计不当或智能体输出不准确而导致错误传播，从而损害最终的任务性能。

Method: 提出了MANGO框架：利用基于过去成功工作流构建的流网络组织协作；结合强化学习与文本梯度联合优化工作流路径和智能体行为；引入跳过机制以避免对已优化智能体进行冗余更新。

Result: 在七个基准测试中，MANGO相比最先进的基线实现了最高12.8%的性能提升，效率提高了47.4%，并能有效泛化到未见过的领域。

Conclusion: MANGO为多智能体系统提供了一种数据驱动的优化方案，显著提升了性能与效率，具有强大的泛化能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems provide a powerful way to extend large language models (LLMs) by decomposing a complex task into specialized subtasks handled by different agents. However, their performance is often hindered by error propagation, arising from suboptimal workflow design or inaccurate agent outputs, which can propagate through the agent collaboration process and degrade final results. To address the challenges, we present MANGO (Multi-Agent Network Gradient Optimization), a data-driven framework that organizes and refines agent collaboration via a flow network constructed from past successful workflows. MANGO integrates reinforcement learning and textual gradients to jointly optimize workflow paths and agent behaviors, while a skipping mechanism prevents redundant updates to well-optimized agents for improving efficiency. Extensive experiments on seven benchmarks show that MANGO achieves up to 12.8% performance improvement over state-of-the-art baselines, enhances efficiency by 47.4%, and generalizes effectively to unseen domains. Our code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/openJiuwen-ai/agent-store/tree/main/community/mango.

</details>


### [67] [From Instance Selection to Fixed-Pool Data Recipe Search for Supervised Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12944)
*Haodong Wu,Jiahao Zhang,Lijie Hu,Yongqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoSelection框架，将SFT数据选择从传统的实例排序问题重构为固定池数据配方搜索问题，通过双层求解器在有限评估预算下自动发现高质量的数据筛选配方。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的监督微调（SFT）数据选择方法通常采用实例排序范式，即对每个样本评分并保留top-k子集。然而，有效的SFT训练子集往往通过有序的策展配方产生，其中过滤、混合和去重操作共同塑造最终的数据分布。现有方法忽视了多操作组合的协同效应，且难以在有限评估预算下找到最优数据组合。

Method: 提出AutoSelection双层求解器：外层基于缓存的任务侧、数据侧和模型侧信号进行固定池物化；内层使用预热探测、已实现子集状态、局部配方编辑、高斯过程辅助排序和停滞触发重 seeding，从而在有限的全SFT评估预算下高效搜索最优配方。该方法不生成、重写或增强训练样本，仅通过搜索现有算子组合构建高质量子集。

Result: 在90K指令池上的实验表明，AutoSelection在三个基础模型上实现了最强的域内推理平均性能，优于全数据训练、随机配方搜索、随机top-k和单算子选择器。额外的域外图推理结果、搜索稳定性分析、结构消融实验和1.5B到7B迁移验证进一步证明，配方结构比单个选择算子更重要。

Conclusion: 数据配方结构在SFT数据选择中具有关键作用，超越单个选择算子的效果。AutoSelection成功地将数据选择从实例级排序提升到配方级搜索，为自动化的数据策展提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data selection is commonly formulated as instance ranking: score each example and retain a top-$k$ subset. However, effective SFT training subsets are often produced through ordered curation recipes, where filtering, mixing, and deduplication operators jointly shape the final data distribution. We formulate this problem as fixed-pool data recipe search: given a raw instruction pool and a library of grounded operators, the goal is to discover an executable recipe that constructs a high-quality selected subset under a limited budget of full SFT evaluations, without generating, rewriting, or augmenting training samples. We introduce AutoSelection, a two-layer solver that decouples fixed-pool materialization based on cached task-, data-, and model-side signals from expensive full evaluation, using warmup probes, realized subset states, local recipe edits, Gaussian-process-assisted ranking, and stagnation-triggered reseeding. Experiments on a 90K instruction pool show that AutoSelection achieves the strongest in-distribution reasoning average across three base models, outperforming full-data training, random recipe search, random top-$k$, and single-operator selectors. Additional Out-of-distribution graph-reasoning results, search-stability analyses, structural ablations, and 1.5B-to-7B transfer checks further show that recipe structure matters beyond individual selection operators. Code is available at https://github.com/w253/AutoSelection.

</details>


### [68] [Separating Shortcut Transition from Cross-Family OOD Failure in a Minimal Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12945)
*Hongmin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个最小二分类模型揭示了训练相关性、学习到的捷径使用与测试时OOD失效三者之间的分离现象。通过理论分析发现，确定性条件下岭正则化可防止OOD失效，而噪声条件下分类器会发生向捷径规则的切换，其后果取决于测试家族的捷径相关性强度与方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常假设捷径特征可以解释分布外(OOD)失效，但训练相关性、学习到的捷径使用与测试时失效之间不一定一致。本文旨在揭示这三者之间的复杂关系，澄清何时捷径相关性会真正导致OOD泛化失败。

Method: 构建一个最小二分类模型，包含一个不变坐标和一个依赖家族的捷径坐标。在确定性条件下分析逻辑回归ERM的行为；在噪声条件下分析岭逻辑回归ERM从不变规则到捷径规则的转换机制；通过解析推导与合成实验验证理论发现。

Result: 确定性条件下，岭正则化保持分类器以不变特征为主导，防止OOD失效；噪声条件下，当训练捷径信号超过不变信号时，分类器切换到捷径规则。该转换是否导致失效取决于held-out家族：较弱的捷径相关性导致正超额风险，符号翻转家族导致高于随机错误率。同一训练端转换在不同held-out情况下产生不同后果。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将捷径吸引、捷径规则转换和跨家族OOD失效三个概念分离，揭示了捷径问题中训练端行为与测试端后果的非线性关系。发现岭正则化在不同条件下扮演不同角色，为理解和缓解OOD泛化问题提供了精确的理论框架。

Abstract: Shortcut features are often invoked to explain out-of-distribution (OOD) failure, but training correlation, learned shortcut use, and test-time failure need not coincide. We study a minimal binary model with one invariant coordinate and one family-dependent shortcut coordinate. In the deterministic regime, positive average shortcut correlation pulls logistic ERM toward positive shortcut weight, but ridge regularization keeps the classifier invariant-dominated and prevents deterministic OOD failure. When the invariant coordinate is noisy, ridge-logistic ERM switches to the shortcut rule once the training shortcut signal exceeds the invariant signal. Whether that transition causes failure depends on the held-out family: weaker shortcut correlation yields positive excess risk, and sign-flipped families yield above-chance error. Synthetic checks match these analytic regimes and show that the same training-side transition can have different held-out consequences. The model separates shortcut attraction, shortcut-rule transition, and cross-family OOD failure.

</details>


### [69] [U-HNO: A U-shaped Hybrid Neural Operator with Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing for Non-stationary PDE Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12965)
*Yingzhe Ma,Xiao Yang,Yuxin Xie,Zihan Xiong,Jinliang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出U-HNO，一种U型混合神经算子，通过稀疏点自适应路由机制(SPAR)实现全局Fourier分支与局部多尺度高斯分支的自适应融合，有效解决PDE求解中平滑全局传输与局部尖锐特征共存的问题，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏微分方程(PDE)的解通常在单一轨迹中同时展现平滑的全局传输和局部化的尖锐特征（如激波锋面、薄界面），这给神经算子带来挑战：基于Fourier的架构能高效处理非局部交互但难以精确解析局部非平滑特征，而空间局部架构虽能恢复精细细节却牺牲了长程传播和推演稳定性。现有混合算子采用固定、空间均匀的融合方式，迫使所有位置做出相同的权衡。

Method: 提出U-HNO架构，核心设计为稀疏点自适应路由(SPAR)机制：(1)在每个空间位置，逐像素硬掩码选择全局Fourier分支或局部多尺度高斯分支占主导；(2)稀疏比率为路由信号局部对比度的函数，使平滑区域和激波对齐区域接收不同的全局-局部计算混合比例。SPAR嵌入于带跳跃连接的分层编码器-瓶颈-解码器主干中，双分支和门控在所有分辨率下运作。训练结合点监督、有限差分H^1梯度项和频带谱一致性正则化器。

Result: 在PDEBench的多个基准测试上验证（包括1D Burgers、Kuramoto-Sivashinsky、KdV，2D平流、Allen-Cahn、Navier-Stokes、Darcy流，以及3D跨声速可压缩Navier-Stokes），U-HNO在大多数任务的相对L^2和H^1指标上达到SOTA推演精度，在以尖锐局部特征为主导的问题上增益最大。消融实验表明移除任何单一组件都会显著增加推演误差。

Conclusion: U-HNO通过空间自适应的稀疏路由机制成功解决了全局-局部特征表示的权衡问题，无需在所有位置采用相同的融合策略，为具有尖锐局部特征的PDE求解提供了有效解决方案，验证了自适应混合架构的优越性。

Abstract: Solutions to many partial differential equations (PDEs) display coexisting smooth global transport and localized sharp features within a single trajectory: shock fronts, thin interfaces, and concentrated high-frequency content sit on top of slowly varying backgrounds. This poses a challenge for neural operators: Fourier-based architectures mix nonlocal interactions efficiently but tend to under-resolve localized non-smooth features, whereas spatially local architectures recover fine detail at the cost of long-range propagation and rollout stability. Existing hybrid operators paper over this tension with a fixed, spatially uniform fusion that forces the same trade-off everywhere.
  We propose U-HNO, a U-shaped hybrid neural operator whose central design is Sparse-Point Adaptive Routing (SPAR): at every spatial location, a per-pixel hard mask selects whether the global Fourier branch or the local multi-scale Gaussian branch should dominate, and the sparsity ratio is a function of the local contrast of the routing signal, so smooth and shock-aligned regions receive different mixtures of global and local computation. SPAR is embedded in a hierarchical encoder-bottleneck-decoder backbone with skip connections so that the dual branches and the gate operate at every resolution. Training combines pointwise supervision with a finite-difference H^1 gradient term and a band-wise spectral consistency regularizer.
  Across benchmarks spanning 1D Burgers, Kuramoto-Sivashinsky, KdV, 2D advection, Allen-Cahn, Navier-Stokes, Darcy flow, and 3D transonic compressible Navier-Stokes from PDEBench, U-HNO achieves state-of-the-art rollout accuracy on the majority of tasks in both relative L^2 and H^1 metrics, with the largest gains on problems dominated by sharp localized features. Ablations show that removing any single component substantially degrades rollout error.

</details>


### [70] [Controlling Logical Collapse in LLMs via Algebraic Ontology Projection over F2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12968)
*Hisashi Miyashita,Mgnite Inc*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出代数本体投影（AOP）方法，将大模型隐状态投影到伽罗瓦域F2中，实现了高达93.33%的零样本包含准确率；引入语义结晶度（SC）指标预测模型性能，并发现系统提示与指令微调的结合可防止后期层崩溃现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型是否以形式可验证的代数结构内部编码本体论关系，试图揭示模型内部的逻辑结构是否可以被形式化访问，而非仅仅被近似。

Method: 提出代数本体投影（AOP）方法，在Liskov替换原则约束下将LLM隐状态投影到伽罗瓦域F2，仅使用42对关系对作为代数密钥；引入语义结晶度（SC）指标量化F2约束满足程度；不进行模型调优，仅通过提示工程实现。

Result: AOP在未见概念对上实现高达93.33%的零样本包含准确率（Gemma-2 Instruct优化提示），在多个模型家族中保持86.67%的一致准确率；代数结构具有强层级依赖性；SC指标可在无留出数据情况下预测零样本准确率；系统提示与指令微调的结合防止了后期层崩溃（10种条件中有7种观察到此现象）。

Conclusion: 研究发现将前向计算重构为代数组织的迭代过程，为开发逻辑结构不仅被近似而且可形式化访问的LLM开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Do large language models internally encode ontological relations in a formally verifiable algebraic structure? We introduce Algebraic Ontology Projection (AOP), which projects LLM hidden states into the Galois Field F2 under Liskov Substitution Principle constraints, using only 42 relational pairs as algebraic keys. AOP achieves up to 93.33% zero-shot inclusion accuracy on unseen concept pairs (Gemma-2 Instruct with optimized prompt), with consistent 86.67% accuracy observed across multiple model families -- with no model tuning, but through prompt alone.
  This algebraic structure is strongly layer-dependent. We introduce Semantic Crystallisation (SC), a metric that quantifies F2 constraint satisfaction relative to a random baseline and predicts zero-shot accuracy without held-out data. System prompts act as algebraic boundary conditions: only their combination with instruction tuning prevents Late-layer Collapse -- a systematic degradation of logical consistency in the final layers, observed in 7 of 10 conditions. These findings reframe forward computation as an iterative process of algebraic organisation, and open a path toward LLMs whose logical structure is not merely approximated, but formally accessible.

</details>


### [71] [Revisiting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards from a Contrastive Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12969)
*Feng Zhang,Xinhong Ma,Ziqiang Dong,Xi Leng,Jianfei Zhao,Xin Sun,Yang Yang,Guanjun Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ConSPO框架，通过对比序列级策略优化解决了GRPO算法的似然错位评分和分数不敏感信用分配两个结构限制，在数学推理任务上显著优于现有RLVR方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO作为RLVR的代表性算法存在两个结构限制：(1)似然错位评分，即优化的是截断比率代理分数而非生成似然；(2)分数不敏感信用分配，即在正负样本间分配信用时未考虑它们的相对分数差距。这些限制影响了模型推理能力的有效提升。

Method: 提出ConSPO框架：(1)用长度归一化的序列对数概率替代GRPO的截断比率分数，使优化分数与自回归生成似然对齐；(2)采用组内InfoNCE风格的对比目标，将正样本与同组负样本对比，使信用分配依赖相对分数；(3)引入课程调度的间隔机制，从早期粗粒度正负排序逐步过渡到后期更强的分离。

Result: 在多样化骨干模型、参数规模和训练数据集上的广泛评估表明，ConSPO在具有挑战性的数学推理基准测试上始终优于多个强RLVR基线方法。

Conclusion: ConSPO通过对比学习范式有效解决了GRPO的结构限制，实现了更好的似然对齐和基于相对分数的信用分配，为RLVR提供了更优的优化框架。

Abstract: RLVR has become a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLMs' reasoning capabilities, and GRPO is one of its most representative algorithms. In this paper, we first show that GRPO admits an equivalent discriminative reformulation as a weighted positive-negative score difference. Under this view, GRPO increases sequence-level scores of verified positive rollouts and decreases those of negative rollouts, where the scores are averages of clipped token-level importance sampling ratios. This reformulation reveals two structural limitations of GRPO: likelihood-misaligned scoring, where clipped ratio-based surrogate scores are optimized instead of generation likelihoods, and score-insensitive credit assignment, where rollout-level credit is assigned without accounting for relative score gaps between positive and negative rollouts in the same group. To address these limitations, we propose ConSPO, a framework for Contrastive Sequence-level Policy Optimization in RLVR. ConSPO replaces GRPO's clipped ratio-based scores with length-normalized sequence log-probabilities, aligning the optimized rollout scores with the likelihoods used in autoregressive generation. It then optimizes a group-wise InfoNCE-style objective that contrasts each positive rollout against negative distractors from the same group, enabling credit assignment to depend on their relative scores. This contrastive formulation amplifies updates for poorly separated positives while concentrating suppressive updates on high-scoring negatives. Moreover, ConSPO introduces a curriculum-scheduled margin, guiding optimization from coarse positive-negative ordering in early training toward stronger separation in later stages. Extensive evaluations across diverse backbone models, parameter scales, and training datasets show that ConSPO consistently outperforms several strong RLVR baselines on challenging mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [72] [CoRe-Gen: Robust Spectrum-to-Structure Generation under Imperfect Fingerprint Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12980)
*Tianbo Liu,Chixiang Lu,Jing Hao,Hengyu Zhang,Lifei Wang,Haibo Jiang,Xiaojuan Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRe-Gen框架，通过合成光谱预训练、频率感知指纹腐蚀和结构感知自回归解码等方法，解决了串联质谱分子结构 elucidation 中由指纹预测误差传播导致的条件不匹配问题，在NPLIB1基准上实现了新的SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的串联质谱分子结构解析方法通常分解为“光谱到指纹预测”和“指纹到结构解码”两个阶段。然而在部署时，解码器依赖的是预测的指纹而非真实指纹，引入了结构化误差并传播到生成过程中。这导致了根本性的条件不匹配问题：在干净输入上训练的模型必须在嘈杂、有偏差的预测下运行，特别是对于长尾子结构，性能受到严重影响。

Method: 本文提出CoRe-Gen框架，包含三个核心策略：1）通过合成光谱预训练编码器来改善中间条件；2）在解码器训练期间使用频率感知的指纹腐蚀来匹配部署时的噪声；3）利用结构感知的自回归解码缓解残余误差，采用组合式SELFIES表示、辅助结构监督和轻量级化学约束。

Result: 在标准基准测试中，CoRe-Gen在NPLIB1数据集上建立了新的最先进性能，Top-1准确率达到19.54%，Top-10准确率达到29.92%；同时在更具挑战性的MassSpecGym基准上保持竞争力。重要的是，该方法保留了自回归解码的效率优势。

Conclusion: CoRe-Gen成功弥合了训练与部署之间的条件不匹配差距，为在现实条件下进行鲁棒的光谱到结构生成提供了一个实用且可扩展的解决方案，在保证计算效率的同时显著提升了分子结构解析的准确性。

Abstract: Molecular structure elucidation from tandem mass spectra (MS/MS) remains challenging, particularly for de novo generation beyond database coverage. A common approach decomposes the task into spectrum-to-fingerprint prediction followed by fingerprint-to-structure decoding, enabling the use of large-scale molecular corpora. However, at deployment, the decoder relies on predicted rather than oracle fingerprints, introducing structured errors that propagate into generation. This results in a fundamental condition mismatch, where models trained on clean inputs must operate under noisy, biased predictions, especially for long-tail substructures.
  We present CoRe-Gen that explicitly addresses this gap. CoRe-Gen improves the intermediate condition via synthetic-spectrum pretraining of the encoder, matches deployment-time noise through frequency-aware fingerprint corruption during decoder training, and mitigates residual errors using structure-aware autoregressive decoding with compositional SELFIES representations, auxiliary structural supervision, and lightweight chemical constraints. Experiments on standard benchmarks show that CoRe-Gen establishes a new state of the art on NPLIB1, achieving 19.54\% Top-1 and 29.92\% Top-10 exact-match accuracy, while remaining competitive on the more challenging MassSpecGym benchmark. Importantly, CoRe-Gen preserves the efficiency advantages of autoregressive decoding, providing a practical and scalable solution for robust spectrum-to-structure generation under realistic conditions.

</details>


### [73] [Decision Tree Learning on Product Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12983)
*Arshia Soltani Moakahr,Faraz Ghahremani,Kiarash Banihashem,MohammadTaghi Hajiaghayi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将自顶向下贪心决策树学习算法的理论保证从均匀分布推广到了任意乘积分布，给出了贪心树相对于最优树的大小上界，并在最优树为满二叉树时改进了现有结果；同时提出了一个无需先验参数的实用算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树学习的自顶向下贪心启发式方法在实际中应用广泛，但其理论分析长期有限。Blanc等人在2020年首次在均匀分布下给出了该方法的严格理论保证，然而均匀分布假设过于理想化。为了弥补理论与实际应用之间的差距，需要将分析扩展到更一般的任意乘积分布情形，并消除对最优树参数的先验依赖。

Method: 研究者将理论分析从均匀分布推广到任意乘积分布，通过建立贪心启发式算法与最优决策树结构属性之间的联系，证明了对于任意可由最优决策树计算的函数，贪心算法构建的ε-近似树的大小上界为exp(Δ_opt D_opt log(e/ε))。此外，设计了一个基于贪心启发式的无参数算法，该算法无需预知最优树的大小或深度即可运行。

Result: 主要结果表明，对于任意乘积分布，贪心启发式算法生成的ε-近似决策树的大小上界为exp(Δ_opt D_opt log(e/ε))。当最优树是满二叉树时，该界限优于Blanc等人的结果，且适用于更广泛的分布类别。同时，提出的无参数算法在实际应用中更具优势，因为它不依赖于最优树参数的先验知识。

Conclusion: 本文成功将自顶向下贪心决策树学习算法的理论保证推广到了更一般的任意乘积分布，填补了现有理论在非均匀分布下的空白。研究结果不仅改进了特定情形下的理论界限，还提供了更加实用的无参数算法，为贪心决策树学习方法提供了坚实的理论基础和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Decision tree learning has long been a central topic in theoretical computer science, driven by its practical importance. A fundamental and widely used method for decision tree construction is the top-down greedy heuristic, which recursively splits on the most influential variable. Despite its empirical success, theoretical analysis of this heuristic has been limited. A recent breakthrough by Blanc et al. (ITCS, 2020) provided the first rigorous theoretical guarantees for the greedy approach, but only under the uniform distribution. We extend this analysis to the more general and practically relevant setting of arbitrary product distributions. Our main result shows that for any function $f$ computable by an optimal decision tree of size $s$, maximum depth $D_{\text{opt}}$, and average depth $Δ_{\text{opt}}$, the greedy heuristic constructs an $ε$-approximating tree whose size grows at most with $\exp\bigl(Δ_{\text{opt}} D_{\text{opt}} \log(e/ε)\bigr)$. In the special case where the optimal tree is a full binary tree, this bound improves upon the bound of Blanc et al. and holds under a strictly broader class of distributions. Moreover, we present an algorithm based on the top-down greedy heuristic that is entirely parameter-free -- it requires no prior knowledge of the optimal tree's size or depth -- offering a practical advantage over Blanc et al.'s method.

</details>


### [74] [Not Just RLHF: Why Alignment Alone Won't Fix Multi-Agent Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12991)
*Adarsh Kumarappan,Ananya Mujoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM多智能体系统在模拟同伴压力下易改变正确答案（此现象称为yield），研究发现此漏洞并非源于RLHF导致的迎合行为，而是存在于预训练模型中。通过激活修补技术定位到中间层窄窗口，发现压力抑制了正确推理特征。引入单一正确异见者可大幅降低yield，建议从机制层面而非提示层面进行防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究LLM多智能体系统在同伴压力下改变正确答案的漏洞机制。学界普遍将此现象归因于RLHF导致的迎合性（sycophancy），但缺乏系统验证。研究者希望厘清该漏洞的真实成因，定位其激活空间位置，并评估现有防御策略的有效性，为构建更鲁棒的多智能体系统提供理论指导。

Method: 研究采用跨四个模型家族的对照实验，比较预训练基座模型与其指令微调变体的yield表现。运用激活修补（activation patching）技术定位因果机制，通过注意力层和MLP层的消融实验确定责任窗口。设计双因素实验框架分解攻击面（通道框架和共识强度），并在不同评审规模（N=4,5,6）下验证稳定性。最后采用激活空间干预验证机制假设，测试异见者和提示级防御的效果。

Result: 预训练基座模型的yield率甚至高于指令微调版本，证伪了RLHF归因假说。漏洞定位在中间层的窄窗口，注意力层承担主要因果权重，修补该窗口上方可恢复96%的正确率差距。攻击面由通道框架和共识强度两个独立因素构成，在多数共识下产生47.5个百分点的yield差距，且在不同评审规模下保持稳定。压力机制抑制正确推理特征而非激活新的迎合电路。单一正确异见者可将yield降低54-73个百分点，而提示级防御在攻击变体下失效。

Conclusion: 该漏洞是预训练模型固有的机制性缺陷，而非RLHF微调产物，防御策略应针对机制本身设计。研究建议在多智能体流程层面引入结构化异见机制，而非依赖易被绕过的提示级防御方案，为构建更稳健的多智能体系统提供了理论依据和实践方向。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent pipelines flip from correct to incorrect answers under simulated peer disagreement at rates we term yield, a vulnerability widely attributed to RLHF-induced sycophancy. We test this attribution across four model families and find it largely wrong: pretrained base models exhibit the same substitution pattern as their Instruct variants, averaging higher yield than Instruct. Using activation patching, we localize the corruption to a narrow mid-layer window where attention carries the causal weight and MLP contribution is negligible; patching above this window restores 96% of the clean-to-pressured P(correct) gap. The attack surface decomposes into two independent factors (channel framing and consensus strength) whose interaction produces a 47.5 percentage-point yield gap at majority consensus, preserved across jury sizes $N \in \{4, 5, 6\}$. Two converging activation-space interventions show that pressure suppresses clean-reasoning features rather than activating a new sycophancy circuit. A single correctly-arguing dissenter reduces yield by 54-73 percentage points across all framings tested, whereas the strongest prompt-level defense fails on attack variants outside its design surface. Mitigations should target the mechanism, structured dissent at the pipeline level, rather than prompt-level defenses.

</details>


### [75] [DP-Muon: Differentially Private Optimization via Matrix-Orthogonalized Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12994)
*Jihwan Kim,Chenglin Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了DP-Muon，一种结合差分隐私与Muon矩阵优化器的训练方法，证明了其隐私保证并分析了优化误差；进一步提出DP-MuonBC消除偏差，在实验中提升了私有微调的效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管DP-SGD已被深入理解，但Muon优化器中逐样本裁剪、高斯噪声、动量与非线性正交化之间的交互缺乏系统性分析，亟需建立DP-Muon的理论框架与优化保证。

Method: 提出DP-Muon流程：对逐样本矩阵梯度进行裁剪，向裁剪后的批次平均值添加高斯噪声，再将动量与Newton-Schulz正交化作为后处理步骤；证明后处理不增加隐私成本，并建立有限视界与消失平稳性保证。进一步分析得出偏差源于Newton-Schulz映射后的矩阵热平滑效应，据此提出DP-MuonBC消除主导偏差项。

Result: 理论证明DP-Muon继承子采样高斯账户的隐私保证，优化误差界限分离了优化误差、裁剪残差、隐私噪声与Newton-Schulz近似误差；实验显示Muon风格矩阵更新改善私有微调，DP-MuonBC在不增加隐私预算的前提下进一步提升效用。

Conclusion: DP-Muon为差分隐私训练提供了一种有效的矩阵优化方案，理论分析阐明了偏差来源，偏差校正版本DP-MuonBC在保持隐私保证的同时提升了模型效用。

Abstract: We study differentially private (DP) training with Muon, a matrix-valued optimizer that updates hidden-layer weights using momentum followed by Newton--Schulz orthogonalization. While DP-SGD is well understood, the interaction between per-example clipping, Gaussian noise, momentum, and nonlinear orthogonalization in Muon has not been systematically analyzed. We formulate DP-Muon, a private Muon procedure that clips per-example matrix gradients, adds Gaussian noise to the clipped lot average, and then applies momentum and Newton--Schulz orthogonalization as post-processing. We prove that DP-Muon inherits the privacy guarantee certified by the corresponding same-lot subsampled Gaussian accountant, with no additional privacy cost from Muon-specific post-processing. On the optimization side, we establish finite-horizon and vanishing stationarity guarantees under per-matrix clipping, with bounds that separate optimization error, clipping residual, privacy noise, and Newton--Schulz approximation error. We further show that the DP-induced bias in Muon arises not in the linear momentum buffer itself, but after the nonlinear Newton--Schulz map, where Gaussian noise induces a matrix-valued heat-smoothing bias. This motivates DP-MuonBC, a bias-corrected variant that removes the leading output-level bias term while preserving the same privacy guarantee. Experiments on E2E and DART show that Muon-style matrix updates improve private fine-tuning, and that DP-MuonBC further improves utility without increasing the privacy budget.

</details>


### [76] [F-GRPO: Factorized Group-Relative Policy Optimization for Unified Candidate Generation and Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12995)
*Rohan Surana,Gagan Mundada,Junda Wu,Xintong Li,Yizhu Jiao,Bowen Jin,Sizhe Zhou,Tong Yu,Ritwik Sinha,Jiawei Han,Jingbo Shang,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出F-GRPO框架，通过因子化策略优化解决LLM生成式检索中候选生成与排序的端到端优化难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统检索管道将候选检索和重排序分离，而LLM将其统一为生成式过程。然而，在单一自回归过程中生成子集并排序面临优化挑战：模型在完整排序列表生成后才获得效用反馈，无法区分性能不佳是源于未能生成相关子集还是未能正确排序，这种信用分配差距使端到端优化不稳定且样本效率低下。现有系统通过分离候选生成与排序来应对，但这种解耦与下游效用仍存在错位，因为排序受限于其接收的候选集。

Method: 提出统一框架，在单一自回归rollout中执行候选生成和排序，通过因子化组相对策略优化（F-GRPO）进行端到端优化。框架将策略因子化为候选生成和排序（共享单个LLM主干），使用顺序不变的覆盖奖励和位置感知效用奖励进行联合训练。针对特定阶段的信用分配问题，在两阶段序列级目标中使用独立的组相对优势来分别处理生成和排序。

Result: 在序列推荐和多跳问答基准测试中，F-GRPO在top-ranked性能上优于GRPO和解耦基线，超越监督替代方案，并与强零样本重排序器保持竞争力，推理时无需架构变更。

Conclusion: F-GRPO成功桥接了候选生成与排序之间的信用分配差距，实现了LLM生成式检索的端到端优化，为统一生成式检索框架提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Traditional retrieval pipelines optimize utility through stages of candidate retrieval and reranking, where ranking operates over a predefined candidate set. Large Language Models (LLMs) broaden this into a generative process: given a candidate pool, an LLM can generate a subset and order it within a single autoregressive pass. However, this flexibility introduces a new optimization challenge: the model must search a combinatorial output space while receiving utility feedback only after the full ranked list is generated. Because this feedback is defined over the completed sequence, it cannot distinguish whether a poor result arises from failing to generate a relevant subset or from failing to rank that subset correctly. This credit assignment gap makes end-to-end optimization unstable and sample-inefficient. Existing systems often address this by separating candidate generation from ranking. However, such decoupling remains misaligned with downstream utility because ranking is limited by the candidate set it receives. To bridge this gap, we propose a unified framework that performs both within a single autoregressive rollout and optimizes them end-to-end via factorized group-relative policy optimization (F-GRPO). Our framework factorizes the policy into candidate generation and ranking while sharing a single LLM backbone, and jointly trains them with an order-invariant coverage reward and a position-aware utility reward. To address the resulting phase-specific credit assignment problem, we use separate group-relative advantages for generation and ranking within a two-phase sequence-level objective. Across sequential recommendation and multi-hop question answering benchmarks, F-GRPO improves top-ranked performance over GRPO and decoupled baselines, outperforms supervised alternatives, and remains competitive with strong zero-shot rerankers, with no architectural changes at inference time.

</details>


### [77] [Frequency Bias and OOD Generalization in Neural Operators under a Variable-Coefficient Wave Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12997)
*Runlong Xie,An Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了神经算子（FNO和DeepONet）在分布偏移下的泛化能力，发现两者在平滑性偏移下表现稳定，但在频率偏移下FNO对高频输入敏感而DeepONet退化更温和，揭示了架构表征偏置对泛化性能的重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在偏微分方程求解中展现出强大性能，但其在结构化分布偏移下的行为尚未得到充分理解。研究者希望探究神经算子在波传播场景下的泛化能力，特别是当输入频率和系数平滑度发生系统性变化时的表现。

Method: 采用一维变系数波动方程作为测试场景，对比分析两种代表性神经算子架构：傅里叶神经算子（FNO）和深度算子网络（DeepONet）。设计结构化的分布外（OOD）设置，独立变化输入频率和系数平滑度两个维度，系统评估模型在这些分布偏移条件下的表现。

Result: 在平滑性偏移下，两种模型均保持稳定性能，FNO误差更低；在频率偏移下，FNO在未见高频输入时误差急剧上升，而DeepONet虽然整体误差较高但退化更温和。分析表明这种差异源于两种架构对频率结构变化的表征和响应方式不同。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了神经算子在分布内性能与分布偏移泛化能力之间存在根本差距，强调了架构表征偏置在开发可靠神经算子中的关键作用，为构建能够在训练分布之外进行物理PDE模拟的更可靠模型提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Neural operators learn to map initial conditions to the terminal solution of partial differential equations (PDEs), providing a surrogate for the full operator mapping. This enables rapid prediction across different input configurations. While recent neural operator architectures have demonstrated strong performance on diverse PDE tasks, their behavior under structured distribution shifts remains insufficiently understood. To investigate this, we study operator learning in a wave propagation setting governed by a one-dimensional variable-coefficient wave equation, using two representative architectures, the Fourier Neural Operator (FNO) and the Deep Operator Network (DeepONet). To examine their generalization under distribution shifts, we consider structured out-of-distribution (OOD) settings that independently vary input frequency and coefficient smoothness. The results show that under smoothness shifts, both models maintain stable performance, with FNO achieving lower error. In contrast, under frequency shifts, FNO exhibits a sharp increase in error under unseen high-frequency inputs, whereas DeepONet shows milder degradation despite higher overall error. Our analysis reveals that these differences arise from how each architecture represents and responds to variations in frequency structure. Together, these findings highlight a fundamental gap between strong in-distribution performance and generalization under distribution shifts in operator learning, underscoring the role of architectural representation bias in developing more reliable neural operators for physics-based PDE simulations beyond the training distribution.

</details>


### [78] [\emph{DRIFT}: A Benchmark for Task-Free Continual Graph Learning with Continuous Distribution Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12998)
*Guiquan Sun,Xikun Zhang,Jingchao Ni,Dongjin Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出任务自由的无任务持续图学习视角，构建了DRIFT基准来模拟现实图数据流的分布漂移，发现现有方法在缺乏任务边界信息时性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的持续图学习方法通常采用基于任务的假设，将数据流划分为具有预定义边界的离散任务序列。然而，现实环境中数据分布持续演化，任务边界信息往往不可用，这种假设难以成立。为了更好地反映现实的非平稳环境，需要从任务自由的视角重新审视持续图学习。

Method: 提出统一框架将数据流建模为潜在任务分布的时变混合，实现对分布漂移的连续建模。基于该框架构建DRIFT基准，通过高斯参数化实现从硬性任务切换到平滑分布漂移的过渡动态谱系。在该无任务设置下评估代表性的持续学习方法。

Result: 在无任务设置下，现有持续学习方法相比传统基于任务的协议表现出显著的性能退化。许多现有方法隐式依赖任务边界信息，在现实的无任务图数据流场景中表现不佳。

Conclusion: 本研究强调了在现实非平稳条件下研究持续图学习的重要性，揭示了现有方法对任务边界信息的隐式依赖，并为该方向的未来研究提供了基准支持。

Abstract: Continual graph learning (CGL) aims to learn from dynamically evolving graphs while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Existing CGL approaches typically adopt a task-based formulation, where the data stream is partitioned into a sequence of discrete tasks with pre-defined boundaries. However, such assumptions rarely hold in real-world environments, where data distributions evolve continuously and task identity is often unavailable. To better reflect realistic non-stationary environments, we revisit continual graph learning from a task-free perspective. We propose a unified formulation that models the data stream as a time-varying mixture of latent task distributions, enabling continuous modeling of distribution drift. Based on this formulation, we construct DRIFT, a benchmark that spans a spectrum of transition dynamics ranging from hard task switches to smooth distributional drift through a Gaussian parameterization. We evaluate representative continual learning methods under this task-free setting and observe substantial performance degradation compared to traditional task-based protocols. Our findings indicate that many existing approaches implicitly rely on task boundary information and struggle under realistic task-free graph streams. This work highlights the importance of studying continual graph learning under realistic non-stationary conditions and provides a benchmark for future research in this direction. Our code is available at https://github.com/gqBond/DRIFT.

</details>


### [79] [JEDI: Joint Embedding Diffusion World Model for Online Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13013)
*Jing Yu Lim,Rushi Shah,Zarif Ikram,Samson Yu,Haozhe Ma,Tze-Yun Leong,Dianbo Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出JEDI，首个端到端在线潜在扩散世界模型，通过联合嵌入预测架构直接从扩散去噪损失中学习潜在空间，相比像素扩散基线减少43%显存、训练和采样速度提升2.5-3倍，在Atari100k上具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散世界模型存在矛盾：像素扩散有效但计算昂贵，最新的潜在扩散方法虽然高效但性能欠佳，且依赖于单独训练的潜在表示而非端到端世界模型目标。JEPA风格的预测表示学习在世界建模中展现出前景，扩散目标在多领域取得进展，两者结合为端到端潜在扩散世界模型提供了动机。

Method: 提出联合嵌入扩散（JEDI）方法，这是首个在线端到端潜在扩散世界模型。JEDI在JEPA框架内直接从扩散去噪损失中学习潜在空间，使用去噪过程学习和预测未来潜在表示，而非依赖重构和预训练模型。理论分析表明传统JEPA目标诱导预测信息瓶颈，条件扩散去噪允许密切相关的预测-压缩分解。

Result: 在Atari100k基准上具有竞争力；在可比较的设置下优于使用单独训练潜在表示的基线；相比像素扩散基线减少43%的VRAM使用；世界模型采样速度提升超过3倍；训练速度提升2.5倍；展现出与像素基线明显不同的任务级性能特征，表明端到端预测潜在表示的影响超越计算效率本身。

Conclusion: JEDI成功解决了现有扩散世界模型中效率与性能的权衡问题，证明了端到端学习潜在表示相比单独训练的潜在表示具有优势，为基于模型的强化学习提供了更高效且有效的新方向，同时揭示了端到端预测潜在表示带来的性能特征变化。

Abstract: Diffusion world models have recently become competitive for online model-based reinforcement learning, but current approaches expose a tension: pixel diffusion is effective but computationally expensive while the latest latent diffusion approach improves efficiency yet performs subpar. The latter also relies on separately trained latents rather than the end-to-end world-model objectives that have driven much of modern MBRL progress. In particular, JEPA-style predictive representation learning has emerged as an especially promising direction for world modeling and MBRL. Concurrently, diffusion-style objectives have gained traction across multiple domains, with iterative refinement as a promising approach for multimodal and stochastic targets. Taken together, these trends motivate Joint Embedding DIffusion (JEDI), the first online end-to-end latent diffusion world model. JEDI learns its latent space directly from the diffusion denoising loss with a JEPA framework, using denoising to learn and predict future latents rather than relying on reconstruction and pretrained models. We provide a theoretical motivation showing that conventional JEPA objectives induce a predictive information bottleneck, and that conditional diffusion denoising admits a closely related predictive-compression decomposition. Empirically, JEDI is competitive on Atari100k and outperforms the baseline with seperately trained latents where directly comparable. Relative to the pixel diffusion baseline, JEDI uses 43% less VRAM, over 3$\times$ faster world-model sampling, and 2.5$\times$ faster training. JEDI also exhibits a markedly different task-level performance profile from the pixel baseline, suggesting that end-to-end predictive latents change more than compute alone.

</details>


### [80] [Rethinking Efficient Graph Coarsening via a Non-Selfishness Principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13021)
*Xu Bai,Bin Lu,Kun Zhang,Shengbo Chen,Xinbing Wang,Chenghu Zhou,Meng Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于非自私原则的线性复杂度图粗化方法NOPE及其加速变体NOPE*，在保证学习性能的同时实现了比现有方法快1-3个数量级的速度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图粗化方法大多依赖成对相似性匹配，每个节点基于全局信息独立寻找最佳匹配对象，这种自私匹配范式导致算法产生巨大的计算和内存开销。

Method: 转向非自私原则，优先考虑邻域的集体干扰效应，提出了具有线性内存消耗和近似线性计算复杂度的NOPE方法；基于局部各向同性假设，进一步提出了更快的变体NOPE*，将O(δ·d)的干扰评估降低到O(d)。

Result: NOPE*比NOPE实现1.8-10倍加速，超越几乎所有基线方法1-3个数量级；在粗化图上的学习性能与原图相当，甚至因图信息紧凑而在某些任务上优于基于LLM的图推理。

Conclusion: 非自私原则能有效解决图粗化中的计算瓶颈问题，所提方法在效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡，为大规模图处理提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Graph coarsening is a graph dimensionality reduction technique that aims to construct a smaller and more tractable graph while preserving the essential structural and semantic properties of the original graph. However, most existing methods rely on pair-wise similarity matching, where each node independently searches for its best partner based on global information. This selfishness matching paradigm incurs substantial computational and memory overhead. To address this problem, we shift to a non-selfishness principle that prioritizes the collective interference of neighborhood in coarsening, and propose an efficient method named NOPE, which achieves linear memory consumption and near-linear computational complexity in the number of nodes. Furthermore, we derive a faster variant NOPE*, which reduces O(δ\dot d) interference evaluation to O(d) based on the local isotropy assumption, and consequently alleviates the computational bottleneck for high-degree nodes. Experimental results show that NOPE* achieves 1.8-10\times speedup over NOPE and surpass almost all baselines with 1-3 orders of magnitude acceleration. Meanwhile, learning on coarsened graphs yields comparable performance to original graphs, and can even show superior performance over LLM-based graph reasoning owing to compact graph information. The code can be available at https://github.com/dazonglian/NOPE-main.

</details>


### [81] [Offline Two-Player Zero-Sum Markov Games with KL Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13025)
*Claire Chen,Yuheng Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Zixuan Xie,Shuze Daniel Liu,Nan Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ROSE框架和SOS-MD算法，在离线双人零和马尔可夫博弈中，仅利用KL正则化（无需显式悲观主义）即可稳定学习，并在单侧集中性条件下实现了快速的$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离线双人零和马尔可夫博弈学习方法通常依赖显式的悲观主义（pessimism）来应对分布偏移问题，这增加了算法设计的复杂性。本文旨在探索是否可以仅通过KL正则化来稳定学习并保证收敛，从而简化算法设计并提升收敛速率。

Method: 首先提出了正则化离线序列均衡（ROSE）理论框架；随后提出了序列离线自博弈镜像下降（SOS-MD）算法，这是一种基于最小二乘价值估计和迭代自博弈更新的无模型算法。

Result: ROSE框架在单侧集中性条件下实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$的快速收敛率，优于未正则化设置下的标准$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$速率；SOS-MD算法的最后迭代也达到了相同的$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$统计速率，且优化误差仅为$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$并随迭代次数趋于零。

Conclusion: KL正则化本身足以在离线双人零和马尔可夫博弈中稳定学习并保证收敛，无需额外的显式悲观主义机制，从而实现了更优的收敛速率。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning Nash equilibria in offline two-player zero-sum Markov games. While existing approaches often rely on explicit pessimism to address distribution shift, we show that KL regularization alone suffices to stabilize learning and guarantee convergence. We first introduce Regularized Offline Sequential Equilibrium (ROSE), a theoretical framework that achieves a fast $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$ convergence rate under \textit{unilateral concentrability}, improving over the standard $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{n})$ rates in unregularized settings. We then propose Sequential Offline Self-play Mirror Descent (SOS-MD), a practical model-free algorithm based on least-squares value estimation and iterative self-play updates. We prove that the last iterate of SOS-MD attains the same $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/n)$ statistical rate up to a vanishing optimization error of order $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/\sqrt{T})$ in the number of self-play iterations $T$.

</details>


### [82] [Understanding and Accelerating the Training of Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13026)
*Chunsan Hong,Sanghyun Lee,Chieh-Hsin Lai,Satoshi Hayakawa,Yuhta Takida,Yuki Mitsufuji,Seungryong Kim,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言局部性偏置导致掩码扩散模型训练缓慢，并提出钟形时间采样策略，在保持性能的同时将训练速度提升约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型作为自回归模型的替代方案虽具潜力，但训练速度显著慢于后者，这阻碍了其向更大规模模型的扩展。作者旨在加速MDM训练同时保持最终性能。

Method: 首先分析MDM训练缓慢的原因，发现语言局部性偏置（token的预测信息集中在邻近位置）是主要因素。基于此分析，提出钟形时间采样训练策略作为简单有效的解决方案。

Result: 在One Billion Word基准测试上，新方法达到相同验证负对数似然的速度比标准训练快约4倍。在生成困惑度、零样本困惑度及多个下游任务基准上也展现出更快的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过识别并解决局部性偏置带来的训练障碍，钟形时间采样策略成功加速了MDM训练，为大规模MDM模型的发展铺平道路。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) have emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive models (ARMs) for language modeling. However, MDMs are known to learn substantially more slowly than ARMs, which may become problematic when scaling MDMs to larger models. Therefore, we ask the following question: how can we accelerate standard MDM training while maintaining its final performance? To this end, we first provide a detailed analysis of why MDM training is slow. We find that the main factor is the locality bias of language: the predictive information for a token is concentrated in nearby positions. We further investigate how this bias slows learning and suggest a simple yet effective remedy: bell-shaped time sampling as a training strategy. Notably, MDMs trained with our training recipe reach the same validation negative log-likelihood (NLL) up to $\sim4\times$ faster than standard training on One Billion Word Benchmark (LM1B). We also show faster improvements in generative perplexity, zero-shot perplexity, and downstream task performance on various benchmarks.

</details>


### [83] [FeatCal: Feature Calibration for Post-Merging Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13030)
*Yanggan Gu,Shuo Cai,Zihao Wang,Wenjun Wang,Yuanyi Wang,Pengkai Wang,Sirui Huang,Su Lu,Jianmin Wu,Hongxia Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FeatCal方法，通过前向逐层校准合并模型权重以减少特征漂移，在CLIP和GLUE基准上显著优于现有基线，且具有更高的样本效率和更低的校准成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并可将多个任务专家模型合并为一个模型，避免联合训练、重训练或部署多个专家模型，但合并后的模型性能通常仍低于任务专家模型。本文旨在通过特征漂移（即合并模型与专家模型在同一输入上产生特征的差异）来研究这一性能差距。

Method: 本文提出FeatCal方法，利用小型校准数据集以前向顺序逐层校准合并模型的权重。该方法将特征漂移分解为上游传播和局部不匹配，追踪其在后续层中的传播和组合方式，并将最终特征漂移与输出漂移关联。FeatCal采用高效的闭式解更新模型权重，无需梯度下降、迭代优化或额外模块。

Result: 在CLIP和GLUE主要基准测试中，FeatCal显著优于最接近的后合并校准基线Surgery和ProbSurgery。在CLIP-ViT-B/32 Task Arithmetic上达到85.5%（基线分别为77.0%/78.8%），在FLAN-T5-base GLUE上达到85.2%（基线分别为83.7%/82.2%）。每任务仅需8个样本即可达到82.9%，每任务256个样本仅需53秒，速度比两个基线快约4倍。

Conclusion: FeatCal通过减少特征漂移并保持接近合并权重，成功提升了模型合并的性能，同时保持了模型合并的优势。该方法在样本效率和校准成本方面均优于现有方法，为模型合并领域提供了一种高效且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Model merging combines task experts into one model and avoids joint training, retraining, or deploying many expert models, but the merged model often still underperforms task experts. We study this performance gap through feature drift, the difference between features produced by the merged model and by the expert on the same input. Our theory decomposes this drift into upstream propagation and local mismatch, tracks how it propagates and combines through later layers in forward order, and links final feature drift to output drift. This view motivates FeatCal, which uses a small calibration set to calibrate the merged model weights layer by layer in forward order, reducing feature drift while staying close to merged weights and preserving the benefits of model merging. FeatCal uses an efficient closed-form solution to update model weights, with no gradient descent, iterative optimization, or extra modules. On the main CLIP and GLUE benchmarks, FeatCal beats Surgery and ProbSurgery, the closest post-merging calibration baselines: 85.5% vs. 77.0%/78.8% on CLIP-ViT-B/32 Task Arithmetic (TA) and 85.2% vs. 83.7%/82.2% on FLAN-T5-base GLUE. On CLIP-ViT-B/32, 8 examples per task reach 82.9%, and 256 examples per task take 53 seconds, about 4x faster than both baselines, showing better sample efficiency and lower calibration cost.

</details>


### [84] [What Information Matters? Graph Out-of-Distribution Detection via Tri-Component Information Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13032)
*Danny Wang,Ruihong Qiu,Zi Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了TIDE框架，通过三成分信息分解显式分离特征特定、结构特定和联合信息，过滤虚假信息，有效提升了图神经网络在分布外（OOD）节点上的检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的图神经网络在节点分类任务中易受特征和结构分布外（OOD）偏移的影响。标准监督学习目标倾向于捕获特征或结构中的虚假信号，导致模型在分布变化时表现脆弱，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出了TIDE（Tri-Component Information Decomposition）框架，显式将信息分解为特征特定、结构特定和联合三个成分。该框架保留与标签相关的联合信息，同时过滤虚假的特征特定和结构特定信息，以增强分布内（ID）与OOD节点的分离度。并采用信息瓶颈目标替代标准监督学习目标。

Result: 在七个数据集上的广泛实验表明，TIDE在保持竞争性ID准确率的同时，相比强基线在FPR95指标上实现了高达34%的提升，验证了其在OOD检测上的有效性。理论分析也证明了信息瓶颈目标在图OOD检测中优于标准SL。

Conclusion: TIDE框架通过三成分信息分解有效解决了图神经网络中的OOD脆弱性问题，显著提升了分布外检测能力，为图数据的鲁棒学习提供了新的思路和理论支持。

Abstract: Graph neural networks are widely used for node classification, but they remain vulnerable to out-of-distribution (OOD) shifts in node features and graph structure. Prior work established that methods trained with standard supervised learning (SL) objectives tend to capture spurious signals from either features and/or structure, leaving the model fragile under distributional changes. To address this, we propose textscTide, a textbfnovel and effective underlineTri-Component underlineInformation underlineDecomposition framework that textbfexplicitly decomposes information into textitfeature-specific, structure-specific and joint components. textscTide aims to textbfpreserve only the label-relevant part of the joint information while textbffiltering out spurious feature- and structure-specific information, thereby enhancing the separation between in-distribution (ID) and OOD nodes. Beyond the framework, we provide theoretical and empirical analyses showing that an information bottleneck objective is preferable to standard SL for graph OOD detection, with higher ID confidence and a greater entropy gap between ID and OOD data. Extensive experiments across seven datasets confirm the efficacy of textscTide, achieving up to a 34% improvement in FPR95 over strong baselines while maintaining competitive ID accuracy.

</details>


### [85] [Large Language Models Lack Temporal Awareness of Medical Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13045)
*Zihan Guan,Qiao Jin,Guangzhi Xiong,Fangyuan Chen,Mengxuan Hu,Qingyu Chen,Yifan Peng,Zhiyong Lu,Anil Vullikanti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文构建了首个医学领域时间感知基准TempoMed-Bench，发现LLMs在医学知识上缺乏时间意识：对最新知识的性能随时间线性下降而非知识截止点突变、对过时历史知识的回忆能力显著弱于最新知识、且存在时间不一致行为，结合搜索工具也无法有效解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLMs医学知识评估方法主要基于非时间性的考试式基准，但现实中医学知识具有动态性并持续演变。缺乏时间上下文的评估可能无法完整评估LLMs对特定时间医学知识的推理能力，且大多数医学数据是历史性的，模型不仅需要回忆正确知识，还需要知道该知识在何时是正确的。

Method: 构建TempoMed-Bench，这是首个通过演变的指南知识评估LLMs在医学领域时间感知的基准。基于该基准进行评估分析，探究LLMs在时间特定医学知识上的表现，并测试集成代理搜索工具是否能缓解该问题。

Result: 发现LLMs在医学知识上缺乏时间意识，具体表现为：(1)对最新医学知识的性能随时间呈逐渐线性下降而非知识截止点的剧烈变化；(2)对过时历史医学知识的回忆能力显著弱于最新建议，准确率仅为最新知识的25.37%-53.89%；(3)常表现出时间不一致行为，相邻年份间的预测不规则波动；(4)集成代理搜索工具后问题仍无法轻易解决（准确率下降3.15%-14.14%）。

Conclusion: 时间感知问题是一个重要但未被充分探索的挑战，该工作凸显了开发能够更好编码时间特定医学知识的LLMs的必要性，激励未来在该方向的研究。

Abstract: The existing methods for evaluating the medical knowledge of Large Language Models (LLMs) are largely based on atemporal examination-style benchmarks, while in reality, medical knowledge is inherently dynamic and continuously evolves as new evidence emerges and treatments are approved. Consequently, evaluating medical knowledge without a temporal context may provide an incomplete assessment of whether LLMs can accurately reason about time-specific medical knowledge.
