Moreover, most medical data are historical, requiring the models not only to recall the correct knowledge, but also to know when that knowledge is correct. To bridge the gap, we built TempoMed-Bench, the first-of-its-kind benchmark for evaluating the temporal awareness of the LLMs in the medical domain through evolving guideline knowledge. Based on the TempoMed-Bench, our evaluation analysis first reveals that LLMs lack temporal awareness in medical knowledge through the key findings: (1) model performance on up-to-date medical knowledge exhibits a gradual linear decline over time rather than a sharp knowledge-cutoff behavior, suggesting that parametric medical knowledge is not strictly bounded by knowledge cutoffs; (2) LLMs consistently struggle more with recalling outdated historical medical knowledge than with up-to-date recommendations: accuracy of historical knowledge is only 25.37%-53.89% of up-to-date knowledge, indicating potential knowledge forgetting effects during training; and (3) LLMs often exhibit temporally inconsistent behaviors, where predictions fluctuate irregularly across neighboring years. We also show that the temporal awareness problem is a challenge that cannot be easily solved when integrated with agentic search tools (-3.15%-14.14%). This work highlights an important yet underexplored challenge and motivates future research on developing LLMs that can better encode time-specific medical knowledge.

</details>


### [86] [Bridging Domain Gaps with Target-Aligned Generation for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13054)
*Minung Kim,Jeongmo Kim,Gwanwoo Choi,Seungyul Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TCE框架，通过目标对齐的覆盖扩展策略，解决了跨域离线强化学习中源域数据利用与分布不匹配的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨域离线强化学习旨在利用预收集数据集将源域策略适应到目标域，但源域与目标域间的环境动力学差异导致分布不匹配，尤其是在目标域数据极度有限的情况下，如何有效利用源域数据成为关键挑战。

Method: 提出Target-aligned Coverage Expansion (TCE)框架，基于理论分析指导源域数据的使用策略：直接整合接近目标域的转换，或通过目标对齐的生成方式扩展状态覆盖。核心采用双评分生成模型，在扩展的状态区域上合成与目标域一致的转换。

Result: 在多种跨域环境上的广泛实验表明，TCE方法持续性地优于现有的最先进跨域离线强化学习基线方法。

Conclusion: TCE框架成功解决了跨域离线强化学习中的数据利用与分布匹配问题，通过智能的数据选择和生成策略，在有限目标域数据条件下实现了更好的策略适应性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain offline reinforcement learning aims to adapt a policy from a source domain to a target domain using only pre-collected datasets, where environment dynamics may differ. A key challenge is to leverage source data while reducing distributional mismatch, particularly when the target dataset is extremely limited. To address this, we propose Target-aligned Coverage Expansion (TCE), a framework that decides how source data should be used, either by directly incorporating target-near transitions or by expanding state coverage through target-aligned generation, guided by theoretical analysis. TCE builds on a dual score-based generative model to synthesize target-consistent transitions over an expanded state region. Extensive experiments across diverse cross-domain environments show that TCE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art cross-domain offline RL baselines.

</details>


### [87] [Ergodic Trajectory Design by Learned Pushforward Maps: Provable Coverage via Conditional Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13063)
*Ehsan Aghazadeh,Masoud Malekzadeh,Ahmad Ghasemi,Hossein Pishro-Nik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于最优传输流匹配的“推前”框架，将遍历覆盖问题解耦为潜在轨迹设计与密度映射，实现了对目标空间密度的高效、可证明收敛的轨迹生成，支持多智能体复用与多种约束的统一处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无人机数据采集、机器人探索和移动监测等应用中，设计时间平均占用与规定空间密度匹配的连续轨迹（遍历覆盖问题）至关重要。对于飞行智能体，这一挑战尤为严峻：轨迹必须在覆盖保真度与严格的能量预算、禁飞区和加速度限制之间取得平衡。现有方法要么在线重新优化每条轨迹（成本随视界增长，且需为每个目标、智能体和实例重新运行），要么依赖需针对每个新约束重新推导的定制化解析构造，缺乏通用性与效率。

Method: 提出了一种“推前（epushforward）”框架，将遍历性与密度匹配解耦：首先，一个解析的潜在轨迹在简单的环形域上提供精确的均匀遍历性；然后，通过最优传输条件流匹配（CFM）离线学习一个单一映射，将潜在占用传输到规定的目标密度。组合后的轨迹相对于学习的推前分布渐近遍历，且与目标的偏差由流匹配训练损失控制。许多可微的操作约束（禁飞区、加速度上限或公平性惩罚）作为加性软惩罚进入训练损失，无需重新推导设计。

Result: 证明了三个结果：（1）加速度-能量界限；（2）在轨迹周期数K方面的$O(1/\sqrt{K})$遍历收敛速率；（3）近似误差界限。这些结果结合成一个端到端的覆盖界限，可从CFM训练诊断中估计（在$v_θ$上给定架构Lipschitz界限时可认证）。一旦针对给定目标密度和约束集训练完成，该映射可服务于无限数量的轨迹和多智能体集群，无需逐智能体重新训练。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了遍历覆盖问题的高效求解，通过离线学习统一处理多种约束，避免了在线优化或定制化推导的局限性，并提供了可证明的收敛性与误差界限，为无人机辅助数据采集、机器人探索和移动监测等应用提供了一种通用、高效且有理论保证的解决方案。

Abstract: Designing continuous trajectories whose time-averaged occupancy provably matches a prescribed spatial density (the \emph{ergodic coverage} problem) is central to UAV-assisted data collection and sensing, robotic exploration, and mobile monitoring. For flying agents in particular, this challenge is acute: trajectories must balance coverage fidelity against tight energy budgets, no-fly zones, and acceleration limits. Existing methods either re-optimize each trajectory online (with cost growing in the horizon and re-running for every target, agent, and realization) or rely on bespoke analytical constructions that must be re-derived for each new constraint. We propose a \emph{epushforward} framework that decouples ergodicity from density matching: an analytic latent trajectory provides exact uniform ergodicity on a simple annular domain, and a single map, learned offline by optimal-transport conditional flow matching, transports this latent occupancy onto the prescribed target density. The composed trajectory is then asymptotically ergodic with respect to the learned pushforward distribution, with deviation from the target controlled by the flow-matching training loss. Once trained for a given target density and constraint set, the map serves an unbounded number of trajectories and a multi-agent fleet without per-agent retraining, and many differentiable operational constraints (no-fly zones, acceleration ceilings, or fairness penalties) enter as additive soft penalties in the training loss without re-deriving the design. We prove three results (an acceleration-energy bound, an $O(1/\sqrt{K})$ ergodic convergence rate in the number of trajectory cycles $K$, and an approximation-error bound) that combine into an end-to-end coverage bound estimable from CFM training diagnostics (certified given an architectural Lipschitz bound on $v_θ$).

</details>


### [88] [Local Inverse Geometry Can Be Amortized](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13068)
*Aaditya L. Kachhadiya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Deceptron框架，通过学习可复用的反向算子来替代传统求解线性系统的曲率感知方法（如Gauss-Newton），在保持一阶等价性的同时显著降低了计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性逆问题中，一阶方法计算廉价但脆弱，而曲率感知方法（如Gauss-Newton和Levenberg-Marquardt）虽能获得更强的优化方向，却需要反复求解基于Jacobian的线性化系统，计算代价高昂。

Method: 构建双向代理模型Deceptron，提出D-IPG迭代求解器，通过Jacobian组合惩罚（JCP）训练反向Jacobian作为正向Jacobian的局部左逆，将残差校正后的测量空间提案拉回潜在空间。

Result: 在七个PDE逆问题基准测试中，D-IPG优于标准基线，在六问题可靠性套件中达到94.8%的平均成功率，在主要基准测试中以高达77倍更低的推理时间求解成本达到相当或更好的恢复质量。

Conclusion: 证明了D-IPG在局部伪逆一致性条件下与阻尼Gauss-Newton一阶等价，偏差由组合误差和条件数控制，为非线性逆问题提供了一种高效且可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: Nonlinear inverse problems often trade inexpensive but fragile first-order updates against curvature-aware methods such as Gauss-Newton and Levenberg-Marquardt, which obtain stronger directions by repeatedly solving Jacobian-based linearized systems. We propose a learned alternative: amortize local inverse geometry into a reusable reverse operator. Our framework learns a bidirectional surrogate, Deceptron, and deploys it through D-IPG (Deceptron Inverse-Preconditioned Gradient), an iterative solver that pulls residual-corrected measurement-space proposals back to latent space. The key mechanism is a Jacobian Composition Penalty (JCP), which trains the reverse Jacobian to act as a local left inverse of the forward Jacobian; its runtime counterpart, RJCP, measures the same inverse-consistency error along optimization trajectories. We prove that D-IPG is first-order equivalent to damped Gauss-Newton under local pseudoinverse consistency, with deviation controlled by composition error and conditioning. Across seven PDE inverse-problem benchmarks, D-IPG outperforms standard baselines, achieves 94.8% mean success across the six-problem reliability suite, and reaches comparable or better recovery quality at up to 77x lower inference-time solve cost on the main benchmarks.

</details>


### [89] [Spectral Flattening Is All Muon Needs: How Orthogonalization Controls Learning Rate and Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13079)
*Tien-Phat Nguyen,Truong Nguyen,Minh-Phuc Truong,Tuc Nguyen,James Bailey,Trung Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Muon优化器表现优异的核心机制是“谱扁平化”，证明其最大稳定步长取决于梯度奇异值的平均值而非最大值，从而突破了标准梯度下降的瓶颈。实验表明Muon能在导致SGD发散的大学习率下保持稳定，并以更快的速度收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器通过对动量缓冲区进行正交化处理（将奇异值替换为1），能够容忍更大的学习率并实现更快的收敛，但其背后的理论机制尚不明确。本文旨在从理论层面解释这一经验现象，阐明Muon为何优于其他优化器。

Method: 本文提出两个核心理论分析：首先，证明Muon的最大稳定步长与梯度的平均奇异值成正比，而非受限于最大奇异值；其次，将Muon重新表述为一种预条件梯度方法，并在Kronecker分解曲率模型下，证明其能改善有效收敛因子，改善程度由梯度协方差谱控制。

Result: 理论分析表明Muon突破了标准梯度下降受最大奇异值限制的瓶颈。大量实验验证了理论结果：在导致SGD前几次迭代即发散的大学习率下，Muon仍保持稳定；即使在相同步长下，Muon也能比SGD更早达到精度里程碑。

Conclusion: 本文从几何角度为Muon优化器的实证成功提供了有原则的理论解释。核心机制是谱扁平化，它通过改变优化景观的几何结构，使得Muon能够以更大的步长稳定迭代，从而加速收敛。

Abstract: Muon orthogonalizes the momentum buffer before each update, replacing its singular values with ones via Newton-Schulz iterations. This simple change lets Muon tolerate far larger learning rates and converge faster than other optimizers, but why? We show that the mechanism is spectral flattening, and develop two results around it. First, we prove that Muon's maximal stable step size scales with the average singular value of the gradient rather than the largest, which bottlenecks standard gradient descent. Second, we recast Muon as a preconditioned gradient method and show, under a Kronecker-factored curvature model, that it improves the effective convergence factor, with the improvement controlled by the spectrum of the gradient covariance. Extensive experiments validate both results: Muon remains stable at learning rates that cause SGD to diverge within the first few iterations, and reaches accuracy milestones several epochs earlier even at identical step sizes. Taken together, our results offer a principled, geometric explanation for Muon's empirical success.

</details>


### [90] [Bayesian Nonparametric Mixed-Effect ODEs with Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13088)
*Julien Martinelli,Maksim Sinelnikov,Harri Lähdesmäki,Quentin Clairon,Mélanie Prague*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MEGPODE，一种贝叶斯非参数混合效应常微分方程（ODE）模型，通过将每个主体的向量场分解为共享的总体成分和主体特定的偏差，并赋予高斯过程（GP）先验，解决了现有方法在处理异质性动态系统时的局限性，并在多个基准测试中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在药代动力学、系统生物学等领域，异质性是固有的，不同主体遵循相关但不同的连续时间动力学。经典的非线性混合效应ODE模型依赖于参数化向量场，容易受到结构错误设定和未建模机制的影响。现有的非参数ODE方法通常假设单一共享动态系统，而非显式的混合效应层次结构。

Method: MEGPODE模型将每个主体的向量场分解为共享的总体成分和主体特定的偏差，两者均赋予高斯过程（GP）先验。为避免在训练过程中对每个主体重复求解ODE，结合了状态空间GP轨迹先验和虚拟配置观测，实现了Kalman平滑轨迹更新和向量场的闭式回归。

Result: 在涵盖振荡、生物医学系统的受控异质性ODE基准测试中，MEGPODE在总体场恢复和主体级轨迹预测方面均优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: MEGPODE提供了一种有效的贝叶斯非参数方法，用于建模具有内在异质性的动态系统，能够在保持原则性不确定性量化的同时，准确捕捉主体特定的动态特征。

Abstract: Dynamical modelling is central to many scientific domains, including pharmacometrics, systems biology, physiology, and epidemiology. In these settings, heterogeneity is often intrinsic: different subjects or units follow related but distinct continuous-time dynamics. Classical nonlinear mixed-effects Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) models address this by combining population-level structure with subject-specific effects, but they rely on a parametric vector field and are therefore vulnerable to structural misspecification and unmodelled mechanisms. This motivates nonparametric approaches that can retain principled uncertainty quantification, yet existing nonparametric ODE methods typically assume a single shared dynamical system rather than an explicit mixed-effect hierarchy over subject-specific dynamics. We propose MEGPODE, a Bayesian nonparametric mixed-effect ODE model in which each subject's vector field is decomposed into a shared population component and a subject-specific deviation, both endowed with Gaussian process (GP) priors. To avoid repeated ODE solves per subject during training, we combine state-space GP trajectory priors with virtual collocation observations, yielding Kalman-smoothing trajectory updates and closed-form regressions for the vector fields. Across controlled heterogeneous ODE benchmarks spanning oscillatory, biomedical systems, MEGPODE improves population-field recovery and subject-level trajectory prediction relative to strong baselines.

</details>


### [91] [Margin-calibrated Classifier Guidance for Property-driven Synthesis Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13101)
*Najwa Laabid,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出序列完成排序（SCR）方法，通过校准分类器来指导逆合成规划，将多步求解率从16.8%大幅提升至95.3%，并有效缩小了无模板与有模板方法之间的多样性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合成规划中，通常使用预训练的单步逆合成模型反复调用来生成反应序列。虽然分类器指导可以在推理过程中引导模型输出满足特定约束或化学家偏好的反应，但研究发现，使用交叉熵损失训练的辅助分类器无法有效覆盖从稀疏单断裂数据集中学习到的无条件token级分布，导致指导效果不佳。

Method: 提出一种名为序列完成排序（SCR）的新方法，该方法采用对比论证和基于边界的损失来校准分类器，使其能够在解码过程中有意义地区分不同的续补序列。研究形式化地证明了边界校准分类器可以扩展在引导束搜索下可达到的满足属性序列的集合。

Result: 在USPTO-190数据集上，SCR方法将多步求解率从无引导生成器的16.8%显著提高到反应类型指导的78.4%和Tanimoto指导的95.3%，为之前基线方法无法解决的33个目标（17.4%）解锁了有效路径，并有效缩小了无模板和有模板方法之间长期存在的多样性差距。

Conclusion: SCR方法成功克服了传统分类器指导在逆合成规划中的不足，通过边界校准显著提升了求解率和路径有效性，为化学合成规划提供了一种有效的指导策略，填补了无模板与有模板方法之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Synthesis planning seeks an efficient sequence of chemical reactions that produce a target molecule. Typically, a pretrained single-step (autoregressive) retrosynthesis model is repeatedly invoked to generate such a sequence. Classifier guidance can, in principle, help steer the output of single-step model toward reactions that satisfy specific constraints or accommodate chemist's preferences during inference without having to retrain the autoregressive generator. We expose the insufficiency of auxiliary classifiers trained with cross-entropy loss to override the unconditional token-level distributions learned from typical sparse single-disconnection reaction datasets. We overcome this issue with a novel method called Sequence Completion Ranking (SCR), which employs contrastive argumentation and a margin-based loss to calibrate the classifier so that it can meaningfully discriminate between continuations during decoding. We formally establish that margin-calibrated classifiers can expand the set of property-satisfying sequences reachable under guided beam search. Empirically, on USPTO-190, given chemist-specified guidance targets, SCR substantially improves multi-step solve rates from $16.8\%$ (unguided generator) to $78.4\%$ with reaction-type guidance and $95.3\%$ with Tanimoto guidance, unlocking valid routes for 33 targets ($17.4\%$) previously unsolvable with baselines. Our method also effectively closes the long-standing diversity gap between template-free and template-based methods.

</details>


### [92] [MLGIB: Multi-Label Graph Information Bottleneck for Expressive and Robust Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13126)
*Chaokai Wu,Haofu Shi,Ningxuan Ma,Jianghong Ma,Xiaofeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多标签图神经网络中的过压缩问题，提出多标签图信息瓶颈（MLGIB）框架，通过保留预测性标签信号并抑制无关噪声来提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图神经网络在深度消息传递中存在过压缩问题，在多标签图场景下，相邻节点往往只共享有限标签，而在许多无关标签上存在差异，导致预测信号被噪声标签信息稀释，形成一种独特的失效模式。

Method: 提出多标签图信息瓶颈（MLGIB），将多标签消息传递建模为无关标签噪声约束下的信息传输问题。构建马尔可夫依赖空间并推导可处理的变分边界，其中下界最大化与目标标签的互信息，上界约束冗余源信息，从而形成端到端的标签感知消息传递架构。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，MLGIB相比现有方法取得了一致的性能提升，验证了该框架的有效性和通用性。

Conclusion: MLGIB通过平衡表达性和鲁棒性，成功解决了多标签图中的过压缩问题，为多标签图学习提供了一个有效的通用框架。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) suffer from over-squashing in deep message passing, where information from exponentially growing neighborhoods is compressed into fixed-dimensional representations. We show that this issue becomes a distinct failure mode in multi-label graphs: neighboring nodes often share only limited labels while differing across many irrelevant ones, causing predictive signals to be diluted by noisy label information. To address this challenge, we propose the Multi-Label Graph Information Bottleneck (MLGIB), which formulates multi-label message passing as constrained information transmission under irrelevant label noise. MLGIB balances expressiveness and robustness by preserving predictive label signals while suppressing irrelevant noise. Specifically, it constructs a Markovian dependence space and derives tractable variational bounds, where the lower bound maximizes mutual information with target labels and the upper bound constrains redundant source information. These bounds lead to an end-to-end label-aware message-passing architecture. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over existing methods, validating the effectiveness and generality of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [93] [ERPPO: Entropy Regularization-based Proximal Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13131)
*Changha Lee,Gyusang Cho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ERPPO算法，通过引入基于熵正则化的动态策略更新机制，解决了MAPPO在多维环境下因观测非平稳性导致的策略提取困难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MAPPO算法在多维环境中由于智能体观测的非平稳性无法提取最优策略，导致目标检测存在模糊性，限制了其在复杂环境中的性能表现。

Method: 首先定义多维观测环境下的目标检测模糊性，训练分布时空模糊性（DSA）学习器来估计非平稳约束下的目标检测不确定性。然后提出熵正则化PPO方法，在高模糊度观测时应用较强的L1正则化以鼓励探索，在低模糊度观测时应用较弱的L2正则化以稳定近端策略优化。

Result: 在基于AirSim的海上搜索场景测试平台上，ERPPO相比MAPPO实现了更高的梯度提升和准确率性能，定性结果证实在视觉不确定条件下有效抑制了虚假检测。

Conclusion: ERPPO通过动态调整正则化强度有效解决了多维环境下的策略优化问题，显著提升了时间关键操作中目标定位的成功概率，减少了检测失败并优化了搜索策略。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) is a variant of the Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) algorithm, specifically tailored for multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL). MAPPO optimizes cooperative multi-agent settings by employing a centralized critic with decentralized actors. However, in case of multi-dimensional environment, MAPPO can not extract optimal policy due to non-stationary agent observation. To overcome this problem, we introduce a novel approach, Entropy Regularization-based Proximal Policy Optimization (ERPPO). For the policy optimization, we first define the object detection ambiguity under multi-dimensional observation environment. Distributional Spatiotemporal Ambiguity (DSA) learner is trained to estimate object detection uncertainty in non-stationary constraints. Then, we enhance PPO with a novel Entropy Regularization term. This regularization dynamically adjusts the policy update by applying a stronger (L1) regularization in high-ambiguity observation to encourage significant exploratory actions and a weaker (L2) regularization in low-ambiguity observation to stabilize the proximal policy optimization. This approach is designed to enhance the probability of successful object localization in time-critical operations by reducing detection failures and optimizing search policy. Experiments on a testbed with AirSim-based maritime searching scenarios show that the proposed ERPPO improves accuracy performance. Our proposed method improves higher gradient than MAPPO. Qualitative results confirm that ERPPO effectiveness in terms of suppressing false detection in visually uncertain conditions.

</details>


### [94] [KAST-BAR: Knowledge-Anchored Semantically-Dynamic Topology Brain Autoregressive Modeling for Universal Neural Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13133)
*Haoning Wang,Wenchao Yang,Shuai Shen,Yang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KAST-BAR模型，通过知识锚定的语义动态拓扑方法解决EEG基础模型在时空拓扑建模和模态差距方面的挑战，在6个下游任务上取得优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型在通用神经解码方面虽然展现出潜力，但仍受限于两个关键问题：对复杂时空拓扑的建模不足，以及低层生理信号与高层文本语义之间固有的模态差距。

Method: 提出知识锚定语义动态拓扑脑自回归模型(KAST-BAR)，包含三个核心组件：(1)双流层次注意力(DSHA)编码器，通过建模局部时间动态和全局空间上下文来捕捉大脑内在的非欧几里得拓扑；(2)知识锚定语义分析器(KASP)，合成基于物理的和实例级的文本描述；(3)语义文本感知细化器(STAR)，使用潜在专家查询动态重构EEG表示。在21个多样化数据集上进行大规模预训练。

Result: KAST-BAR有效地将专家级医学知识整合到EEG信号表示中，在6个下游任务上持续取得优越性能。

Conclusion: 通过知识锚定的语义动态拓扑方法，KAST-BAR成功解决了EEG基础模型的关键挑战，为EEG信号的通用神经解码提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: While EEG foundation models have shown significant potential in universal neural decoding across tasks, their advancement remains constrained by the inadequacy modeling of complex spatiotemporal topology, as well as the inherent modality gap between low-level physiological signals and high-level textual semantics. To address these challenges, we propose a Knowledge-Anchored Semantically-Dynamic Topology Brain Autoregressive Model (KAST-BAR), which dynamically aligns physiological representations derived from multi-level brain topology with an expert-level semantic space. Specifically, we design a Dual-Stream Hierarchical Attention (DSHA) encoder that accurately captures the brain's intrinsic non-Euclidean topology by modeling local temporal dynamics with global spatial contexts. On this basis, a Knowledge-Anchored Semantic Profiler (KASP) is proposed to synthesize physically-grounded and instance-level textual profiles, which subsequently drive a Semantic Text-Aware Refiner (STAR) to dynamically reconstruct EEG representations using Latent Expert Queries. By conducting large-scale pre-training on 21 diverse datasets to build a foundation model, KAST-BAR effectively integrates expert-level medical knowledge into EEG signal representations, consistently achieving superior performance across six downstream tasks. Our code is available at https://github.com/KAST-BAR/KAST-BAR

</details>


### [95] [Collaborating in Multi-Armed Bandits with Strategic Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13145)
*Idan Barnea,Ofir Schlisselberg,Yishay Mansour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体贝叶斯老虎机问题中的协作学习，提出了一种名为CAOS的机制，能够在无货币转移的情况下，仅通过信息共享激励策略性智能体进行协作探索，并实现接近完全合作系统的性能表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多智能体协作学习中，多个智能体可以通过信息共享加速学习，但策略性智能体可能倾向于“搭便车”而避免探索。以往研究多假设智能体是短寿命的（只做一次决策），而本文关注更现实的场景：智能体是持久存在的，会参与多个时间段的决策。这引发了如何在没有货币转移的情况下，仅通过信息共享激励智能体协作探索的问题。

Method: 提出了一种名为CAOS（Collaborative Algorithm with Optimal Incentives through Sharing）的机制。该机制不依赖货币转移，仅通过信息共享作为激励手段。机制设计旨在将协作探索维持为纳什均衡，使策略性智能体在自利行为下仍选择参与探索。

Result: CAOS机制能够将协作探索维持为纳什均衡，并提供强有力的遗憾保证（strong regret guarantees）。实验结果证明，仅通过信息共享即可实现协作探索，尽管存在策略性行为，其性能表现接近于完全合作系统。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在没有货币转移的情况下，仅通过信息共享激励，可以在策略性智能体之间实现有效的协作探索。CAOS机制成功地将协作维持为均衡状态，其性能接近完全合作系统，为多智能体协作学习提供了可行的激励机制解决方案。

Abstract: We study collaborative learning in multi-agent Bayesian bandit problems, where strategic agents collectively solve the same bandit instance. While multiple agents can accelerate learning by sharing information, strategic agents might prefer to free-ride and avoid exploration. We consider a setting with persistent agents that participate in multiple time periods. This is in contrast to most previous works on incentives in multi-agent MAB, which assume short-lived agents, namely each agent has a single decision to make and optimizes their expected reward in that single decision. As in the multi-agent MAB model with incentives, our model does not have monetary transfers, and the only incentives are through information sharing.
  We propose \texttt{CAOS}, a mechanism that sustains collaboration as a Nash equilibrium while achieving strong regret guarantees. Our results demonstrate that collaborative exploration can be sustained purely through information sharing, achieving performance close to that of fully cooperative systems despite strategic behavior.

</details>


### [96] [Understanding Generalization through Decision Pattern Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13148)
*Huiqi Deng,Yibo Li,Quanshi Zhang,Peng Zhang,Hongbin Pei,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了决策模式偏移（DPS）这一新视角，通过衡量模型内部决策模式在测试时的稳定性来量化泛化失败，发现DPS与泛化差距呈强线性相关，并能统一解释多种泛化退化场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注数据、表示或输出等外部可观察因素的变化，对模型内部决策机制从训练到测试的演变过程缺乏深入理解，无法有效解释深度神经网络为何在未见样本上泛化失败。

Method: 提出决策模式偏移（DPS）方法：利用GradCAM将每个样本的决策模式表示为通道贡献向量，捕捉特征通道如何共同支持预测；定义DPS指标，通过计算样本决策模式与类平均模式的偏差来量化泛化失败。

Result: 在多个数据集和架构上的实验表明：(i) 决策模式形成高度结构化、类一致的空间，具有强类内凝聚和低类间混淆；(ii) DPS幅度与泛化差距呈线性相关（Pearson相关系数几乎均>0.8）；(iii) DPS谱将理想泛化、分布内退化、域偏移、分布外和快捷学习等多种泛化退化场景组织为连续轨迹。

Conclusion: 研究表明泛化失败本质上是模型内部决策机制的系统化漂移，DPS为早期泛化风险检测、失效模式诊断和通道级缺陷定位开辟了新的可能性。

Abstract: Understanding why deep neural networks (DNNs) fail to generalize to unseen samples remains a long-standing challenge. Existing studies mainly examine changes in externally observable factors such as data, representations, or outputs, yet offer limited insight into how a model's internal decision mechanism evolves from training to test. To address this gap, we introduce Decision Pattern Shift (DPS), a new perspective that defines generalization through the stability of internal decision patterns and quantifies failure as their deviation from those learned during training. Specifically, we represent each sample's decision pattern as a GradCAM-based channel-contribution vector, which captures how feature channels collectively support a prediction, and we propose the DPS metric to measure its discrepancy from the class-average pattern. Empirical analyses across multiple datasets and architectures show that, (i) decision patterns form a highly structured, class-consistent space with strong intra-class cohesion and low inter-class confusion, enabling direct analysis of a model's decision logic; (ii) the DPS magnitude correlates linearly with the generalization gap (nearly all Pearson r > 0.8), revealing generalization as a systematic drift in the model's internal decision mechanism; (iii) the DPS spectrum organizes diverse generalization degradation scenarios (covering ideal generalization, in-distribution degradation, domain shift, out-of-distribution, and shortcut learning) into a continuous trajectory, providing a unified explanation of their failure modes. These findings open up new possibilities for early generalization-risk detection, failure-mode diagnosis, and channel-level defect localization.

</details>


### [97] [Continual Fine-Tuning of Large Language Models via Program Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13162)
*Hung Le,Svetha Venkatesh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProCL框架，借鉴神经科学中的互补学习系统理论，将LoRA适配器组织为结构化的程序记忆槽，通过注意力机制动态检索，有效解决了LLM在持续学习场景下适应新任务与保留旧知识之间的平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LoRA在持续学习设置中难以平衡快速适应和知识保留，现有方法将低秩空间视为同质化更新区域，缺乏调节短期更新如何随时间整合的机制，容易导致灾难性遗忘。

Method: 提出ProCL（Program memory for Complementary Learning）框架：1）将LoRA适配器组织为结构化的程序记忆槽；2）通过输入条件注意力动态检索记忆槽，实现快速局部适应；3）鼓励相似输入重用共享适配器区域，保留未使用容量给未来数据；4）与底层适配器结合，后者以分布式表示逐步积累跨任务知识；5）完全在LoRA参数化范围内运作，不增加推理成本。

Result: 在多个多样化基准测试上进行的实验表明，ProCL相比其他持续LoRA策略具有更好的知识保留能力和更低的灾难性遗忘，有效平衡了可塑性和稳定性。

Conclusion: ProCL通过程序记忆结构和互补学习机制，成功解决了LLM持续学习中的稳定性-可塑性困境，为参数高效的持续学习提供了新的解决方案，且不增加额外推理开销。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT), particularly Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), has become a standard approach for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) under limited compute. However, in continual settings where models are updated sequentially with small datasets, conventional LoRA updates struggle to balance rapid adaptation and knowledge retention. Existing methods typically treat the low-rank space as a homogeneous update region, lacking mechanisms to regulate how short-term updates are consolidated over time. We propose a continual LoRA framework with \textbf{Pro}gram memory, inspired by \textbf{C}omplementary \textbf{L}earning Systems in neuroscience. Our approach, dubbed \textbf{ProCL}, organizes LoRA adapters into structured program memory slots that are dynamically retrieved through input-conditioned attention. This enables rapid and localized adaptation, encouraging similar inputs to reuse shared adapter regions while reserving unused capacity for future data. The slots are then combined with the underlying adapter, which maintains a distributed representation that gradually accumulates knowledge across tasks to balance plasticity and stability. Our method operates entirely within the LoRA parameterization and incurs no additional inference cost. Experiments on diverse benchmarks demonstrate improved retention and reduced catastrophic forgetting over other continual LoRA strategies.

</details>


### [98] [Do Heavy Tails Help Diffusion? On the Subtle Trade-off Between Initialization and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13175)
*Hamza Cherkaoui,Hélène Halconruy,Antonio Ocello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过理论和实验研究表明，在扩散模型中使用重尾噪声反而会增大采样误差，质疑了这一生成模型设计趋势的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有研究提出在扩散模型和流模型中引入重尾噪声，以期更好地恢复目标分布的尾部并提高生成多样性。直觉上，如果数据本身具有重尾特性，使用重尾噪声似乎比轻尾高斯噪声更匹配。本文旨在重新审视这一设计范式。

Method: 通过结合理论和实证研究的方法，针对由重尾噪声和轻尾噪声驱动的两种代表性扩散模型，建立了采样误差界限的理论分析框架，并在合成数据集和真实数据集上进行了实验验证。

Result: 研究发现重尾噪声会使统计估计问题变得更加困难，导致采样误差界限变差；实验结果与理论预测的误差权衡相符，验证了重尾噪声在实际应用中的局限性。

Conclusion: 研究结果质疑了生成模型中日益增长的重尾噪声设计趋势，挑战了使用重尾噪声来改善稀有区域探索的做法，表明这一方向可能需要重新评估。

Abstract: Recent works have proposed incorporating heavy-tailed (HT) noise into diffusion- and flow-based generative models, with the goals of better recovering the tails of target distributions and improving generative diversity. This motivation is intuitive: if the data are heavy-tailed, HT noise may appear better matched than light-tailed (LT) Gaussian noise. However, replacing Gaussian noise by HT noise also changes the underlying estimation problem. In this paper, we revisit this paradigm through a combined theoretical and empirical study, establishing sampling-error bounds for two representative diffusion models driven by HT and LT noise. We show that HT noise makes the statistical estimation problem harder, leading to less favorable sampling-error bounds. We support these findings with experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets, empirically recovering the predicted error trade-off. Our results call into question a growing design trend in generative modeling and challenge the use of HT noise to improve rare-region exploration.

</details>


### [99] [Stable Attention Response for Reliable Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13181)
*Penghui Wen,Zexin Hu,Sen Zhang,Patrick Filippi,Xiaogang Zhu,Allen Benter,Thomas Bishop,Zhiyong Wang,Kun Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了注意力响应能量的跨样本不稳定性是导致降水临近预报不可靠的重要原因，并提出了HARECast框架通过头级能量正则化来稳定注意力机制，在单模态和多模态架构中均达到了最先进的预报性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于注意力的降水临近预报方法主要关注增强表征学习和预测能力，但忽视了注意力响应在样本间的稳定性。本文旨在解决这一被忽视的问题，因为不稳定的注意力响应能量会导致预报不可靠。

Method: 提出了HARECast（头级注意力响应能量调节框架），该方法显式建模头级注意力响应能量，并通过分组正则化目标函数减少跨样本波动，稳定注意力响应。该框架通用性强，可应用于单模态和多模态预报架构，在标准预测流程中集成了重建分支和扩散预测器。

Result: 在SEVIR和MeteoNet两个常用基准数据集上的实验表明，HARECast达到了最先进的性能水平。经验证，不准确的预报确实与注意力和层级上更大的注意力响应能量方差相关。

Conclusion: 跨样本注意力响应能量的不稳定性是预报错误的重要来源，通过显式建模和稳定化头级注意力响应能量可以有效提升降水临近预报的可靠性和准确性，该方法为注意力机制的稳定性研究提供了新视角。

Abstract: Precipitation nowcasting remains challenging due to the highly localized, rapidly evolving, and heterogeneous nature of atmospheric dynamics. Although recent methods increasingly adopt attention-based architectures in both unimodal and multimodal settings, they mainly emphasize stronger representation learning and prediction capacity, while paying less attention to the stability of attention responses across samples. In this work, we show that cross-sample instability of attention-response energy is an important and previously underexplored source of forecasting unreliability. Empirically, inaccurate forecasts are associated with larger attention-response energy variance across heads and layers. Theoretically, we show that cross-sample variability can propagate through self-attention, and enlarge a lower bound on prediction error. Based on this insight, we propose HARECast, a Head-wise Attention Response Energy-regulated framework for precipitation nowcasting. HARECast explicitly models head-wise attention-response energy and stabilizes it through a group-wise regularization objective that reduces cross-sample fluctuations. The proposed formulation is generic and applicable to both unimodal and multimodal nowcasting architectures. We instantiate HARECast in a standard forecasting pipeline with reconstruction branches and a diffusion-based predictor, and evaluate it on commonly used benchmarks--SEVIR and MeteoNet. Experimental results demonstrate that HARECast achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [100] [N-vium: Mixture-of-Exits Transformer for Accelerated Exact Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13190)
*Aleksander Lorenc,Frédéric Berdoz,Joël Mathys,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了N-vium（混合出口Transformer），通过在多个深度附加预测头并学习混合分布，在标准硬件上实现深度并行化，在无困惑度损失的前提下获得57.9%的时钟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归Transformer推理加速方法主要通过减少每个token的计算量（FLOPs）来实现，通常需要近似计算并导致模型质量下降。作者旨在探索一种新路径：通过并行化深度计算来提高有效FLOPs/秒，而非单纯减少每token的计算量。

Method: 提出N-vium架构：在Transformer的多个深度层附加预测头，将下一个token的分布定义为这些出口的混合分布，采用token自适应路由机制。该公式严格泛化了标准Transformer（当路由将所有中间头的权重设为零时完全恢复标准形式）。采用精确采样方法，并通过延迟上层计算并与后续token批处理来恢复完整的KV缓存。

Result: 在最大规模（15亿参数）模型上，相比参数和数据匹配的标准Transformer，实现了57.9%的时钟加速，且没有任何困惑度损失。证明了该方法可以在不牺牲模型质量的情况下显著提升推理效率。

Conclusion: N-vium成功实现了在标准硬件上的深度并行化推理加速，证明了提高有效FLOPs/秒而非单纯减少计算量的可行性。该方法严格泛化标准Transformer，在保持模型质量的同时显著提升推理速度，为自回归模型的推理优化提供了新的技术路线。

Abstract: Improving the inference efficiency of autoregressive transformers typically means reducing FLOPs per token, usually through approximations that degrade model quality. We introduce N-vium, a mixture-of-exits transformer that partially parallelizes computation across depth on standard hardware, increasing effective FLOPs per second rather than minimizing compute per token. N-vium attaches prediction heads at multiple depths and defines the next-token distribution as a learned mixture over these exits, with token-adaptive routing. This formulation strictly generalizes the standard transformer, which is recovered exactly when routing assigns zero mass to all intermediate heads. Sampling from the mixture is exact, and complete KV caches are recovered by deferring the upper-layer computation and batching it with later tokens. We pretrain N-vium at scales up to 1.5B parameters. Our largest model reaches 57.9% wall-clock speedup over a parameter- and data-matched standard transformer at no perplexity cost.

</details>


### [101] [ECG-NAT: A Self-supervised Neighborhood Attention Transformer for Multi-lead Electrocardiogram Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13194)
*Mahsa Gazeran,Sayvan Soleymanbaigi,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Amjad Seyedi,Fardin Akhlaghian Tab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ECG-NAT框架，一种结合生成式预训练和判别式微调的自监督学习方法，用于多导联ECG心律失常分类。该模型通过分层邻域注意力机制高效捕获多尺度时间特征，在仅使用1%标注数据的情况下达到88.1%的准确率，兼具高性能与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自监督学习方法在ECG心律失常分类中难以同时捕获全局上下文特征和局部形态模式，缺乏层次化的多尺度特征提取能力。ECG信号具有变异性大、噪声干扰多、标注数据有限等特点，需要一种能够同时捕获精细的心跳级形态特征和宏观的心律级依赖关系，且具有计算效率的模型架构。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段的自监督学习方法ECG-NAT。第一阶段为生成式预训练，利用掩码自编码器在多个多样化数据集上重建部分掩码的ECG信号，从无标注数据中学习鲁棒的领域不变表示。第二阶段为判别式微调，采用结合监督对比损失和交叉熵损失的双损失函数。模型核心是分层邻域注意力机制，能够以低计算成本高效捕获从局部心跳形态到宏观心律模式的多尺度时间特征。

Result: ECG-NAT在基准数据集上表现优异，在仅使用1%标注数据的低资源场景下达到了88.1%的分类准确率。该框架在保持卓越分类性能的同时实现了计算效率，适合实时ECG诊断应用。

Conclusion: ECG-NAT框架成功解决了ECG心律失常分类中的多尺度特征提取和标注数据依赖问题，通过层次化注意力机制和自监督学习策略，实现了高效准确的ECG分类。该方法在低资源环境下具有显著优势，为实时ECG诊断提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Electrocardiogram (ECG) arrhythmia classification remains challenging due to signal variability, noise, limited labeled data, and the difficulty in achieving both accuracy and efficiency in models. While self-supervised learning reduces label dependency, most methods target either global contextual features or local morphological patterns, but rarely implement hierarchical multi-scale feature extraction. ECG signals require architectures that simultaneously capture fine-grained beat-level morphology and broader rhythm-level dependencies with computational efficiency. To overcome this limitation, this paper proposes the Electrocardiogram Neighborhood Attention Transformer (ECG-NAT), a novel self-supervised learning approach tailored for multi-lead ECG classification. Our two-stage approach begins with generative pretraining, using a masked autoencoder to reconstruct partially masked ECG signals across multiple diverse datasets, enabling the model to learn robust, domain-invariant representations from unlabeled data. This is followed by discriminative fine-tuning with a dual-loss function that combines supervised contrastive and cross-entropy losses, aligning representation learning with label prediction. The hierarchical attention mechanism efficiently captures multi-scale temporal features from localized beat morphology to broader rhythm patterns at low computational cost. ECG-NAT achieves robust performance on benchmark datasets, with 88.1\% accuracy using only 1\% labeled data, demonstrating strong efficacy in low-resource settings. The framework combines superior classification performance with computational efficiency, making it practical for real-time ECG diagnosis. The code will be made available upon acceptance at: https://github.com/Mahsagazeran/ECG-NAT.

</details>


### [102] [McCast: Memory-Guided Latent Drift Correction for Long-Horizon Precipitation Nowcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13197)
*Penghui Wen,Yu Luo,Lintao Wang,Mengwei He,Patrick Filippi,Thomas Francis Bishop,Zhiyong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出McClast方法，通过记忆引导的潜在漂移校正机制，主动校正自回归预测中的轨迹偏差，解决了长时序预测中的误差累积问题，在SEVIR和MeteoNet基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的降水临近预报方法采用自回归预测方式，在长时序推演中会累积误差，导致预测轨迹偏离物理合理的演化路径。现有研究主要通过提高单步预测精度来缓解该问题，但忽略了气象系统的全局时间演化特性，且缺乏在推演过程中主动校正漂移的机制。

Method: 提出McClast方法，核心是漂移校正记忆库（DCBank）。该方法不将记忆视为被动条件，而是利用时间组织的记忆主动校正自回归潜在演化。DCBank两阶段工作：首先通过校正潜在提取器从当前预测和参考潜在状态预测初始校正，然后通过校正感知记忆检索模块利用时序组织的历史记忆细化校正，从而校准偏离的轨迹。

Result: 在SEVIR和MeteoNet两个广泛使用的基准数据集上进行实验，McClast达到了最先进的性能，特别是在具有挑战性的长时序预测场景中表现突出。通过显式校正潜在演化而非仅提高单步预测精度，生成了时间上更连贯和可靠的长期预测。

Conclusion: 通过显式校正潜在演化轨迹而非仅关注单步预测精度，McClast成功解决了自回归预测中的漂移问题，为降水临近预报提供了更可靠的长时序预测方案。记忆引导的漂移校正机制为序列预测任务提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Existing precipitation nowcasting methods typically adopt an autoregressive formulation, where future states are predicted from previous outputs. However, such an approach accumulates errors over long rollouts, causing forecasts to drift away from physically plausible evolution trajectories. Although various studies have attempted to alleviate this problem by improving step-wise prediction accuracy, they largely neglect the global temporal evolution of meteorological systems and lack mechanisms to actively correct drift during rollouts. To address this issue, we propose McCast, a memory-guided latent drift correction method for precipitation nowcasting. Rather than treating memory as an unordered dictionary of latent states for passive conditioning, McCast leverages temporally organized memory to actively correct autoregressive latent evolution. Specifically, McCast introduces a Drift-Corrective Memory Bank (DCBank) that explicitly estimates the temporally consistent drift corrections to calibrate the divergent trajectory. DCBank performs drift correction in two stages: a Corrective Latent Extractor first predicts an initial correction from the current prediction and a reference latent state, and a Correction-Aware Memory Retrieval module then refines the initial correction using temporally organized historical memory. By explicitly correcting latent evolution, instead of improving step-wise prediction accuracy only, McCast produces more temporally coherent and reliable long-horizon forecasts. Experiments on two widely used benchmarks, SEVIR and MeteoNet, show that McCast achieves state-of-the-art performance, particularly in challenging long-horizon forecasting scenarios.

</details>


### [103] [A Hybrid Tucker-LSTM Tensor Network Model for SOC Prediction in Electric Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13200)
*Han Wang,Ying Wang,Bing Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合Tucker张量分解与LSTM网络的混合方法，用于电动汽车电池荷电状态（SOC）估算。利用全生命周期实车数据，该方法在所有指标上均显著优于标准LSTM，MSE降低70.5%，为基于张量的电池管理分析开辟了新方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的SOC估算对电动汽车电池管理策略至关重要，但传统估算器存在两个根本缺陷：随时间累积的误差，以及对无法反映真实世界动态的简化电池模型的依赖。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的混合方法，将Tucker张量分解与LSTM网络相结合。输入包括充电状态、里程、电压、电流、电池单体差异和时间特征。利用Tucker分解在保持时间结构的同时进行降维，从而实现与标准LSTM的直接、公平比较。

Result: Tucker-LSTM在所有指标上均明确优于基准模型：MSE下降70.5%（从21.07降至6.22），MAE改善48.7%（从3.37%降至1.73%），RMSE从4.59%降至2.49%，R²从0.918升至0.976。实验结果表明张量分解能很好地压缩高维电池数据而不损失预测保真度。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了张量分解在压缩高维电池数据方面的有效性，为电动汽车电池管理中基于张量的分析技术开辟了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Accurate state of charge estimation is critical for the success of electric vehicle battery management strategies, but it is well known that conventional estimators suffer from two fundamental shortcomings: cumulative errors that grow over time and reliance on simplified battery models that do not reflect real world dynamics. Therefore, this paper presents a novel hybrid approach combining Tucker tensor decomposition with LSTM networks, using full - lifecycle EV field data for SOC prediction. The inputs are charge status, mileage, voltage, current, cell differentials, and temporal features. Tucker decomposition is skillfully used to reduce dimensionality while maintaining the temporal structure, hence allowing a direct, fair comparison with standard LSTM. The result is unequivocal: Tucker - LSTM outperforms the baseline on all metrics, with MSE dropping 70.5\% (from 21.07 to 6.22 ), MAE improving 48.7\% (from 3.37\% to 1.73\%), RMSE falling from 4.59\% to 2.49\%, and $R^2$ rising from 0.918 to 0.976. Since the experimental results demonstrably demonstrate that tensor decomposition compresses high-dimensional battery data very well without loss of predictive fidelity, this paper naturally opens up a new direction for tensor-based analytics in electric vehicle battery management.

</details>


### [104] [Switching Successor Measures for Hierarchical Zero-shot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13207)
*Stefan Stojanovic,Alexandre Proutiere*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了切换后继度量方法，能够在零样本强化学习中实现分层控制，无需额外监督或手工设计子目标，并可处理通用奖励函数任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分层强化学习方法依赖于受限的设计选择（如固定的时间抽象或目标条件化目标），使其主要局限于目标达成任务，难以应用于通用奖励函数场景。

Method: 提出了切换后继度量，这是后继度量的扩展，可从经典后继度量中自然导出；在此基础上提出FB π-Switch算法，直接从前向-后向（FB）表征中提取高层子目标选择策略和低层控制策略，使分层行为从单一学习表征中涌现。

Result: 在目标条件化和通用基于奖励的任务上，FB π-Switch优于非分层基线，在目标条件化设置中匹配最先进的分层方法性能。

Conclusion: 结构化的后继表征为超越目标达成任务的分层零样本强化学习提供了灵活基础，扩展了分层强化学习的适用范围。

Abstract: Hierarchical reinforcement learning can improve generalization by decomposing long-horizon decision-making into simpler subproblems. However, existing approaches often rely on restrictive design choices, such as fixed temporal abstractions or goal-conditioned objectives, which largely confine them to goal-reaching tasks and limit their applicability to general reward functions. In this paper, we introduce switching successor measures, an extension of successor measures that enables hierarchical control in zero-shot reinforcement learning without additional supervision, fixed horizons, or manually designed subgoals. We show that switching successor measures arise naturally from classical successor measures while preserving their underlying structure. Building on this result, we propose FB $π$-Switch, an algorithm that extracts both a high-level subgoal-selection policy and a low-level control policy directly from forward-backward (FB) representations, allowing hierarchical behavior to emerge from a single learned representation. Experiments on both goal-conditioned and general reward-based tasks show that FB $π$-Switch improves over non-hierarchical baselines and matches state-of-the-art hierarchical methods in goal-conditioned settings. These results demonstrate that structured successor representations provide a flexible foundation for hierarchical zero-shot reinforcement learning beyond goal-reaching tasks. Our project website is available at: https://stestokth.github.io/switching-successors/.

</details>


### [105] [Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization for LLM Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13230)
*Xinyu Liu,Kechen Jiao,Chunyang Xiao,Runsong Zhao,Junhao Ruan,Bei Li,Jiahao Liu,Qifan Wang,Xin Chen,Jingang Wang,Tong Xiao,JingBo Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对逆向KL（RKL）在策略蒸馏中因师生分布差异大而失效的问题，提出了Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization (TGPO) 算法，通过引入教师模型的密集方向性指导，显著提升了大语言模型的蒸馏性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管逆向KL（RKL）被视为统一探索与教师监督的有前途范式，但作者发现了一个关键局限：当学生模型与教师模型的分布差异显著时，标准RKL往往因提供无信息量的负反馈而无法带来有意义的提升，导致蒸馏效率低下。

Method: 提出了Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization (TGPO) 算法。该方法是一种在策略（on-policy）算法，通过利用以学生模型滚动输出为条件的教师预测，引入了密集的方向性指导。由于TGPO保持在线策略特性，它无需额外的数据标注即可无缝集成到现有的RLVR框架中。

Result: 在复杂推理基准测试上的实验表明，TGPO显著优于标准基线方法，并且对不同的教师模型表现出良好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: TGPO有效解决了标准RKL在师生分布差异大时的失效问题，为大规模语言模型蒸馏提供了一种高效且鲁棒的新方法。

Abstract: The convergence of reinforcement learning and imitation learning has positioned Reverse KL (RKL) as a promising paradigm for on-policy LLM distillation, aiming to unify exploration with teacher supervision. However, we identify a critical limitation: when the student and teacher distributions diverge significantly, standard RKL often fails to yield meaningful improvement due to uninformative negative feedback. To address this inefficiency, we propose Teacher-Guided Policy Optimization (TGPO), an on-policy algorithm that incorporates dense directional guidance by leveraging teacher predictions conditioned on the student's rollout. Because TGPO remains on-policy, the algorithm integrates seamlessly with existing RLVR frameworks without requiring additional data annotation. Experiments on complex reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that TGPO significantly outperforms standard baselines and is robust to different teachers.

</details>


### [106] [Machine Learning-Driven Multimodal Spectroscopic Liquid Biopsy for Early Multicancer Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13218)
*Alejandro Leonardo García Navarro,Javier Cachón Ortiz,Javier González Colsa,Samuel García Díaz,Carlos Viadero Valderrama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合FTIR、拉曼和EEM荧光光谱的多模态光谱液体活检框架，通过低层数据融合和XGBoost机器学习模型，实现了对乳腺癌和结直肠癌的高精度检测（ROC-AUC分别达0.997和0.994），优于单一模态方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症是全球主要死因之一，亟需开发快速、微创、无标记且可扩展的诊断策略。光谱液体活检作为一种有前景的替代方案，能够对生物流体中的生化变化进行整体表征，为多癌种检测提供了新的思路。

Method: 采集乳腺癌患者、结直肠癌患者和健康对照的血清样本，分别进行FTIR光谱、拉曼光谱和EEM荧光光谱三种模态的测量。经过模态特定的预处理后，采用低层数据融合（LLDF）整合不同光谱测量中编码的互补生化信息，并使用XGBoost模型进行分类。研究评估了七种实验配置，包括三种单模态、三种双模态组合和一种三模态融合方法。

Result: 虽然多种单一模态已能实现较高的判别性能，但多模态融合提供了最均衡的整体结果。多模态融合方法在乳腺癌检测中达到0.997的ROC-AUC，在结直肠癌检测中达到0.994的ROC-AUC，同时具备高度均衡的灵敏度和特异性值。

Conclusion: 多模态光谱液体活检框架通过整合FTIR、拉曼和EEM荧光光谱的互补信息，显著提升了多癌种检测的性能，为开发快速、微创、无标记的癌症诊断策略提供了有力支持，展现出良好的临床应用前景。

Abstract: Cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, making the development of rapid, minimally invasive, label-free and scalable diagnostic strategies a major challenge in modern oncology. In this context, spectroscopic liquid biopsy has emerged as a promising alternative, as it enables the holistic characterization of biochemical alterations in biological fluids. In this work, we propose a multimodal spectroscopic liquid biopsy framework for multicancer detection based on the combination of Fourier Transform Infrared (FTIR) spectroscopy, Raman spectroscopy, and Excitation-Emission Matrix (EEM) fluorescence spectroscopy together with Machine Learning (ML) methodologies. Serum samples from breast cancer patients, colorectal cancer patients, and healthy controls were analyzed through the three spectroscopic modalities. After modality-specific preprocessing, low-level data fusion (LLDF) was employed to integrate the complementary biochemical information encoded within the different spectroscopic measurements, and classification was performed using XGBoost models. Seven experimental configurations were evaluated, including the three unimodal approaches, all pairwise bimodal configurations, and the full multimodal approach of FTIR, Raman, and EEM fluorescence. The results show that although several individual modalities achieved high discrimination performance, the multimodal fusion provided the most balanced overall results, reaching a ROC-AUC of 0.997 for breast cancer and 0.994 for colorectal cancer, together with highly balanced sensitivity and specificity values.

</details>


### [107] [Delightful Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13287)
*Ian Osband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“Delight-gated exploration”（DE）的探索策略，通过结合期望改进和惊奇度作为“愉悦感”指标，仅在其超过一定阈值时进行探索，从而在有限的资源预算下实现更高效的学习和决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的探索算法在行动空间过大时，往往无法在预算内解决不确定性，实践者通常采用$\varepsilon$-greedy策略，虽然限制了干扰，但其探索行为是盲目的。因此，需要一种更智能的探索策略，在有限的预算内更有效地分配探索资源。

Method: 引入“愉悦门控探索”（DE）策略，计算探索行动的预期愉悦感（即期望改进与惊奇度的乘积），只有当该值超过设定的门限价格时才执行该探索行动。该策略类似于潘多拉规则中的保留价值规则，其中惊奇度充当了有效的检查成本。已解决的选项会被排除在门控之外，新选项在超过先验确定的阈值时会被关闭。

Result: 在伯努利多臂赌博机、线性多臂赌博机和表格MDP环境中，DE策略使用相同的超参数无需重新调整，表现出比汤普森采样和$\varepsilon$-greedy更低的遗憾增长，特别是在未解决的探索机制中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: DE策略通过将稀缺资源定价为上行潜力与惊奇度的乘积，实现了更高效的探索与学习。其核心思想是“愉悦感”不仅改善学习，也改善决策，因为其有效地衡量了探索的价值。该方法具有跨任务的通用性，且无需针对不同任务重新调整超参数。

Abstract: Most exploration algorithms search broadly until uncertainty is resolved. When the action space is too large to resolve within budget, practitioners default to $\varepsilon$-greedy, which bounds disruption but spends its override blindly. We introduce \textit{Delight-gated exploration} (DE), a host--override rule that spends exploratory actions only when their prospective delight (expected improvement times surprisal) exceeds a gate price. This practical heuristic recovers a classical result: Pandora's reservation-value rule for costly search, with surprisal setting the effective inspection cost. Resolved arms exit the gate, fresh arms shut off above a prior-determined threshold, and selected linear-bandit overrides consume finite information budget. Across Bernoulli bandits, linear bandits, and tabular MDPs, the same hyperparameters transfer without retuning, and DE shows much weaker regret growth than Thompson Sampling and $\varepsilon$-greedy in the tested unresolved regimes. Delight improves acting for the same reason it improves learning: it prices scarce resources by the product of upside and surprisal.

</details>


### [108] [Mix, Don't Tune: Bilingual Pre-Training Outperforms Hyperparameter Search in Data-Constrained Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13225)
*Paul Jeha,Anastasiia Sedova,Louis Béthune,Skyler Seto,Jes Frellsen,Pierre Ablin,Natalie Schluter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在低资源语言预训练中，引入高资源辅助语言数据（混合策略）比激进超参数调优效果更显著，性能提升相当于2-13倍目标语言数据量，且模型越大优势越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界上大多数语言的预训练面临数据受限问题，需要重复使用训练数据多次，导致泛化能力下降。现有两种解决方案：激进超参数调优（如高权重衰减）和引入高资源辅助语言数据混合，但缺乏对这两种方法的系统性比较研究。

Method: 在4种模型规模（150M至1.43B参数）下进行约1000次预训练实验，以阿拉伯语作为低资源目标语言、英语作为辅助语言，系统性地比较超参数调优和数据混合策略的效果，并分析验证损失与下游任务准确率之间的关系。

Result: 三个核心发现：（1）数据混合在验证损失和下游任务准确率上均优于超参数调优，且差距随模型规模增大而扩大；（2）混合策略带来的性能提升相当于2-3倍（验证损失）和2-13倍（下游任务准确率）的目标语言数据量；（3）目标语言验证损失系统性地低估了混合策略的价值，因为混合既通过多样化训练信号进行正则化，又补充了目标语料无法提供的知识，而验证损失仅捕获前者。

Conclusion: 实践建议：在预训练中引入高资源语言数据，优先优化混合比例而非超参数调优，并通过μP方法从小型代理模型迁移超参数。

Abstract: For most languages of the world, language model pre-training operates in a data-constrained regime where models must repeat their training data many times, degrading generalization. Two remedies exist: aggressive hyperparameter tuning such as high weight decay, and mixing in data from a high-resource auxiliary language to directly aid the low-resource target. While hyperparameter tuning regularizes the model by shrinking weights to restrict network capacity, auxiliary data mixing uses a tunable mixing ratio to expand the training distribution and diversify the training signal with new knowledge. Both offer a principled way to improve training in a data-constrained domain. We compare these levers systematically across four model scales from 150M to 1.43B parameters, using Arabic as the low-resource target and English as the auxiliary, over approximately 1000 pre-training runs. Three findings emerge. First, mixing yields larger improvements than hyperparameter tuning on both validation loss and downstream task accuracy, and the gap grows with model size. Second, we quantify how much mixing helps: it boosts performance by an amount equivalent to 2--3$\times$ the unique target data on validation loss and 2--13$\times$ on downstream task accuracy, with the gain scaling steeply with model size. Third, this divergence reveals that target-language validation loss systematically underestimates mixing's value. Mixing regularizes by diversifying the training signal and contributes knowledge the repeated target corpus cannot supply; validation loss captures only the first effect. Our practical recommendations are: mix in a high-resource language, prioritize the mixing ratio over hyperparameter tuning, and transfer hyperparameters from a small proxy model via $μ$P.

</details>


### [109] [Q-Flow: Stable and Expressive Reinforcement Learning with Flow-Based Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13435)
*JaeHyeok Doo,Byeongguk Jeon,Seonghyeon Ye,Kimin Lee,Minjoon Seo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Q-Flow框架，通过将终端轨迹价值显式传播到中间潜在状态，解决了流模型在强化学习中优化不稳定的问题，在OGBench上平均提升10.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流模型作为强化学习策略具有高表达能力，但现有方法为了优化稳定性往往限制其表达能力，存在稳定性与表达能力的权衡问题；朴素的基于梯度的优化需要通过数值求解器进行反向传播，导致不稳定。

Method: 提出Q-Flow框架，利用流动态的确定性特性，将终端轨迹价值显式传播到策略诱导流中的中间潜在状态，从而可以使用中间价值梯度进行稳定的策略优化，无需展开数值求解器。

Result: 在离线学习设置下的OGBench基准测试中，Q-Flow持续优于最先进基线方法，平均提升10.6个百分点，同时在同一框架内实现了稳定的在线适应。

Conclusion: Q-Flow成功弥合了优化稳定性与表达能力之间的差距，为流模型在强化学习中的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: There is growing interest in utilizing flow-based models as decision-making policies in reinforcement learning due to their high expressive capacity. However, effectively leveraging this expressivity for value maximization remains challenging, as naive gradient-based optimization requires backpropagating through numerical solvers and often leads to instability. Existing approaches typically address this issue by restricting the expressive capacity of flow-based policies, resulting in a trade-off between optimization stability and representational flexibility. To resolve this, we introduce Q-Flow, a framework that leverages the deterministic nature of flow dynamics to explicitly propagate terminal trajectory value to intermediate latent states along the policy-induced flow. This formulation enables stable policy optimization using intermediate value gradients without unrolling the numerical solver, effectively bridging the gap between stability and expressivity. We evaluate Q-Flow in the offline learning setting on the challenging OGBench suite, where it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by an average of 10.6 percentage points, while also enabling stable online adaptation within the same framework.

</details>


### [110] [EMO: Frustratingly Easy Progressive Training of Extendable MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13247)
*Linghao Jin,Chufan Shi,Huijuan Wang,Nuan Wen,Zhengzhong Liu,Eric Xing,Xuezhe Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EMO渐进式训练框架，通过逐步扩展专家池解决稀疏MoE模型的效率悖论，在保持性能的同时提升训练效率并降低成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏MoE模型存在效率悖论：虽然增加专家数量不增加计算量，但会显著增加内存和通信开销，导致实际训练效率低下。作者认为这源于当前训练策略从一开始就分配过多专家，而早期训练阶段的数据并不需要如此大的容量。

Method: 提出EMO（渐进式训练框架），将MoE容量视为可扩展的内存，在训练过程中逐步扩展专家池。通过在缩放定律中显式建模稀疏性，推导出渐进扩展的阶段最优token预算，实现计算资源的最优分配。

Result: 在大规模实验中，EMO能够匹配固定专家数量设置的性能，同时显著提升实际训练时间效率并降低GPU成本。实验验证了渐进式扩展策略在保持大专家池优势的同时有效减少了训练开销。

Conclusion: EMO为可扩展的MoE训练提供了一条简单而有效的路径，成功解决了MoE效率悖论，在保持大规模专家池带来的性能优势的同时，显著降低了训练时间和成本，为MoE模型的高效训练提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models offer a powerful way to scale model size without increasing compute, as per-token FLOPs depend only on k active experts rather than the total pool of E experts. Yet, this asymmetry creates an MoE efficiency paradox in practice: adding more experts balloons memory and communication costs, making actual training inefficient. We argue that this bottleneck arises in part because current MoE training allocates too many experts from the beginning, even though early-stage data may not fully utilize such capacity. Motivated by this, we propose EMO, a simple progressive training framework that treats MoE capacity as expandable memory and grows the expert pool over the course of training. EMO explicitly models sparsity in scaling law to derive stage-wise compute-optimal token budgets for progressive expansion. Empirical results show that EMO matches the performance of a fixed-expert setup in large-scale experiments while improving wall-clock efficiency. It offers a surprisingly simple yet effective path to scalable MoE training, preserving the benefits of large expert pools while reducing both training time and GPU cost.

</details>


### [111] [Discovery of Hidden Miscalibration Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13484)
*Katarzyna Kobalczyk,Mihaela van der Schaar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的校准诊断框架，通过学习校准感知的输入空间表示来发现模型中隐藏的、依赖于输入的误校准区域（误校准场），揭示了传统全局校准指标掩盖的结构性问题，并证明该方法能有效修正局部置信度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的校准评估方法将可靠性视为仅与置信度分数相关的函数，通过比较模型置信度与经验正确性来进行。然而，这种观点忽略了重要的结构信息：模型可能在某些类型的输入上系统性地过度自信，而在其他输入上自信不足。这种局部校准失败会被全局可靠性诊断所掩盖，导致无法发现和解决特定输入区域的校准问题。

Method: 作者提出了一个诊断框架来发现隐藏的误校准区域（regimes），无需预定义数据切片。具体方法包括：1）定义相应的误校准场（miscalibration field）；2）学习输入空间的校准感知表示（calibration-aware representation）；3）在学习到的几何空间中，利用核平滑（kernel smoothing）估计有符号的局部误校准。这种方法能够识别输入空间中系统性的校准偏差区域。

Result: 在四个真实世界的大语言模型（LLM）基准测试和十二个LLM上的实验表明：1）依赖于输入的校准异质性（calibration heterogeneity）普遍存在；2）发现的误校准场具有实际应用价值，能够支持局部置信度修正；3）在系统误校准区域，该方法相比基于置信度的方法（如等渗回归和温度缩放）能更有效地降低校准误差。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了传统校准评估方法掩盖的局部校准问题，提出的误校准场发现框架能够有效识别和处理输入依赖的校准异质性。该方法不仅具有诊断价值，还能指导针对性的校准修正，在系统误校准区域优于传统方法，为提升模型可靠性提供了新的思路和工具。

Abstract: Calibration is commonly evaluated by comparing model confidence with its empirical correctness, implicitly treating reliability as a function of the confidence score alone. However, this view can hide substantial structure: models may be systematically overconfident on some kinds of inputs and underconfident on others, causing global reliability diagnostics to obscure localised calibration failures. To address this, we formulate the problem of discovering hidden miscalibration regimes without assuming access to predefined data slices. We define the corresponding miscalibration field and propose a diagnostic framework for estimating it. Our approach learns a calibration-aware representation of the input space and estimates signed local miscalibration by kernel smoothing in the learned geometry. Across four real-world LLM benchmarks and twelve LLMs, we find that input-dependent calibration heterogeneity is prevalent. We further show that the discovered fields are actionable: they support local confidence correction and reduce calibration error in systematically miscalibrated regions where confidence-based methods such as isotonic regression and temperature scaling are less effective.

</details>


### [112] [Unified generalization analysis for physics informed neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13260)
*Yuka Hashimoto,Tomoharu Iwata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为物理信息神经网络（PINNs）及其变分形式（VPINNs）建立了一个统一的泛化界分析框架，揭示了微分算子非线性对泛化性能的显著影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对PINNs和VPINNs的泛化分析研究仍然有限，通常需要稳定性条件或线性椭圆性等限制性假设，缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 利用泰勒展开将非线性微分算子表示为高维空间上的线性算子，引入基于Koopman的分析方法，建立了包含输入变量微分操作的神经网络泛化界。

Result: 证明了高秩网络即使在涉及微分算子的场景下也能获得良好的泛化性能；同时揭示了微分算子的非线性会呈指数级扩大泛化界。

Conclusion: 本文成功为PINNs和VPINNs提供了统一的泛化界理论框架，阐明了微分算子非线性对泛化性能的重要影响，为物理驱动神经网络的理论分析提供了新见解。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) and their variational counterparts (VPINNs) are neural networks that incorporate physical laws, making them useful for scientific problems. Existing generalization analyses for PINNs and VPINNs remain limited, often requiring restrictive assumptions such as stability conditions or linear ellipticity. In this paper, we derive generalization bounds for neural networks that involve differentiation with respect to input variables, covering PINNs and VPINNs under a unified framework. We apply Taylor expansion to represent nonlinear differential operators as linear operators on a high-dimensional space, enabling the use of Koopman-based analysis and showing that high-rank networks can generalize well even in settings involving differential operators. We also show that the nonlinearity of the differential operator exponentially enlarges the bound, highlighting its significant impact on generalization.

</details>


### [113] [HLS-Seek: QoR-Aware Code Generation for High-Level Synthesis via Proxy Comparative Reward Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13536)
*Qingyun Zou,Feng Yu,Hongshi Tan,Yao Chen,Bingsheng He,WengFai Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HLS-Seek框架，通过比较代理奖励模型和不确定性感知的MC dropout切换机制，在避免奖励欺骗的同时大幅提升训练效率，实现了超越GPT-5.1等前沿模型的语法正确性和功能正确性，并在QoR评估中取得了最优延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LLM的HLS方法仅关注功能正确性，完全忽略了服务质量（QoR，包括延迟和资源利用率），而QoR才是HLS的核心目标。传统的强化学习需要昂贵的综合循环，效率低下。

Method: 提出HLS-Seek框架，核心包含两个创新：1）基于比较的代理奖励模型，利用RL只需相对比较而非绝对综合结果的特性，达到99.53%的帕累托优势准确率；2）不确定性感知的蒙特卡洛dropout切换机制，对低置信度候选选择性调用真实Vitis HLS综合并在线更新代理，防止奖励欺骗并实现自改进奖励系统。

Result: 在仅有7B参数的情况下，HLS-eval基准测试中实现81.5%的语法正确率（pass@1）和81.4%的功能正确率（Func@5），超越GPT-5.1及其他前沿模型；训练速度比真实奖励RL快8.5倍；在QoR评估中，16/30内核上达到最低延迟，在9个内核上帕累托支配HLS专用基线方法。

Conclusion: HLS-Seek成功解决了LLM-based HLS方法忽略QoR的关键问题，通过创新的比较代理奖励机制和自改进系统，在保证功能正确性的同时显著提升了硬件性能质量，为HLS自动化设计提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: High-Level Synthesis (HLS) compiles algorithmic C/C++ descriptions into hardware, with Quality of Results (QoR) -- latency and resource utilization -- critically governed by pragma configurations and code structure. Existing LLM-based HLS approaches train for functional correctness but ignore QoR entirely. We observe that reinforcement learning (RL) for HLS does not require absolute synthesis results -- only relative comparisons between candidates. Based on this insight, we propose \textbf{HLS-Seek}, a QoR-aware NL-to-HLS framework that replaces expensive synthesis-in-the-loop RL with a comparative proxy reward model achieving 99.53\% Pareto-dominance accuracy. To prevent reward hacking, we introduce \textit{uncertainty-aware Monte Carlo (MC) dropout switching} that selectively invokes real Vitis HLS synthesis for low-confidence candidates and online updates the proxy, creating a self-improving reward system. HLS-Seek achieves 81.5\% syntax correctness pass@1 and 81.4\% Func@5 on HLS-eval with only 7B parameters, surpassing GPT-5.1 and other frontier models while achieving 8.5$\times$ faster training than real-reward RL. On QoR evaluation, HLS-Seek achieves the lowest latency on 16/30 kernels and Pareto-dominates HLS-specific baselines on 9 kernels.

</details>


### [114] [Chem-GMNet: A Sphere-Native Geometric Transformer for Molecular Property Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13262)
*Deepak Warrier,Raja Sekhar Pappala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GM-Net，一种球面原生Transformer架构，在分子性质预测任务上以更少参数量击败或匹配同等规模的ChemBERTa-2基线，无需预训练即可在ESOL任务上超越预训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于SMILES的化学语言模型将SMILES视为通用文本处理，依赖数百万分子的自监督预训练来弥补领域知识的缺失。作者思考：化学领域具有丰富的结构先验，是否应该设计领域原生的Transformer而非依靠规模来弥补通用模型的不足？

Method: 提出GM-Net（几何度量网络），每个模块都由球面原生对应物替换，实例化为Chem-GMNet。包含三个核心模块：(1) SH-Embedding：将token表示为S^{k-1}上可学习的方向，通过Gegenbauer特征映射进行提升；(2) DualSKA：融合线性时间门控Sphere-Flow递归（其持久状态被证明是输入分布的截断多极展开）和softmax Sphere-Kernel分支；(3) SH-FFN：球面投影→Gegenbauer提升→矩读取。

Result: 在DeepChem骨架划分基准测试中：(1) 随机初始化下，Chem-GMNet在10个MoleculeNet端点中7个获胜，参数量减少约35%；(2) 在相同10M-SMILES ZINC语料上预训练后，在8个共享端点中6个匹配或超越ChemBERTa-2 MLM-10M；(3) 球面维度k=8增至k=10时，ESOL RMSE降至0.938，无需预训练即可在此端点超越预训练的ChemBERTa-2。

Conclusion: 化学等具有丰富结构先验的领域确实值得开发领域原生的Transformer架构。GM-Net通过球面几何设计有效编码化学结构先验，在减少参数和预训练需求的同时实现优异性能，为领域专用架构设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: Modern SMILES-based chemical language models obtain strong MoleculeNet performance by treating SMILES as generic text and compensating with multi-million-molecule self-supervised pretraining. We ask: when a domain carries structural priors as rich as chemistry's, does it warrant a domain-native transformer rather than a generic one rescued by scale? We answer affirmatively with \textbf{GM-Net} (Geometric Measure Network), a transformer family in which every module is replaced by a sphere-native counterpart, and instantiate it as \textbf{Chem-GMNet}. Three blocks follow: SH-Embedding (tokens as learnable directions on $S^{k-1}$ lifted through a Gegenbauer feature map); DualSKA (a per-head fusion of a linear-time gated Sphere-Flow recurrence whose persistent state we prove is the truncated multipole expansion of the input distribution, and a softmax Sphere-Kernel branch over the same Schoenberg-valid kernel); and SH-FFN (sphere projection $\to$ Gegenbauer lift $\to$ moment readout). On canonical DeepChem scaffold splits, against same-shape ChemBERTa-2 baselines under the chemberta3-faithful protocol: (i) random-initialised, Chem-GMNet wins on 7 of 10 MoleculeNet endpoints at $\sim\!35\%$ fewer parameters; (ii) pretrained on the same 10M-SMILES ZINC corpus as ChemBERTa-2 MLM-10M, it matches or beats the public release on 6 of 8 shared endpoints (5/7 excluding a known ClinTox release anomaly). A $(k,L)$ ablation shows that increasing the sphere dimension from $k\!=\!8$ to $k\!=\!10$ at fixed $L\!=\!3$ lowers ESOL RMSE to $0.938$ at scratch, beating pretrained ChemBERTa-2 MLM-10M on this endpoint without any pretraining at all.

</details>


### [115] [Temper and Tilt Lead to SLOP: Reward Hacking Mitigation with Inference-Time Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13537)
*Ye Wang,Jing Liu,Toshiaki Koike-Akino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种推理时对齐技术的扩展方法，通过引入参考模型温度调整，将对齐推广到生成式奖励模型集成的场景（SLOP），并提出了校准算法以缓解奖励黑客问题，在保持对齐性能的同时提升了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时对齐技术理论分析将其视为对奖励模型优化倾斜分布的近似采样，但缺乏对参考模型温度调整的理论扩展，且在面对奖励黑客问题时鲁棒性不足，需要一种能够结合多个生成式奖励模型并保持对齐性能的方法。

Method: 引入参考模型温度调整机制，将推理时对齐技术推广到生成式奖励模型集成场景，采用锐化对数意见池（SLOP）组合多个奖励模型；提出SLOP权重参数校准算法，通过参数调整缓解奖励黑客问题。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的校准算法能够有效缓解奖励黑客现象，在保持对齐性能的同时显著提升了系统的鲁棒性，验证了参考模型温度调整和SLOP集成方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入参考模型温度调整和SLOP集成方法，成功扩展了推理时对齐技术的适用范围，为轻量级替代或补充强化学习提供了更鲁棒的解决方案，使得对齐技术能够持续适应演化的对齐目标和奖励需求。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment techniques offer a lightweight alternative or complement to costly reinforcement learning, while enabling continual adaptation as alignment objectives and reward targets evolve. Existing theoretical analyses justify these methods as approximations to sampling from distributions optimally tilted toward a given reward model. We extend these techniques by introducing reference-model temperature adjustment, which leads to further generalization of inference-time alignment to ensembles of generative reward models combined as a sharpened logarithmic opinion pool (SLOP). To mitigate reward hacking, we propose an algorithm for calibrating SLOP weight parameters and experimentally demonstrate that it improves robustness while preserving alignment performance.

</details>


### [116] [LightSplit: Practical Privacy-Preserving Split Learning via Orthogonal Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13265)
*Mert Cihangiroglu,Alessandro Pegoraro,Phillip Rieger,Antonino Nocera,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LightSplit框架，通过在切割层应用轻量级固定正交随机投影，在大幅降低通信开销的同时有效抑制隐私泄露风险，实现了分割学习中效率与隐私的统一优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分割学习（SL）在切割层接口面临两大核心挑战：高维激活值导致巨大的通信开销，同时暴露的表征容易受到重构攻击。现有方法通常独立地解决效率或隐私问题，往往依赖额外的复杂机制（如稀疏化、量化或噪声注入），缺乏统一高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出LightSplit方法，在切割层应用轻量级固定正交随机投影。基于香农信息论，该投影作为信息瓶颈，限制实例特定信息并抑制可利用的样本信号。方法将高维激活投影为低维表示进行传输，无需架构修改，保持端到端可微性，且因投影不可逆，部分原始表征在客户端被不可逆地丢弃。

Result: 在IID和non-IID设置下进行了广泛评估。结果显示，在传输维度降低高达32倍的情况下，LightSplit仍能保持基线准确率的95%以上，同时维持稳定的训练动态。

Conclusion: LightSplit成功地在分割学习中实现了通信效率与隐私保护的双重目标，通过轻量级、无额外训练组件的设计，证明了无需牺牲模型性能即可有效降低信息暴露和通信成本的可行性。

Abstract: Split learning (SL) enables collaborative training by partitioning a neural network across clients and a central server, but the cut-layer interface introduces a key challenge: high-dimensional activations incur substantial communication overhead while exposing representations vulnerable to reconstruction attacks. Existing approaches typically address efficiency or privacy in isolation, relying on additional mechanisms such as sparsification, quantization, or noise injection. We propose LightSplit, which limits information exposure and reduces communication overhead by applying a lightweight fixed orthogonal random projection at the cut layer. Based on Shannon's information theory, this projection acts as an information bottleneck that restricts instance-specific information and suppresses exploitable per-sample signals. By transmitting low-dimensional projections instead of raw activations, the server operates on lifted representations without requiring architectural modifications, ensuring compatibility with existing SL architectures. By avoiding additional trainable components on the client, the method remains lightweight and suitable for edge devices while preserving end-to-end differentiability via exact gradient propagation. As the projection is non-invertible, part of the original representation is irreversibly discarded at the client, LightSplit reduces the information available for reconstruction and limits information exposure. We extensively evaluate LightSplit on state-of-the-art benchmarks in both IID and non-IID settings across varying projection dimensions and client scales. Our results show that the method retains more than 95% of the baseline accuracy at up to 32x reduction in transmitted dimensionality while maintaining stable training dynamics.

</details>


### [117] [Decoupled and Divergence-Conditioned Prompt for Multi-domain Dynamic Graph Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13540)
*Haonan Yuan,Qingyun Sun,Junhua Shi,Xingcheng Fu,Jianxin Li,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个多领域动态图基础模型DyGFM，通过语义-时序解耦和发散条件提示机制解决了跨领域预训练中的负迁移问题，在节点分类和链接预测任务上显著优于12个现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中动态图普遍存在，但来自不同领域的动态图具有内在不一致的语义和时序模式，导致多领域预训练困难。现有的“预训练-微调”范式面临严重的负知识迁移问题，且目前尚不存在多领域动态图基础模型。

Method: 提出了DyGFM模型，包含三个核心组件：1）双分支预训练策略，实现语义-时序解耦以分离可迁移语义与领域特定动态；2）跨领域路由机制，采用发散感知专家选择来缓解领域适应过程中的负迁移；3）发散条件提示生成器，注入针对语义和时序特征的轻量级可学习图提示，实现高效的下游微调。

Result: 在连续动态图基准测试上的大量实验表明，DyGFM在节点分类和链接预测任务上持续优于12个最先进的基线模型，在效果和效率方面均取得优越性能。

Conclusion: DyGFM成功实现了跨多个领域的动态图统一建模，为构建通用动态图基础模型提供了有效解决方案，证明了语义-时序解耦和发散条件提示机制在解决多领域动态图学习挑战中的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic graphs are ubiquitous in real-world systems, and building generalizable dynamic Graph Foundation Models has become a frontier in graph learning. However, dynamic graphs from different domains pose fundamental challenges to unified modeling, as their semantic and temporal patterns are inherently inconsistent, making the multi-domain pre-training difficult. Consequently, the widely used "pretrain-then-finetune" paradigm often suffers from severe negative knowledge transfer. To the best of our knowledge, there exists no multi-domain dynamic GFM. In this work, we propose DyGFM, a Dynamic Graph Foundation Model over multiple domains based on decoupled and divergence-conditioned prompting. To disentangle transferable semantics from the domain-specific dynamics, we introduce a dual-branch pre-training strategy with semantic-temporal decoupling. To alleviate negative transfer during domain adaptation, we further develop a cross-domain routing mechanism with divergence-aware expert selection. To enable efficient downstream fine-tuning, we design a divergence-conditioned prompt generator that injects lightweight, learnable graph prompts tailored to semantic and temporal traits. Extensive experiments on continuous dynamic graph benchmarks demonstrate that DyGFM consistently outperforms 12 state-of-the-art baselines on both node classification and link prediction tasks, achieving superior effectiveness and efficiency.

</details>


### [118] [Byzantine-Robust Distributed Sparse Learning Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13283)
*Yuxuan Wang,Lixin Zhang,Kangqiang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对高维稀疏线性模型的拜占庭容错分布式估计框架，通过结合局部ℓ₁正则化鲁棒估计与服务器端鲁棒聚合，在温和条件下实现了接近最优的统计速率，并保持了通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高维稀疏线性模型在分布式环境下的拜占庭容错估计问题，旨在开发一种既能抵御恶意节点攻击，又能保持统计效率和计算效率的通用框架。

Method: 将局部ℓ₁正则化鲁棒估计与服务器端的鲁棒聚合相结合，框架适用于伪Huber回归、分位数回归和稀疏SVM等多种方法。

Result: 证明了所提估计器具有非渐近保证，在温和条件下能够达到接近最优的统计速率，同时保持通信高效性；模拟实验显示在多种拜占庭攻击下，该方法在估计、支持恢复和分类准确率方面具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维稀疏模型的分布式估计提供了一种兼具理论保证与实用性的拜占庭容错解决方案。

Abstract: We revisit Byzantine robust distributed estimation for high-dimensional sparse linear models. By combining local $\ell_1$-regularized robust estimation with robust aggregation at the server, the framework applies to pseudo-Huber regression, quantile regression, and sparse SVM. We show that the resulting estimators yield non-asymptotic guarantees and attain near-optimal statistical rates under mild conditions, while remaining communication-efficient. Simulations confirm strong robustness in estimation, support recovery and classification accuracy under various Byzantine attacks.

</details>


### [119] [Self-Supervised On-Policy Reinforcement Learning via Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13554)
*Asim Osman,Sasha Abramowitz,Mark Bergh,Ulrich Armel Mbou Sob,Ruan John de Kock,Omayma Mahjoub,Oussama Hidaoui,Noah De Nicola,Arnol Manuel Fokam,Felix Chalumeau,Daniel Rajaonarivonivelomanantsoa,Siddarth Singh,Refiloe Shabe,Juan Claude Formanek,Simon Verster Du Toit,Arnu Pretorius*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CPPO算法，这是首个on-policy的对比强化学习方法。它无需手工设计的奖励函数，在18个任务中，有14个显著超越现有CRL基线，12个匹配或超越使用手工奖励的PPO性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对比强化学习(CRL)算法都依赖于off-policy优化，且主要局限于连续动作空间，在离散环境中的研究很少。这导致CRL与广泛使用且有效的现代on-policy训练流程（适用于连续和离散环境中的单智能体和多智能体RL）脱节。

Method: 提出了对比近端策略优化(CPPO)，这是一种on-policy对比RL算法。CPPO直接从对比Q值导出策略优势，并通过标准PPO目标进行优化，无需奖励函数或经验回放缓冲区。该方法建立了CRL与on-policy训练流程之间的首个连接。

Result: 在连续和离散、单智能体和协作多智能体任务上进行了评估。CPPO在18个任务中有14个显著超越之前的CRL基线，在18个任务中有12个匹配或超越使用手工密集奖励的PPO性能。

Conclusion: CPPO成功建立了on-policy训练与对比强化学习之间的连接，证明了on-policy方法不仅可行，而且能在无需手工奖励函数的情况下，在大多数任务上达到或超越使用手工奖励的传统PPO方法的性能。

Abstract: Contrastive reinforcement learning (CRL) learns goal-conditioned Q-values through a contrastive objective over state-action and goal representations, removing the need for hand-crafted reward functions. Despite impressive success in achieving viable self-supervised learning in RL, all existing CRL algorithms rely on off-policy optimisation and are mostly constrained to continuous action spaces, with little research invested in discrete environments. This leaves CRL disconnected from widely used and effective, modern on-policy training pipelines adopted across both single-agent and multi-agent RL in continuous and discrete environments. To establish a first connection, we introduce Contrastive Proximal Policy Optimisation (CPPO). CPPO is an on-policy contrastive RL algorithm that derives policy advantages directly from contrastive Q-values and optimises them via the standard PPO objective, without requiring a reward function or a replay buffer. We evaluate CPPO across continuous and discrete, single-agent and cooperative multi-agent tasks. Whilst the existence of an on-policy approach is inherently useful, we observe that \textbf{CPPO not only significantly outperforms the previous CRL baselines in 14 out of 18 tasks, but also matches or exceeds PPO's performance, which uses hand-crafted dense rewards, in 12 out of the 18 tasks tested.}

</details>


### [120] [Dynamical Predictive Modelling of Cardiovascular Disease Progression Post-Myocardial Infarction via ECG-Trained Artificial Intelligence Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13568)
*Riccardo Cavarra,Lupo Lovatelli,Shaheim Ogbomo-Harmitt,Shahid Aziz,Adelaide De Vecchi,Andrew King,Oleg Aslanidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合对比学习和监督多任务头的预训练AI模型，利用患者特异性时序信息进行ECG建模，在心肌梗死后不良结局预测任务上显著优于从头训练的模型（AUC 0.794 vs 0.608），证明了临床结构化的预训练策略在医学小样本数据场景下的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心肌梗死是主要死亡原因，亟需预测其不良结局。然而基于ECG的预后模型表现不佳，主要原因是深度学习需要大量标注数据，而医学领域此类数据稀缺。基础模型可通过自监督从未标注ECG中学习，但医学相关的训练策略仍未被充分探索。

Method: 提出一种预训练AI模型，结合对比学习与监督多任务头，整合患者特异性时序信息；随后在心肌梗死后结局预测任务上进行微调。

Result: 所提模型在心肌梗死后结局预测任务上显著优于从头训练的模型，AUC从0.608提升至0.794，证明临床结构化ECG建模在有限数据条件下能显著提升分类性能。

Conclusion: 临床结构化的ECG预训练建模策略能有效缓解医学数据稀缺问题，显著提升小样本数据条件下的心肌梗死后结局预测性能，为医学影像时序数据的建模提供了新思路。

Abstract: Myocardial infarction (MI) is a leading cause of death, and its adverse outcomes are urgent to predict. Yet ECG-based prognostic models underperform because deep learning requires large, labelled datasets, which are scarce in medicine. Foundation models can learn from unlabelled ECGs via selfsupervision, but medically relevant training strategies remain underexplored. We propose a pretrained artificial intelligence model that combines patient-specific temporal information using contrastive learning with supervised multitask heads, then fine-tunes on post-MI outcome prediction. The proposed model outperformed a model trained from scratch (0.794 vs 0.608 AUC) showing that clinically structured ECG modelling improves classification in limited data regimes.

</details>


### [121] [PaMM: Periodic Motif Memory for Atomistic Models with an Explicit Local-Structure Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13297)
*Ryan Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PaMM（周期性模体记忆），通过显式的原子对和三原子组查找特征增强UMA eSCN-MD边缘编码器，在固定训练预算下显著提升了周期性原子模型的能量和力预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性晶体在平移晶胞和化学相关结构中反复出现相似的局部配位模体，但当前的等变原子模型通常仅在密集边特征中隐式编码这些模式，缺乏显式的结构化表示。

Method: 设计PaMM周期性模体记忆模块，通过原子对模体键(Z_j, Z_i, b_r)和三原子组模体键(Z_j, Z_i, Z_k, b_θ)进行哈希映射到固定大小查找表，采用轻量级的门控和仿射变换变体与基线边表示融合，引入显式模体记忆作为归纳偏置。

Result: 在UMA-S OMAT匹配设置的10k步检查点，两种PaMM变体均优于基线：门控变体获得最佳能量MAE，仿射变体获得最佳力MAE；20k步匹配实验保持相同优势；控制实验表明配对/三原子组结构化组织而非通用容量增加是性能提升的关键；在OMAT24源族内评估显示在留出生成族上获得小幅一致的增益。

Conclusion: 在UMA-S + OMAT研究体系中，显式配对/三原子组模体记忆是周期性原子建模的有用归纳偏置，但不声称广泛跨数据集迁移、唯一优选融合变体或强科学可解释性。

Abstract: Periodic crystals repeatedly instantiate similar local coordination motifs across translated cells and chemically related structures, but current equivariant atomistic models usually encode these patterns only implicitly in dense edge features. We introduce PaMM, a periodic motif memory that augments the UMA eSCN-MD edge encoder with explicit pair and triplet lookup features. Pair motifs are keyed by $(Z_j, Z_i, b_r)$ and triplet motifs by $(Z_j, Z_i, Z_k, b_θ)$, hashed into fixed-size tables and fused with the baseline edge representation through lightweight gate-only and affine-equipped variants. We evaluate PaMM in a matched UMA-S OMAT setting and focus on a narrow question: whether explicit motif memory helps at a fixed intermediate training budget. At the 10k-step checkpoint, both PaMM variants improve over the plain baseline; gate-only gives the best energy MAE, while the affine-equipped variant gives the best force MAE. A matched 20k follow-up keeps the same operating-point picture. Aligned controls show that the gain weakens for pair-only, triplet-only, random-bucket, and parameter-matched MLP alternatives, suggesting that the benefit is tied to structured pair/triplet organization rather than generic added capacity. A within-OMAT24 source-family evaluation also shows small but consistent gains across held-out generation families. We therefore make a focused claim: in the studied UMA-S + OMAT regime, explicit pair/ triplet motif memory is a useful inductive bias for periodic atomistic modeling. We do not claim broad cross-dataset transfer, a uniquely preferred fusion variant, or strong scientific interpretability beyond a more inspectable local-structure interface.

</details>


### [122] [Beyond Perplexity: A Geometric and Spectral Study of Low-Rank Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13652)
*Namrata Shivagunde,Vijeta Deshpande,Sherin Muckatira,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种低秩预训练方法与全秩训练的差异，发现即使验证困惑度相近，低秩方法在损失景观几何结构、权重谱特性和激活表示等方面仍与全秩训练存在显著差异，验证困惑度并非可靠的模型质量代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩预训练方法的比较主要依赖单次运行的验证困惑度，但困惑度是较差的解质量代理指标——不同方法可能达到相同困惑度但收敛到不同的损失景观区域和内部表示。核心问题在于：低秩方法是否能产生与全秩训练泛化能力相当的模型，还是秩约束从根本上改变了所达到的解？
