Method: 在三种模型规模（60M、130M、350M）下，对五种低秩预训练方法进行系统比较：GaLore和Fira（内存高效优化器）、CoLA和SLTrain（架构重参数化）、ReLoRA（带周期性重置的适配器式更新）。从四个维度、16个指标进行评估：沿随机/Top-K PCA方向的1维损失景观、检查点间的1维插值、权重和学习更新的谱结构、与全秩训练的激活相似性。

Result: 低秩方法与全秩训练以及彼此之间都不等价，即使验证困惑度接近。全秩训练在随机方向上收敛到比低秩方法更尖锐的盆地，而在Top-1 PCA方向上情况相反。每种方法收敛到几何上不同的盆地。低秩激活在训练后期与全秩在较后层发生偏离，其中GaLore最接近全秩训练。验证困惑度在每个规模上并不总能转化为下游性能，加入几何和谱指标可改善预测能力。

Conclusion: 低秩预训练方法在解空间中收敛到与全秩训练几何和谱特性不同的区域，验证困惑度不足以单独作为模型质量评估指标，需要结合几何和谱指标进行更全面的评估。不同低秩方法各有特点，其中GaLore在激活相似性方面最接近全秩训练。

Abstract: Pre-training large language models is dominated by the memory cost of storing full-rank weights, gradients, and optimizer states. Low-rank pre-training has emerged to address this, and the space of methods has grown rapidly. A central question remains open: do low-rank methods produce models that generalize comparably to full-rank training, or does the rank constraint fundamentally alter the solutions reached? Existing comparisons rely almost entirely on validation perplexity from single-seed runs, often carried forward from prior literature. Yet perplexity is a poor proxy for solution quality; two methods can match on perplexity while converging to different loss landscape regions and internal representations. We close this gap by characterizing the solutions found by five low-rank pre-training methods, GaLore and Fira (memory-efficient optimizers), CoLA and SLTrain (architecture reparameterizations), and ReLoRA (adapter-style updates with periodic resets), against full-rank training at three model scales (60M, 130M, 350M). We evaluate each along 16 metrics across four dimensions: 1-D loss landscape along random/top-K PCA directions, 1-D interpolation between checkpoints, spectral structure of the weights and learned updates, and activation similarity to full-rank training. We show that low-rank methods are not equivalent to full-rank training, nor to one another, even when validation perplexity is close. Full-rank training settles into a sharper basin than low-rank methods along random directions, while the reverse holds for the top-1 PCA direction. Each method converges to a geometrically distinct basin. Low-rank activations diverge from full-rank in later layers as training progresses, with GaLore tracking full-rank most closely. Further, validation perplexity does not translate to downstream performance at every scale. Adding geometric and spectral metrics improves the prediction.

</details>


### [123] [Safe Bayesian Optimization for Uncertain Correlations Matrices in Linear Models of Co-Regionalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13302)
*Jannis Lübsen,Annika Eichler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将多任务贝叶斯优化中使用不确定相关矩阵的安全保证，从内在共区域化模型扩展到了线性共区域化模型，并验证了新方法能带来性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的安全多任务贝叶斯优化安全保证仅适用于内在共区域化模型（ICM），而线性共区域化模型（LMC）通过组合多个特征能更灵活地建模任务间的相关性，因此需要将安全保证扩展至LMC以支持更广泛的应用场景。

Method: 作者针对具有线性共区域化模型核的高斯过程，推导了向量值函数的均匀误差界限，从而为安全优化提供理论保证。

Result: 在安全多任务贝叶斯优化基准测试的数值比较中，证明了使用线性共区域化模型相比传统方法具有潜在的性能提升。

Conclusion: 成功将安全多任务贝叶斯优化的安全保证扩展到了更灵活的线性共区域化模型，在理论推导和实验验证上均证明了该方法的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: This paper extends safety guarantees for multi-task Bayesian optimization with uncertain correlation matrices from intrinsic co-reginalization models to linear models of co-reginalization. The latter allows for more flexible modeling of the inter-task correlations by composing multiple features. We derive uniform error bounds for vector-valued functions sampled from a Gaussian process with a linear model of co-reginalization kernel. Furthermore, we show the potential improvement of performance using linear models of co-reginalization in a numerical comparison on a safe multi-task Bayesian optimization benchmark.

</details>


### [124] [A Hierarchical Language Model with Predictable Scaling Laws and Provable Benefits of Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13687)
*Jason Gaitonde,Frederic Koehler,Elchanan Mossel,Joonhyung Shin,Allan Sly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过合成语言理论分析证明，自回归生成需要Ω(n)的上下文长度才能忠实生成长度为n的序列，而引入推理机制可将所需工作记忆降至Θ(log n)，实现指数级改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自回归生成中上下文长度和推理能力的作用机制，现有研究缺乏精确的理论分析框架。作者希望通过可精确分析的合成语言，揭示上下文长度与推理在层次结构生成中的定量关系。

Method: 提出基于树广播过程的合成语言族，引入精确k-gram假设替代上下文长度为k的Transformer进行理论分析。针对两种设置推导生成序列分布统计量的渐近预测：(1)Ising广播过程（软约束语言）；(2)着色广播过程（硬约束语言）。

Result: (1)Ising过程：生成和的方差随上下文深度呈对数线性缩放，峰度收敛至高斯分布，任何亚线性上下文都会偏离真实语言；(2)着色过程：有界上下文自回归高概率产生不一致序列。证明下界Ω(n)和推理模型上界Θ(log n)的指数级差距，Transformer实验定量验证理论预测。

Conclusion: 自回归生成需要线性上下文长度，而推理机制可实现指数级改进，为理解语言模型的上下文长度需求和推理价值提供了精确的理论基础和实证支持。

Abstract: We introduce a family of synthetic languages with hierarchical structure -- generated by a broadcast process on trees -- for which the role of context length and reasoning in autoregressive generation can be analyzed precisely. At the heart of our analytic approach is an \emph{exact $k$-gram ansatz} in place of transformers with context length $k$, a substitution we then validate empirically. Using this ansatz we derive explicit asymptotic predictions for distributional statistics of the sequences produced by a trained model, instantiated in two settings. For the \emph{Ising broadcast process} (a soft-constrained language), we prove that the variance of the generated sum scales log-linearly in the context depth and its kurtosis converges to that of a Gaussian -- both deviating from the true language for any sublinear context. For the \emph{coloring broadcast process} (a hard-constrained language) in the freezing regime, bounded-context autoregression produces sequences that, with high probability, are inconsistent with \emph{any} valid coloring of the underlying tree. Together these results imply an $Ω(n)$ lower bound on the context length required to faithfully sample length-$n$ sequences. In contrast, we prove that an autoregressive \emph{reasoning} model with only $Θ(\log n)$ working memory can sample exactly from the true language -- an exponential improvement. We confirm both the lower-bound predictions and the reasoning-based upper bound empirically with transformers trained on the synthetic language; the trained models track our asymptotic predictions quantitatively across a wide range of context sizes.

</details>


### [125] [MPINeuralODE: Multiple-Initial-Condition Physics-Informed Neural ODEs for Globally Consistent Dynamical System Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13305)
*Lake Yang,Antonio Malpica-Morales,Frank Ioannis Papadakis Wood,Serafim Kalliadasis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MPINeuralODE方法，通过结合软物理信息残差和多初始条件多重打靶课程，显著提升了神经ODE在未见初始条件和长时间预测上的泛化能力，在Lotka-Volterra任务上相比基线降低26%的误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经普通微分方程（Neural ODEs）在训练轨迹上表现良好，但在未见初始条件和长时间预测上泛化能力较差，无法准确恢复底层向量场。

Method: 提出MPINeuralODE框架，结合两个互补组件：（1）软物理信息残差项，用于锚定向量场幅值；（2）多初始条件（MIC）多重打靶课程，用于扩大支持域。物理项与MIC策略在结构上互补，前者锚定向量场，后者扩大支撑区域。

Result: 在Lotka-Volterra系统上，MPINeuralODE在数据驱动方法中实现了最低的样本外误差和长时间预测MSE，相比基线Neural ODE降低26%，同时在哈密顿漂移指标上基本匹配PINN消融实验结果。通过样本外误差、长时间稳定性和哈密顿漂移三个维度评估，验证了该方法能有效恢复底层向量场。

Conclusion: MPINeuralODE成功解决了Neural ODE泛化性能差的问题，物理信息约束与多初始条件训练策略的有机结合使学习到的动力学能够更好地恢复真实向量场，为动态系统建模提供了更可靠的方案。

Abstract: Neural ordinary differential equations (Neural ODEs) often fit training trajectories while generalizing poorly to unseen initial conditions and long horizons. We propose MPINeuralODE, which combines a soft physics-informed residual with a Multiple-Initial-Condition (MIC) multiple-shooting curriculum whose ingredients are structurally complementary: the physics term anchors the vector-field magnitude on the support that MIC enlarges. We evaluate along three axes: out-of-sample error, long-horizon stability, and Hamiltonian drift, which together expose whether the learned dynamics recover the underlying vector field. On Lotka-Volterra, MPINeuralODE achieves the lowest out-of-sample and long-horizon MSE among data-driven methods, with a 26% reduction over the baseline Neural ODE, while essentially matching the PINN ablation on Hamiltonian drift.

</details>


### [126] [The WidthWall: A Strict Expressivity Hierarchy for Hypergraph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13690)
*Fengqing Jiang,Yuetai Li,Yichen Feng,Kaiyuan Zheng,Luyao Niu,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Linda Bushnell,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了超图神经网络（HGNNs）的表达能力与同态密度之间的理论联系，发现了“宽度墙”这一根本架构限制，并据此统一解释了15种HGNN架构的表达能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管超图神经网络（HGNNs）被用于学习高阶交互数据，但目前尚不清楚这些模型究竟能表示哪些高阶结构。理解HGNNs的表达能力界限对于模型设计和应用至关重要。

Method: 作者利用同态密度（homomorphism densities）来形式化超图模式检测与计数能力，结合经典的同态计数完备性与不变量逼近理论，证明了同态密度能生成所有连续超图不变量，并按超树宽度（hypertree width）组织成严格的层级结构。

Result: 发现了“宽度墙”（Width Wall）：这是一个根本的架构限制，任何有限隐藏维度、训练过程或固定深度的HGNN都无法表示需要比其架构宽度更宽模式的不变量。该框架统一表征了15种HGNN架构，精确识别了团扩张（clique expansion）造成的信息损失，并在真实世界超图的节点分类任务中验证了宽度墙的预测能力。

Conclusion: 本研究为HGNNs的表达能力提供了统一的理论框架，揭示了模型架构的根本限制，为设计密度感知模型以突破有界宽度消息传递的表达能力提供了理论依据和实践指导。

Abstract: Hypergraphs provide a natural framework to model higher-order interactions in scientific, social, and biological systems. Hypergraph neural networks (HGNNs) aim to learn from such data, yet it remains unclear which higher-order structures these models can represent. We show that hypergraph expressivity is governed by which small patterns an architecture can detect and count. We formalize this via homomorphism densities, which measure how often a structural motif appears in a hypergraph. Combining classical homomorphism-count completeness with invariant approximation, we show that homomorphism densities generate all continuous hypergraph invariants and organize them into a strict hierarchy indexed by hypertree width. This yields a Width Wall: a fundamental architectural limit beyond which no hidden dimension, training procedure or fixed-depth HGNN can represent invariants requiring wider patterns. Our framework provides a unified characterization of 15 HGNN architectures, precisely identifies information lost by clique expansion, and motivates density-aware models that extend expressivity beyond bounded-width message passing. We experimentally validate this finding on an APPLICATION NODE CLASSIFICATION SUITE of real-world hypergraphs, where the Width Wall predicts when graph-reduction baselines fail and when density features help.

</details>


### [127] [Supervised Deep Multimodal Matrix Factorization for Interpretable Brain Network Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13312)
*Amjad Seyedi,Lifang He,Songlin Zhao,Akwum Onwunta,Nicolas Gillis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SD3MF框架，将对称非负矩阵三因子分解从无监督单图聚类扩展为监督的多模态图预测，在多模态连接组数据集上优于CNN和GNN等深度学习基线，同时提供可解释的生物学洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对整合性脑网络分析的需求，现有的无监督单图聚类方法难以处理多模态图数据的监督预测问题，因此需要一种能够融合多模态信息、学习层次化表示并进行监督预测的可解释框架。

Method: 提出监督深度多模态矩阵因子化(SD3MF)框架：1)将对称非负矩阵三因子分解(SNMTF)扩展到监督的多模态图预测；2)为每个模态学习深度层次化分解，并通过共享潜在表示对齐不同视图的受试者；3)采用编码器-解码器架构联合优化图重建和监督预测；4)通过自适应权重实现数据驱动的多模态融合；5)利用社区级交互矩阵表示每个受试者，生成可解释和判别性特征。

Result: 在多模态连接组数据集上的实验表明，SD3MF在性能上持续优于CNN和GNN等强大的深度学习基线方法，同时能够提供生物学上可解释的见解，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Conclusion: SD3MF成功地将矩阵分解方法与现代深度学习架构结合，为整合性脑网络分析提供了一个既具有优越预测性能又保持模型可解释性的解决方案，代码已开源以确保可重复性。

Abstract: We present Supervised Deep Multimodal Matrix Factorization (SD3MF), an interpretable framework for integrative brain network analysis that generalizes Symmetric Nonnegative Matrix Tri-Factorization (SNMTF) from unsupervised single-graph clustering to supervised prediction over populations of multimodal graphs. SD3MF learns deep hierarchical factorizations for each modality together with a shared latent representation that aligns subjects across views. An encoder-decoder formulation jointly optimizes graph reconstruction and supervised prediction, while adaptive weights enable data-driven multimodal fusion. By representing each subject through community-level interaction matrices, the model yields interpretable and discriminative features. Experiments on multimodal connectome datasets show that SD3MF consistently outperforms strong deep learning baselines such as CNNs and GNNs, while enabling biologically interpretable insights. Code for reproducibility is available at: https://github.com/amjadseyedi/SD3MF.

</details>


### [128] [Robust and Explainable Bicuspid Aortic Valve Diagnosis Using Stacked Ensembles on Echocardiography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13730)
*Christos Chrysanthos Nikolaidis,Vasileios Sachpekidis,Nikolas Moustakidis,Theofilos Moustakidis,Pavlos S. Efraimidis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种可解释AI模型，利用常规经胸超声心动图（TTE）的胸骨旁长轴（PLAX）视频，实现了二叶主动脉瓣（BAV）与三叶主动脉瓣（TAV）的高精度自动分类（F1-score 0.907），并提供可视化解释以辅助非专家环境下的早期诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经胸超声心动图（TTE）是诊断二叶主动脉瓣（BAV）的一线影像学手段，但其诊断性能高度依赖操作者专业水平和图像质量，存在主观性和差异性。研究旨在开发一种客观、可靠且可解释的AI工具，利用常规采集的切面进行自动化辅助诊断，以降低对专家经验的依赖并改善资源受限环境下的诊断能力。

Method: 研究提出了一种多骨干视频集成深度学习模型，使用常规采集的胸骨旁长轴（PLAX）电影序列作为输入。训练与评估采用防泄露的分层外层交叉验证协议，数据集包含90例患者研究（48例BAV，42例TAV）。模型结合了帧级Grad-CAM技术定位关键解剖区域，并利用全局聚合的SHAP值量化各视频骨干对预测的贡献，从而实现透明的案例级可审计性。

Result: 在固定的外层划分和10个随机种子测试中，校准后的堆叠集成模型实现了0.907的外层交叉验证F1分数和0.877的召回率。帧级Grad-CAM成功将关键证据定位至主动脉根部和瓣叶平面，SHAP值量化了各模型组件的贡献，验证了模型的卓越分类性能与可解释性。

Conclusion: 基于PLAX的视频集成模型能够利用常规超声电影序列实现可靠的BAV/TAV分类，结合Grad-CAM与SHAP的可解释性分析支持透明的诊断审计。该技术有望在非专科或资源有限的临床环境中促进BAV的早期检测与辅助诊断。

Abstract: Transthoracic echocardiography (TTE) is the first-line imaging modality for diagnosing bicuspid aortic valve (BAV), yet diagnostic performance varies with operator expertise and image quality. We developed an explainable AI model that distinguishes BAV from tricuspid aortic valves (TAV) using routinely acquired parasternal long-axis (PLAX) cine loops. A multi-backbone video ensemble was trained and evaluated using a leakage-aware, stratified outer cross-validation protocol on $N{=}90$ patient studies (48 BAV, 42 TAV). Across fixed outer splits and 10 random seeds, the calibrated stacked ensemble achieved an outer-CV F1-score of $0.907$ and recall of $0.877$. Frame-level Grad-CAM localized salient evidence to the aortic root and leaflet plane, while globally aggregated SHAP values quantified each video backbone's contribution to the stacked prediction, enabling transparent, case-level auditability. These findings indicate that PLAX-based video ensembles can support reliable BAV/TAV classification from routine echocardiographic cine loops and may facilitate earlier detection in non-specialist or resource-limited clinical settings.

</details>


### [129] [Shortcut Mitigation via Spurious-Positive Samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13340)
*Phuong Quynh Le,Jörg Schlötterer,Sari Sadiya,Gemma Roig,Christin Seifert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对性的模型分析方法，通过识别模型依赖虚假属性的小样本集，定位并正则化中间层的相关神经元，从而在无需额外标注或平衡数据的情况下缓解模型的shortcut问题，提升鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的捷径缓解策略通常依赖于训练数据标注、组平衡的留出数据或训练数据中存在所有组合（即所有虚假属性与类别的组合），但这些条件在实际应用中往往难以满足。因此，需要一种不依赖这些苛刻条件的鲁棒性提升方法。

Method: 提出一种针对性的模型分析方法：首先识别出一小部分模型依赖虚假属性的样本实例；然后基于"该特征不应被用于预测"的推理思路，定位模型中间层中与此高度相关的神经元；最后对这些神经元的影响进行正则化约束。

Result: 该方法能够确保模型依赖信息性特征而非虚假属性进行预测，从而避免"因错误理由而预测正确"的现象；在不使用额外平衡留出数据或标注的情况下，有效提升了模型对shortcut的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够在现实条件受限（缺乏完整组别、标注或平衡数据）的场景下有效缓解shortcut问题，为提升模型鲁棒性提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Shortcut mitigation strategies commonly rely on training data annotations, group-balanced held-out data or the presence of all groups, i.e., all combinations of (spurious) attributes and classes, in the training data. However, these requirements are rarely met in practice. We instead propose a method for targeted model analysis to identify a small set of instances in which the model relies on spurious attributes. Using that set and following ``this feature should not be used for prediction'' reasoning, we identify highly relevant neurons in an intermediate layer and regularize their impact. This ensures that models learn to depend on informative features rather than being right for the wrong reasons, thereby improving robustness without requiring additional balanced held-out data or annotations.

</details>


### [130] [Contextual Bandits for Resource-Constrained Devices using Probabilistic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13346)
*Marco Angioli,Kevin Johansson,Antonello Rosato,Amy Loutfi,Denis Kleyko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出概率超维上下文赌博机算法，通过随机子集更新和时间衰减概率，实现了在低精度（最低3比特）下无需周期性二值化即可防止溢出，在降低计算成本的同时保持了接近全精度的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的超维上下文赌博机（HD-CB）算法采用累积式学习规则，导致动作向量数值随时间增长，需要高精度存储；在低精度设备上通过周期性二值化防止溢出，但会丢失幅度信息并降低决策质量。因此，迫切需要一种能够在资源受限设备上高效运行且不损失性能的低精度方案。

Method: 提出概率HD-CB算法，引入概率更新规则替代确定性累积：在每个时间步仅随机更新向量分量的子集，使用时间衰减的更新概率，并将分量数值约束在预定义范围[-k,+k]内。该方法支持低精度分量，无需周期性二值化即可防止溢出，并按更新分量比例降低期望更新成本。

Result: 在Open Bandit Pipeline的标准化合成CB基准测试上进行离线策略评估，结果表明：概率HD-CB在相同精度下始终优于二值化HD-CB；仅需每分量3比特即可逼近标准HD-CB的性能。

Conclusion: 概率HD-CB成功解决了超维CB算法在资源受限设备上的精度与性能权衡问题，实现了低精度、低成本更新和高决策质量的统一，为在边缘设备上部署CB算法提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Contextual bandits (CB) are online sequential decision-making problems under partial feedback that underpin many adaptive services. There is a growing demand to deploy CB agents directly on-device, under strict constraints on memory, compute, and energy. However, standard linear CB algorithms are often impractical for resource-constrained devices with their unfavorable scaling in computational and memory costs. Recently, HD-CB, a CB approach based on hyperdimensional computing principles, has been proposed to model and solve CB problems by moving into high-dimensional spaces. HD-CB offers faster convergence, favorable scalability, and improves memory efficiency compared to linear CB algorithms. However, its learning rule is accumulation-based: the values of action vectors grow over time, requiring high precision. While periodic binarization can prevent overflow in low-precision components, it may discard important information about magnitudes and degrade decision quality. This paper introduces probabilistic HD-CB, a low-precision variant that replaces deterministic accumulation with a probabilistic update rule. At each step, only a random subset of vector components is updated, with a time-decaying update probability, and component values are constrained to a predefined range [-k,+k]. This approach enables low-precision components, prevents overflow without periodic binarization, and reduces the expected update cost in proportion to the fraction of updated components. Off-policy evaluation on standardized synthetic CB benchmarks using the Open Bandit Pipeline shows that probabilistic HD-CB consistently outperforms binarized HD-CB at equal precision, while approaching the performance of HD-CB with as few as 3 bits per component.

</details>


### [131] [High-Rate Quantized Matrix Multiplication II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13768)
*Or Ordentlich,Yury Polyanskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了已知协方差矩阵情况下的量化矩阵乘法，将注水理论应用于LLM权重量化，发现WaterSIC方案具有基无关性和近优性能，GPTQ配合随机旋转也能达到近似最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大语言模型（LLM）中广泛存在的仅权重后训练量化场景，研究者希望利用权重矩阵列的协方差矩阵信息来优化量化算法的性能。现有的实用算法（如GPTQ）在分配量化比特率时采用均匀分配策略，而信息论中的加权均方误差源编码问题已有经典的注水解决方案，这为改进LLM量化提供了理论依据。

Method: 1. 建立了仅权重量化与加权均方误差(WMSE)源编码问题之间的联系；
2. 利用经典的注水理论来指导不同坐标间的比特率分配；
3. 分析了WaterSIC方案在高码率下的性能，证明了其基无关性（由协方差矩阵行列式决定，对随机旋转免疫）；
4. 通过实验对比GPTQ在随机旋转下的表现与WaterSIC的性能差距。

Result: 1. WaterSIC方案在高码率下的性能由协方差矩阵的行列式决定，具有基无关性，不受随机旋转变换影响；
2. WaterSIC的性能距离信息论失真极限仅相差一个乘性因子$\frac{2πe}{12}$（约0.25比特/元素）；
3. GPTQ虽然受基选择影响，但在随机旋转下针对Llama-3-8B的实际协方差矩阵，其性能距离WaterSIC仅差0.1比特（具体取决于层类型）。

Conclusion: 注水理论可以有效指导LLM量化算法的比特率分配，提升量化性能。WaterSIC作为一种使用标量INT量化器的方案，在高码率下具有基无关性和近似最优的性能。更重要的是，研究发现GPTQ配合随机旋转也能达到接近最优的性能，这为实际应用中的量化算法选择提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: This is the second part of the work investigating quantized matrix multiplication (MatMul). In part I we considered the case of calibration-free quantization, whereas here we discuss the setting where covariance matrix $Σ_X$ of the columns of the second factor is available. This setting arises in the ubiquitous task of weight-only post-training quantization of LLMs.
  Weight-only quantization is related to the problem of weighted mean squared error (WMSE) source coding, whose classical (reverse) waterfilling solution dictates how one should distribute rate between coordinates of the vector. We show how waterfilling can be used to improve practical LLM quantization algorithms (GPTQ), which at present allocate rate equally. A recent scheme (known as ``WaterSIC'') that only uses scalar INT quantizers is analyzed and its high-rate performance is shown to be (a) basis free (i.e., characterized by the determinant of $Σ_X$ and, thus, unlike existing schemes, is immune to applying random rotations); and (b) within a multiplicative factor of $\frac{2πe}{12}$ (or 0.25 bit/entry) of the information-theoretic distortion limit. GPTQ's performance, in turn, is affected by the choice of basis, but for a random rotation and actual $Σ_X$ from Llama-3-8B we find it to be within 0.1 bit (depending on the layer type) of WaterSIC, suggesting that GPTQ with random rotation is also near optimal, at least in the high-rate regime.

</details>


### [132] [GeoFlowVLM: Geometry-Aware Joint Uncertainty for Frozen Vision-Language Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13352)
*Mayank Nautiyal,Li Ju,Andreas Hellander,Ekta Vats,Prashant Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoFlowVLM，一种事后适配器，通过黎曼流匹配在乘积超球面上学习双编码器视觉-语言模型的联合嵌入分布，从而同时量化偶然不确定性（跨模态歧义）和认知不确定性（分布外检测）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准双编码器视觉-语言模型通过ℓ2归一化将图像和文本映射到共享单位超球面上的确定性点，无法暴露偶然不确定性（跨模态歧义）和认知不确定性（训练分布支持不足）。现有事后方法最多只能恢复两种不确定性之一，或忽略了这些模型嵌入的超球几何特性。

Method: 提出GeoFlowVLM作为事后适配器，通过带有单一掩蔽速度场的黎曼流匹配，在乘积超球面S^{d-1} × S^{d-1}上学习成对的ℓ2归一化双编码器VLM嵌入的联合分布。从单一模型推导出两个量：(1)条件检索熵，用于量化偶然歧义，具有通过Fano型界限的决策论解释；(2)边缘典型性认知分数，通过联合负对数似然的精确链式法则分解进行论证。

Result: 一致性结果表明，在总体极限下，训练网络将联合流和两个跨模态条件流暴露为其各自域上的有效黎曼流匹配速度场。该分解分离出一个结构上具有判别性而非认知性的跨模态逐点互信息项，经验上它是唯一持续无信息的独立组件。在三个检索基准的两个方向上，熵以近乎理想的单调校准跟踪Recall@1；在四个零样本分类基准上，边缘典型性总和产生持续校准的选择性准确性。

Conclusion: GeoFlowVLM成功地在超球几何框架下统一了偶然不确定性和认知不确定性的量化，为双编码器视觉-语言模型提供了理论基础扎实且经验验证有效的不确定性估计方法，解决了现有方法无法同时处理两种不确定性的问题。

Abstract: Standard dual-encoder vision-language models that map images and text to deterministic points on a shared unit hypersphere through $\ell_2$ normalization typically expose neither \emph{aleatoric} uncertainty (cross-modal ambiguity) nor \emph{epistemic} uncertainty (lack of training-distribution support). Existing post-hoc methods either recover at most one of the two uncertainty components, or ignore the hyperspherical geometry of these models' embeddings. We propose \textbf{GeoFlowVLM} as a post-hoc adapter that learns the joint distribution of paired $\ell_2$-normalised dual-encoder VLM embeddings on the product hypersphere $\mathbb{S}^{d-1} \times \mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ via Riemannian flow matching with a single masked velocity field. A consistency result shows that, in the population limit, the trained network exposes the joint flow and both cross-modal conditional flows as valid Riemannian flow-matching velocity fields on their respective domains. We derive two quantities from this single model: a conditional retrieval entropy that quantifies aleatoric ambiguity with a decision-theoretic interpretation via a Fano-type bound, and a marginal-typicality epistemic score justified by an exact chain-rule decomposition of the joint NLL. This decomposition isolates a cross-modal pointwise-mutual-information term that is structurally discriminative rather than epistemic, and is empirically the only consistently uninformative standalone component. Empirically, the entropy tracks Recall@1 with near-ideal monotonic calibration across three retrieval benchmarks in both directions, and the marginal-typicality sum yields consistently calibrated selective accuracy across four zero-shot classification benchmarks.

</details>


### [133] [Building Interactive Real-Time Agents with Asynchronous I/O and Speculative Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13360)
*Coleman Hooper,Minwoo Kang,Suhong Moon,Nicholas Lee,Eric Wen,John Wawrzynek,Michael W. Mahoney,Yakun Sophia Shao,Amir Gholami,Kurt Keutzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了异步I/O和推测性工具调用方法，使复杂的多轮工具调用智能体实现实时低延迟交互，在云端模型上实现1.3-1.7倍加速，在边缘模型上实现1.6-2.2倍加速，且精度损失很小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI技术在客服和个人助手等下游应用中的需求增长，实时低延迟响应变得至关重要（如语音交互需低于1秒）。然而，LLM在执行包含工具调用的智能体工作流时会产生数秒延迟，这限制了其在实时延迟敏感应用中的部署。

Method: 提出了两个核心方法：1）异步I/O（Asynchronous I/O），将核心智能体推理-行动线程与等待用户或环境信息解耦，允许在等待外部延迟时重叠智能体处理；2）推测性工具调用（Speculative Tool Calling），在智能体不确定是否已接收完整信息时管理任务执行。对于边缘小模型，还提出了基于时钟的训练方法，使模型适应流式输入和异步响应，并展示了SFT的合成数据生成策略。

Result: 在强云端模型上，该方法可直接应用于现有实时云API，提供1.3-1.7倍加速，精度损失很小。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct模型上，跨多个工具调用基准测试实现1.6-2.2倍加速。

Conclusion: 该工作成功实现了复杂多轮工具调用智能体的实时交互，既支持云端模型的即插即用加速，也支持边缘模型的高效推理，为延迟敏感的智能体应用提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: There is a growing demand for agentic AI technologies for a range of downstream applications like customer service and personal assistants. For applications where the agent needs to interact with a person, real-time low-latency responsiveness is required; for example, with voice-controlled applications, under 1 second of latency is typically required for the interaction to feel seamless. However, if we want the LLM to reason and execute an agentic workflow with tool calling, this can add can add several seconds or more of latency, which is prohibitive for real-time latency-sensitive applications. In our work, we aim to enable real-time interaction even for agents with complex multi-turn tool calling. We propose Asynchronous I/O, which decouples the core agent reason-and-act thread from waiting for additional information from either the user or environment, thereby allowing for overlapping agentic processing while waiting on external delays. We also propose Speculative Tool Calling as a method to manage task execution when the agent is still unsure if it has received the full information or if additional user information may later be provided. For strong cloud models, our method can be applied out-of-the-box to existing real-time cloud APIs, providing 1.3-1.7$\times$ speedups with minor accuracy loss. To enable real-time interaction with small edge-scale models, we also present a clock-based training methodology that adapts the model to handle streaming inputs and asynchronous responses, and demonstrate a synthetic data generation strategy for SFT. Altogether, this approach provides 1.6-2.2$\times$ speedups with the Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct and Llama-3.2-3B-Instruct models across multiple tool calling benchmarks.

</details>


### [134] [ENSEMBITS: an alphabet of protein conformational ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13789)
*Kaiwen Shi,Carlos Oliver*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Ensembits，首个蛋白质构象集合分词器，通过捕捉动态构象信息，在动力学预测任务上超越现有方法，并在功能预测任务中匹配或超越静态分词器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的蛋白质结构分词器（PSTs）仅捕捉静态结构的局部几何信息，忽略了蛋白质集合揭示的关联运动和替代构象状态。随着该领域从静态结构预测向集合生成发展，需要能够编码动态信息的离散词汇表。

Method: Ensembits使用Residual VQ-VAE在大规模分子动力学语料库上训练，采用帧蒸馏目标函数。该方法解决了动态分词化的固有挑战：跨构象推导信息丰富的几何描述符、变尺寸集合的置换不变编码、以及克服动态数据稀疏性。

Result: 在RMSF预测上超越所有相关方法；在基于token的ANOVA测试中是表现最强的独立结构分词器；在EC、GO、结合位点/亲和力预测和零样本突变效应预测任务中，尽管使用更少的预训练数据，仍匹配或超越静态分词器；蒸馏目标使得Ensembits能从单一预测结构预测动力学token。

Conclusion: Ensembits作为首个蛋白质构象集合分词器，成功将动态信息引入蛋白质语言建模和设计，为从静态结构预测向集合生成发展的领域提供了所需的离散词汇表，缓解了动态数据稀疏性问题。

Abstract: Protein structure tokenizers (PSTs) are workhorses in protein language modeling, function prediction, and evolutionary analysis. However, existing PSTs only capture local geometry of static structures, and miss the correlated motions and alternative conformational states revealed by protein ensembles. Here we introduce Ensembits, the first tokenizer of protein conformational ensembles. Ensembits address challenges inherent to tokenizing dynamics: deriving informative geometric descriptors across conformations, permutation-invariance encoding of variable-size ensembles, and conquering sparsity in dynamics data. Trained with a Residual VQ-VAE using a frame distillation objective on a large molecular dynamics corpus, Ensembits outperforms all related methods on RMSF prediction, and is the strongest standalone structural tokenizer on an token-conditioned ANOVA test on per-residue motion amplitude. Ensembits further matches or exceeds static tokenizers on EC, GO, binding site/affinity prediction, and zero-shot mutation-effect prediction despite using far less pretraining data. Notably, the distillation objective enables Ensembits to predict dynamics token from one single predicted structure, which alleviates dynamics data sparsity. As the field moves from static structure prediction toward ensemble generation, Ensembits offer the discrete vocabulary needed to bring dynamics into protein language modeling and design.

</details>


### [135] [Phasor Memory Networks: Stable Backpropagation Through Time for Scalable Explicit Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13370)
*Sungwoo Goo,Hwi-yeol Yun,Sangkeun Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Phasor Memory Network (PMNet)，通过Unitary Phasor Dynamics和Hierarchical Learnable Anchors解决了显式记忆架构在语言建模中长期存在的梯度不稳定性问题，在受控实验中展现出优异的长上下文处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十多年来，像Neural Turing Machine这样的显式记忆架构虽然在理论上具有吸引力，但在语言建模中由于时间反向传播(BPTT)过程中的灾难性梯度不稳定性而实际不可行。研究者希望打破这一僵局，解决显式记忆架构的结构性问题。

Method: 提出Phasor Memory Network (PMNet)架构，核心创新包括：(1) Unitary Phasor Dynamics：将循环状态更新约束为复数单位圆上的相位旋转，从而保持梯度范数并固有地防止发散，无需专门的初始化；(2) Hierarchical Learnable Anchors：层次化可学习锚点结构。该方法从结构上解决记忆波动性问题，而非依赖暴力扩展。

Result: (1) 在合成Copy-Paste任务中，PMNet利用85槽层次化记忆树，在完全超出局部滑动窗口注意力感受野的时间距离上实现接近100%的精确检索；(2) 作为仅119M参数、在18.8B token上训练的紧凑模型，PMNet的零样本长上下文鲁棒性匹配三倍大小的Mamba模型；(3) 消融研究和梯度分析证实显式记忆的历史失败是结构对齐问题，PMNet有效克服了这一问题。

Conclusion: PMNet成功解决了显式记忆架构长期存在的梯度不稳定性问题，证明该问题是可解决的结构对齐问题而非固有缺陷，为可扩展序列建模提供了理论基础。这为显式记忆架构在语言建模中的实际应用开辟了新路径。

Abstract: For over a decade, explicit memory architectures like the Neural Turing Machine have remained theoretically appealing yet practically intractable for language modeling due to catastrophic gradient instability during Backpropagation Through Time. In this work, we break this stalemate with \textit{Phasor Memory Network} (PMNet), a novel architecture that structurally resolves memory volatility through \textit{Unitary Phasor Dynamics} and \textit{Hierarchical Learnable Anchors}. Rather than relying on brute-force scaling, we present a mechanistic proof-of-concept in a controlled byte-level setting. By constraining recurrent state updates to phase rotations on a complex unit circle, PMNet preserves gradient norms and inherently prevents divergence without the need for specialized initialization. We empirically demonstrate the active actuation of the memory module through a synthetic Copy-Paste task, where PMNet utilizes an expansive \textit{85-slot hierarchical memory tree} ($=\sum^{4}_{h=1}4^{h-1}$) to achieve near 100\% exact retrieval across temporal distances that completely exceed the local sliding window attention's receptive field. Furthermore, despite being a compact 119M parameter model trained on 18.8B tokens, PMNet matches the zero-shot long-context robustness of a Mamba model that is three times larger. Our ablation studies and gradient analyses confirm that the historical failure of explicit memory was a structural alignment problem, which PMNet effectively overcomes, providing a theoretically grounded foundation for scalable sequence modeling.

</details>


### [136] [Di-BiLPS: Denoising induced Bidirectional Latent-PDE-Solver under Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13790)
*Zhonghao Li,Chaoyu Liu,Qian Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Di-BiLPS框架，通过变分自编码器、潜在扩散模块和对比学习，在极稀疏观测条件下有效解决了偏微分方程的正向和逆向问题，实现了SOTA性能、高效推理和零样本超分辨率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中观测数据通常极其稀疏，严重限制了经典数值求解器和现有神经方法的适用性。现有神经方法在中等稀疏度下表现尚可，但在极稀疏情况下精度显著下降，且高分辨率推理效率受限。

Method: 提出Di-BiLPS统一神经框架：1) 使用变分自编码器(VAE)将高维输入压缩到紧凑的潜在空间；2) 引入潜在扩散模块建模不确定性；3) 采用对比学习对齐表征；4) 提出基于方差保持扩散过程的PDE约束去噪算法提升推理效率。整个框架在潜在空间中运行，实现高效推理并保持灵活的输入输出映射。

Result: 在多个PDE基准测试中，Di-BiLPS在极稀疏输入条件下(低至3%)持续达到SOTA性能，同时大幅降低计算成本。该方法还支持零样本超分辨率，能够对连续时空域进行预测。

Conclusion: Di-BiLPS成功解决了极稀疏观测条件下PDE问题的核心挑战，在正向和逆向问题上均表现出色，为科学计算中的稀疏数据问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Partial differential equations (PDEs) are fundamental for modeling complex natural and physical phenomena. In many real-world applications, however, observational data are extremely sparse, which severely limits the applicability of both classical numerical solvers and existing neural approaches. While neural methods have shown promising results under moderately sparse observations, their inference efficiency at high resolutions is limited, and their accuracy degrades substantially in the extremely sparse regime. In this work, we propose the Di-BiLPS, a unified neural framework that effectively handle both forward and inverse PDE problems under extremely sparse observations. Di-BiLPS combines a variational autoencoder to compress high-dimensional inputs into a compact latent space, a latent diffusion module to model uncertainty, and contrastive learning to align representations. Operating entirely in this latent space, the framework achieves efficient inference while retaining flexible input-output mapping. In addition, we introduce a PDE-informed denoising algorithm based on a variance-preserving diffusion process, which further improves inference efficiency. Extensive experiments on multiple PDE benchmarks demonstrate that Di-BiLPS consistently achieves SOTA performance under extremely sparse inputs (as low as 3%), while substantially reducing computational cost. Moreover, Di-BiLPS enables zero-shot super-resolution, as it allows predictions over continuous spatial-temporal domains.

</details>


### [137] [Beyond Oversquashing: Understanding Signal Propagation in GNNs Via Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13383)
*Eden Nagar,Ya-Wei Eileen Lin,Ron Levie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受量子力学启发的Schrödinger GNN，通过引入可观测量的概念来建模图上的信号传播，解决了传统GNN中的过平滑和过压缩问题，并证明了其在信号路由能力上优于标准谱GNN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图神经网络（GNN）在通过图位移算子或消息传递机制进行信号传播时，往往会导致信息丢失，表现为信号在图上过度扩散而非有目的地在感兴趣区域间路由，这一现象被称为过平滑（oversmoothing）和过压缩（oversquashing）。为了解决这些问题，需要一种新的信号传播建模方法。

Method: 受量子力学启发，引入可观测量的概念来建模信号传播，具体包括：建模信号在图中的位置、信号在该位置的集中程度，以及应用GNN时信号向感兴趣位置传播的程度。基于这些概念，提出了新型谱GNN——Schrödinger GNN。

Result: 利用新提出的概念，理论上证明了标准谱GNN的信号传播能力较差；实验表明Schrödinger GNN在图上的信号路由能力显著优于标准谱GNN。

Conclusion: 通过量子力学启发的可观测性建模，揭示了传统GNN信号传播的局限性，并提供了一种具有优越信号路由能力的新型GNN架构。

Abstract: Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) perform computations on graphs by routing the signal between graph regions using a graph shift operator or a message passing scheme. Often, the propagation of the signal leads to a loss of information, where the signal tends to diffuse across the graph instead of being deliberately routed between regions of interest. Two notions that depict this phenomenon are oversmoothing and oversquashing. In this paper, we propose an alternative approach for modeling signal propagation, inspired by quantum mechanics, using the notion of observables. Specifically, we model the place in the graph where the signal lies, how much the signal is concentrated there, and how much of the signal is propagated towards a location of interest when applying a GNN. Using these new concepts, we prove that standard spectral GNNs have poor signal propagation capabilities. We then propose a new type of spectral GNN, termed Schrödinger GNN, which we show has a superior capacity to route the signal across the graph.

</details>


### [138] [Improving Reproducibility in Evaluation through Multi-Level Annotator Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13801)
*Deepak Pandita,Flip Korn,Chris Welty,Christopher M. Homan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出多层自助法来模拟标注者行为，分析了在AI评估中达到统计显著性所需的项目数量(N)和每项响应数(K)之间的权衡，以解决AI评估的可重复性危机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的安全性和可信度评估面临可重复性危机，人工标注者存在偏差和主观性，且缺乏足够数据研究标注者池增长如何改善实验可重复性，标准评估实践通常依赖少量标注且缺少持久的标注者标识符。

Method: 提出多层自助法(bootstrapping)方法来真实模拟标注者行为，利用包含大量评分和持久标注者标识符的数据集，分析项目数量(N)和每项响应数(K)之间的权衡关系。

Result: 揭示了达到统计显著性所需的项目数量(N)和每项响应数(K)之间的具体权衡关系，为AI模型评估提供了可靠的实验设计指导。

Conclusion: 通过多层自助法可以有效建模标注者行为变异，为AI评估实验设计提供了基于数据的指导原则，有助于改善评估的可重复性和可靠性。

Abstract: As generative AI models such as large language models (LLMs) become more pervasive, ensuring the safety, robustness, and overall trustworthiness of these systems is paramount. However, AI is currently facing a reproducibility crisis driven by unreliable evaluations and unrepeatable experimental results. While human raters are often used to assess models for utility and safety, they introduce divergent biases and subjective opinions into their annotations. Overcoming this variance is exceptionally challenging because very little data exists to study how experimental repeatability actually improves as the annotator pool grows. Standard evaluation practices typically rely on a small number of annotations per item (often 3 to 5) and lack the persistent rater identifiers necessary to model individual variance across items. In this work, we introduce a multi-level bootstrapping approach to realistically model annotator behavior. Leveraging datasets with a large number of ratings and persistent rater identifiers, we analyze the tradeoffs between the number of items ($N$) and the number of responses per item ($K$) required to achieve statistical significance.

</details>


### [139] [Teaching and Learning under Deductive Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13384)
*Jan Arne Telle,Brigt Håvardstun,Jose Hernandez-Orallo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个考虑学习者（如人类和LLM）推理错误的教学框架，在PAC设定下研究了最优教学集的计算复杂性，并提供了算法和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器教学与学习模型通常假设学习者在内部演绎推理中不会出错，然而人类和处于少样本学习 regime 的大语言模型（LLM）是两类重要的反例。它们可能无法通过一致性检查，且表现出随机性的失败。因此，需要建立一个新的理论框架来显式地考虑这些演绎错误。

Method: 本文引入了一个考虑演绎错误的教学与学习框架，重点研究机器教学场景。研究在改进的Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) 设定下进行，针对估计的错误水平，分析教师如何找到一个PAC教学集，使得学习者以高概率猜测出近似正确的假设。作者研究了计算最优PAC教学集相关的六个不同问题的计算复杂性，给出了以教学集大小为参数的XP算法，并基于指数时间假设（ETH）等标准复杂性假设给出了紧确的运行时间界限。

Result: 理论方面，针对六个关于计算最优PAC教学集的问题，给出了XP算法及其紧确的运行时间下界（基于ETH等假设）。实验方面，通过一项小型实验研究，探讨了哪种教学与学习协议最能代表在某些LLM教学会话中观察到的行为。

Conclusion: 本文成功构建了考虑演绎错误的机器教学理论框架，填补了对非完美推理学习者建模的空白。理论结果揭示了该设定下计算最优教学集的复杂性，实验结果为理解LLM的教学行为提供了初步见解。

Abstract: Most models of machine teaching and learning assume the learner makes no errors in its internal deductive inference. However, humans and large language models in few-shot learning regimes are two important examples of learners where this does not hold. They fail on some consistency checks, and they can fail stochastically. In this paper we introduce a teaching and learning framework that takes these deductive errors into account. We specifically study the case of machine teaching, as different characterizations of the teacher can account for both machine teaching and learning. In an overhauled Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) setting, we study theoretically that, for some estimated error level, the teacher must find a PAC teaching set that with high probability will lead the learner to guess a hypothesis that is approximately correct. We study the computational complexity of six different problems related to computing optimal PAC teaching sets. We give XP algorithms parametrized by size of teaching set, with tight runtime bounds under standard complexity assumptions like ETH. These results are complemented with a small experimental study of which teaching and learning protocols can best represent the observed behavior in some LLM teaching sessions.

</details>


### [140] [Topology-Preserving Neural Operator Learning via Hodge Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13834)
*Dongzhe Zheng,Tao Zhong,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD)框架，利用Hodge正交性分离不可学习的拓扑自由度与可学习的几何动力学，构建混合欧拉-拉格朗日架构，在几何网格上实现了物理场方程求解算子的高精度高效逼近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理场方程在几何网格上的求解算子面临谱干扰问题，传统方法难以区分拓扑主导成分与局部动力学成分，导致模型学习效率低且难以保持物理不变量。

Method: 基于Hodge理论和算子分裂推导算子级分解，利用离散微分形式捕捉拓扑主导成分，通过正交辅助环境空间表示复杂局部动力学，构建具有代数级归纳偏置的混合欧拉-拉格朗日架构。

Result: 在几何图数据集上，该方法在精度和效率方面均优于现有方法，并显著增强了对物理不变量的保真度。

Conclusion: Hodge正交性通过将不可学习拓扑自由度与可学习几何动力学隔离，从根本上解决了谱干扰问题，为几何网格上的物理场学习提供了一种原理性的结构保持方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we study solution operators of physical field equations on geometric meshes from a function-space perspective. We reveal that Hodge orthogonality fundamentally resolves spectral interference by isolating unlearnable topological degrees of freedom from learnable geometric dynamics, enabling an additive approximation confined to structure-preserving subspaces. Building on Hodge theory and operator splitting, we derive a principled operator-level decomposition. The result is a Hybrid Eulerian-Lagrangian architecture with an algebraic-level inductive bias we call Hodge Spectral Duality (HSD). In our framework, we use discrete differential forms to capture topology-dominated components and an orthogonal auxiliary ambient space to represent complex local dynamics. Our method achieves superior accuracy and efficiency on geometric graphs with enhanced fidelity to physical invariants. Our code is available at https://github.com/ContinuumCoder/Hodge-Spectral-Duality

</details>


### [141] [Support-Conditioned Flow Matching Is Kernel Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13386)
*Daniel Matsui Smola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从高斯最优传输路径的理论视角揭示了交叉注意力条件生成的本质：其精确速度场等价于带宽随时间递减的Nadaraya-Watson核平滑器，并据此预测并验证了三种失效模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解生成模型中基于交叉注意力的条件机制的理论本质，揭示其与经典核方法的联系，并识别其固有局限性。

Method: 在高斯最优传输路径下推导有限支撑集诱导的精确速度场，证明其为Nadaraya-Watson核平滑器；通过理论分析预测三种失效模式，并在高斯混合、球壳及DINOv2 ImageNet特征上进行实验验证。

Result: 单个高斯核注意力头精确计算该速度场；理论预测的三个失效模式（高维下的最近邻坍缩、各向同性核与数据几何失配、非参数估计支撑不足）在实验中得到确认；IP-Adapter的交叉注意力在实践中实现了近似的NW平滑。

Conclusion: 交叉注意力条件生成可被严格理解为经典核平滑理论的一种实现，该理论框架有效预测了实际模型的行为与局限，为改进条件生成机制提供了明确的理论指导。

Abstract: Generative models are often conditioned on a small set of examples via cross-attention. Under the Gaussian optimal-transport path, we show that the exact velocity field induced by a finite support set is a Nadaraya--Watson kernel smoother whose bandwidth decreases with flow time, from broad averaging at early steps to nearest-neighbor at late steps. A single Gaussian-kernel attention head exactly computes this field, connecting cross-attention conditioning to classical kernel theory. The theory predicts three failure regimes: nearest-neighbor collapse of the kernel at high dimension, mismatch between the isotropic kernel and the data geometry, and insufficient support for nonparametric estimation. Experiments on Gaussian mixtures, spherical shells, and DINOv2 ImageNet features confirm that learned conditioning improves in precisely these regimes, and that IP-Adapter's cross-attention implements approximate NW smoothing in practice.

</details>


### [142] [Taming the Long Tail: Rebalancing Adversarial Training via Adaptive Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13395)
*Lilin Zhang,Yimo Guo,Yue Li,Jiancheng Shi,Xianggen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RobustLT框架，通过自适应调整对抗训练中的扰动，同时解决了长尾数据分布下的对抗脆弱性和类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的对抗训练研究主要集中在平衡数据集上，忽视了现实世界中长尾数据分布带来的挑战。鉴于对抗样本中的扰动本质上会改变训练分布，作者旨在从理论层面探究其影响，以解决长尾数据上的对抗鲁棒性问题。

Method: 首先从理论层面分析了长尾数据对抗训练存在的两个关键局限：由类别不平衡导致的训练目标偏斜和对抗分布的不稳定演化。基于发现扰动可同时解决对抗脆弱性和类别不平衡问题，提出了RobustLT这一即插即用框架，在对抗训练过程中自适应地调整扰动。

Result: 大量实验证明，RobustLT在长尾数据集上能够持续有效地提升模型的对抗鲁棒性，并改善类别平衡性。

Conclusion: RobustLT框架成功地将对抗鲁棒性与长尾识别问题相结合，为解决现实世界非平衡数据分布下的模型安全性问题提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks are highly vulnerable to adversarial examples, i.e.,small perturbations that can significantly degrade model performance. While adversarial training has become the primary defense strategy, most studies focus on balanced datasets, overlooking the challenges posed by real-world long-tail data. Motivated by the fact that perturbations in adversarial examples inherently alter the training distribution, we theoretically investigate their impact. We first revisit adversarial training for long-tail data and identify two key limitations: (i) a skewed training objective caused by class imbalance, and (ii) unstable evolution of adversarial distributions. Furthermore, we show that perturbations can simultaneously address both adversarial vulnerability and class imbalance. Based on these insights, we propose RobustLT, a plug-and-play framework that adaptively adjusts perturbations during adversarial training. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RobustLT consistently enhances adversarial robustness and class-balance on long-tailed datasets. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/zhang-lilin/RobustLT}{https://github.com/zhang-lilin/RobustLT}.

</details>


### [143] [The Diffusion Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13399)
*Akhil Premkumar,Sarah Lucioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将扩散模型用作编码器的新型变分自编码器架构，通过交替训练方案解决了编码器与解码器之间的同步问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分自编码器（VAE）依赖重参数化技巧实现端到端训练，但这将编码器限制在简单的分布族中，牺牲了表达能力。作者希望利用扩散模型强大的表达能力来替代传统编码器，从而突破这一限制。

Method: 提出了一种受期望最大化（EM）算法启发的交替训练方案。由于扩散编码器和解码器在更新潜在变量估计时方向相反，该方法通过交替优化步骤来协调二者，在保持标准扩散模型简单高效训练目标的同时，实现了编码器与解码器之间的可靠同步。

Result: 成功构建了基于扩散模型的编码器，该编码器具备更强的表达能力。所提出的交替训练机制有效解决了编码器与解码器之间更新方向冲突的问题，实现了两者之间更可靠的同步。

Conclusion: 该方法证明了利用扩散模型作为VAE编码器的可行性，在保留扩散模型训练优势的同时，突破了传统VAE编码器在分布族选择上的限制，为构建更强大的生成模型提供了新思路。

Abstract: We construct a new kind of encoder, leveraging the expressive power of diffusion models. In a traditional variational autoencoder, the encoder and decoder jointly negotiate a latent representation of the input. This is made possible by the reparameterization trick, which simplifies training at the cost of restricting the encoder to a simple family of distributions. Replacing this encoder with a diffusion model requires rethinking how the decoder pressure can be transmitted back to the encoder, given that they tend to update their internal estimates of the latent in opposing directions. We solve this problem with an alternating training scheme, inspired by the expectation-maximization algorithm. Our method enables more reliable synchronization between encoder and decoder, while preserving the simple and efficient training objective of standard diffusion models.

</details>


### [144] [Trajectory-Level Data Augmentation for Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13401)
*Tobias Schmähling,Matthias Burkhardt,Tobias Windisch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向离线强化学习的数据增强方法，利用任务结构和几何关系对有限的次优轨迹进行扩充，从而在主动定位问题中实现高性能的离线策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主动定位等实际问题中，往往只能获取有限数量的次优轨迹数据，这导致传统的离线强化学习方法难以训练出高性能的策略。如何从这些有限且次优的数据中提取更多有效信息，成为一个亟待解决的问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于轨迹的数据增强技术。该方法利用任务结构以及奖励、价值函数与记录策略数学性质之间的几何关系，对离线数据进行扩充，从而支持在数据收集阶段使用次优的记录策略。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能够提高数据质量，显著提升离线强化学习的性能。研究在不同维度和部分可观测条件下的定位任务中验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法为处理次优轨迹数据的离线强化学习问题提供了一种有效的解决方案，具备理论支撑和实证支持，对于数据受限的实际应用场景具有重要意义。

Abstract: We propose a data augmentation method for offline reinforcement learning, motivated by active positioning problems. Particularly, our approach enables the training of off-policy models from a limited number of suboptimal trajectories. We introduce a trajectory-based augmentation technique that exploits task structure and the geometric relationship between rewards, value functions, and mathematical properties of logging policies. During data collection, our augmentation supports suboptimal logging policies, leading to higher data quality and improved offline reinforcement learning performance. We provide theoretical justification for these strategies and validate them empirically across positioning tasks of varying dimensionality and under partial observability.

</details>


### [145] [When is Warmstarting Effective for Scaling Language Models?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13405)
*Neeratyoy Mallik,Maciej Janowski,Johannes Hog,Herilalaina Rakotoarison,Josif Grabocka,Frank Hutter,Aaron Klein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现模型增长策略在2倍增长因子下最有效，存在增长上限，超过后从头训练更高效；简单的架构无关增长策略优于复杂方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型增长旨在通过从检查点扩展模型来加速大模型训练并节省资源，但在大规模训练中实际应用有限。作者指出两个被忽视的因素：(1) 过度强调保持小模型初始化时的性能，限制了新架构的算子设计；(2) 缺乏对增长与超参数、缩放行为交互的分析，且文献中增长因子不一致。

Method: 在密集MLP和密集语言模型上进行系统实验，研究不同增长因子的效果；提出简单的架构无关增长策略；通过多组消融实验分析增长因子上限；拟合缩放定律以提供预测性指导。

Result: 发现保持基础模型初始增长后性能并非最终强性能的必要条件；简单的架构无关增长策略优于复杂的热启动算子；存在增长因子g的上限，超过该值从头训练更高效；2倍增长因子最可靠，在每参数20 tokens预算下收益最明显，随预算增加收益递减。

Conclusion: 为实践者提供了模型增长的实用指南和经验性限制：2倍增长因子是推荐选择，增长策略应简单且架构无关；明确了增长因子的有效性边界，填补了文献中的空白；拟合的缩放定律为决策何时增长及增长多少提供预测性指导。

Abstract: Model growth from a given checkpoint aims to accelerate training of a larger model, offering potential resource savings. Despite recent interest, warmstarting has seen limited practical adoption in large-scale training. We attribute this to two underexplored factors: (1) an overemphasis on preserving the smaller model's performance at initialization, which constrains operator design for new architectures, and (2) insufficient analysis of how growth interacts with hyperparameters and scaling behavior, compounded by inconsistent growth factors across the literature.
  We show that preserving the base model's initial post-growth performance is not necessary for strong final performance, and that simple, architecture-agnostic growth strategies can outperform more complex warmstarting operators. Crucially, we empirically identify an upper bound on the growth factor $g$ beyond which training from scratch is more efficient. We observe this across multiple ablation setups. Notably, this limit is also present, but unreported, in prior published results. Across our experiments on dense MLPs and dense language models, we find that a $2\times$ growth factor is the most reliable in yielding convergence speedups, with gains most pronounced under 20 tokens/parameter budgets and diminishing as budget increases. We fit scaling laws over these observations to provide predictive guidance for practitioners deciding when and how much to grow. Together, our analysis provides practical guidelines and empirical limits for model growth.

</details>


### [146] [Vector-Quantized Discrete Latent Factors Meet Financial Priors: Dynamic Cross-Sectional Stock Ranking Prediction for Portfolio Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13407)
*Namhyoung Kim,Jae Wook Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PRISM-VQ模型，通过整合专家先验因子、向量量化的离散潜在因子和结构条件混合专家，解决了横截面股票收益预测中信号噪比低和机制演变的问题，在CSI 300和S&P 500上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横截面股票收益预测面临低信噪比和市场机制演变的挑战。经典因子模型虽具有可解释性但灵活性受限；深度学习模型性能强劲却往往未能充分利用金融先验知识。需要在保持可解释性的同时，有效利用金融先验并提高模型灵活性。

Method: 提出PRISM-VQ（基于先验信息的股票模型与向量量化）动态因子框架。该框架整合三个核心组件：(1)专家先验因子；(2)从横截面结构学习的向量量化离散潜在因子；(3)结构条件混合专家模型生成时变因子载荷。向量量化作为信息瓶颈，抑制噪声并捕获稳健的市场结构，离散代码同时作为潜在因子和时间专家专业化的路由信号。

Result: 在CSI 300和S&P 500数据集上的实验表明，该模型在横截面收益预测和投资组合表现方面均优于强基线方法，同时保持了良好的可解释性。

Conclusion: PRISM-VQ成功地在经典因子模型的可解释性与深度学习模型的灵活性之间取得平衡。向量量化机制有效抑制了噪声，离散代码的双重作用（潜在因子和路由信号）增强了模型的预测能力和时序适应能力。代码已开源。

Abstract: Predicting cross-sectional stock returns is challenging due to low signal-to-noise ratios and evolving market regimes. Classical factor models offer interpretability but limited flexibility, while deep learning models achieve strong performance yet often underutilize financial priors. We address this gap with PRISM-VQ (PRior-Informed Stock Model with Vector Quantization), a dynamic factor framework that integrates expert prior factors, vector-quantized discrete latent factors learned from cross-sectional structure, and a structure-conditioned Mixture-of-Experts to generate time-varying factor loadings. Vector quantization acts as an information bottleneck that suppresses noise while capturing robust market structure, with discrete codes serving both as latent factors and as routing signals for temporal expert specialization. Experiments on CSI 300 and S&P 500 show consistent improvements in cross-sectional return prediction and portfolio performance over strong baselines while preserving interpretability. Our code is available at https://github.com/finxlab/PRISM-VQ.

</details>


### [147] [DP-KFC: Data-Free Preconditioning for Privacy-Preserving Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13418)
*Marc Molina Van den Bosch,Riccardo Taiello,Albert Sund Aillet,Andrea Protani,Miguel Angel Gonzalez Ballester,Luigi Serio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-KFC算法，通过注入结构化合成噪声来估计Fisher信息矩阵，构建二阶预条件器，解决了差分隐私优化中各向异性损失景观与各向同性噪声不匹配的问题，在强隐私机制下无需消耗隐私预算或引入分布偏移即可提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 差分隐私随机梯度下降（DP-SGD）存在根本性的几何不匹配问题：深度网络的损失景观具有高度各向异性，而DP-SGD注入的是各向同性噪声。二阶预条件方法虽能解决此问题，但现有的曲率估计方法要么需要消耗隐私预算使用私有数据，要么引入分布偏移使用公共数据，在医疗等受监管的专业领域难以应用。

Method: 本文提出DP-KFC算法。核心思想是将Fisher信息矩阵分解为架构敏感性和输入相关性两部分。方法利用结构化合成噪声探测网络以恢复架构敏感性部分，并利用模态特定的频率统计量来近似输入相关性部分。该过程无需访问私有数据或公共数据，从而在构建KFAC预条件器时不消耗隐私预算且不引入分布偏移。

Result: 在强隐私机制（$\varepsilon \leq 3$）下，DP-KFC在多种数据模态上始终优于DP-SGD及自适应基线方法。实验表明，DP-KFC的性能可与使用私有数据的预条件器相媲美，而使用公共数据的预条件器变体性能下降高达4.8%，证明了该方法在不消耗隐私预算且不引入分布偏移的情况下有效估计曲率的能力。

Conclusion: DP-KFC成功解决了差分隐私优化中的几何不匹配问题，实现了无需额外数据开销的二阶优化。该方法特别适用于医疗等受监管且数据稀缺的专业领域，为隐私保护的深度学习提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Differentially private optimization suffers from a fundamental geometric mismatch: deep networks have highly anisotropic loss landscapes, yet DP-SGD injects isotropic noise. Second-order preconditioning can resolve this, but estimating curvature typically requires private data (consuming privacy budget) or public data (introducing distribution shift). We show that the Fisher Information Matrix decouples into architectural sensitivity, recoverable via synthetic noise, and input correlations, approximable from modality-specific frequency statistics. We propose DP-KFC, which constructs KFAC preconditioners by probing networks with structured synthetic noise, requiring neither private nor public data. Empirically, DP-KFC consistently outperforms DP-SGD and adaptive baselines across diverse modalities in strong privacy regimes ($\varepsilon \leq 3$). DP-KFC matches private-data preconditioners while public-data variants degrade by up to $4.8\%$, showing that curvature can be estimated without consuming privacy budget or introducing distribution shift. This enables privacy-preserving learning in specialized domains (e.g., medical applications) where regulatory constraints make data scarce.

</details>


### [148] [LIFT: Last-Mile Fine-Tuning for Table Explicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13424)
*Divij Khaitan,Ashish Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Lift（末段微调）方法：先用预训练大模型从非结构化文本提取初始表格，再用微调后的小模型修复错误。在有限训练数据下性能优于端到端微调，且对输入格式变化更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端微调方法在训练数据有限时性能受限，且对输入格式变化敏感，需要一种既能利用大模型能力又能在数据稀缺场景下保持高性能的解决方案。

Method: 两阶段流程：1) 使用预训练大语言模型从非结构化剪贴板文本中提取初始表格；2) 使用微调后的小语言模型（1B-24B参数）修复提取表格中的错误。仅需1000个训练样本即可有效工作。

Result: 在三个数据集的2596个表格基准测试中，Lift在TEDS指标上匹配或超越端到端SLM微调，在1000训练样本下TEDS提升高达0.144分，且对输入格式变化具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 末段微调方法在训练数据有限或需要输入变化鲁棒性但不牺牲精度的场景下，相比自调试和端到端微调方法提供了更有吸引力的选择。

Abstract: We propose last-mile fine-tuning, or Lift, a pipeline in which a pre-trained large language model extracts an initial table from unstructured clipboard text, and a fine-tuned small language model (1B-24B parameters SLM) repairs errors in the extracted table. On a benchmark of 2,596 tables from three datasets, Lift matches or exceeds end-to-end SLM fine-tuning on tree-edit-distance-based similarity (TEDS) metric while requiring as little as 1,000 training examples - where it outperforms end-to-end fine-tuning by up to 0.144 TEDS points. We term this approach last-mile fine-tuning and show it also more robust to input format variability. Comparisons with self-debug and end-to-end fine-tuning approaches show that last-mile fine-tuning provides an attractive option when training data is limited or when robustness to input variation is sought without compromising on accuracy.

</details>


### [149] [Strategic PAC Learnability via Geometric Definability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13426)
*Yuval Filmus,Shay Moran,Elizaveta Nesterova,Nir Rosenfeld,Alexander Shlimovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究策略分类中个体特征操纵对学习问题复杂度的影响。研究发现即使在简单情况下，策略行为也可能将易学习问题转化为不可学习问题。通过引入几何可定义性假设（一阶公式定义），证明在该假设下可学习性得以保持，样本复杂度受定义公式复杂度控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略分类研究个体可以通过修改特征（付出代价）来影响分类器决策的学习设置。现有工作表明在一些自然设置中（如线性分类器和范数代价），诱导假设类的复杂度可以得到控制。但尚不清楚在一般情况下，诱导假设类的样本复杂度如何依赖于底层假设类和控制可行操纵的代价结构。

Method: 首先通过反例证明在一般情况下（甚至简单情况如VC维为1的实数轴假设类，在简单区间邻域下），策略行为可能导致诱导假设类具有无限VC维。然后引入几何可定义性假设：假设类和代价诱导的邻域关系都可以通过$\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$上的一阶公式定义（即可用算术运算、指数、对数和比较来描述）。这一假设涵盖了广泛的自然类和代价函数。

Result: 存在VC维为1的假设类，即使在最简单的区间邻域下，诱导假设类也具有无限VC维，说明策略行为可能将易学习问题转化为不可学习问题。在几何可定义性假设下，可学习性得以保持，样本复杂度由定义公式的复杂度控制。该方法涵盖了$\ell_p$距离、Wasserstein距离和信息论散度等多种代价函数。

Conclusion: 策略行为在一般情况下会破坏可学习性，但在几何可定义性假设下可以保证可学习性的保持。这为理解策略分类的学习复杂度提供了新的理论框架，平衡了表达能力和学习能力，为实际应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Strategic classification studies learning settings in which individuals can modify their features, at a cost, in order to influence the classifier's decision. A central question is how the sample complexity of the induced (strategic) hypothesis class depends on the complexities of the underlying hypothesis class and the cost structure governing feasible manipulations. Prior work has shown that in several natural settings, such as linear classifiers with norm costs, the induced complexity can be controlled. We begin by showing that such guarantees fail in general - even in simple cases: there exist hypothesis classes of VC dimension $1$ on the real line such that, even under the simplest interval neighborhoods, the induced class has infinite VC dimension. Thus, strategic behavior can turn an easy learning problem into a non-learnable one. To overcome this, we introduce structure via a geometric definability assumption: both the hypothesis class and the cost-induced neighborhood relation can be defined by first-order formulas over $\mathbb{R}_{\mathtt{exp}}$. Intuitively, this means that hypotheses and costs can be described using arithmetic operations, exponentiation, logarithms, and comparisons. This captures a broad range of natural classes and cost functions, including $\ell_p$ distances, Wasserstein distance, and information-theoretic divergences. Under this assumption, we prove that learnability is preserved, with sample complexity controlled by the complexity of the defining formulas.

</details>


### [150] [Efficient Sensor Fusion for Gesture Recognition on Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13462)
*Pietro Bartoli,Christian Veronesi,Tommaso Bondini,Andrea Giudici,Franco Zappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于低分辨率ToF和红外热成像传感器融合的轻量级手势识别系统，在资源受限的MCU上实现了92.3%的准确率，功耗仅50mW，适合智能眼镜等可穿戴设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对智能眼镜增强现实应用中的手势识别需求，传统基于视觉的方法存在功耗高、计算延迟大和用户隐私泄露等问题，亟需一种轻量级、保护隐私且能在资源受限设备上运行的解决方案。

Method: 采用8×8多区域ToF传感器（VL53L8CH）和8×8红外阵列（AMG8833）分别捕获深度和热线索；设计了一种具有专门分组卷积架构的紧凑型CNN，在微控制器上高效融合这两种模态数据。

Result: 在包含7种静态手势的自定义数据集上，通过k折交叉验证验证，系统准确率达92.3%，宏F1分数为0.93，显著优于单传感器基线；在STM32F4和STM32H7 MCU上的设备端基准测试显示仅需6,343个参数，推理延迟为毫秒级，系统总功耗为50mW。

Conclusion: 该多模态融合方案有效平衡了识别性能与资源消耗，证明了在资源受限的可穿戴设备上实现高精度、隐私保护的实时手势识别的可行性。

Abstract: Gesture recognition is a cornerstone of Human-Computer Interaction (HCI) for smart eyewear, enabling natural and device-free control in augmented reality environments. Traditional vision-based approaches face significant challenges regarding power consumption, computational latency, and user privacy. This paper proposes a lightweight, privacy-preserving gesture recognition system based on the fusion of low-resolution Time-of-Flight (ToF) and Infrared (IR) thermal sensors. We used an 8 times 8 multizone ToF sensor (VL53L8CH) and an 8 times 8 IR array (AMG8833) to capture complementary depth and thermal cues. A compact Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) with a specialized grouped-convolution architecture is designed to fuse these modalities efficiently on a microcontroller (MCU). Experimental results on a custom dataset of 7 static gestures, validated via k-fold cross-validation, demonstrate that the proposed fusion strategy significantly outperforms single-sensor baselines with an accuracy of 92.3% and a macro F1-score of 0.93. Finally, on-device benchmarks on STM32F4 and STM32H7 MCUs confirm the system's suitability for resource-constrained wearables, requiring only 6,343 parameters and achieving millisecond-level inference latency with a total system power of 50 mW.

</details>


### [151] [A Unified Three-Stage Machine Learning Framework for Diabetes Detection, Subtype Discrimination, and Cognitive-Metabolic Hypothesis Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13464)
*Vishal Pandey,Ruzina Haque Laskar,Rishav Tewari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个可复现的三阶段机器学习框架，用于糖尿病检测、亚型聚类分析以及血糖-认知关联研究。在NCSU糖尿病数据集上，SVM-RBF和逻辑回归达到最高ROC-AUC（0.825），SHAP分析确定了葡萄糖、BMI和年龄为关键预测因子；K-Means聚类成功发现临床合理的亚型分组；研究还证实了血糖控制与认知功能之间存在显著正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病影响着全球超过5.37亿成年人，是预防性医疗保健的重大挑战。现有机器学习研究主要将糖尿病预测表述为二元分类问题，而亚型导向分析和血糖-认知关联的研究相对不足。

Method: 提出了一个可复现的三阶段机器学习框架：第一阶段，在NCSU糖尿病数据集上使用分层五折交叉验证对五种监督分类器和堆叠集成进行基准测试，采用ROC-AUC、平衡准确率、召回率和F1分数等评估指标，并使用SHAP进行可解释性分析；第二阶段，对确诊糖尿病患者应用轮廓系数验证的K-Means聚类（k=2，轮廓系数≈0.116），使用葡萄糖、胰岛素和年龄特征进行无监督亚型分区；第三阶段，对Ohio纵向认知数据集（n=373）进行统计分析，使用Holm校正进行多重检验校正。

Result: 在糖尿病检测任务中，SVM-RBF和逻辑回归达到最高ROC-AUC（0.825±0.026），随机森林达到最高准确率（0.762±0.030）；SHAP分析确定葡萄糖、BMI和年龄为主导预测生物标志物；K-Means聚类成功恢复出临床合理的亚型导向分区，无需真实亚型标签；统计分析揭示血糖控制与认知功能之间存在显著正相关（ρs=0.208，p=5.29×10^-5），且通过Holm校正。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持统计基础扎实且可解释的机器学习流程在可复现糖尿病分析和亚型感知探索性分析中的实用性。该框架为糖尿病精准医疗和多维度分析提供了有效的方法论支持。

Abstract: Diabetes mellitus affects over 537 million adults worldwide and remains a major challenge in preventive healthcare. Existing machine-learning studies primarily formulate diabetes prediction as a binary classification problem, while subtype-oriented analysis and glycaemic-cognitive associations remain comparatively underexplored. We present a reproducible three-stage machine learning framework for diabetes detection, subtype-oriented clustering, and metabolic-cognitive association analysis. In Stage 1, five supervised classifiers together with a stacking ensemble are benchmarked on the NCSU Diabetes Dataset using stratified five-fold cross-validation and evaluation metrics including ROC-AUC, balanced accuracy, recall, and F1-score. SVM-RBF and Logistic Regression achieve the highest ROC-AUC ($0.825 \pm 0.026$), while Random Forest achieves the highest accuracy ($0.762 \pm 0.030$). SHAP explainability identifies Glucose, BMI, and Age as the dominant predictive biomarkers. In Stage 2, silhouette-validated K-Means clustering ($k=2$, silhouette $\approx 0.116$) is applied to confirmed diabetic cases using Glucose, Insulin, and Age, recovering clinically plausible subtype-oriented partitions without requiring ground-truth subtype labels. In Stage 3, statistical analysis of the Ohio Longitudinal Cognitive Dataset ($n=373$) reveals a significant positive association between glycaemic control and cognitive function ($ρ_s = 0.208$, $p = 5.29 \times 10^{-5}$), which survives Holm correction. The findings support the utility of statistically grounded and interpretable ML pipelines for reproducible diabetes analytics and subtype-aware exploratory analysis.

</details>


### [152] [Twincher: Bijective Representation Learning for Robust Inversion of Continuous Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13470)
*Arkady Gonoskov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Twincher架构，通过学习对噪声不敏感的双射表示，实现连续正向过程的鲁棒逆推断，相比基线方法在数据效率和鲁棒性上表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI进展主要由大规模神经架构驱动，缺乏针对资源高效感知与规划的归纳偏置和推理策略。作者旨在解决连续正向过程$p \\mapsto y$的鲁棒逆问题，使学习的$y$表示与$p$双射对齐，同时对噪声或模型失配引起的扰动保持不敏感。

Method: 提出Twincher架构类，基于结构化微分同胚变换堆栈，结合定制对抗训练策略，学习具有双射对齐性质且对扰动不敏感的表示。提供了公开API用于训练和推理。

Result: 在合成系统上验证了该方法能高效学习双射表示，从而实现鲁棒高效的迭代逆推断。相比基线逆建模方法，展现出更优的数据效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 本研究为双射表示学习在机器人、视觉和物理AI领域的潜力提供了初步证据，证明了通过双射表示实现鲁棒逆推断的可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI have been primarily driven by large-scale neural architectures that excel at function approximation, rather than by tailored inductive biases and inference or learning strategies that could be important for resource-efficient real-world perception and planning through the solution of inverse problems. In this work, we consider the possibility of enabling robust inversion of continuous forward processes $p \mapsto y$ by learning representations of $y$ that are bijectively aligned with $p$ while remaining insensitive to perturbations in $y$ caused by noise or model mismatch. We propose Twincher, a class of architectures based on stacks of structured diffeomorphic transformations and tailored adversarial training strategies that enable learning such bijective representations. We provide a public API for training and inference and empirically demonstrate the ability of the proposed architecture to efficiently learn bijective representations of synthetic systems, thereby enabling robust and efficient iterative inverse inference. Compared to a baseline inverse-modeling approach, the method exhibits improved data efficiency and robustness, providing initial evidence for the potential of bijective representation learning in robotics, vision, and physical AI.

</details>


### [153] [OSDN: Improving Delta Rule with Provable Online Preconditioning in Linear Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13473)
*Chenyu Zhou,Hongpei Li,Yuerou Liu,Jianghao Lin,Dongdong Ge,Yinyu Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OSDN，通过在线对角预条件子动态调整特征级学习率，在保持DeltaNet硬件友好并行性的同时，显著提升了大模型的上下文联想回忆能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性注意力和状态空间模型虽能实现常数内存，但在上下文联想回忆任务上表现不佳。Delta规则通过在线梯度下降写入token缓解了该问题，但其步长仅依赖单一标量门控，忽略了内部目标的特征级曲率，限制了模型性能。

Method: 提出在线缩放Delta网络（OSDN）。该方法引入对角预条件子，通过超梯度反馈在线更新，对特征级步长进行精细调控。代数上，这种右预条件等价于对写入侧键进行逐特征缩放，从而严格保留了DeltaNet硬件友好的分块并行流程，避免了高维状态开销。此外，引入自适应预条件遗忘（APF）机制以处理非平稳上下文，动态刷新过时的校准信息。

Result: 在340M参数规模下，OSDN在JRT风格上下文回忆任务上相比DeltaNet提升了32%；在13亿参数规模下，回忆残差比降低了39%，同时在困惑度和LongBench等通用下游任务上保持性能持平，证明了该机制在十亿参数规模下的有效迁移与放大能力。

Conclusion: 通过利用内部回归损失的精确二次结构，建立了针对右牛顿比较器的超几何收敛性及算法对齐的token局部残差收缩界。实验表明，OSDN的在线预条件机制能有效提升模型在上下文联想回忆任务上的表现，且该优势能成功扩展至十亿参数规模。

Abstract: Linear attention and state-space models offer constant-memory alternatives to softmax attention, but often struggle with in-context associative recall. The Delta Rule mitigates this by writing each token via one step of online gradient descent. However, its step size relies on a single scalar gate that ignores the feature-wise curvature of the inner objective. We propose Online Scaled DeltaNet (OSDN), which augments the scalar gate with a diagonal preconditioner updated online via hypergradient feedback. Crucially, this right-preconditioning is algebraically equivalent to a per-feature scaling of the write-side key. This equivalence allows OSDN to strictly preserve the hardware-friendly chunkwise parallel pipeline of DeltaNet without incurring high-dimensional state overhead. Theoretically, by exploiting the exact-quadratic structure of the inner regression loss, we establish super-geometric convergence against a right-Newton comparator and prove an algorithm-aligned token-local residual contraction bound. To handle non-stationary contexts, we further introduce Adaptive Preconditioner Forgetting (APF) to dynamically refresh stale calibration. Empirically, OSDN demonstrates strong performance across scales. At the 340M-parameter scale, OSDN improves JRT-style in-context recall by 32% over DeltaNet. Scaling to 1.3B parameters, it achieves a 39% reduction in the recall residual ratio while maintaining parity on general downstream tasks (e.g., perplexity and LongBench) -- demonstrating that our online-preconditioning mechanism effectively transfers and amplifies at the billion-parameter scale.

</details>


### [154] [Effective Context in Transformers: An Analysis of Fragmentation and Tokenization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13485)
*Amirmehdi Jafari Fesharaki,Mohammadamin Rami,Aslan Tchamkerten*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了有限上下文信息论框架，证明表示单元的选择（如字节、字符、词元）会显著影响Transformer的预测性能：细粒度表示可能因“碎片化”现象损害预测，而词元化则可扩展有效上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer在固定上下文窗口下处理序列数据时，不同的表示方式（字节、字符、子词词元）尽管可以无损编码相同数据，但向模型暴露的信息不同。这引发了一个基本问题：表示方式的选择如何改变有限上下文预测器的能力？作者旨在从理论上解释为何字节/字符级模型（如ByT5、CANINE）相比子词词元化模型存在性能差距。

Method: 研究在马尔可夫信源上进行，分为两个方向：
1. **碎片化分析**：引入“碎片化”概念——一种无损重编码，将每个源符号替换为多个更小单元。通过理论证明分析其如何增加最优有限上下文对数损失。
2. **词元化分析**：研究贪婪词元化方法（BPE、WordPiece等），分析其如何将源符号组合成更大单元，使短词元窗口表现得像更长的源上下文窗口。提出依赖于词元窗口可靠覆盖所需源历史的程度及词元化器压缩率的损失保证。

Result: 1. **碎片化现象**：即使扩大上下文窗口以覆盖相关源历史，使用更小表示单元仍可能损害预测。证明了碎片化可以严格增加最优有限上下文对数损失，说明该差距是表示方式内在属性，而非优化或容量问题。
2. **词元化优势**：词元化可使短词元窗口模拟更长的源上下文窗口。给出了具体的损失保证，描述了何时可实现这一效果，该保证取决于词元窗口可靠覆盖源历史的程度及词元化器的压缩率。
3. 提出了一种简单的诊断方法：测量固定词元窗口可靠包含多少源上下文。

Conclusion: 本研究建立了有限上下文信息论框架，用于推理Transformer中的表示选择问题。两个方向的研究共同揭示了表示粒度与上下文利用率之间的权衡关系，为理解字节/字符级模型与子词词元化模型之间的性能差距提供了理论基础，并为实际词元化器设计提供了诊断工具。

Abstract: Transformers predict over a representation of a sequence. The same data can be written as bytes, characters, or subword tokens, and these representations may be lossless. Yet, under a fixed context window, they need not expose the same information to the model. This raises a basic question: how does the choice of representation change what a finite-context predictor can achieve?
  We study this question on Markov sources and uncover two complementary phenomena. First, we observe that moving to smaller representation units can hurt prediction even when the context window is enlarged to cover the relevant source history. To explain this, we introduce fragmentation: a lossless recoding that replaces each source symbol by several smaller units. We prove that fragmentation can strictly increase the optimal finite-context log-loss, showing that the gap is not merely an optimization or capacity issue, but can be intrinsic to the representation. This gives a theoretical account of the finite-context gap observed in byte- and character-level models such as ByT5 and CANINE relative to subword-tokenized models. Second, we study the opposite direction: greedy tokenization -- BPE, WordPiece, and related methods -- which groups source symbols into larger units. We show that tokenization can make a short token window behave like a longer source-context window, and we give a loss guarantee describing when this is achievable. The guarantee depends on how reliably token windows span the needed source history, together with the compression rate of the tokenizer. This also yields a simple diagnostic for real tokenizers: measuring how much source context a fixed token window reliably contains. Together, the two directions establish a finite-context information-theoretic framework for reasoning about representation choices in Transformers.

</details>


### [155] [Path-independent Flow Matching for Multi-parameter Generative Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13487)
*Francisco Téllez,AmirHossein Zamani,Philippe Martin,Shuang Ni,Guy Wolf,Eugene Belilovsky,Sina Sanjari,Yanlei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了路径无关流匹配（PiFM），将流匹配扩展到多参数域，学习产生路径无关传输的向量场，并在实验中表现出优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的流匹配采用单参数公式，无法捕捉多参数变化中的路径无关性。路径无关性至关重要，因为它确保变换仅依赖于初始和目标分布，而不依赖于具体的路径选择。

Method: 引入路径无关流匹配（PiFM），学习诱导流产生分布间路径无关传输的向量场；在适当假设下近似Wasserstein重心；提出可处理且无需模拟的目标函数，对多参数条件概率路径进行回归。

Result: PiFM将流匹配推广到高维参数域，同时强制执行结构条件以确保组合变换的一致性；理论上证明在合适假设下PiFM近似Wasserstein重心；实验表明PiFM在合成和真实数据上插值路径无关轨迹及生成期望的分布外样本方面优于其他方法。

Conclusion: PiFM成功实现了多参数域上的路径无关传输学习，建立了与Wasserstein重心的理论联系，并通过无需模拟的目标函数实现了高效训练，为分布插值和分布外样本生成提供了有效方法。

Abstract: Flow Matching is a powerful framework for learning transport maps between probability distributions. Yet its standard single-parameter formulation is not designed to capture multi-parameter variations where the resulting transport should be path-independent. Path independence is crucial because it ensures that transformations depend only on the initial and target distributions, not on the specific path. In this work, we introduce Path-independent Flow Matching (PiFM), a method for learning vector fields whose induced flows yield path-independent transport between distributions. We show that PiFM generalizes Flow Matching to higher-dimensional parameter domains while enforcing structural conditions that ensure consistency of composed transformations. In addition, we show that, under suitable assumptions, PiFM approximates the Wasserstein barycenter, linking the framework to a notion of distributional interpolation. To enable practical training, we propose a tractable, simulation-free objective that regresses onto multi-parameter conditional probability paths. We showcase empirically that PiFM outperforms other approaches on both synthetic and real world data in interpolating path-independent trajectories and generating desired out of distribution samples.

</details>


### [156] [Mixed neural posterior estimation for simulators with discrete and continuous parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13551)
*Jan Boelts,Cornelius Schröder,Jonas Beck,Jakob H. Macke,Michael Deistler,Daniel Gedon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将神经后验估计（NPE）扩展至包含离散和连续参数的混合参数空间，通过联合训练自回归分类器与生成模型实现准确且校准的后验推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经后验估计（NPE）方法主要针对连续参数空间，但在科学模拟中许多模型的参数空间是混合的，同时包含离散和连续维度。这一限制阻碍了NPE在更广泛科学问题中的应用，因此需要开发能够处理混合参数空间的方法。

Method: 提出一种扩展的NPE方法，设计一个推理网络来联合处理离散和连续参数。该网络将联合后验分解为离散和连续两个部分：使用自回归分类器处理离散参数，使用生成模型处理连续参数，两者在基于仿真的单一目标下联合训练。此外，还提出了诊断工具来评估混合后验近似的校准情况。

Result: 在可处理的玩具示例和真实世界科学模拟器上进行测试，该方法能够生成准确且校准良好的后验分布，验证了其在混合参数空间推断中的有效性。

Conclusion: 成功将NPE扩展至混合参数空间，提出的联合推断方法在多种场景下表现优异，为具有复杂参数结构的科学模型提供了一种新的推断工具，并在sbi Python包中开源实现。

Abstract: Neural Posterior Estimation (NPE) enables rapid parameter inference for complex simulators with intractable likelihoods. NPE trains an inference network to estimate a probability density over parameters given data, typically assumed to be \emph{continuous}. However, many scientific models involve parameter spaces that are \emph{mixed}, that is, they contain both discrete and continuous dimensions. We address this limitation by extending NPE to mixed parameter spaces through an inference network that jointly handles discrete and continuous parameters. The inference network factorizes the joint posterior into discrete and continuous components, combining an autoregressive classifier for the discrete parameters with a generative model for the continuous parameters, trained jointly under a single simulation-based objective. In addition, we propose a diagnostic tool to assess the calibration of the mixed posterior approximation. Across tractable toy examples and real-world scientific simulators, our joint inference approach yields accurate and calibrated posteriors. The inference framework is available in the \texttt{sbi} Python package.

</details>


### [157] [Uncertainty-Aware Prediction of Lung Tumor Growth from Sparse Longitudinal CT Data via Bayesian Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13560)
*Lingfei Kong,Haoran Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种贝叶斯物理信息神经网络框架，用于在稀疏且不规则的临床CT随访数据中进行肺肿瘤生长预测，在保持预测精度的同时提供了校准良好的不确定性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床实践中，患者的纵向CT随访扫描通常稀疏、不规则且存在测量变异性，这导致传统的确定性模型难以进行可靠的肿瘤生长预测和风险评估。

Method: 将Gompertz生长动力学与低维贝叶斯推断相结合，构建贝叶斯物理信息神经网络；采用两阶段推断策略，先进行最大后验（MAP）估计，再使用哈密顿蒙特卡洛（HMC）采样来估计后验预测分布和不确定性区间。

Result: 在NLST数据集（30名患者）上，该方法实现了约0.20的队列级对数空间RMSE，且95%可信区间覆盖率校准良好；推断的后验参数相关性符合预期的生物学生长行为，能有效捕捉异质性肿瘤生长模式。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯物理信息建模在仅有有限纵向随访扫描数据的情况下，能够实现具有不确定性感知的肿瘤生长评估，具有潜在的临床应用价值。

Abstract: This work studies lung tumor growth prediction from sparse and irregular longitudinal computed tomography (CT) observations with measurement variability. A Bayesian physics-informed neural network is developed by combining Gompertz growth dynamics with low-dimensional Bayesian inference in the log-volume domain. The framework employs a two-stage inference strategy combining maximum a posteriori (MAP) estimation and Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (HMC) sampling to estimate posterior predictive distributions and uncertainty intervals. The method was evaluated on longitudinal data from the National Lung Screening Trial (30 patients). Results show that the model captures heterogeneous tumor growth patterns while maintaining reasonable prediction accuracy under limited observations. Compared with deterministic modeling approaches, the proposed approach additionally provides calibrated uncertainty estimates. The inferred posterior parameter correlations were consistent with expected biological growth behavior. The proposed framework achieved a cohort-level log-space RMSE of approximately 0.20 together with well-calibrated 95% credible interval coverage across 30 patients. These findings suggest that Bayesian physics-informed modeling may be useful for uncertainty-aware tumor growth assessment when only limited longitudinal follow-up scans are available.

</details>


### [158] [Spatiotemporal downscaling and nowcasting of urban land surface temperatures with deep neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13566)
*Solomiia Kurchaba,Angela Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合静止卫星和极轨卫星观测数据的深度学习方法，生成了高时空分辨率（1公里/15分钟）的地表温度产品，并实现了高精度的临近预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的卫星反演地表温度产品面临着空间分辨率和时间分辨率之间的根本权衡问题，无法同时满足高空间和高时间分辨率的需求，这限制了其在城市气候和生态研究等领域的应用。

Method: 研究采用两阶段深度学习方法：首先利用U-Net模型将SEVIRI/MSG数据（3公里/15分钟）映射到Terra/Aqua MODIS数据（1公里/日4次），实现时空降尺度；随后基于ConvLSTM架构构建临近预报模型，预测未来15-75分钟的地表温度变化。训练数据覆盖欧洲人口超百万的大城市。

Result: 降尺度模型在独立测试集上达到RMSE=1.92°C、偏差MBE=0.01°C；临近预报模型在15-75分钟预测时限内RMSE为0.57-1.15°C，偏差为-0.1至0.14°C，显著优于持续性预报和气候滚动中值基准方法。针对独立MODIS过境数据的验证进一步证实了模型的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功解决了卫星地表温度产品的时空分辨率权衡问题，开发的高时空分辨率地表温度预报模型可直接应用于业务化卫星监测，为城市气候和生态研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Land Surface Temperature (LST) is a key variable for various applications, such as urban climate and ecology studies. Yet, existing satellite-derived LST products provide either high spatial or high temporal resolution, resulting in a fundamental trade-off between the two. To address this trade-off, we combine observations from a geostationary and a polar orbiting satellite and provide LST fields at high spatial and high temporal resolution (1 km at 15-min intervals). We demonstrate their application for intraday forecasting of LSTs. To estimate LST fields at high spatiotemporal resolution, a U-Net model is trained to map LST fields from SEVIRI/MSG (3 km and 15 min resolution) to LST fields from Terra/Aqua MODIS (1 km, 4 overpasses per day) that are collocated in space and time. The presented model has been trained on LSTs across large European cities with a population exceeding 1 million inhabitants, and achieves an RMSE = $1.92$°C and near-zero bias MBE = $0.01$°C on the hold-out test set. As a second step, we present an LST nowcasting model based on ConvLSTM architecture, trained across downscaled LST fields with forecast lead times of 15 to 75 minutes. The nowcasting model outperforms a persistence and a Climatological Rolling Median benchmarks, with RMSEs of $0.57$ to $1.15$°C for the considered lead times and biases ranging from $-0.1$ to $0.14$°C. An additional validation conducted against independent MODIS overpasses confirms robust performance. Our LST forecast model at high spatiotemporal resolution is directly applicable to operational satellite-based LST monitoring.

</details>


### [159] [Rethinking Generalization in Graph Neural Networks: A Structural Complexity Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13597)
*Peiyao Wang,Liang Bai,Xian Yang,Richard Yi Da Xu,Jiye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从图结构角度研究GNN泛化能力，证明过多边会导致过拟合，提出基于有效边数的结构复杂度度量及结构熵正则化方法来提升GNN泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GNN泛化分析主要遵循传统机器学习范式，关注模型复杂度而忽视了图结构对泛化的根本性影响，因此需要系统研究图结构如何影响GNN的泛化能力。

Method: 1）理论证明引入更多边会使输入表示过度适应输出模型从而导致过拟合；2）基于有效边数量制定结构复杂度度量，推导Rademacher复杂度泛化界；3）提出结构熵正则化方法，通过调节有效边来平衡欠拟合与过拟合。

Result: 证明了GNN泛化能力显式依赖于结构复杂度（除传统参数相关因素外），提出的结构熵正则化方法能够有效控制结构复杂度，从而提升GNN的泛化性能。

Conclusion: 图结构确实影响GNN泛化，通过控制有效边数量可以改善泛化性能，为GNN泛化分析提供了新的结构视角理论框架。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) have emerged as a fundamental tool for learning from graph-structured data, achieving strong performance across a wide range of applications.
