However, understanding their generalization capabilities remains challenging due to the complex structural dependencies inherent in such data. Existing generalization analyses largely follow the classical machine learning paradigm, focusing primarily on model complexity while overlooking the fundamental role of graph structure. Therefore, in this work, we systematically investigate this role by asking: does the graph structure actually influence generalization, and if so, by how much? To answer the first question and validate our intuition, we theoretically prove that incorporating more edges into the prediction process transforms the input representations to be overly accommodating to the output model, thereby inducing overfitting. To address the second question, we formulate a structural complexity measure based on the number of effective edges and derive a Rademacher complexity-based generalization bound. In doing so, we demonstrate that GNN generalization depends explicitly on structural complexity, alongside traditional parameter-dependent factors. Motivated by these theoretical findings, we propose a structural entropy regularization method. This approach controls structural complexity by regulating effective edges to balance underfitting and overfitting, ultimately improving the generalization performance of GNNs.

</details>


### [160] [Deep Learning as Neural Low-Degree Filtering: A Spectral Theory of Hierarchical Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13612)
*Yatin Dandi,Matteo Vilucchio,Luca Arnaboldi,Hugo Tabanelli,Florent Krzakala*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了神经低阶过滤（Neural LoFi）框架，将梯度训练的层次化特征学习转化为显式的迭代谱过程，为研究深度学习特征学习提供了可处理的数学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解深度神经网络如何从数据中学习有用的内部表示仍然是深度学习理论中的一个核心开放问题，需要超越懒惰训练机制的理论框架来解释多层特征学习过程。

Method: 引入神经低阶过滤方法，作为基于梯度训练的风格化极限，其中每层的动力学解耦：给定当前表示，下一层选择与标签具有最大可访问低阶相关的方向，形成显式的迭代谱过程。

Result: 理论预测了表示如何逐层选择，解释了概念涌现的样本复杂性，揭示了深度如何通过低阶组合性逐步构造新特征。实验表明该方法优于懒惰随机特征基线，恢复有意义的结构化滤波器，并预测与真实数据集上早期梯度下降特征发现相一致的表示。

Conclusion: Neural LoFi为研究多层特征学习提供了数学上显式的框架，超越懒惰训练机制，成功解释了深度学习中表示选择的层次化过程和特征涌现机制。

Abstract: Understanding how deep neural networks learn useful internal representations from data remains a central open problem in the theory of deep learning. We introduce Neural Low-Degree Filtering (Neural LoFi), a stylized limit of gradient-based training in which hierarchical feature learning becomes an explicit iterative spectral procedure. In this limit, the dynamics at each layer decouple: given the current representation, the next layer selects directions with maximal accessible low-degree correlation to the label. This yields a tractable surrogate mechanism for deep learning, together with a natural kernel-space interpretation. Neural LoFi provides a mathematically explicit framework for studying multi-layer feature learning beyond the lazy regime. It predicts how representations are selected layer by layer, explains how emergence of concepts arises with given sample complexity,and gives a concrete mechanism by which depth progressively constructs new features from old ones through low-degree compositionality. We complement the theory with mechanistic experiments on fully connected and convolutional architectures, showing that Neural LoFi improves over lazy random-feature baselines, recovers meaningful structured filters, and predicts representations aligned with early gradient-descent feature discovery with real datasets.

</details>


### [161] [Multimodal Graph-based Classification of Esophageal Motility Disorders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13623)
*Alexander Geiger,Lars Wagner,Daniel Rueckert,Alois Knoll,Dirk Wilhelm,Alissa Jell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合高分辨率阻抗测压（HRIM）数据与患者个人信息的图神经网络多模态学习方法，用于食管动力障碍的分类，证明了该方法的准确性优于传统视觉模型和单一HRIM特征模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 食管动力障碍的诊断面临巨大挑战，主要源于高分辨率阻抗测压（HRIM）数据的复杂性以及临床解读的差异性。现有的诊断方法缺乏对多源信息的整合，因此需要开发一种能够融合生理信号与患者临床特征的新方法以提高诊断准确性。

Method: 研究提出了一种多模态机器学习分类框架。首先，利用关键词检测和大语言模型从结构化问卷和文本记录中提取患者的人口统计学、临床和症状信息。其次，将HRIM数据建模为时空图，节点表示食管压力值，边编码空间邻接和阻抗动态。随后，应用图神经网络（GNN）学习生理特征表示，并将其与患者嵌入向量融合，进行吞咽事件的多类别分类。研究通过消融实验评估了患者特征和图建模的影响，并与基于视觉的分类器进行了对比。

Result: 在104名患者的数据集上进行的实验表明，所提出的多模态方法在所有分类类别上均优于仅依赖HRIM特征的模型；同时，基于图的建模方法相比视觉基线模型也展现出性能提升。消融研究证实了多种模态特征的互补贡献。

Conclusion: 将患者级数据与基于图的HRIM信号表示相结合，是提高食管动力障碍分类准确性的有效途径，该方法展现了良好的临床应用前景。

Abstract: Diagnosing esophageal motility disorders pose significant challenges due to the complexity of high-resolution impedance manometry (HRIM) data and variability in clinical interpretation. This work explores the feasibility of a multimodal Machine Learning (ML)-based classification approach that combines HRIM recordings with patient-specific information and incorporates a graph-based modeling of esophageal physiology. We analyze HRIM recordings with corresponding patient information from 104 patients with esophageal motility disorders. Patient data includes demographic, clinical, and symptom information extracted from structured questionnaires and free-text notes using keyword detection and large language model-based processing. HRIM data is represented as spatio-temporal graphs, where nodes correspond to pressure values along the esophagus and edges encode spatial adjacency and impedance dynamics. A graph neural network (GNN) is applied to learn physiologically meaningful representations, which are fused with patient embeddings for multi-category, multi-class classification of swallow events. The impact of patient features and graph-based modeling is evaluated by ablation studies and comparison to vision-based classifier baselines. The proposed multimodal approach indicates improvements over models that rely solely on HRIM-derived features across all classification categories. Additionally, the graph-based modeling provides gains compared to vision-based baselines. Our experiments systematically assess the complementary contribution of multiple modalities, as well as demonstrate the feasibility of our proposed graph-based approach. Our initial findings demonstrate that integrating patient-level data with graph-based representations of HRIM signals appears to be a promising direction for more accurate classification of esophageal motility disorders.

</details>


### [162] [Achieving $ε^{-2}$ Sample Complexity for Single-Loop Actor-Critic under Minimal Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13639)
*Ishaq Hamza,Zaiwei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次在单循环、单时间尺度的离策略演员-评论家算法中证明了$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$的样本复杂度，仅需策略诱导不可约马尔可夫链的极小假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中，达到$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$样本复杂度通常需要嵌套循环更新和/或对策略有强假设（如均匀混合和均匀探索）。本文旨在填补这一空白，在更弱的假设和更简单的算法实现下获得相同的复杂度保证。

Method: 采用耦合Lyapunov漂移框架来分析单循环实现中的耦合更新方程和离策略学习可能带来的无界迭代问题。具体建立了演员的几何收敛率和评论家的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/T)$收敛率，并通过交叉主导性质将两个Lyapunov漂移不等式结合起来。

Result: 在策略更新类别广泛（包括近似策略迭代和自然策略梯度方法）且仅需策略诱导不可约马尔可夫链存在的极小假设下，首次证明了单循环、单时间尺度离策略演员-评论家方法的$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$样本复杂度保证，用于找到$ε$-最优策略。

Conclusion: 提出的耦合Lyapunov漂移分析框架具有独立的研究价值，可推广应用于其他具有无界迭代的耦合迭代算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish last-iterate convergence rates for off-policy actor--critic methods in reinforcement learning. In particular, under a single-loop, single-timescale implementation and a broad class of policy updates, including approximate policy iteration and natural policy gradient methods, we prove the first $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity guarantee for finding an $ε$-optimal policy under minimal assumptions, namely, the existence of a policy that induces an irreducible Markov chain. This stands in stark contrast to the existing literature, where an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(ε^{-2})$ sample complexity is achieved only through nested-loop updates and/or under strong, algorithm-dependent assumptions on the policies, such as uniform mixing and uniform exploration. Technically, to address the challenges posed by the coupled update equations arising from the single-loop implementation, as well as the potentially unbounded iterates induced by off-policy learning, our analysis is based on a coupled Lyapunov drift framework. Specifically, we establish a geometric convergence rate for the actor and an $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/T)$ convergence rate for the critic, and combine the two Lyapunov drift inequalities through a cross-domination property. We believe this analytical framework is of independent interest and may be applicable to other coupled iterative algorithms with unbounded

</details>


### [163] [Multi-Objective and Mixed-Reward Reinforcement Learning via Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13641)
*Yang Bai,Kaiyuan Liu,Ziyuan Zhuang,Jiahong Zhou,Rongxiang Weng,Xin Chen,Jingang Wang,Xunliang Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RDPO方法，通过幅度感知分位数归一化和马氏白化技术解决多任务混合奖励设置中的奖励异构和维度相关性问题，提升了LongCat-Flash模型的指令遵循和写作质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂强化学习环境中，多任务和混合奖励设置常面临奖励分布异构和维度相关的挑战，导致标量优势估计不稳定。

Method: 提出奖励去相关策略优化(RDPO)，包含两个核心步骤：(1)使用幅度感知分位数归一化稳定跨二值、分数和连续奖励的优势分配；(2)在每个活跃奖励子空间内应用马氏白化消除聚合前的相关性冗余。

Result: 在LongCat-Flash后训练中应用RDPO，提升了指令遵循能力、写作质量和对困难提示的鲁棒性，同时在推理和编程评估上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: RDPO通过显式处理奖励异构性和相关性，有效解决了多任务混合奖励环境中的优化稳定性问题，具有广泛适用性。

Abstract: Complex reinforcement learning environments frequently employ multi-task and mixed-reward formulations. In these settings, heterogeneous reward distributions and correlated reward dimensions often destabilize the construction of scalar advantages. To address these challenges, we propose Reward-Decorrelated Policy Optimization (RDPO), a reward-processing method designed to explicitly target both failure modes. RDPO first utilizes Magnitude-Aware Quantile normalization to stabilize prompt-level advantage allocation across binary, fractional, and continuous rewards. It then applies Mahalanobis whitening within each active reward subspace to mitigate correlation redundancy prior to aggregation. When applied during the post-training of LongCat-Flash, RDPO enhances instruction following, writing quality, and robustness to hard prompts while remaining broadly competitive on reasoning and coding evaluations.

</details>


### [164] [Graph Neural Networks with Triangle-Based Messages for the Multicut Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13673)
*Jannik Irmai,Lucas Fabian Naumann,Bjoern Andres*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对多割问题（multicut problem）定制的图神经网络架构，通过仅对边赋予特征并基于三角形计算消息，在解的质量上超越了最先进的启发式求解器，同时保持了可行的运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多割问题是一个NP难的组合优化问题，在生物信息学、数据挖掘和计算机视觉等领域有广泛应用。尽管已有图神经网络应用于该问题，但尚未针对其特定的目标函数和约束进行充分适配，因此需要设计更专门的架构以提升性能。

Method: 提出了一种适配多割问题的图神经网络架构，其核心特点为：1）特征仅分配给图的边；2）消息传递的计算基于底层图中的三角形结构。这种方法更好地契合了多割问题的性质。

Result: 在多达200个节点的合成和真实实例上的实验表明，该方法在解的质量上优于最先进的启发式求解器，且运行时间可行。在某些实例中，该方法能在几秒内找到最优解，而精确求解器则需要数小时才能找到并验证最优解。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过针对多割问题的特定目标函数和约束定制图神经网络架构，可以显著提升求解效率和解的质量，为组合优化问题的求解提供了一种有效的深度学习方法。

Abstract: The multicut problem is an NP-hard combinatorial optimization problem with diverse applications in fields such as bioinformatics, data mining and computer vision. Graph neural networks have been defined for the multicut problem but can be adapted further to its specific objective function and constraints. In this article, we introduce such an adapted graph neural network architecture in which features are assigned only to edges, and the computation of messages is based on triangles in the underlying graph. Experiments with synthetic and real-world instances with up to 200 nodes show that our method outperforms state-of-the-art heuristic solvers in terms of solution quality while maintaining feasible runtimes. For some instances, our method finds optimal solutions in seconds whereas exact solvers need hours to find and certify optimal solutions.

</details>


### [165] [Three-Stage Learning Unlocks Strong Performance in Simple Models for Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13678)
*Zhenan Yu,Guangxin Jiang,Jin Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出STAIR训练范式，通过三阶段渐进式策略（共享映射→通道微调→残差学习）释放简单时序预测模型的潜力，在九个长期预测基准上匹配或超越复杂模型，同时保持简洁的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明简单线性模型和MLP预测器在长期时序预测中表现优异，但现有竞争基线仍依赖频率域建模、显式分解、多尺度混合等复杂结构先验，忽视了简单时序映射模型的训练与组织方式。

Method: 提出STAIR（分阶段时序适应）训练范式：(1)共享时序映射学习变量间共性动态；(2)通道级微调适应变量特定模式；(3)残差学习补充跨变量信息。引入Shared-to-Individual Fine-tuning和alpha-RevIN缓解严格通道独立限制和标准RevIN过强归一化先验。核心预测器保持为浅层MLP（另有线性变体分析）。

Result: 在九个长期时序预测基准上，STAIR匹配或超越近期强基线模型，同时保持简单的时序骨干网络，验证了简洁有效建模视角的可行性。

Conclusion: 通过渐进式训练策略释放简单模型潜力，STAIR证明了无需复杂架构模块即可获得竞争性能，为长期时序预测提供了简洁高效的建模范式。

Abstract: Recent studies on long-term time series forecasting have shown that simple linear models and MLP-based predictors can achieve strong performance without increasingly complex architectures. However, many competitive baselines still rely on structural priors such as frequency-domain modeling, explicit decomposition, multi-scale mixing, or sophisticated cross-variable interaction modules, while paying less attention to how simple temporal mappings should be trained and organized. In this paper, we propose STAIR, short for Stagewise Temporal Adaptation via Individualization and Residual Learning, a training paradigm for long-term time series forecasting that aims to unlock the capacity of simple temporal mapping models without introducing complex architectural modules. STAIR decomposes forecasting ability into three progressive stages: it first learns common temporal dynamics across variables through a shared temporal mapping, then adapts the shared model to each variable via channel-wise fine-tuning to capture variable-specific patterns, and finally complements the backbone with cross-variable information through residual learning. We further introduce Shared-to-Individual Fine-tuning and alpha-RevIN to mitigate the limitations of strict channel independence and the overly strong normalization prior induced by standard RevIN. This design gradually increases modeling flexibility while keeping the core temporal predictor as a shallow MLP in the main experiments, with linear variants analyzed separately. Experiments on nine long-term forecasting benchmarks show that STAIR matches or outperforms recent strong baselines while preserving a simple temporal backbone, providing a concise and effective modeling perspective for long-term time series forecasting.

</details>


### [166] [Sampling from Flow Language Models via Marginal-Conditioned Bridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13681)
*Iskander Azangulov,Leo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种后验预测采样器，用于改善Flow Language Models的生成质量，通过在每一步采样干净的单热点终点并构建解析桥，实现了比标准方法更好的质量-多样性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flow Language Models是一类新兴的语言模型，采用连续流匹配处理单热点编码的词元序列。其去噪器具有特殊结构：每个去噪均值块是该位置干净词元的后验边际分布。然而，标准的DDPM风格采样器将这些边际分布坍缩为单一的条件均值终点，并桥接到这个单形值点，这通常不是一个有效的单热点序列。因此需要一种更自然的FLM采样器。

Method: 提出后验预测采样器：在每个反向步骤中，首先从FLM词元边际分布定义的因子化后验中采样一个干净的单热点终点，然后从以该终点为条件的解析Ornstein-Uhlenbeck桥中采样下一个连续状态。该方法无需训练，使用与标准采样相同的模型评估，并为词元级解码控制（如温度缩放和核截断）提供了原则性接口。

Result: 理论证明：在精确后验边际下，终点近似误差恰好是词元位置间的条件多重信息；诱导的单步桥核保留所有词元级后验预测边际分布，仅损失残差跨位置依赖性；通过Girsanov路径空间比较证明，边际条件桥的去噪误差项不大于冻结条件均值桥，且当中间坐标级桥观测揭示关于干净词元的额外信息时严格改善。实验表明该采样器改善了质量-多样性权衡。

Conclusion: 后验预测采样器为FLM提供了一种原则性的采样方法，在理论上具有误差上界保证，在实践中改善了生成质量，为词元级解码控制提供了统一接口。

Abstract: Flow Language Models (FLMs) are a recently introduced class of language models which adapt continuous flow matching for one-hot encoded token sequences. Their denoisers have a special structure absent from generic continuous diffusion models: each block of the denoising mean is a posterior marginal distribution over the clean token at that position. Standard DDPM-style samplers collapse these marginals to a single conditional-mean endpoint and bridge toward this simplex-valued point, which is generally not a valid one-hot sequence. We argue that the natural sampler for an FLM is instead posterior-predictive. At each reverse step, we sample a clean one-hot endpoint from the factorized posterior defined by the FLM token marginals, and then sample the next continuous state from the analytic Ornstein--Uhlenbeck bridge conditioned on that endpoint. The method is training-free, uses the same model evaluations as standard sampling, and gives a principled interface for token-level decoding controls such as temperature scaling and nucleus truncation. We show that, under exact posterior marginals, the endpoint approximation error is exactly the conditional multi-information among token positions. The induced one-step bridge kernel preserves all token-wise posterior-predictive marginals and loses only the residual cross-position dependence. Finally, we prove a Girsanov path-space comparison showing that the marginal-conditioned bridge has a no-larger denoising-error term than the frozen conditional-mean bridge, with strict improvement whenever intermediate coordinate-wise bridge observations reveal additional information about the clean token. Experiments with FLMs show that the sampler improves the quality--diversity tradeoff. Code is available at: github.com/imbirik/mcb.

</details>


### [167] [Scale-Sensitive Shattering: Learnability and Evaluability at Optimal Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13684)
*Shashaank Aiyer,Yishay Mansour,Shay Moran,Han Shao,Tom Waknine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了有界实值函数类的尺度敏感PAC学习基本定理，证明了γ尺度均匀收敛、γ/2尺度不可知学习性和γ'尺度fat-shattering维度的有限性三者等价，消除了先前结果中不可避免的因子损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Anthony和Bartlett (1999)提出了关于控制可学习性的精确尺度问题，Phil Long猜测在尺度间存在不可避免的2倍因子差距。现有上界（Bartlett和Long, 1998）存在这样的损失。本文旨在确定精确的尺度关系，并解决Alon等(1997)和Rudelson与Vershynin (2006)关于度量熵界的开放问题。

Method: 核心技术是直接界定经验ℓ_∞覆盖数，避免了标准方法中通过填充数的绕行。该方法提供了在fat-shattering尺度γ下的精确渐近度量熵界。

Result: （1）对每个有界实值函数类和γ>0，证明了γ尺度均匀收敛、γ/2尺度不可知学习性以及γ'>γ尺度fat-shattering维度有限性三者的等价性；（2）获得了精确的渐近度量熵界：在γ/2尺度下为O(log²n)，在2γ尺度下为O(log n)；（3）证明了O(log²n)界在某些情况下是紧的；（4）建立了有界积分概率度量的尖锐二分法：每个IPM要么可估计，要么无法在c<3的乘法因子内弱评估，而3-弱可评估性总是成立。

Conclusion: 本文解决了学习理论中多个长期悬而未决的问题，否定了存在不可避免因子损失的猜想，并为学习问题的尺度敏感性分析提供了完整的理论框架。同时指出了关于定量样本复杂度和可评估性的若干开放问题。

Abstract: We study the optimal scale at which real-valued function classes exhibit uniform convergence and learnability. Our main result establishes a scale-sensitive generalization of the fundamental theorem of PAC learning: for every bounded real-valued class and every $γ>0$, uniform convergence at scale $γ$, agnostic learnability at scale $γ/2$, and finiteness of the fat-shattering dimension at every scale $γ'>γ$ are equivalent. This resolves a question by Anthony and Bartlett (Cambridge Univ. Press 1999) on the precise scales governing learnability, refuting a conjecture attributed there to Phil Long that a multiplicative 2-factor gap is unavoidable, and improves the upper bounds of Bartlett and Long (JCSS 1998), which incur such a loss.
  The key technical ingredient is a direct bound on empirical $\ell_\infty$ covering numbers, avoiding the standard detour through packing numbers. As a consequence, we obtain sharp asymptotic metric-entropy bounds in terms of the fat-shattering scale $γ$: an $O(\log^2 n)$ bound holds already at scale $γ/2$, while an $O(\log n)$ bound holds at scale $2γ$. We further show that the $O(\log^2 n)$ bound is sometimes tight. These results resolve open questions by Alon et al. (JACM 1997) and Rudelson and Vershynin (Ann. of Math. 2006).
  As an application, we establish a sharp dichotomy for bounded integral probability metrics: every such IPM is either estimable or cannot be weakly evaluated within any multiplicative factor $c<3$, while $3$-weak evaluability always holds, resolving an open question from Aiyer et al. (ICML 2026). We also highlight several open questions on quantitative sample complexity and evaluability.

</details>


### [168] [Polyhedral Instability Governs Regret in Online Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13692)
*Yuetai Li,Fengqing Jiang,Yichen Feng,Kaiyuan Zheng,Luyao Niu,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Linda Bushnell,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了组合动作在线决策问题，证明其遗憾上界由“多面体不稳定性”（即活跃区域切换次数）决定。在完全信息反馈和固定划分假设下，遗憾界为 Θ(√((1+RS_T)·T·log V_max))，该结果插值于专家问题和维度依赖的在线凸优化速率之间。应用于在线次模-凹博弈时，遗憾界简化为 Θ(√((1+SC_T)·T·log n))。实验验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组合动作的在线决策问题通过凸松弛转化为具有分段线性目标和多面体结构的在线凸优化问题。现有研究缺乏对这类问题遗憾上界的精细刻画，特别是未能充分利用问题内在的多面体结构特性。本文旨在揭示并量化“多面体不稳定性”（活跃区域切换次数）对此类问题遗憾上界的决定性影响。

Method: 作者在完全信息反馈和固定划分假设下，引入“区域切换次数”（RS_T）和每个区域最大顶点数（V_max）作为关键参数。通过理论分析，证明了遗憾上界为 Θ(√((1+RS_T)·T·log V_max))。针对在线次模-凹博弈（使用Lovász凸化），进一步将RS_T转化为排列切换次数SC_T，推导出具体的遗憾界。研究通过最短路径和影响力最大化等合成与实际组合问题的实验验证了理论发现。

Result: 主要理论结果表明，在所述设定下，遗憾上界为 Θ(√((1+RS_T)·T·log V_max))，该结果在专家类问题（高V_max）和维度依赖的OCO问题（低V_max）之间进行插值。对于在线次模-凹博弈，遗憾界精确匹配为 Θ(√((1+SC_T)·T·log n))。实验结果证实了预测的缩放规律，并表明低不稳定性机制在实践中无需显式枚举动作即可出现。

Conclusion: 本研究成功建立了组合动作在线决策问题中多面体不稳定性与遗憾上界之间的精确理论联系。所提出的框架不仅统一了现有的专家问题和在线凸优化速率，还为实际应用中的低不稳定性现象提供了理论解释。这为设计更高效的在线组合优化算法提供了新的理论基础和方向。

Abstract: Many online decision problems over combinatorial actions are addressed via convex relaxations, leading to online convex optimization with piecewise linear objectives and induced polyhedral structure. We show that regret in such problems is governed by \emph{polyhedral instability}: the number of changes of the active region. Under full information feedback and fixed partition assumptions, if $\mathrm{RS}_T$ denotes the number of region switches and $V_{\max}$ the maximum number of vertices per region, we prove $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{RS}_T)\,T\,\log V_{\max}})$ interpolating between experts-like and dimension-dependent OCO rates. For online submodular--concave games under Lovász convexification, this reduces to the permutation-switch count $\mathrm{SC}_T$, yielding the matching rate $\Regret_T= Θ(\sqrt{(1+\mathrm{SC}_T)\,T\,\log n})$. Experiments on synthetic and real combinatorial problems (shortest path, influence maximization) validate the predicted scaling and indicate that low-instability regimes can arise in practice without explicit enumeration of actions.

</details>


### [169] [MILM: Large Language Models for Multimodal Irregular Time Series with Informative Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13711)
*Hsing-Huan Chung,Shijun Li,Yoav Wald,Xing Han,Suchi Saria,Joydeep Ghosh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MILM模型，通过将多模态不规则时间序列转换为XML格式并采用两阶段训练策略，有效利用采样模式信息，在EHR数据集上取得了最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态不规则时间序列（如电子健康记录）中，观测的时间点和通道模式本身包含预测信号，但现有方法未能充分利用这些采样模式信息。大语言模型具有处理异构数据的能力，适合处理此类包含数值和文本的复杂数据。

Method: 提出MILM模型：1）将多模态不规则时间序列表示为XML格式的时间有序三元组；2）设计两阶段训练策略：第一阶段使用值遮蔽的数据仅从采样模式学习预测，第二阶段使用完整数据联合建模采样模式和观测值；3）对比单阶段直接训练的MILM-Direct模型。

Result: MILM-2S和MILM-Direct在多个EHR数据集上分别取得最佳和次佳的平均性能。值遮蔽实验证实采样模式确实携带预测信号，且MILM-2S能学习利用这些模式。在值待定评估（预测时部分值不可用）中，MILM-2S相比MILM-Direct的优势比标准评估更大。

Conclusion: 两阶段训练策略能有效学习多模态不规则时间序列中的采样模式信息，提升模型预测性能。在部分观测值不可用的实际应用场景中，该方法优势更为明显，保留值待定观测的时间和通道信息可进一步提升预测效果。

Abstract: Multimodal irregular time series (MITS) consist of asynchronous and irregularly sampled observations from heterogeneous numerical and textual channels. In healthcare, for example, patients' electronic health records (EHR) include irregular lab measurements and clinical notes. The irregular timing and channel patterns of observations carry predictive signal alongside the numerical values and textual content. LLMs are natural candidates for processing such heterogeneous data, given their extensive pretrained knowledge spanning textual and numerical domains. We introduce MILM (Multimodal Irregular time series Language Model), which represents MITS as time-ordered triplets in Extensible Markup Language (XML) format and fine-tunes an LLM through a two-stage strategy for MITS classification. The first stage trains on value-redacted MITS to predict from sampling patterns alone, and the second stage trains on full MITS to jointly model sampling patterns and observed values. Our two-stage model (MILM-2S) and its single-stage counterpart (MILM-Direct) achieve the best and second-best average performance on multiple EHR datasets. Further value redaction evaluations confirm that sampling patterns carry predictive signal and that MILM-2S learns to exploit them. In the value pending evaluation we introduce, where some values are unavailable at prediction time, MILM-2S outperforms MILM-Direct by a larger margin compared to standard evaluation. For MILM-2S, preserving the time and channel of value-pending observations as additional sampling information further improves in-hospital mortality prediction.

</details>


### [170] [Tight Sample Complexity Bounds for Entropic Best Policy Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13717)
*Amer Essakine,Claire Vernade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过改进浓度界限分析和设计新的停止规则，成功消除了风险敏感强化学习中最佳策略识别问题在样本复杂度上存在的指数级差距，使上界达到了下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对有限视界风险敏感强化学习中的最佳策略识别问题，现有研究表明下界为Ω(e^{|β|H})，而最先进的上界为O(e^{2|β|H})，两者之间存在一个常数倍的指数级差距。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 提出了一种基于前向模型的算法，使用KL散度探索奖励并适配熵风险准则。主要技术创新包括：利用指数效用的平滑性推导更紧的浓度界限，以及设计了一种新的停止规则以充分利用这种紧致性。

Result: 成功将样本复杂度的上界改进至匹配下界，消除了先前工作中存在的额外指数因子e^{|β|H}的差距。

Conclusion: 证明了先前上界中的额外指数因子源于对指数效用浓度控制的过度松弛，通过更精细的分析可以实现紧的理论保证。

Abstract: We study best-policy identification for finite-horizon risk-sensitive reinforcement learning under the entropic risk measure. Recent work established a constant gap in the exponential horizon dependence between lower and upper bounds on the number of samples required to identify an approximately optimal policy. Precisely, known lower bounds scale in $Ω(e^{|β| H})$ where $H$ is the horizon of the MDP, while the state-of-the-art upper bound achieves at best $O(e^{2|β| H})$ (arXiv:2506.00286v2) using a generative model. We show that this extra exponential factor can be traced to overly loose concentration control for exponential utilities. To close this open gap, we revisit the analysis of this problem through a forward-model based algorithm building on KL-based exploration bonuses that we adapt to the entropic criterion. The improvement we get is due to two main novel technical innovations. We leverage the smoothness properties of the exponential utility to derive sharper concentration bounds, and we propose a new stopping rule that exploits further this tightness to obtain a sample complexity that matches the lower bound.

</details>


### [171] [Distinguishing performance gains from learning when using generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13731)
*Lixiang Yan,Samuel Greiff,Jason M. Lodge,Dragan Gašević*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 生成式AI虽能提升学习成绩，但未能促进深度学习所需的高阶认知与元认知加工。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前生成式AI在教育领域的应用日益普及，虽然能够提升学习者的表现，但这些应用方式存在局限性，无法有效促进深度认知和元认知加工过程，而这些过程对于高质量学习至关重要。

Method: 本文通过分析当前生成式AI在教育中的整合应用现状，探讨其对学习过程的影响，特别是关注认知和元认知加工层面的作用机制。

Result: 研究发现生成式AI在教育中的应用虽然能够提升学习者的表面成绩表现，但未能有效促进深度认知和元认知加工，这些高阶思维过程是高质量学习所必需的。

Conclusion: 生成式AI在教育中的应用存在明显局限，需要在提升表面成绩之外，更加注重培养学习者的深度认知和元认知能力，以实现真正的高质量学习效果。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly being integrated into education, where it can boost learners' performance. However, these uses do not promote the deep cognitive and metacognitive processing that are required for high-quality learning.

</details>


### [172] [Learning POMDP World Models from Observations with Language-Model Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13740)
*Valentin Six,Frederik Panse,Mathis Fajeau,Lancelot Da Costa,Mridul Sharma,Alfonso Amayuelas,Tim Z. Xiao,David Hyland,Philipp Hennig,Bernhard Schölkopf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Pinductor方法，利用大语言模型先验知识从少量观测-动作轨迹中学习部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)世界模型，在仅使用较少信息的情况下匹配了需要特权信息的基线方法性能，显著提升了样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在环境中有效行动需要学习环境内部模型，POMDP提供了灵活的建模框架，但从观测-动作轨迹学习POMDP具有挑战性且通常需要大量环境交互。研究旨在探索语言模型先验是否能通过利用先验知识来减少昂贵的交互成本。

Method: 提出Pinductor(POMDP-inductor)方法：大语言模型从少量观测-动作轨迹提出候选POMDP模型，并迭代优化以最大化基于信念的似然分数。该方法无需访问隐藏状态信息。

Result: Pinductor在使用严格更少信息的情况下，匹配了需要特权访问隐藏状态的LLM-based POMDP学习方法的性能和样本效率，同时显著超越表格POMDP基线的样本效率。性能随LLM能力提升而提升，在语义信息被保留时性能优雅降级。

Conclusion: 语言模型先验是部分可观测条件下样本高效世界模型学习的实用工具，为真实环境中的通用智能体迈出一步。

Abstract: Whether navigating a building, operating a robot, or playing a game, an agent that acts effectively in an environment must first learn an internal model of how that environment works. Partially-observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) provide a flexible modeling class for such internal world models, but learning them from observation-action trajectories alone is challenging and typically requires extensive environment interaction. We ask whether language-model priors can reduce costly interaction by leveraging prior knowledge, and introduce \emph{Pinductor} (POMDP-inductor): an LLM proposes candidate POMDP models from a few observation-action trajectories and iteratively refines them to optimize a belief-based likelihood score. Despite using strictly less information, \emph{Pinductor} matches the performance and sample efficiency of LLM-based POMDP learning methods that assume privileged access to the hidden state, while significantly surpassing the sample efficiency of tabular POMDP baselines. Further results show that performance scales with LLM capability and degrades gracefully as semantic information about the environment is withheld. Together, these results position language-model priors as a practical tool for sample-efficient world-model learning under partial observability, and a step toward generalist agents in real-world environments. Code is available at https://github.com/atomresearch/pinductor.

</details>


### [173] [GHGbench: A Unified Multi-Entity, Multi-Task Benchmark for Carbon Emission Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13743)
*Yifan Duan,Siyuan Zheng,Lihuan Li,Chao Xue,Flora Salim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发布了GHGbench，一个面向企业和建筑层面温室气体预测的开放数据集与基准，包含企业与建筑两个赛道、多种基线模型及系统性的基准发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的实体级碳排放预测开放数据集与基准在获取途径、规模、粒度与评估方面仍呈碎片化，缺乏统一、开放的标准平台，限制了模型的可比性与方法的进步。

Method: 构建GHGbench，包含企业赛道（32,000+条公司-年度记录，覆盖12,000+家公司，含Scope 1+2与Scope 3披露及财务/行业信号）和建筑赛道（整合491,591条建筑-年度记录，来自13个开放数据源，统一模式，覆盖26个大都市区，含气候协变量与多模态遥感嵌入）。定义规范的数据划分，主任务为分布内与跨区域/城市迁移，补充证据为时间留出与短期预测；基线涵盖梯度提升树、表格基础模型、MLP、FT-Transformer、多模态融合，并引入LLM面板作为辅助，采用多种子配对自举检验进行评估。

Result: 三大基准发现：（i）建筑排放的结构性难度高于企业排放；（ii）分布内到分布外的差距远大于模型间的差距，表格基础模型在多城市建筑排放任务上首次显著超越调优树模型；（iii）多模态遥感嵌入在表格泛化失效处提供有效增益；同时暴露灾难性的城市迁移与行业因子查询上限作为系统性失效模式。

Conclusion: GHGbench为企业和建筑层面的温室气体预测提供了统一、开放的基准，揭示了建筑排放预测的更高难度与分布外泛化挑战，验证了表格基础模型与多模态融合的价值，并指出现有方法在城市迁移等场景下的系统性不足，为后续研究提供了可复现的基线与诊断工具。

Abstract: Open datasets and benchmarks for entity-level carbon-emission prediction remain fragmented across access, scale, granularity, and evaluation. We introduce GHGbench, an open dataset and benchmark for company- and building-level greenhouse-gas prediction. The company track contains 32,000+ company-year records from 12,000+ firms with Scope 1+2 and Scope 3 disclosures and financial/sectoral signals; the building track harmonises 491,591 building-year records from 13 open sources into a single schema across 26 metropolitan areas (10 U.S., 15 Australian, 1 Singaporean), with climate covariates and multimodal remote-sensing embeddings. GHGbench defines canonical splits with in-distribution and cross-region/city transfer as primary tasks and temporal hold-out plus short-horizon forecasting as supplementary appendix evidence; headline baselines span gradient-boosted trees, a tabular foundation model, MLP, FT-Transformer, and multimodal fusion, with an LLM panel as auxiliary, all evaluated under multi-seed paired-bootstrap tests. Three benchmark-level findings emerge: (i) building emissions are structurally harder than company emissions; (ii) the in-distribution to out-of-distribution gap dwarfs any within-model gap across both the company track and the building track, and a tabular foundation model is, to our knowledge, the first baseline to open a paired-bootstrap-significant gap over tuned trees on a multi-city building-emissions task; (iii) multimodal remote-sensing embeddings help precisely where tabular generalisation breaks. GHGbench also exposes catastrophic city transfer and the sector-factor lookup ceiling as systematic failure modes. Code and reconstruction recipes are available at GHGbench.

</details>


### [174] [Min Generalized Sliced Gromov Wasserstein: A Scalable Path to Gromov Wasserstein](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13753)
*Ashkan Shahbazi,Xinran Liu,Ping He,Soheil Kolouri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了min-GSGW方法，通过学习耦合的非线性切片器来高效求解Gromov-Wasserstein问题，在保持刚体运动不变性的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Gromov-Wasserstein (GW)求解器计算成本高昂，难以应用于大规模几何匹配和形状分析任务。需要开发一种既保持GW问题关键性质（如刚体运动不变性）又能高效计算的方法。

Method: 提出min-Generalized Sliced Gromov-Wasserstein (min-GSGW)方法：1) 引入广义切片器到切片GW框架中；2) 学习耦合的非线性切片器，为两个输入测度分配兼容的推前值，使得投影域中的单调耦合能够提升为原始空间中针对GW目标的传输方案；3) 开发摊销变体，用学习的切片器替代逐实例优化，处理未见的输入对。该方法直接在原始空间中最小化GW目标值。

Result: 在动物网格匹配、马网格插值和ShapeNet部件迁移任务上进行实验。结果表明min-GSGW能够产生有意义的几何对应关系和GW目标值，且计算成本显著低于现有GW求解器。方法成功证明了刚体运动不变性。

Conclusion: min-GSGW成功地将广义切片器引入切片GW框架，构建了基于切片的高效GW传输方案，在保持几何匹配所需的关键性质的同时实现了计算效率的大幅提升，为形状分析和几何匹配任务提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We propose min Generalized Sliced Gromov--Wasserstein (min-GSGW), a sliced formulation for the Gromov--Wasserstein (GW) problem using expressive generalized slicers. The key idea is to learn coupled nonlinear slicers that assign compatible push-forward values to both input measures, so that monotone coupling in the projected domain lifts to a transport plan evaluated against the GW objective in the original spaces. The resulting plan induces a GW objective value, and min-GSGW minimizes this cost directly in the original spaces. We further show that min-GSGW is rigid-motion invariant, a crucial property for geometric matching and shape analysis tasks. Our contributions are threefold: 1) we introduce generalized slicers into the sliced GW framework, 2) we construct a slicing-based efficient GW transport plan; and 3) we develop an amortized variant that replaces per-instance optimization with a learned slicer for unseen input pairs. We perform experiments on animal mesh matching, horse mesh interpolation, and ShapeNet part transfer. Results show that min-GSGW produces meaningful geometric correspondences and GW objective values at substantially lower computational cost than existing GW solvers.

</details>


### [175] [Fast and effective algorithms for fair clustering at scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13759)
*Claudio Mantuano,Manuel Kammermann,Philipp Baumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个公平聚类通用框架及三种启发式算法，能够精确控制代价与公平性的权衡，并在大规模数据集上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚类在公平性敏感领域的应用日益增多，需要在划分聚类时确保受保护群体在各簇中有足够的代表性。然而聚类代价与公平性往往存在冲突，现有方法在控制二者权衡方面能力有限，且难以兼顾大规模数据的可扩展性与解的质量。

Method: 提出了一个公平聚类通用框架，允许用户精确设定公平性目标水平；基于该框架设计了三种启发式算法：第一种注重解的质量并支持额外约束，第二种在保持高质量解的同时提升可扩展性，第三种追求极致可扩展性，可在秒级内处理百万级对象的实例。

Result: 在基准数据集上的全面数值实验表明，所提三种启发式算法均优于现有方法，能够高效生成高质量且满足公平性约束的聚类结果。

Conclusion: 本研究为公平聚类问题提供了一个灵活高效的解决方案，实现了对代价-公平性权衡的精确控制，并能适应从高质量解到大规模应用的多种实际需求，源代码已公开以便复现和推广。

Abstract: Clustering is an unsupervised machine learning task that consists of identifying groups of similar objects. It has numerous applications and is increasingly used in fairness-sensitive domains where objects represent individuals, such as customers, employees, or students. We address a fair clustering problem in which objects belong to protected groups. The problem consists of partitioning the objects into a predefined number of clusters while attaining a user-defined target level of fairness, meaning that each protected group is sufficiently represented in each cluster. The objective is to minimize the clustering cost, defined as the sum of squared Euclidean distances between the objects and the centers of their clusters. Since clustering cost and fairness are generally in conflict, managing the trade-off between them is essential in practical applications. Existing methods provide limited control over this trade-off and either fail to scale to large datasets or, when they scale, produce low-quality solutions. We propose a general framework for fair clustering that provides precise control over the cost-fairness trade-off and introduce three heuristics based on it. The first heuristic focuses on solution quality and the flexibility to incorporate additional constraints, the second improves scalability while retaining high solution quality, and the third is designed for maximum scalability, producing solutions for instances with millions of objects in seconds. The proposed heuristics outperform existing approaches in comprehensive numerical experiments on benchmark datasets. The source code of our heuristics and instructions for reproducing the experiments are publicly available on GitHub.

</details>


### [176] [Toward AI-Driven Digital Twins for Metropolitan Floods: A Conditional Latent Dynamics Network Surrogate of the Shallow Water Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13761)
*Phillip Si,Yuan Qiu,Omar Sallam,Jeremy Feinstein,Ziang He,Eugene Yan,Peng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了CLDNet模型，通过低维潜在神经ODE和坐标解码器实现城市尺度流域洪水的快速预测，在保持高精度的同时实现了约115倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI驱动的洪水数字孪生需要快速的水动力代理模型用于集合预报和数据同化，然而即使是GPU加速的二维浅水方程求解器在大规模城市流域上仍需约55分钟完成96小时模拟，使得原生分辨率下的计算代价过高。

Method: 提出条件潜在动态网络(CLDNet)：包含由降雨驱动的低维潜在神经ODE，以及基于坐标的解码器(以静态地形特征为条件)，可在任意查询点重建水深和流量。点对点解码将内存与网格规模解耦，原生支持不规则流域，支持单节点城市尺度训练和直接在精确测站坐标查询。

Result: 在25万单元的Texas基准测试和Des Plaines流域114场真实降雨事件上验证，相对基准模型RME降低约一半，CSI达约86%（0.5m淹没阈值），96小时全流域预报仅需约29秒（约115倍加速），优于规则网格VAE-ConvLSTM和FNO基线。

Conclusion: CLDNet成功实现了不规则城市流域的快速洪水预报，在精度和效率间取得良好平衡，为洪水数字孪生的实时集合预报和数据同化提供了可行方案。

Abstract: AI-driven flood digital twins demand fast hydrodynamic surrogates for ensemble forecasting and observation assimilation. Yet even GPU-accelerated two-dimensional shallow water equation (SWE) solvers still require $\sim 55$ minutes per $96$-hour run on a $\sim 4.2$-million-active-cell metropolitan basin (the Des~Plaines River basin at $30\,\mathrm{m}$ resolution), making such workloads prohibitive at native resolution. We present the Conditional Latent Dynamics Network (CLDNet): a low-dimensional latent neural ODE driven by rainfall, paired with a coordinate-based decoder conditioned on static terrain (elevation, slope, Manning roughness) that reconstructs depth and discharge at arbitrary query points. Pointwise decoding decouples memory from grid size and handles irregular watersheds natively, enabling metropolitan-scale training on a single compute node and direct queries at exact gauge coordinates without raster snapping. We evaluate CLDNet on a synthetic $250{,}000$-cell Texas benchmark and on a new Des~Plaines case study of $114$ real-rainfall Stage~IV storms whose reference simulator we validate against United States Geological Survey (USGS) gauges at the April~2013 flood-of-record (Nash--Sutcliffe efficiency $0.57$--$0.94$ on mean-recentered water-surface elevation). CLDNet roughly halves the relative root-mean-squared error of an unconditional baseline, outperforms regular-grid VAE--ConvLSTM and FNO baselines on the Texas benchmark (both presuppose a Cartesian grid and do not apply to the irregular Des~Plaines watershed), reaches a critical success index of $\approx 86\%$ at the $0.5\,\mathrm{m}$ inundation threshold, and produces a full $96$-hour basin-wide forecast in $\sim 29$ seconds -- a $\sim 115\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [177] [Attention Once Is All You Need: Efficient Streaming Inference with Stateful Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13784)
*Victor Norgren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向流式场景的有状态Transformer推理引擎，通过持久化KV缓存将预填充开销移出关键路径，利用空闲GPU周期预计算查询，实现查询延迟与累积上下文大小解耦，在流式市场数据基准上相比主流引擎获得高达5.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Transformer推理引擎基于请求驱动，每次查询需支付O(n)的预填充成本。在数据持续到达、查询探测不断增长上下文的流式工作负载中，该成本过高，亟需一种更适合流式场景的计算模型。

Method: 提出以有状态会话为核心的数据驱动计算模型：维护持久化KV缓存，新数据到达时增量更新，将预填充移出关键路径；引入Flash Queries，利用数据到达间隙的空闲GPU周期预评估已注册问题并缓存答案；设计多租户连续批处理调度器，结合单元预算准入与前缀感知分组预填充，支持数十个有状态会话在单GPU上共存，同时保留完整的二次方自注意力。

Result: 在流式市场数据基准测试中，相比vLLM、SGLang、TensorRT-LLM、llama.cpp等传统推理引擎实现最高5.9倍加速；查询延迟在累积上下文增长时保持恒定。

Conclusion: 有状态会话模型有效解决了流式工作负载中预填充开销过高的问题，Flash Queries实现了传统无状态引擎无法支持的低延迟预计算模式，多租户调度器确保了资源高效共享，验证了该范式的优越性。

Abstract: Conventional transformer inference engines are request-driven, paying an O(n) prefill cost on every query. In streaming workloads, where data arrives continuously and queries probe an ever-growing context, this cost is prohibitive. We introduce a data-driven computational model centred on stateful sessions: a persistent KV cache advanced incrementally as new data arrives, so prefill is moved off the critical path and query latency becomes O(|q|), independent of accumulated context size. Building on this, Flash Queries reclaim idle GPU cycles between data arrivals to pre-evaluate registered questions and return cached answers before the user asks, a pattern that is structurally impossible in stateless engines because they discard intermediate state between requests. A multi-tenant continuous-batching scheduler with cell-budget admission and prefix-aware grouped prefill lets dozens of stateful sessions coexist on a single GPU while preserving full quadratic self-attention. On streaming market-data benchmarks the reference implementation achieves up to 5.9x speedup over conventional inference engines (vLLM, SGLang, TensorRT-LLM, llama.cpp), holding query latency constant as accumulated context grows.

</details>


### [178] [Interpretable Machine Learning for Antepartum Prediction of Pregnancy-Associated Thrombotic Microangiopathy Using Routine Longitudinal Laboratory Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13786)
*Chuanchuan Sun,Zhen Yu,Qin Fan,Qingchao Chen,Feng Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习方法（梯度提升模型）分析常规纵向实验室检测数据，实现了对妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）的早期风险预测，模型在独立测试集中AUROC达到0.872。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 妊娠相关血栓性微血管病（P-TMA）虽罕见但危及生命，其早期风险预测极具挑战性。相关的实验室异常表现细微、多维，且常被妊娠期血小板减少和妊娠相关蛋白尿等常见生理变化所掩盖，与良性产科及肾脏疾病存在严重重叠，传统的单变量或基于规则的方法难以有效捕捉这种复杂性。

Method: 这项回顾性研究纳入了300例妊娠病例（142例P-TMA，158例对照），保留了146个纵向实验室预测变量。数据按8:2比例分为训练集和独立测试集。研究评估了逻辑回归、支持向量机、随机森林、极端随机树和梯度提升五种算法，通过交叉验证选择最优模型，并进行了全局特征重要性和预测变量分布模式的可解释性分析。

Result: 通过交叉验证预先指定梯度提升为最终模型。该模型在独立测试集中实现了0.872的AUROC（95% CI: 0.769-0.952）和0.883的AUPRC（95% CI: 0.780-0.959），敏感性为0.750，特异性为0.812。研究发现第6周的胱抑素C有潜力作为早期监测指标。

Conclusion: 常规护理期间获取的纵向临床实验室检测数据包含对P-TMA风险具有信息价值和临床合理性的信号。机器学习方法能够有效提取这些隐含的、时间依赖的风险特征，为P-TMA的早期识别和干预提供了新的可能性。

Abstract: Background: Pregnancy-associated thrombotic microangiopathy (P-TMA) is rare but life-threatening. Early risk prediction before overt clinical presentation remains challenging, as the associated laboratory abnormalities are subtle, multidimensional, and frequently masked by common physiological changes such as gestational thrombocytopenia and pregnancy-related proteinuria, thus overlapping heavily with benign obstetric and renal conditions. This complexity is poorly captured by univariate or rule-based approaches; however, it is addressable by machine learning, which can extract latent, time-dependent risk signatures from longitudinal clinical tests. Methods: This retrospective study included 300 pregnancies comprising 142 P-TMA cases and 158 controls. After exclusion of identifiers and non-informative variables, 146 longitudinal laboratory predictors were retained. Participants were divided into a training cohort (80%) and a held-out test cohort (20%) using stratified sampling. Five algorithms were evaluated: logistic regression, support vector machine with radial basis function kernel, random forest, extra trees, and gradient boosting. The final model was selected by mean cross-validated AUROC, refitted on the full training cohort, and evaluated once in the held-out test cohort. Interpretability analyses examined global feature importance and distributional patterns of leading predictors. Results: Gradient boosting was prespecified by cross-validation in the training cohort. The model achieved an AUROC of 0.872 (95% CI: 0.769-0.952) and an AUPRC of 0.883 (95% CI: 0.780-0.959) in a held-out test cohort, with sensitivity of 0.750 and specificity of 0.812. Conclusions: Longitudinal clinical laboratory tests obtained during routine care contained informative and clinically plausible signals for P-TMA risk. Notably, cystatin C at week 6 showed promise as an early monitoring indicator.

</details>


### [179] [Force-Aware Neural Tangent Kernels for Scalable and Robust Active Learning of MLIPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13788)
*Eszter Varga-Umbrich,Zachary Weller-Davies,Paul Duckworth,Jules Tilly,Olivier Peltre,Shikha Surana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经切核(NTK)的可扩展、力感知主动学习方法，用于机器学习原子间势(MLIPs)的基础模型微调。该方法能够高效筛选大规模候选池，同时利用能量-力联合监督，在分布偏移情况下保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对机器学习原子间势主动学习面临的三大实际挑战：1)如何扩展到大规模候选池；2)如何有效利用能量-力监督信息；3)候选池与目标分布存在偏差时如何保持鲁棒性。现有方法在处理这些挑战时存在效率低下或性能不稳定的问题。

Method: 1) 提出基于分块特征空间后验方差筛选的线性扩展采集框架，避免候选集和训练集核矩阵的物质化，实现对~20万结构的高效筛选；2)通过混合参数-坐标导数将神经切核(NTK)扩展到力感知设置，推导出力NTK和联合能量-力NTK，为矢量场预测提供自然的相似性度量；3)利用预训练MLIP嵌入和NTK进行采集策略设计。

Result: 在OC20数据集上，联合能量-力NTK方法在所有指标和分布划分上均取得最低的能量和力MAE及RMSE；在T1x、PMechDB和RGD基准测试中，力NTK方法与已有基线方法性能相当，但比基于委员会的方法效率显著更高；在T1x控制候选池偏移案例研究中，基于预训练MLIP嵌入和NTK的采集方法保持鲁棒性，而委员会方法表现出更高方差。

Conclusion: 单个预训练MLIP即可实现可扩展、力感知且分布鲁棒的主动学习，为基础模型微调提供了高效的解决方案。该方法在保持竞争性能的同时显著提升了计算效率，为机器学习原子间势的实际应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Active learning for machine-learning interatomic potentials (MLIPs) must address several challenges to be practical: scaling to large candidate pools, leveraging energy-force supervision, and maintaining robustness when candidate pools are biased relative to the target distribution. In this work, we jointly address these challenges. We first introduce a linearly scaling acquisition framework based on chunked feature-space posterior-variance shortlisting. By avoiding materialisation of the candidate and train set kernels, this approach enables screening of ~200k structures within hours and applies broadly to acquisition strategies that score candidates based on molecular similarity metrics. We then extend the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) to a force-aware setting via mixed parameter-coordinate derivatives, yielding a force NTK and a joint energy-force NTK that provide natural similarity metrics for vector-field prediction. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the joint energy-force NTK on the OC20 dataset, where force-aware acquisition is crucial: it achieves the lowest energy and force MAE and RMSE across all metrics and distribution splits. Across T1x, PMechDB, and RGD benchmarks, our force NTK methods remain competitive with established baselines while being significantly more efficient than committee-based approaches. Under a controlled candidate-pool shift case study on T1x, acquisition based on pretrained MLIP embeddings and NTKs remains robust, whereas committee-based methods exhibit higher variance. Overall, these results show that a single pretrained MLIP can enable scalable, force-aware, and distribution-robust active learning for foundation-model fine-tuning.

</details>


### [180] [Provable Quantization with Randomized Hadamard Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13810)
*Ying Feng,Piotr Indyk,Michael Kapralov,Dmitry Krachun,Boris Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合抖动（dithering）技术的随机化Hadamard变换量化方法，在保持O(d log d)低计算复杂度的同时，证明了其均方误差理论上界可以渐近匹配密集随机旋转矩阵的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量量化通常通过随机投影后接标量量化来实现。密集随机旋转虽有良好的理论保证，但计算成本为Θ(d²)；随机化Hadamard变换(HD)可将成本降至O(d log d)，但其离散结构导致分析困难，压缩保证较弱或仅凭经验。因此，本文旨在寻找一种既能保持低计算成本，又能提供强理论保证的方法。

Method: 本文研究了一种带抖动的量化方法，仅使用单次随机化Hadamard变换。具体而言，量化器对输入向量应用HD变换，在量化前减去一个随机标量偏移量（即注入额外随机性），该操作的计算成本极低。论文对此方法进行了严格的理论分析。

Result: 证明了该方法是无偏的，并且其均方误差（MSE）界可以渐近匹配使用真正随机旋转矩阵所能达到的界。特别地，证明了抖动版TurboQuant在每坐标b比特下的MSE为(π√3/2 + o(1))·4^{-b}，其中o(1)项随量化级数增加，对所有单位向量和所有维度一致趋于零。

Conclusion: 通过引入抖动技术，单次随机化Hadamard变换量化方案成功克服了结构离散性带来的分析难题，在极低的计算开销下实现了与密集随机旋转相当的理论压缩性能，为相似性搜索、联邦学习等应用提供了高效且有理论保障的量化原语。

Abstract: Vector quantization via random projection followed by scalar quantization is a fundamental primitive in machine learning, with applications ranging from similarity search to federated learning and KV cache compression. While dense random rotations yield clean theoretical guarantees, they require $Θ(d^2)$ time. The randomized Hadamard transform $HD$ reduces this cost to $O(d \log d)$, but its discrete structure complicates analysis and leads to weaker or purely empirical compression guarantees.
  In this work, we study a variant of this approach: dithered quantization with a single randomized Hadamard transform. Specifically, the quantizer applies $HD$ to the input vector and subtracts a random scalar offset before quantizing, injecting additional randomness at negligible cost. We prove that this approach is unbiased and provides mean squared error bounds that asymptotically match those achievable with truly random rotation matrices. In particular, we prove that a dithered version of TurboQuant achieves mean squared error $\bigl(π\sqrt{3}/2 + o(1)\bigr) \cdot 4^{-b}$ at $b$ bits per coordinate, where the $o(1)$ term vanishes uniformly over all unit vectors and all dimensions as the number of quantization levels grows.

</details>


### [181] [Uncertainty-Driven Anomaly Detection for Psychotic Relapse Using Smartwatches: Forecasting and Multi-Task Learning Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13816)
*Nikolaos Tsalkitzis,Panagiotis P. Filntisis,Petros Maragos,Niki Efthymiou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种基于智能手表的精神病复发检测框架，并通过晚期融合策略将它们结合，在e-Prevention挑战赛数据集上实现了比冠军基线相对提升8%的性能，证明了整合心脏、运动和睡眠等多种数字表型对高保真检测至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字表型技术能够连续被动监测行为和生理特征，为精神病复发的早期检测提供了一种有前景的新范式。本研究旨在开发和系统研究基于智能手表的日常复发检测框架，以应对现实世界可穿戴设备数据变异性的挑战。

Method: 开发了两种基于Transformer编码器的框架：第一种通过预测心脏动力学并标记预测与观察特征之间的偏差作为异常指标；第二种采用多任务学习公式，融合睡眠与运动及心脏衍生信号，学习时间感知嵌入并预测测量时间。两者均通过多层感知机集成估计预测不确定性，输出每日异常评分。最后提出晚期融合策略，将两种架构的异常信号协同结合为统一的决策评分。

Result: 在第二届e-Prevention大挑战数据集上的基准测试显示，融合模型实现了比竞赛冠军基线相对提升8%的性能。研究表明两种框架虽然各自具有强大的预测能力，但它们捕获了互补的生理特征，消融实验进一步支持了融合多种数字表型（心脏、运动、睡眠）的重要性。

Conclusion: 整合多样化的数字表型（心脏、运动和睡眠）对于现实世界环境中精神病复发的高保真检测至关重要。所提出的融合方法通过结合互补的生理特征，有效提高了检测性能，为数字表型在精神健康监测中的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Digital phenotyping enables continuous passive monitoring of behavior and physiology, offering a promising paradigm for early detection of psychotic relapse. In this work, we develop and systematically study two smartwatch-based frameworks for daily relapse detection. The first forecasts cardiac dynamics and flags deviations between predicted and observed features as indicators of abnormality. The second adopts a multi-task formulation that fuses sleep with motion and cardiac-derived signals, learning time-aware embeddings and predicting measurement timing. Both pipelines use Transformer encoders and output a daily anomaly score, derived from predictive uncertainty estimated via an ensemble of multilayer perceptrons to improve robustness to real-world wearable variability. While each framework independently demonstrates strong predictive power, we show that they capture complementary physiological signatures. Consequently, we propose a late-fusion strategy that synergistically combines the anomaly signals from both architectures into a unified decision score. We benchmark our methodology on the 2nd e-Prevention Grand Challenge dataset, where our fused model achieves a 8% relative improvement over the competition-winning baseline. Our results, supported by extensive ablation studies, suggest that the integration of diverse digital phenotypes, cardiac, motion, and sleep, is essential for the high-fidelity detection of psychotic relapse in real-world settings.

</details>


### [182] [Reducing cross-sample prediction churn in scientific machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13826)
*Gordan Prastalo,Kevin Maik Jablonka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 科学机器学习模型在独立训练数据上的预测存在显著不一致性（cross-sample prediction churn）。传统参数端方法无法缓解此问题，而数据端方法（K-bootstrap bagging和twin-bootstrap）能有效降低预测不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前科学机器学习仅报告预测性能，忽略了同一模型在不同训练数据上预测结果的一致性问题。研究者发现两个在独立bootstrap数据上训练的分类器虽然整体准确率差异很小，但在具体样本的预测标签上存在8.0-21.8%的不一致率，这一重要缺陷此前未被关注。

Method: 提出cross-sample prediction churn指标量化预测不一致性。测试了参数端方法（深度集成、MC dropout、随机权重平均）和数据端方法。开发了两种数据端方法：（1）K-bootstrap bagging；（2）Twin-bootstrap，即两个网络在独立bootstrap数据上联合训练，通过对称KL散度一致性损失约束预测。

Result: 在9个化学基准数据集上，参数端方法无法降低预测churn。K-bootstrap bagging在所有数据集上降低churn率40-54%，不损失准确率，计算成本为K倍ERM。Twin-bootstrap在2倍ERM计算成本下，相比bagging-K=2进一步降低churn率中位数45%。

Conclusion: Cross-sample prediction churn应成为科学机器学习基准测试的标准评估指标，因为它能区分参数端方法和数据端方法在预测稳定性上的本质差异。数据端方法比参数端方法更有效解决预测一致性问题。

Abstract: Scientific machine learning reports predictive performance. It does not report whether the same prediction would survive a different draw of training data. Across $9$ chemistry benchmarks, two classifiers trained on independent bootstraps of the same training set agree on aggregate accuracy to within $1.3\text{--}4.2$ percentage points but disagree on the class label of $8.0\text{--}21.8\%$ of test molecules. We call this gap \emph{cross-sample prediction churn}. The standard parameter-side techniques (deep ensembles, MC dropout, stochastic weight averaging) do not reduce this gap; two data-side methods do. The first is $K$-bootstrap bagging, which cuts the rate $40\text{--}54\%$ on every dataset at no accuracy cost ($K{\times}$-ERM compute). The second is \emph{twin-bootstrap}, our proposal: two networks trained jointly on independent bootstraps with a sym-KL consistency loss between their predictions, which at matched $2{\times}$-ERM compute reduces churn a further median $45\%$ beyond bagging-$K{=}2$. Cross-sample prediction churn deserves a column alongside predictive performance in scientific-ML benchmark reports, because without it the parameter-side and data-side methods are indistinguishable on the metric they actually differ on.

</details>


### [183] [QLAM: A Quantum Long-Attention Memory Approach to Long-Sequence Token Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13833)
*Hoang-Quan Nguyen,Sankalp Pandey,Khoa Luu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子长注意力记忆（QLAM），一种利用量子叠加特性增强状态空间模型的混合量子-经典序列建模方法，在保持线性时间复杂度的同时实现了更优的长程依赖捕获能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的序列建模方法存在局限性：Transformer的注意力机制虽然能有效建模长程依赖，但随序列长度呈二次方复杂度，限制了其可扩展性；状态空间模型（SSMs）虽具有线性时间复杂度优势，但其通过加性或线性转移形成的记忆机制限制了捕获复杂全局交互的能力。因此需要一种既能保持线性复杂度又能增强记忆表达能力的新方法。

Method: 提出量子长注意力记忆（QLAM）作为SSMs的量子扩展。核心创新是将隐藏状态表示为量子态，其振幅编码了历史信息的叠加态。状态通过以输入为条件的参数化量子电路进行演化，实现非经典的全局更新机制。与显式计算成对交互的注意力机制不同，QLAM通过量子态演化隐式捕获全局依赖，并通过依赖于查询的测量操作检索任务相关信息。

Result: 在序列化图像分类基准测试上评估了QLAM，包括sMNIST、sFashion-MNIST和sCIFAR-10（图像被展平为token序列）。在所有任务中，QLAM始终优于循环基线模型和基于Transformer的模型，证明了其有效性。

Conclusion: QLAM成功地将量子系统的叠加特性引入状态空间模型，在保持SSMs的循环结构和线性时间特性的同时，从根本上丰富了记忆表示能力，为序列建模提供了一种新的有前景的方向。

Abstract: Modeling long-range dependencies in sequential data remains a central challenge in machine learning. Transformers address this challenge through attention mechanisms, but their quadratic complexity with respect to sequence length limits scalability to long contexts. State-space models (SSMs) provide an efficient alternative with linear-time computation by evolving a latent state through recurrent updates, but their memory is typically formed via additive or linear transitions, which can limit their ability to capture complex global interactions across tokens. In this work, we introduce one of the first studies to leverage the superposition property of quantum systems to enhance state-based sequence modeling. In particular, we propose Quantum Long-Attention Memory (QLAM), a hybrid quantum-classical memory mechanism that can be viewed as a quantum extension of state-space models. Instead of maintaining a classical latent state updated through additive dynamics, QLAM represents the hidden state as a quantum state whose amplitudes encode a superposition of historical information. The state evolves through parameterized quantum circuits conditioned on the input, enabling a non-classical, globally update mechanism. In this way, QLAM preserves the recurrent and linear-time structure of SSMs while fundamentally enriching the memory representation through quantum superposition. Unlike attention mechanisms that explicitly compute pairwise interactions, QLAM implicitly captures global dependencies through the evolution of the quantum state, and retrieves task-relevant information via query-dependent measurements. We evaluate QLAM on sequential variants of standard image classification benchmarks, including sMNIST, sFashion-MNIST, and sCIFAR-10, where images are flattened into token sequences. Across all tasks, QLAM consistently improves over recurrent baselines and transformer-based models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [Think Twice, Act Once: Verifier-Guided Action Selection For Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12620)
*Nishad Singhi,Christian Bialas,Snehal Jauhri,Vignesh Prasad,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Marcus Rohrbach,Anna Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VeGAS框架，通过测试时验证器引导的动作选择机制，提升多模态大语言模型具身智能体的鲁棒性和泛化能力，在挑战性任务上相对性能提升高达36%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于多模态大语言模型的具身智能体在处理分布外场景时表现脆弱，直接使用现成MLLM作为验证器无效，亟需提升其在复杂任务中的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出VeGAS测试时框架，采样候选动作集成并通过生成式验证器选择最可靠动作；设计LLM驱动的数据合成策略，自动构建失败案例课程以丰富验证器训练时的错误分布。

Result: 在Habitat和ALFRED具身推理基准测试中，VeGAS持续提升泛化性能，在最具挑战性的多物体长视野任务上相较强CoT基线实现高达36%的相对性能增益。

Conclusion: VeGAS通过显式验证步骤有效增强了MLLM具身智能体的鲁棒性，无需修改底层策略即可显著提升泛化能力，验证了测试时验证器引导策略的有效性。

Abstract: Building generalist embodied agents capable of solving complex real-world tasks remains a fundamental challenge in AI. Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have significantly advanced the reasoning capabilities of such agents through strong vision-language knowledge and chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning, yet remain brittle when faced with challenging out-of-distribution scenarios. To address this, we propose Verifier-Guided Action Selection (VegAS), a test-time framework designed to improve the robustness of MLLM-based embodied agents through an explicit verification step. At inference time, rather than committing to a single decoded action, VeGAS samples an ensemble of candidate actions and uses a generative verifier to identify the most reliable choice, without modifying the underlying policy. Crucially, we find that using an MLLM off-the-shelf as a verifier yields no improvement, motivating our LLM-driven data synthesis strategy, which automatically constructs a diverse curriculum of failure cases to expose the verifier to a rich distribution of potential errors at training time. Across embodied reasoning benchmarks spanning the Habitat and ALFRED environments, VeGAS consistently improves generalization, achieving up to a 36% relative performance gain over strong CoT baselines on the most challenging multi-object, long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [185] [Macro-Action Based Multi-Agent Instruction Following through Value Cancellation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12655)
*Wo Wei Lin,Ethan Rathbun,Enrico Marchesini Xiang Zhi Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAVIC方法，通过在指令边界校正贝尔曼备份来解决MARL中自然语言指令中断行为导致的值估计不一致问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的多智能体强化学习需要适应外部自然语言指令，这些指令可能中断正在执行的行为并与长期目标冲突。现有方法在指令上下文中对奖励进行条件化会导致贝尔曼更新耦合不同指令上下文的值估计，当指令中断宏动作时产生不一致的值，这是一个根本性的失败模式。

Method: 提出宏动作值校正方法（MAVIC），在指令边界处校正贝尔曼备份：校正传入的指令目标并恢复当前目标下的延续值。与奖励塑形不同，MAVIC直接修改引导目标本身，从而在统一策略下实现随机指令切换的一致值估计。该方法包含理论分析和演员-评论家实现。

Result: 在日益复杂的协作多智能体环境中，MAVIC实现了高指令遵从性，同时保持了基础任务性能。

Conclusion: MAVIC有效解决了MARL中指令中断导致的值估计不一致问题，能够在保持基础任务性能的同时实现高指令遵从性，为现实世界中需要适应外部指令的多智能体系统提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) in real-world use cases may need to adapt to external natural language instructions that interrupt ongoing behavior and conflict with long-horizon objectives. However, conditioning rewards on instructions introduces a fundamental failure mode as Bellman updates couple value estimates across instruction contexts, leading to inconsistent values when instructions interrupt macro-actions. We propose Macro-Action Value Correction for Instruction Compliance (MAVIC), which corrects Bellman backups at instruction boundaries by correcting the incoming instruction objective and restoring the continuation value under the current objective. Unlike reward shaping, MAVIC modifies the bootstrapping target itself, enabling consistent value estimation under stochastic instruction switching within a unified policy. We provide theoretical analysis and an actor-critic implementation, and show that MAVIC achieves high instruction compliance while preserving base task performance in increasingly complex cooperative multi-agent environments.

</details>


### [186] [Do Androids Dream of Breaking the Game? Systematically Auditing AI Agent Benchmarks with BenchJack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12673)
*Hao Wang,Hanchen Li,Qiuyang Mang,Alvin Cheung,Koushik Sen,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示主流智能体基准测试普遍存在“奖励黑客”漏洞，提出自动化红队系统BenchJack用于检测和修复这些缺陷，显著提升了基准测试的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的智能体基准测试已成为衡量前沿AI能力、指导模型选择与部署的事实标准，但“奖励黑客”（agent通过非预期方式最大化分数）现象在前沿模型中自发出现。现有评测流程缺乏对抗性思维，导致基准测试存在安全隐患，亟需从设计层面保障其安全性。

Method: 1. 从历史奖励黑客事件中提炼出8类反复出现的缺陷模式，形成Agent-Eval检查清单；2. 开发BenchJack自动化红队系统，驱动编码智能体以“全知”方式审计基准测试并合成奖励黑客攻击；3. 扩展为迭代式生成对抗流水线，发现新缺陷并打补丁以提升基准鲁棒性；4. 在覆盖软件工程、网页导航、桌面计算和终端操作的10个流行智能体基准上进行验证。

Result: BenchJack合成的奖励黑客攻击在大多数基准上获得近乎完美的分数却未解决任何实际任务，共暴露出跨8个类别的219个独立缺陷。扩展流水线将四个无致命设计缺陷的基准的可黑客任务比例从近100%降至10%以下，在三次迭代内完全修补了WebArena和OSWorld。

Conclusion: 评测流程尚未内化对抗性思维，主动审计有助于弥补快速发展的基准测试领域的安全缺口。基准测试必须从设计之初就具备安全性，通过系统化的缺陷分类和自动化对抗工具，可以有效识别和修复奖励黑客漏洞，提升评测的可靠性与公信力。

Abstract: Agent benchmarks have become the de facto measure of frontier AI competence, guiding model selection, investment, and deployment. However, reward hacking, where agents maximize a score without performing the intended task, emerges spontaneously in frontier models without overfitting. We argue that benchmarks must be secure by design. From past incidents of reward hacks, we derive a taxonomy of eight recurring flaw patterns and compile them into the Agent-Eval Checklist for benchmark designers. We condense the insights into BenchJack, an automated red-teaming system that drives coding agents to audit benchmarks and identify possible reward-hacking exploits in a clairvoyant manner. Moreover, we extend BenchJack to an iterative generative-adversarial pipeline that discovers new flaws and patches them iteratively to improve benchmark robustness. We apply BenchJack to 10 popular agent benchmarks spanning software engineering, web navigation, desktop computing, and terminal operations. BenchJack synthesizes reward-hacking exploits that achieve near-perfect scores on most of the benchmarks without solving a single task, surfacing 219 distinct flaws across the eight classes. Moreover, BenchJack's extended pipeline reduces the hackable-task ratio from near 100% to under 10% on four benchmarks without fatal design flaws, fully patching WebArena and OSWorld within three iterations. Our results show that evaluation pipelines have not internalized an adversarial mindset, and that proactive auditing could help close the security gap for the fast-paced benchmarking space.

</details>


### [187] [Revealing Interpretable Failure Modes of VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12674)
*Isha Chaudhary,Vedaant V Jain,Kavya Sachdeva,Sayan Ranu,Gagandeep Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了REVELIO框架，用于系统性地发现视觉-语言模型（VLM）中可解释的失效模式。该框架结合了多样性感知的集束搜索和高斯过程汤普森采样策略，在自动驾驶和室内机器人领域成功发现了最先进VLMs中未被报告的漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言模型（VLMs）因其强大的推理能力和泛化能力被广泛应用于安全关键场景，但在特定现实情况下会出现灾难性失效。目前缺乏系统性的方法来识别这些失效模式，而这些失效模式通常是由多个可解释的领域相关概念（如行人距离、恶劣天气条件）组合而成的，搜索空间呈指数级增长，识别难度极大。

Method: 提出了REVELIO框架来系统性地发现VLM中可解释的失效模式。该方法将失效模式定义为可解释的领域相关概念的组合。为了解决指数级离散组合搜索空间的挑战，REVELIO结合了两种搜索策略：（1）多样性感知的集束搜索，用于高效地映射失效景观；（2）高斯过程汤普森采样策略，用于对复杂失效模式进行更广泛的探索。

Result: 在自动驾驶领域，模型表现出弱空间定位能力，未能考虑主要障碍物，导致会引发模拟碰撞的建议；在室内机器人任务中，VLMs要么遗漏安全隐患，要么表现得过于保守，产生误报并降低操作效率。该框架成功发现了最先进VLMs中未被报告的漏洞。

Conclusion: REVELIO通过识别结构化和可解释的失效模式，为针对性的VLM安全改进提供了可操作的洞察，有助于提升VLM在安全关键应用中的可靠性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used in safety-critical applications because of their broad reasoning capabilities and ability to generalize with minimal task-specific engineering. Despite these advantages, they can exhibit catastrophic failures in specific real-world situations, constituting failure modes.
  We introduce REVELIO, a framework for systematically uncovering interpretable failure modes in VLMs. We define a failure mode as a composition of interpretable, domain-relevant concepts-such as pedestrian proximity or adverse weather conditions-under which a target VLM consistently behaves incorrectly. Identifying such failures requires searching over an exponentially large discrete combinatorial space. To address this challenge, REVELIO combines two search procedures: a diversity-aware beam search that efficiently maps the failure landscape, and a Gaussian-process Thompson Sampling strategy that enables broader exploration of complex failure modes.
  We apply REVELIO to autonomous driving and indoor robotics domains, uncovering previously unreported vulnerabilities in state-of-the-art VLMs. In driving environments, the models often demonstrate weak spatial grounding and fail to account for major obstructions, leading to recommendations that would result in simulated crashes. In indoor robotics tasks, VLMs either miss safety hazards or behave excessively conservatively, producing false alarms and reducing operational efficiency. By identifying structured and interpretable failure modes, REVELIO offers actionable insights that can support targeted VLM safety improvements.

</details>


### [188] [Learning Transferable Latent User Preferences for Human-Aligned Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12682)
*Alina Hyk,Sandhya Saisubramanian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CLIPR框架，通过最少对话输入学习可迁移的自然语言规则来表示潜在用户偏好，从而提升LLM决策的人类对齐性并降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在决策中难以产生符合人类预期的解决方案。人类对齐决策需要同时考虑显式目标和潜在用户偏好，但现有方法要么依赖大量重复的用户交互，要么无法在不同任务和上下文中泛化潜在偏好，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出CLIPR（Conversational Learning for Inferring Preferences and Reasoning）框架。该框架设定LLM负责高层推理，从有限交互中推断潜在用户偏好。CLIPR通过自适应反馈迭代优化，学习可行动、可迁移的自然语言规则来表示潜在偏好，并将这些规则应用于分布内和分布外的模糊任务。

Result: 在三个数据集上的评估和用户研究表明，CLIPR在提高对齐性和减少推理成本方面持续优于现有方法。该框架能够从最少的对话输入中学习可迁移的偏好规则，并有效应用于多个环境中的模糊任务。

Conclusion: CLIPR框架成功解决了LLM决策中的人类对齐问题，通过学习可迁移的自然语言偏好规则，实现了在有限用户交互下的高效偏好推断和决策优化，为LLM在实际应用中的人类对齐提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as reasoning modules in many applications. While they are efficient in certain tasks, LLMs often struggle to produce human-aligned solutions. Human-aligned decision making requires accounting for both explicitly stated goals and latent user preferences that shape how ambiguous situations should be resolved. Existing approaches to incorporating such preferences either rely on extensive and repeated user interactions or fail to generalize latent preferences across tasks and contexts, limiting their practical applicability. We consider a setting in which an LLM is used for high-level reasoning and is responsible for inferring latent user preferences from limited interactions, which guides downstream decision making. We introduce CLIPR (Conversational Learning for Inferring Preferences and Reasoning), a framework that learns actionable, transferable natural language rules that represent latent user preferences from minimal conversational input. These rules are iteratively refined through adaptive feedback and applied to both in-distribution and out-of-distribution ambiguous tasks across multiple environments. Evaluations on three datasets and a user study show that CLIPR consistently outperforms existing methods in improving alignment and reducing inference costs.

</details>


### [189] [On the Size Complexity and Decidability of First-Order Progression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12691)
*Jens Classen,Daxin Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了局部效应、正规和无环这三类动作的一阶演进大小仅呈多项式增长，且当知识库属于可判定片段时，演进结果保持在同一片段内，确保了可判定性和实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识库演进通常需要二阶逻辑，而识别一阶特例一直是动作推理的核心议题。尽管已知局部效应、正规和无环三类动作允许一阶演进，但缺乏对其演进大小的系统分析，这对实际应用至关重要。

Method: 在情景演算（Situation Calculus）框架下，对局部效应、正规和无环这三类表达力递增的动作类别进行研究，分析其知识库一阶演进的大小增长特性，并考察知识库属于可判定片段（如双变量一阶逻辑或带常量的全称理论）时演进的性质。

Result: 在合理假设下，这三类动作的一阶演进大小仅呈多项式增长。更重要的是，当知识库属于可判定片段（如双变量一阶逻辑或带常量的全称理论）时，演进结果保持在同一逻辑片段内。

Conclusion: 本文填补了对一阶演进大小系统分析的空白，证明了多项式增长的复杂度上界，并保证了在可判定片段内的封闭性，为一阶演进的实际应用提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Progression, the task of updating a knowledge base to reflect action effects, generally requires second-order logic. Identifying first-order special cases, by restricting either the knowledge base or action effects, has long been a central topic in reasoning about actions. It is known that local-effect, normal, and acyclic actions, three increasingly expressive classes, admit first-order progression. However, a systematic analysis of the size of such progressions, crucial for practical applications, has been missing. In this paper, using the framework of Situation Calculus, we show that under reasonable assumptions, first-order progression for these action classes grows only polynomially. Moreover, we show that when the KB belongs to decidable fragments such as two-variable first-order logic or universal theories with constants, the progression remains within the same fragment, ensuring decidability and practical applicability.

</details>


### [190] [DisaBench: A Participatory Evaluation Framework for Disability Harms in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12702)
*Eugenia Kim,Ioana Tanase,Christina Mallon*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DisaBench，一个针对大语言模型残疾相关危害的安全评估基准，包含12类残疾危害分类法、跨7个生活领域的评估方法以及175个提示的数据集，揭示了现有通用安全基准在识别残疾相关细微危害方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的大语言模型通用安全基准未能充分评估与残疾相关的危害，且缺乏针对这一特定领域的深入研究与评估工具。

Method: 与残障人士及红队测试专家共同创建了包含12个残疾危害类别的分类法；开发了分类法驱动的评估方法，在7个生活领域配对良性提示和对抗性提示；构建了包含175个提示及525个人工标注提示-响应对的数据集，并由四名具有残疾生活经历的评估者进行标注。

Result: 发现危害率因残疾类型而异，且在非文本模态中会加剧；术语驱动的危害受文化和时间限制，而非普遍可评估；标准安全评估虽能捕捉明显的失败，但会遗漏仅领域专家才能识别的细微危害。

Conclusion: 残疾危害具有个人性、交叉性和社区定义性，无法与个人完整背景隔离，通用基准系统性地遗漏了这些内容。将通过Hugging Face和开源红队测试框架发布数据集、分类法和方法，以便直接集成到现有安全流程中。

Abstract: General-purpose safety benchmarks for large language models do not adequately evaluate disability-related harms. We introduce DisaBench: a taxonomy of twelve disability harm categories co-created with people with disabilities and red teaming experts, a taxonomy-driven evaluation methodology that pairs benign and adversarial prompts across seven life domains, and a dataset of 175 prompts with human-annotated labels on 525 prompt-response pairs. Annotation by four evaluators with lived disability experience reveals three findings: harm rates vary sharply by disability type and will compound in non-text modalities, terminology-driven harm is culturally and temporally bound rather than universally assessable, and standard safety evaluation catches overt failures while missing the subtle harms that only domain expertise can recognize. Disability harm is simultaneously personal, intersectional, and community-defined: it cannot be isolated from the full context of who a person is, and general-purpose benchmarks systematically miss it. We will release the dataset, taxonomy, and methodology via Hugging Face and an open-source red teaming framework for direct integration into existing safety pipelines with no additional infrastructure.

</details>


### [191] [CHAL: Council of Hierarchical Agentic Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12718)
*Tommaso Giovannelli,Griffin D. Kent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CHAL框架，将多智能体辩论从真值任务转向可辩驳领域，通过图结构信念模式和梯度优化机制实现结构化的信念优化，使AI推理过程可审计、可解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体辩论方法存在结构局限性：辩论导致信念轨迹呈现鞅过程、性能提升主要来自多数投票、以及LLM表现出信心升级而非校准。作者认为辩论的真正价值不在真值任务，而在可辩驳领域，即任何立场原则上都可能被更好的推理击败。

Method: 提出分层代理语言议会（CHAL）框架：1）每个智能体维护CHAL信念模式（CBS），这是一种图结构信念表示，采用贝叶斯启发的架构；2）通过梯度信息动态机制促进信念修正，将信念论点的强度作为可微分目标；3）将元认知价值系统（涵盖认识论、逻辑学和伦理学）提升为可配置的超参数，以支配智能体推理和裁决结果。

Result: 消融实验显示：裁决者的价值系统决定辩论在潜在信念空间中的整体轨迹；议会多样性能够优化所有参与者的信念；该框架能够跨广泛领域进行泛化。这是首个将多智能体辩论视为可辩驳领域上结构化信念优化的框架。

Conclusion: CHAL框架产生的可审计信念产物为可辩驳论证建立了专用评估套件的基础，对构建推理和价值承诺透明、对齐且受人类监督的AI系统具有更广泛的意义。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate has emerged as a promising approach for improving LLM reasoning on ground-truth tasks, yet current methodologies face certain structural limitations: debate tends to induce a martingale over belief trajectories, majority voting accounts for most observed gains, and LLMs exhibit confidence escalation rather than calibration across rounds. We argue that the genuine value of debate, and dialectic systems as a whole, lies not in ground-truth tasks but in defeasible domains, where every position can in principle be defeated by better reasoning. We present the Council of Hierarchical Agentic Language (CHAL), a multi-agent dialectic framework that treats defeasible argumentation as an engine for belief optimization. Each agent maintains a CHAL Belief Schema (CBS), a graph-structured belief representation with a Bayesian-inspired architecture, that facilitates belief revision through a gradient-informed dynamic mechanism by leveraging the strength of the belief's thesis as a differentiable objective. Meta-cognitive value systems spanning epistemology, logic, and ethics are elevated to configurable hyperparameters governing agent reasoning and adjudication outcomes. We provide a series of ablation experiments that demonstrate systematic and interpretable effects: the adjudicator's value system determines the debate's overall trajectories in latent belief space, council diversity refines beliefs for all participants, and the framework generalizes across broad fields. CHAL is, to our knowledge, the first framework to treat multi-agent debate as structured belief optimization over defeasible domains. Further, the auditable belief artifacts it produces establish the foundation for dedicated evaluation suites for defeasible argumentation, with broader implications for building AI systems whose reasoning and value commitments are transparent, aligned, and subject to human oversight.

</details>


### [192] [BEHAVE: A Hybrid AI Framework for Real-Time Modeling of Collective Human Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12730)
*Helene Malyutina*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BEHAVE框架，将人类群体视为复杂动力系统，通过行为场建模集体动态，实现对群体稳定性、升级或崩溃的预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI系统仅在个体层面建模或在事件发生后检测，无法捕捉决定群体稳定、升级或崩溃的集体动态特性。群体作为复杂动力系统，表现出涌现性、非线性、反馈回路、临界点敏感性和相变等特征，其状态分布于相互影响回路中。

Method: 引入BEHAVE框架，将集体动态建模为基于可观测物理信号的交互空间上的连续行为场。将运动微信号（位置、速度、身体朝向、手势活动）构建为有向交互图，聚合成捕获非冗余集体状态轴的行为场基。框架基于一个定理和两个结构性命题，表征张力场、场基和临界性指数，并使用神经网络实现感知和预测层。

Result: 在7方谈判快照上展示了完整的工作流程；重校准后的行为场可应用于人群安全、危机团队动态、教育和临床场景。

Conclusion: BEHAVE作为一个计算系统，能够从数据中学习、表示和预测集体动态，为理解群体行为提供了新的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Existing AI systems for modeling human behavior operate at the level of individuals or detect events after they occur. As a result, they systematically fail to capture the collective dynamics that determine whether a group remains stable or transitions into escalation or breakdown. We propose a different foundation: a group of interacting humans constitutes a complex dynamical system in the precise mathematical sense, exhibiting emergence, nonlinearity, feedback loops, sensitivity near critical points, and phase transitions between qualitatively distinct regimes. The state of such a system is not located within any single participant; it is distributed across mutual influence loops and observable through the micro-dynamics of the body.
  We introduce BEHAVE (Behavioral Engine for Human Activity Vector Estimation), a formal framework that models collective dynamics as continuous behavioral fields defined over an interaction space derived from observable physical signals. Kinematic micro-signals (position, velocity, body orientation, gestural activity) are structured into a directed interaction graph and aggregated into a basis of behavioral fields capturing distinct, non-redundant axes of collective state. The framework rests on one theorem and two structural propositions characterizing the tension field, the field basis, and the criticality index. Perception and forecasting layers are implemented using neural models, enabling data-driven learning and approximation of system dynamics. BEHAVE is formulated as a computational system for learning, representing, and forecasting collective dynamics from data. A working pipeline is demonstrated on a 7-agent negotiation snapshot. The same fields, recalibrated, apply to crowd safety, crisis-team dynamics, education, and clinical contexts.

</details>


### [193] [State-Centric Decision Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12755)
*Sungheon Jeong,Ryozo Masukawa,Sanggeon Yun,Mahdi Imani,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了状态中心决策过程(SDP)框架，通过让智能体逐谓词构建状态空间，解决了语言环境缺少MDP分析所需结构的问题，在五个基准测试上取得最佳无训练结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言环境（如网页浏览器、代码终端和交互式仿真）仅输出原始文本而非状态，缺乏MDP分析所需的基本结构：没有显式状态空间、没有观测到状态的映射、没有经认证的转换、也没有终止准则。

Method: 引入状态中心决策过程(SDP)运行时框架，让智能体在行动过程中逐步构建缺失的输入。每一步智能体：1)承诺一个描述世界应然状态的自然语言谓词；2)采取行动使其成立；3)根据谓词检验观测。通过的谓词成为认证状态，由此产生的轨迹携带四个缺失对象。

Result: 在涵盖规划、科学探索、网络推理和多跳问答的五个基准测试上评估SDP，在所有五个基准上都取得了最佳的无训练结果，且随着时间范围增长优势进一步扩大。认证轨迹还支持反应式智能体无法进行的分析，包括每谓词信用分配、故障定位、部分进度测量和模块化算子替换。

Conclusion: SDP通过让智能体主动构建状态结构，成功弥合了语言环境与MDP分析框架之间的差距，不仅提升了任务性能，还为智能体行为提供了更丰富的分析工具和可解释性。

Abstract: Language environments such as web browsers, code terminals, and interactive simulations emit raw text rather than states, and provide none of the runtime structure that MDP analysis requires. No explicit state space, no observation-to-state mapping, no certified transitions, and no termination criterion. We introduce the State-Centric Decision Process (SDP), a runtime framework that constructs these missing inputs by having the agent build them, predicate by predicate, as it acts. At each step the agent commits to a natural-language predicate describing how the world should look, takes an action to make it true, and checks the observation against it. Predicates that pass become certified states, and the resulting trajectory carries the four objects language environments do not provide, namely a task-induced state space, an observation-to-state mapping, certified transitions, and a termination criterion. We evaluate SDP on five benchmarks spanning planning, scientific exploration, web reasoning, and multi-hop question answering. SDP achieves the best training-free results on all five, with the advantage widening as the horizon grows. The certified trajectories additionally support analyses unavailable to reactive agents, including per-predicate credit assignment, failure localization, partial-progress measurement, and modular operator replacement.

</details>


### [194] [PROMETHEUS: Automating Deep Causal Research Integrating Text, Data and Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12835)
*Sridhar Mahadevan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出PROMETHEUS框架，将文献、报告、代码等科学语料转化为因果地图集（Causal Atlases），通过层论（sheaf theory）方法组织和导航局部因果声明，支持证据溯源和反事实评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型能从文本中提取局部因果声明，但这些声明通常以扁平化摘要形式呈现，缺乏持久化、可导航的世界模型结构。当声明之间存在冲突、漂移或证据不足时，缺乏系统性的组织与诊断方法。

Method: 引入PROMETHEUS框架，基于层论（sheaf theory）构建Topos世界模型。将研究基底划分为显式覆盖区域，每个局部区域包含因果片段、结构化声明表、预测测试、支持统计和溯源信息；通过限制映射比较重叠区域，利用粘合诊断揭示一致性、漂移、矛盾和欠定性。当论文提供源数据、模拟输出或代码时，可评估反事实并重建层状世界模型。

Result: 通过三项文献地图集案例研究（海洋温度对海洋种群的影响、GLP-1减肥证据、白藜芦醇/红酒健康声明）展示了深度因果文本研究的 locality、证据和粘合张力；四项反事实案例研究（微塑料强迫论文、印度河流域水文论文、Sachs蛋白信号研究、歌唱小鼠研究）验证了当论文提供源数据或代码时，PROMETHEUS能评估反事实并重建世界模型的能力。

Conclusion: PROMETHEUS将科学语料转化为可导航的因果地图集，不仅提供单一全局图谱，更是研究工具，帮助导航语料内容、定位声明来源、评估支持强度，并揭示局部声明无法组装成连贯全局视图的张力区域。

Abstract: Large language models can extract local causal claims from text, but those claims become more useful when organized as persistent, navigable world models rather than as flat summaries.
