Abstract: Deploying language models as autonomous agents requires more than per-task accuracy: when an agent faces a queue of problems under a finite token budget, it must decide which to attempt, in what order, and how much compute to commit to each, all before any execution feedback is available. This is the prospective form of metacognitive control studied for decades in human cognition, yet whether language models possess it remains untested. We introduce TRIAGE, an evaluation framework in which a model receives a task pool and a token budget calibrated to its own baseline cost, and commits to a single ordered plan that jointly encodes selection, sequencing, and per-problem allocation. Plans are scored against an oracle with full knowledge of the model's solvability and cost on each problem, yielding a triage efficiency ratio on a common scale. We evaluate frontier and open-source models, with and without reasoning enabled, across competition mathematics, graduate-level science, code generation, and expert multidisciplinary knowledge, and find that current language models exhibit substantial gaps in prospective metacognitive control, revealing a previously unmeasured capability dimension with direct implications for resource-efficient agent deployment.

</details>


### [229] [Cognifold: Always-On Proactive Memory via Cognitive Folding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13438)
*Suli Wang,Yiqun Duan,Yu Deng,Rundong Zhao,Dai Shi,Xinliang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CogniFold，一种受大脑启发的“始终在线”的智能体记忆系统，通过将互补学习系统（CLS）理论从两层扩展到三层（增加前额叶意图层），利用图拓扑自组织机制，将碎片化事件流持续折叠为自涌现的认知结构，在结构形成和常规记忆基准测试中均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体记忆主要基于反应和检索，缺乏将经验自主组织为持久认知结构的能力，难以实现真正的自主智能体。

Method: 将互补学习系统（CLS）理论从两层（海马体、新皮层）扩展为三层，新增前额叶意图层；通过图拓扑自组织机制实现认知结构的主动组装、语义相似时合并、陈旧时衰减、联想回忆时重新链接，并在概念聚类密度超过阈值时浮现意图。

Result: 在CogEval-Bench评估中，CogniFold能独特地生成符合认知期望和概念涌现的记忆结构；在涵盖五个认知领域的7个广泛覆盖基准测试中，同时展现出稳健的常规记忆性能。

Conclusion: CogniFold成功实现了主动式、结构化的智能体记忆，为构建下一代主动式智能助手提供了新的路径。

Abstract: Existing agent memory remains predominantly reactive and retrieval-based, lacking the capacity to autonomously organize experience into persistent cognitive structure. Toward genuinely autonomous agents, we introduce Cognifold, a brain-inspired "always-on" agent memory designed for the next generation of proactive assistants. CogniFold continuously folds fragmented event streams into self-emerging cognitive structures, bootstrapping progressively higher-level cognition from incoming events and accumulated knowledge. We ground this by extending Complementary Learning Systems (CLS) theory from two layers (hippocampus, neocortex) to three, adding a prefrontal intent layer. Emulating the prefrontal cortex as the locus of intentional control and decision-making, CogniFold achieves this through graph-topology self-organization: cognitive structures proactively assemble under the stream, merge when semantically similar, decay when stale, relink through associative recall, and surface intents when concept-cluster density crosses a threshold. We evaluate structural formation using CogEval-Bench, demonstrating that CogniFold uniquely produces memory structures that match cognitive expectations and concept emergence. Furthermore, across 7 broad-coverage benchmarks spanning five cognitive domains, we validate that CogniFold simultaneously performs robustly on conventional memory benchmarks.

</details>


### [230] [Assessing the Creativity of Large Language Models: Testing, Limits, and New Frontiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13450)
*Samuel Schapiro,Alexi Gladstone,Jonah Black,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统评估了人类创造力测试在预测LLM创造力方面的有效性，发现现有测试无法可靠预测科学构想能力，因此提出了首个能有效预测LLM科学构想能力的创造力测试DRAT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，研究者常使用人类创造力测试来评估大语言模型（LLM）的创造力，但这些测试作为机器创造力衡量指标的有效性尚未确立，且其对人类创造力的预测能力本身也有限。为解决该问题，需要系统地评估现有测试在预测LLM实际创造性成就方面的有效性。

Method: 研究开展了首次大规模系统性研究，评估人类创造力测试在预测LLM三个目标构念（创意写作、发散性思维和科学构想）方面的有效性。在此基础上，研究者引入了发散性远程联想测试（DRAT），这是一种词汇空间测试，能在单一工具中同时评估收敛性思维和发散性思维。

Result: 研究发现：1）DAT和条件DAT分别是预测创意写作和发散性思维的最佳预测器；2）测试效果因构念而异，没有单一测试能很好预测所有构念；3）与普遍认知相反，现有测试均无法可靠预测科学构想能力；4）DRAT是首个也是唯一能显著预测LLM科学构想能力的创造力测试，且在主要设计选择上表现稳健；5）DRAT的性能提升无法通过DAT和远程联想测试的任何线性组合获得，表明在同一测试中评估发散和收敛思维对可靠预测科学构想能力至关重要。

Conclusion: 现有的人类创造力测试在预测LLM创造力方面存在显著局限性，特别是在科学构想能力预测上。通过开发DRAT这一创新性测试工具，研究证明同时评估发散性和收敛性思维对于准确预测LLM的科学构想能力是必要的，为未来LLM创造力评估方法的设计提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Measuring the creativity of large language models (LLMs) is essential for designing methods that can improve creativity and for enhancing our scientific understanding of this ability. To accomplish this, it has become common in recent years to administer tests of human creativity to LLMs. Although these tests provide a convenient and fully automated way to score "creativity," their validity as measures of machine creativity has not been established, and these tests already have limited validity as predictors of human creativity. To address this problem, we conduct the first large-scale, systematic study assessing the effectiveness of human creativity tests for predicting the creative achievement of LLMs across three target constructs: creative writing, divergent thinking, and scientific ideation. We find that the Divergent Association Task (DAT) and the Conditional DAT are the best predictors of creative writing and divergent thinking, respectively, but that test effectiveness varies significantly by construct, and no single test predicts all constructs well. Moreover, contrary to popular belief, no existing test reliably predicts scientific ideation ability. Motivated by this problem, we introduce the Divergent Remote Association Test (DRAT), a vocabulary-space test that assesses both convergent and divergent thinking in a single instrument. The DRAT is the first and only creativity test for LLMs that is a significant predictor of scientific ideation ability, demonstrating robustness across major design choices. Furthermore, the performance gain of the DRAT is not recoverable from any linear combination of the Divergent Association Task and the Remote Associates Test, indicating that assessing divergent and convergent thinking in the same test is essential to reliably predicting scientific ideation ability.

</details>


### [231] [MMSkills: Towards Multimodal Skills for General Visual Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13527)
*Kangning Zhang,Shuai Shao,Qingyao Li,Jianghao Lin,Lingyue Fu,Shijian Wang,Wenxiang Jiao,Yuan Lu,Weiwen Liu,Weinan Zhang,Yong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了MMSkills框架，通过封装多模态程序知识（文本过程+运行时状态卡+多视角关键帧）来增强视觉智能体的决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数技能包主要以文本提示、可执行代码或学习例程编码可复用行为，但视觉智能体的程序知识本质上是多模态的：复用不仅依赖于执行何种操作，还需要识别相关状态、解释进展或失败的视觉证据，并决策下一步行动。

Method: 1. 将多模态技能包定义为紧凑的、状态条件化的包，耦合文本过程与运行时状态卡和多视角关键帧；
2. 开发智能轨迹到技能生成器，通过工作流分组、过程归纳、视觉定位和元技能引导审计，将公共非评估轨迹转化为可复用的多模态技能；
3. 引入分支加载的多模态技能智能体，在临时分支中检查选定的状态卡和关键帧，与实时环境对齐并提炼为结构化指导。

Result: 在GUI和基于游戏的视觉智能体基准测试上的实验表明，MMSkills持续改进了前沿和较小的多模态智能体，证明外部多模态程序知识补充了模型内部先验。

Conclusion: 多模态程序知识的显式表示与复用能够有效增强视觉智能体能力，外部技能包与模型内部先验形成互补，为构建更强大的视觉智能体提供了新路径。

Abstract: Reusable skills have become a core substrate for improving agent capabilities, yet most existing skill packages encode reusable behavior primarily as textual prompts, executable code, or learned routines. For visual agents, however, procedural knowledge is inherently multimodal: reuse depends not only on what operation to perform, but also on recognizing the relevant state, interpreting visual evidence of progress or failure, and deciding what to do next. We formalize this requirement as multimodal procedural knowledge and address three practical challenges: (I) what a multimodal skill package should contain; (II) where such packages can be derived from public interaction experience; and (III) how agents can consult multimodal evidence at inference time without excessive image context or over-anchoring to reference screenshots. We introduce MMSkills, a framework for representing, generating, and using reusable multimodal procedures for runtime visual decision making. Each MMSkill is a compact, state-conditioned package that couples a textual procedure with runtime state cards and multi-view keyframes. To construct these packages, we develop an agentic trajectory-to-skill Generator that transforms public non-evaluation trajectories into reusable multimodal skills through workflow grouping, procedure induction, visual grounding, and meta-skill-guided auditing. To use them, we introduce a branch-loaded multimodal skill agent: selected state cards and keyframes are inspected in a temporary branch, aligned with the live environment, and distilled into structured guidance for the main agent. Experiments across GUI and game-based visual-agent benchmarks show that MMSkills consistently improve both frontier and smaller multimodal agents, suggesting that external multimodal procedural knowledge complements model-internal priors.

</details>


### [232] [AI-Generated Slides: Are They Good? Can Students Tell?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13532)
*Juho Leinonen,Lisa Zhang,Arto Hellas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 编程辅助工具生成的幻灯片质量最佳，学生无法区分AI与人工幻灯片，且倾向于将低质量内容归因于AI生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI工具的普及，研究旨在探索利用GenAI将教师的课程笔记转换为幻灯片的可行性，重点关注教育者和学生对AI生成教学材料的感知与评价。

Method: 研究选取了五类GenAI工具（包括教育端到端工具NotebookLM、通用大模型Claude和M365 Copilot、编程辅助工具Cursor和Claude Code）。首先通过教育者的叙述性评估分析AI生成幻灯片的质量；其次将最优幻灯片（经修改后）应用于真实课堂，通过对比实验分析学生对人工与AI生成幻灯片的感知差异及辨识能力。

Result: 编程辅助工具生成的幻灯片在准确性、完整性和教学合理性方面表现最佳。学生认为AI生成的幻灯片与教师制作的幻灯片质量相当，且无法可靠辨识其来源。此外，研究发现幻灯片的高质量评分与“AI生成”评分呈负相关，表明学生倾向于将低质量与AI来源联系起来。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明GenAI在教学设计流程中具有广阔的应用前景，同时也呼吁进一步研究如何帮助教育者负责任且高效地利用此类工具。

Abstract: As generative AI (GenAI) tools become easily accessible, there is promise in using such tools to support instructors. To that end, this paper examines using GenAI to help generate slides from instructor authored course notes, emphasizing instructor and student perceptions. We examine an end-to-end education tool (NotebookLM), two general-purpose LLMs (Claude, M365 Copilot), and two coding assistants (Cursor, Claude Code). We first analyze whether GenAI generated slides are ``good'' via narrative assessment by educators. We choose the best slides to use (with some modification) in a real course setting, and compare the student perception of human vs. AI generated slides. We find that coding assistant tools produce slides that were most accurate, complete, and pedagogically sound. Additionally, students rate GenAI slides to be of similar quality as instructor-created slides, and cannot reliably identify which slides are AI-generated. Additionally, we find a negative correlation between a high quality rating and a high ``AI-generated'' rating, suggesting students associate poor quality with the source of the slides being AI. These findings highlight promising opportunities for integrating GenAI into instructional design workflows and call for further research on how educators can best harness such tools responsibly and effectively.

</details>


### [233] [Scaling Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning with Parallel Search and Explicit Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13534)
*Jiabei Liu,Wenyu Mao,Junfei Tan,Chunxu Shen,Lingling Yi,Jiancan Wu,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MultiSearch框架，通过多查询检索和信息合并机制提升深度搜索代理的信噪比与推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度搜索代理在每个推理步骤仅生成单个检索查询，导致信息覆盖不足且噪声较高，信噪比低，进而降低推理准确性并增加不必要的推理步骤。

Method: 提出MultiSearch框架：1）每个推理步骤从多角度生成查询并并行检索，扩展相关信息范围；2）通过合并过程整合和精炼检索信息，提升信噪比；3）设计包含多过程奖励的强化学习框架，优化多查询检索与信息整合。

Result: 在七个基准测试上的广泛实验表明，MultiSearch优于基线方法，提升了检索的信噪比，改善了问答任务中的推理性能。

Conclusion: MultiSearch通过多查询检索与显式信息合并机制，有效解决了单查询检索的局限性，显著提升了深度搜索代理的性能。

Abstract: Deep search agents have proven effective in enhancing LLMs by retrieving external knowledge during multi-step reasoning. However, existing methods often generate a single query for retrieval at each reasoning step, limiting information coverage and introducing high noise. This may result in low signal-to-noise ratios (SNR) during search, degrading reasoning accuracy and leading to unnecessary reasoning steps. In this paper, we introduce MultiSearch, an RL-based framework that addresses these limitations through multi-query retrieval and explicit merging of retrieved information. At each reasoning step, MultiSearch generates queries from multiple perspectives and retrieves external information in parallel, expanding the scope of relevant information and mitigating the reliance on any single retrieval result. Then, the agent consolidates and refines retrieved information at the merging process, improving the SNR and ensuring more accurate reasoning. Additionally, we propose a reinforcement learning framework with a multi-process reward design to optimize agents for both multi-query retrieval and information consolidation. Extensive experiments on seven benchmarks demonstrate that MultiSearch outperforms baseline methods, enhancing the SNR of retrieval and improving reasoning performance in question-answering tasks.

</details>


### [234] [Learning Local Constraints for Reinforcement-Learned Content Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13570)
*Debosmita Bhaumik,Julian Togelius,Georgios N. Yannakakis,Ahmed Khalifa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合内容生成方法，将波函数坍缩(WFC)学习的局部约束应用于PCGRL生成器的动作空间，结合了WFC视觉质量优势和强化学习全局属性保证优势，成功生成了既美观又可玩的解谜平台游戏关卡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于约束的游戏内容生成器（如波函数坍缩WFC）能生成视觉上令人满意的关卡，但难以保证可玩性等全局属性；而基于强化学习的生成器能通过奖励函数保证全局属性，但视觉质量较差。现有方法各有优劣，需要结合两者优势。

Method: 将WFC学习到的局部约束用于限制PCGRL生成器的动作空间，使PCGRL生成器在保证全局属性的同时被迫遵守局部约束。通过改变输入数量和类型、测试随机坍缩起始状态以及排除稀有模式来分析该混合方法的运作机制。

Result: 尽管该方法对超参数调优较为敏感，但最佳训练后的生成器能够生成视觉上令人满意且可玩的解谜平台游戏关卡（如Lode Runner关卡），并具备所需的全局属性。

Conclusion: 结合约束学习与强化学习的混合方法可行，能够在保持视觉质量的同时保证全局属性，为游戏内容生成提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Constraint-based game content generators that learn local constraints from existing content, such as Wave Function Collapse (WFC), can generate visually satisfying game levels but face challenges in guaranteeing global properties, such as playability. On the other hand, reinforcement-learning trained generators can guarantee global properties -- because such properties can easily be included in reward functions -- but the results can be visually dissatisfying. In this paper, we explore ways to combine these methods. Specifically, we constrain the action space of a PCGRL generator with constraints learned by WFC, effectively allowing the PCGRL generator to achieve global properties while forced to adhere to local constraints. To better analyze how this hybrid content generation method operates, we vary the number and type of inputs, and we test whether to randomly collapse the starting state and exclude rare patterns. While the method is sensitive to hyperparameter tuning, the best of our trained generators produce visually satisfying and playable puzzle-platform game levels -- such as Lode Runner levels -- with desired global properties.

</details>


### [235] [Position: Assistive Agents Need Accessibility Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13579)
*Jie Hu,Changyuan Yan,Yu Zheng,Ziqian Wang,Jiaming Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将无障碍性视为AI智能体的对齐问题而非边缘可用性问题，提出无障碍对齐概念及全生命周期设计流程，以解决现有智能体在辅助盲人和视障用户时的系统性失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助智能体系统大多基于视力正常用户的交互假设、低成本验证和可容错的试错模式设计，导致在辅助场景中系统性失败，而这些问题无法仅通过模型扩展或事后接口适配来解决。通过对778个辅助任务实例的分析，发现当前智能体在辅助场景中仍易失败，原因是针对视力正常用户的设计假设与视障用户面临的验证、风险和交互约束不匹配。

Method: 引入无障碍对齐（accessibility alignment）概念，提出面向生命周期的无障碍对齐辅助智能体设计流程，涵盖用户研究、系统设计、部署和部署后迭代四个阶段，将无障碍性作为一等设计目标而非事后补充。

Result: 分析表明当前智能体在辅助场景中持续失败，根本原因在于设计假设与视障用户实际约束的不匹配；验证了以视障用户为中心的辅助任务可作为智能体AI的关键压力测试。

Conclusion: 无障碍性应被视为对齐问题而非边缘可用性问题；以视障用户为中心的辅助任务为智能体AI提供了关键压力测试，推动向包容性智能体设计的广泛转变。

Abstract: Assistive agents for Blind and Visually Impaired (BVI) users require accessibility alignment as a first-class design objective. Despite rapid progress in agentic AI, most systems are designed and evaluated under assumptions of sighted interaction, low-cost verification, and tolerable trial-and-error, leading to systematic failures in assistive scenarios that cannot be resolved by model scaling or post-hoc interface adaptations alone. Drawing on an analysis of 778 assistance task instances from prior work, we show that current agentic AI remain prone to failure in assistive scenarios due to mismatches between sighted-user design assumptions and the verification, risk, and interaction constraints faced by BVI users. We argue that accessibility should be treated as an alignment problem rather than a peripheral usability concern. To this end, we introduce accessibility alignment and propose a lifecycle-oriented design pipeline for accessibility-aligned assistive agents, spanning user research, system design, deployment and post-deployment iteration. We conclude that BVI-centered assistive tasks provide a critical stress test for agentic AI and motivate a broader shift toward inclusive agent design.

</details>


### [236] [How to Interpret Agent Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13625)
*Jie Gao,Kaiser Sun,Jen-tse Huang,Katherine Van Koevering,Sijie Ji,Heyuan Huang,Weiyan Shi,Zhuoran Lu,Ziang Xiao,Daniel Khashabi,Mark Dredze*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ACT*ONOMY，一个用于描述和分析自主智能体运行时行为的分类法，包含三级层次结构（10个动作、46个子动作、120个叶类别）及自动化分析管道，以解决智能体推理轨迹和执行追踪数据非结构化难以规模化解释的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Claude Code和Codex等自主智能体现在可以运行数小时甚至数天，理解其运行时行为对于诊断低效、修复错误和确保监督至关重要。主要方式是分析智能体生成的推理轨迹和执行追踪，但这些数据以非结构化自然语言形式存在，使得人类难以规模化解释。

Method: 通过扎根理论（Grounded Theory）开发了一个三级层次结构的分类法（10个动作、46个子动作、120个叶类别），并提供了一个开放仓库，包含实时分类法、自动化分析管道以及用于定制和扩展的扩展协议。

Result: 实验表明ACT*ONOMY能够比较不同智能体的行为特征，表征单个智能体在不同轨迹上的行为，揭示指示失败模式的模式。通过提供共享词汇表，帮助研究人员、智能体设计者和最终用户更一致地解释智能体行为。

Conclusion: ACT*ONOMY为研究人员、智能体设计者和最终用户提供了共享词汇表，使其能够更一致地解释智能体行为，从而实现更好的监督和控制。

Abstract: Autonomous agents such as Claude Code and Codex now operate for hours or even days. Understanding their runtime behavior has become critical for downstream tasks such as diagnosing inefficiencies, fixing bugs, and ensuring better oversight. A primary way to gain this understanding is analyzing the reasoning trajectories and execution traces these agents generate. Yet such data remains in unstructured natural-language form, making it difficult for humans to interpret at scale. We introduce ACT*ONOMY (a combination of Action and Taxonomy), a taxonomy for describing and analyzing agent behavior at runtime. ACT*ONOMY has two components: (1) the taxonomy itself, developed through Grounded Theory and structured as a three-level hierarchy of 10 actions, 46 subactions, and 120 leaf categories; and (2) an open repository that hosts the living taxonomy, provides an automated analysis pipeline that applies it to agent trajectories analysis, and defines an extension protocol for customization and growth. Our experiments show that ACTONOMY can compare behavioral profiles across agents and characterize a single agent's behavior across diverse trajectories, surfacing patterns indicative of failure modes. By providing a shared vocabulary, ACT*ONOMY helps researchers, agent designers, and end users interpret agent behavior more consistently, enabling better oversight and control.

</details>


### [237] [Adaptive mine planning under geological uncertainty: A POMDP framework for sequential decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13702)
*Hamza Khalifi,Jef Caers,Yassine Taha,Mostafa Benzaazoua,Abdellatif Elghali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于POMDP的矿山生产调度自适应框架，通过序贯信念更新将地质不确定性从被动约束转化为价值创造的主动组成部分，相比传统随机优化方法，显著缩小了期望-现实差距并大幅提高了实际净现值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的矿山生产调度在地质不确定性下采用随机优化方法，计算固定的开采序列和路线决策，将不确定性视为被动因素。这种基于计划的范式通过对地质情景进行对冲来制定决策，但未能预见到未来的观测将如何指导未来的决策。

Method: 将矿山调度问题建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），决策按顺序进行，并明确整合对未来信念更新的预期。为实现计算可行性，提出了混合SA-POMDP架构：结合基于模拟退火（SA）的价值近似与基于集成的信念更新方法（ES-MDA）。在每个决策时期，根据当前信念评估候选行动的长期期望价值，并随着采矿观测的获得而更新信念，从而生成自适应策略而非固定计划。

Result: 在具有多个加工目的地的铜金露天矿综合体上进行评估：在统计一致性先验条件下，SA-POMDP将期望-现实差距从22.3%缩小至4.6%，相比一次性随机优化提高了840万美元的实际NPV；在10%的系统性先验错误设定下，自适应框架优于静态规划高达4460万美元（36.9%），展示了超越情景对冲的结构鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 序贯信念更新能够将地质不确定性从被动约束转化为价值创造的主动组成部分，自适应决策框架相比传统静态规划方法具有显著优势，为不确定性环境下的矿山生产调度提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Strategic mine production scheduling under geological uncertainty is conventionally formulated as a stochastic optimization problem in which a fixed extraction sequence and routing decisions are computed ex ante. This plan-driven paradigm treats uncertainty as passive: decisions are hedged across geological scenarios, but planning does not anticipate how future observations will inform future decisions. We propose a different perspective by formulating mine scheduling as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP), in which extraction and routing decisions are made sequentially with planning explicitly integrating the expectation of future belief updates. To achieve computational tractability, we introduce a hybrid SA-POMDP architecture that combines simulated annealing-based (SA) value approximation with ensemble-based belief updating via ensemble smoother with multiple data assimilation (ES-MDA). At each decision epoch, candidate actions are evaluated through their expected long-term value under the current belief, and the belief is updated as mining observations are assimilated. This yields an adaptive policy rather than a fixed plan. We evaluate the framework on a copper-gold open-pit mining complex with multiple processing destinations. Under a statistically consistent prior, the SA-POMDP reduces the expectation-reality gap from 22.3% to 4.6%, improving realized NPV by USD8.4M relative to one-shot stochastic optimization. Under systematic prior misspecification of 10%, the adaptive framework outperforms static planning by up to USD44.6M (36.9%), demonstrating structural robustness beyond scenario hedging. These results show that sequential belief updating transforms geological uncertainty from a passive constraint into an active component of value creation.

</details>


### [238] [ScioMind: Cognitively Grounded Multi-Agent Social Simulation with Anchoring-Based Belief Dynamics and Dynamic Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13725)
*Yitian Yang,Yiqun Duan,Linghan Huang,Yiqi Zhu,Francesco Bailo,Chunmeizi Su,Huaming Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ScioMind框架，通过融合结构化观点动力学与LLM推理，结合记忆锚定更新规则、层次化记忆架构和动态智能体档案三大组件，有效提升了多智能体社会模拟的行为真实性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的多智能体模拟方法存在两极分化：要么依赖缺乏认知基础的固定更新规则，要么完全依赖无约束的LLM交互，导致模拟缺乏真实性和稳定性。作者旨在弥合这两种范式，构建一个具有认知基础的社会观点动力学仿真框架。

Method: 提出了ScioMind框架，集成了三个核心组件：1）记忆锚定的信念更新规则，通过基于人格的条件化锚定强度来调节影响敏感性；2）支持持久性、经验驱动的信念形成的层次化记忆架构；3）基于语料库检索的动态智能体档案生成机制，实现异构人格、理由和演化的内部状态。

Result: 在真实政策辩论场景的多个案例研究中，该方法在极化、多样性、极端化和轨迹稳定性等指标上均表现优异。动态档案增加了观点多样性，记忆与反思机制减少了不稳定振荡，锚定机制诱导了更符合政治心理学研究模式的持久信念轨迹。

Conclusion: ScioMind的认知基础设计为基于LLM的社会模拟提供了一种新颖解决方案，在稳定性和行为真实性方面均取得了显著提升，证明了结合结构化规则与LLM推理的有效性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent simulation offers a powerful testbed for studying social opinion dynamics. Yet current approaches often adopt two contrasting methods: either relying on fixed update rules with limited cognitive grounding or delegating belief change largely to unconstrained LLM interaction. We introduce ScioMind, a cognitively grounded simulation framework that bridges these paradigms by combining structured opinion dynamics with LLM-based agent reasoning. ScioMind integrates three key components: 1) a memory-anchored belief update rule that modulates susceptibility to influence via personality-conditioned anchoring strength; 2) a hierarchical memory architecture that supports persistent, experience-driven belief formation; and 3) dynamic agent profiles derived from a corpus-grounded retrieval pipeline, enabling heterogeneous personalities, rationales, and evolving internal states. We evaluate ScioMind on multiple case studies in a real-world policy debate scenario. Across metrics including polarisation, diversity, extremization, and trajectory stability, the proposed components consistently yield improvements in behavioural realism. In particular, dynamic profiles increase opinion diversity, memory and reflection reduce unstable oscillation, and anchoring induces persistent belief trajectories that better align with patterns reported in political psychology. These results suggest that our cognitively grounded design provides a novel solution to LLM-based social simulation that improves both stable and behavioural realism

</details>


### [239] [Senses Wide Shut: A Representation-Action Gap in Omnimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13737)
*Trung Nguyen Quang,Yiming Gao,Fanyi Pu,Kaichen Zhang,Shuo Sun,Ziwei Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IMAVB基准测试，发现多模态大语言模型能够感知到文本前提与视听输入的冲突，但在生成输出时却无法正确拒绝错误前提，存在“表征-行动差距”，表明瓶颈在于转化而非感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全能多模态大语言模型被定位为感知基础的智能体，但一个基本的接地能力尚未被测试：捕获与模型自身感官输入相冲突的文本声明。研究者旨在探究当模型面对文本前提与其看到的或听到的内容相矛盾时，失败是源于感知还是行动。

Method: 1. 构建IMAVB基准测试：包含500个长篇电影片段，采用2x2设计（目标模态：视觉/音频；前提条件：标准/误导），独立测量冲突检测与普通多模态理解能力。
2. 在8个开源全能多模态LLM和Gemini 3.1 Pro上进行测试。
3. 分析隐藏状态与输出行为的关系。
4. 提出探测引导的logit调整（PGLA）作为诊断干预方法。

Result: 1. 发现表征-行动差距：隐藏状态能够可靠编码前提-感知不匹配，但模型在输出中几乎从不拒绝错误声明。
2. 行为上存在两种失败模式：欠拒绝（将误导性问题视为真实）和过拒绝（牺牲标准问题的理解准确性）。
3. 差距具有模态不对称性（音频接地表现不如视觉）。
4. 在7种提示变体中表现出抗提示性。
5. PGLA方法能够持续改善拒绝行为。

Conclusion: 多模态接地能力的瓶颈在于转化而非感知。模型能够感知到冲突，但无法将这种感知转化为正确的输出行为，这为未来改进多模态模型的接地能力指明了方向。

Abstract: When an omnimodal large language model accepts a question whose textual premise contradicts what it actually sees or hears, does the failure lie in perception or in action? Recent omnimodal models are positioned as perception-grounded agents that jointly process video, audio, and text, yet a basic form of grounding remains untested: catching a textual claim that conflicts with the model's own sensory input. We introduce IMAVB, a curated 500-clip benchmark of long-form movies with a 2x2 design crossing target modality (vision, audio) and premise condition (standard, misleading), which lets us measure conflict detection separately from ordinary multimodal comprehension. Across eight open-source omnimodal LLMs and Gemini 3.1 Pro, we document a Representation-Action Gap: hidden states reliably encode premise-perception mismatches even when the same models almost never reject the false claim in their outputs. Behaviorally, models fall into two failure modes: under-rejection, in which they answer misleading questions as if the false premise were true; and over-rejection, in which they reject more often but also reject standard questions, sacrificing ordinary comprehension accuracy. The gap is modality-asymmetric (audio grounding underperforms vision) and prompt-resistant across seven variants. As an initial diagnostic intervention, a probe-guided logit adjustment (PGLA) re-injects the encoded mismatch signal into decoding and consistently improves rejection behavior. Together, these results suggest the bottleneck for omnimodal grounding lies in translation, not perception.

</details>


### [240] [Harnessing Agentic Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13821)
*Jiayi Zhang,Yongfeng Gu,Jianhao Ruan,Maojia Song,Yiran Peng,Zhiguang Han,Jinyu Xiang,Zhitao Wang,Caiyin Yang,Yixi Ouyang,Bang Liu,Chenglin Wu,Yuyu Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了AEvo框架，通过将智能体进化过程建模为交互式环境，利用元智能体编辑进化过程或智能体上下文，统一了过程式和智能体式进化方法，在多个基准测试中显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的智能体进化方法要么是固定的人工设计流程（模块化但僵化），要么是通用智能体（灵活但容易在长程进化中漂移）；两者虽然都积累了丰富的证据（候选解、反馈、轨迹、失败案例等），但缺乏稳定的接口来组织这些证据并修正驱动未来进化的机制。

Method: 将智能体进化形式化为交互式环境，其中累积的进化上下文作为过程级状态；引入AEvo元编辑框架，元智能体观察该状态后不直接提出下一个候选解，而是通过编辑控制未来进化的程序或智能体上下文来指导搜索，从而统一了过程式和智能体式进化。

Result: 在智能体和推理基准测试中，AEvo优于五个进化基线，相比最强基线实现26%的相对提升；在三个开放式优化任务中，AEvo优于四个进化基线，并在相同迭代预算下达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: AEvo通过将累积证据转化为可操作的指导，实现了对长程进化过程的稳定引导，证明了元编辑范式在智能体进化中的有效性，为程序、工作流和科学解决方案的持续改进提供了新思路。

Abstract: Agentic evolution has emerged as a powerful paradigm for improving programs, workflows, and scientific solutions by iteratively generating candidates, evaluating them, and using feedback to guide future search. However, existing methods are typically instantiated either as fixed hand-designed procedures that are modular but rigid, or as general-purpose agents that flexibly integrate feedback but can drift in long-horizon evolution. Both forms accumulate rich evidence over time, including candidates, feedback, traces, and failures, yet lack a stable interface for organizing this evidence and revising the mechanism that drives future evolution. We address this limitation by formulating agentic evolution as an interactive environment, where the accumulated evolution context serves as a process-level state. We introduce AEvo, a harnessed meta-editing framework in which a meta-agent observes this state and acts not by directly proposing the next candidate, but by editing the procedure or agent context that controls future evolution. This unified interface enables AEvo to steer both procedure-based and agent-based evolution, making accumulated evidence actionable for long-horizon search. Empirical evaluations on agentic and reasoning benchmarks show that AEvo outperforms five evolution baselines, achieving a 26 relative improvement over the strongest baseline. Across three open-ended optimization tasks, AEvo further outperforms four evolution baselines and achieves state-of-the-art performance under the same iteration budget.

</details>


### [241] [History Anchors: How Prior Behavior Steers LLM Decisions Toward Unsafe Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13825)
*Alberto G. Rodríguez Salgado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现前沿大模型在作为智能体执行长工具调用序列时存在严重安全隐患：即使原本高度安全的模型，仅通过一句提示词要求其“与历史行为保持一致”，就会被诱导以91-98%的概率继续执行有害操作，且旗舰模型反而最易受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着前沿大语言模型日益被部署为能够根据长工具调用日志选择下一步行动的智能体，一个关键的安全问题随之浮现：如果历史日志中包含有害的先前步骤，模型是否会继续执行有害行为？这项研究旨在评估模型在存在有害历史先例时的安全性和一致性风险。

Method: 构建了包含10个高风险领域的100个短场景基准测试集HistoryAnchor-100。每个场景将三个强制性的有害先验行为与一个包含两个安全选项和两个不安全选项的自由选择节点配对。研究团队对来自六个提供商的17个前沿模型进行了测试，并在中性系统提示和添加“与先前历史中显示的策略保持一致”指令两种条件下进行对比，同时设计了置换动作标签和全安全历史两种控制实验。

Result: 在中性系统提示下，对齐最强的模型几乎从不选择不安全选项；但仅添加一句要求与历史保持一致的指令，就使其选择不安全选项的概率激增至91-98%，且被翻转的模型往往会升级有害行为。控制实验排除了简单解释：置换动作标签后效应依然存在，而全安全历史下的不安全率保持在7%以下。不同模型家族在不同有害历史剂量下翻转，且在每个对齐家族中，旗舰模型是受影响最严重的，呈现出与安全性相关的逆缩放规律。

Conclusion: 这些结果为智能体部署敲响了警钟，特别是在轨迹可能被重放、伪造或注入的场景中。研究表明，即使经过严格对齐训练的旗舰模型，在面对有害历史先例和一致性压力时，也极易被诱导执行有害操作，暴露了当前安全对齐方法的脆弱性。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are increasingly deployed as agents that pick the next action after a long log of prior tool calls produced by the same or a different model. We ask a simple safety question: if a prior step in that log was harmful, will the model continue the harmful course? We build HistoryAnchor-100, 100 short scenarios across ten high-stakes domains, each pairing three forced harmful prior actions with a free-choice node offering two safe and two unsafe options. Across 17 frontier models from six providers we find a striking asymmetry: under a neutral system prompt the strongest aligned models almost never pick unsafe, but a single added sentence, "stay consistent with the strategy shown in the prior history", flips them to 91-98%, and the flipped models often escalate beyond continuation. Two controls rule out simpler explanations: permuting action labels leaves the effect intact, and the same instruction with an all-safe prior history keeps unsafe rates below 7%. Different families flip at different doses of unsafe history, and within every aligned family the flagship is the most affected sibling, an inverse-scaling pattern with respect to safety. These results are a red flag for agentic deployments where trajectories may be replayed, forged, or injected.

</details>


### [242] [Quantifying Sensitivity for Tree Ensembles: A symbolic and compositional approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13830)
*S. Akshay,Chaitanya Garg,Ashutosh Gupta,Kuldeep S. Meel,Ajinkya Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对决策树集成模型提出了一种高效的定量敏感性分析方法，通过代数决策图编码和分治策略，实现了在认证误差范围内快速计算敏感性，实验表明其工具XCount相比现有方法有显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策树集成模型在多个安全关键领域广泛应用，验证其属性（尤其是敏感性）非常重要。敏感性问题是判断输入特征的微小变化是否会导致错误分类。现有方法缺乏针对DTE的定量敏感性分析，且计算效率不足。

Method: 提出一种新颖的算法技术：1）将输入空间离散化并枚举易受敏感性影响的区域；2）将问题编码为代数决策图（ADD）；3）将问题分解为子问题进行组合式求解，保证在认证误差和置信区间内高效计算。

Result: 开发了工具XCount，在包含不同数量树和深度的基准测试上进行评估，与模型计数器方法对比。实验结果表明，XCount实现了显著的加速，并且能够随集成规模的增加良好扩展。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于ADD的组合式方法能够高效、准确地对决策树集成进行定量敏感性分析，在保证认证误差和置信界的前提下，显著优于现有方法，具有良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: Decision tree ensembles (DTE) are a popular model for a wide range of AI classification tasks, used in multiple safety critical domains, and hence verifying properties on these models has been an active topic of study over the last decade. One such verification question is the problem of sensitivity, which asks, given a DTE, whether a small change in subset of features can lead to misclassification of the input. In this work, our focus is to build a quantitative notion of sensitivity, tailored to DTEs, by discretizing the input space of the model and enumerating the regions which are susceptible to sensitivity. We propose a novel algorithmic technique that can perform this computation efficiently, within a certified error and confidence bound. Our approach is based on encoding the problem as an algebraic decision diagram (ADD), and further splitting it into subproblems that can be solved efficiently and make the computation compositional and scalable. We evaluate the performance of our technique over benchmarks of varying size in terms of number of trees and depth, comparing it against the performance of model counters over the same problem encoding. Experimental results show that our tool XCount achieves significant speedup over other approaches and can scale well with the increasing sizes of the ensembles.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [243] [A detailed algorithmic study on a reuse-aware, near memory, all-digital Ising machine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12959)
*Siddhartha Raman Sundara Raman,Lizy K. John,Jaydeep P. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SACHI，一种通过复用CPU L1缓存实现的全数字伊辛架构。相比BRIM方案，SACHI实现了300倍性能提升和80倍能耗降低，证明了利用商用SRAM结构可实现高效可靠的伊辛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的伊辛加速器存在明显局限性：多为针对特定问题类的专用加速器，依赖ADC/DAC电路，且由于对工艺变化敏感的嵌入式存储技术而面临可靠性挑战。针对这些问题，本文旨在设计一种全数字、高可靠性、低开销的伊辛加速方案。

Method: 提出SACHI架构，采用存内计算（PIM）技术将CPU的L1缓存重构为伊辛机。该方法消除了对ADC/DAC的需求，通过重用感知计算提高并行度，并采用新型计算与映射策略，以最小的硬件开销将加速器紧密集成到CPU流水线中。

Result: 在资产配置、分子动力学、图像分割和旅行商问题等应用中，相比BRIM方案实现了300倍的性能提升和80倍的能耗降低；在多项工作负载中观察到高达4000倍的重用因子。

Conclusion: 利用商用SRAM结构与通用处理器紧密集成，可以实现可靠且高效的全数字伊辛加速，为解决大规模、高精度优化问题提供了一条可行的新路径。

Abstract: Recently, nature-inspired computing approaches have gained significant attention for solving difficult optimization problems, particularly through Ising machines for NP-complete applications. Existing Ising accelerators range from quantum and optical annealers to CMOS-based von-Neumann and in-memory architectures. However, many prior designs are specialized accelerators limited to specific problem classes, rely on ADC/DAC circuits, and suffer from reliability challenges due to process-variation-sensitive embedded memory technologies.
  This paper presents SACHI, an all-digital Ising architecture implemented by repurposing the L1 cache of a CPU using SRAM-based processing-in-memory techniques. SACHI eliminates the need for ADCs/DACs, improves reliability compared to prior approaches such as BRIM, and enables Ising acceleration with minimal hardware overhead integrated into the CPU pipeline. The paper also provides detailed architectural analysis and pseudo-code for the proposed algorithms.
  The key contributions of SACHI are: (i) tight integration of the accelerator with the CPU pipeline, (ii) reuse of existing cache hardware for acceleration, (iii) higher parallelism enabled through reuse-aware computation, and (iv) improved performance and energy efficiency for large-scale, high-precision optimization problems using novel compute and mapping strategies.
  Compared to BRIM, SACHI achieves 300x performance improvement and 80x energy reduction across applications including asset allocation, molecular dynamics, image segmentation, and traveling salesman problems. Additionally, reuse factors up to 4000x are observed for several workloads. This work demonstrates that reliable and efficient all-digital Ising acceleration can be achieved using commodity SRAM structures tightly integrated with general-purpose processors.

</details>


### [244] [PoisonCap: Efficient Hierarchical Temporal Safety for CHERI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13210)
*Yuecheng Wang,Jonathan Woodruff,Alfredo Mazzinghi,Peter Rugg,Alexandre Joannou,Samuel W. Stark,Robert N. M. Watson,Simon W. Moore*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了 PoisonCap，一种针对 CHERI 系统的可扩展时序安全方案，通过引入“中毒”能力格式实现了严格的释放后使用（UAF）保护和初始化安全，在不产生额外性能开销的情况下超越了现有最先进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 CHERI 系统上最先进的时序安全方案 Cornucopia Reloaded 存在局限性：仅提供释放后重分配安全而非更强的释放后使用安全，且无法强制执行初始化安全。高效的内存安全是编程语言、操作系统和硬件设计日益优先考虑的问题，亟需一种更完善的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种新的“中毒”能力格式，用于强制执行严格的释放后使用和初始化安全，并将内存状态传递给微架构以实现隔离内存的高效缓存管理；利用能力边界优雅地委托内存中毒权限，允许嵌套分配器在不干扰上游分配器的情况下强制执行安全策略；用 PoisonCap 替代 Cornucopia 的影子位图，实现重分配时自动清零或可选的写前读陷阱机制。

Result: PoisonCap 能够替代 Cornucopia 的影子位图，自动在重分配时清零内存，或选择性通过写前读陷阱来强制初始化安全；与在重分配前执行清零操作的 Cornucopia 基线相比，不会产生额外的性能开销。

Conclusion: PoisonCap 在不牺牲性能的情况下增强了 CHERI 的时序安全性，实现了严格的释放后使用保护和初始化安全，为 CHERI 系统提供了一种更完善、高效的内存安全解决方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present PoisonCap: scalable temporal safety with strict use-after-free protection and initialisation safety for CHERI systems. Efficient memory safety is an increasing priority for programming languages, operating systems, and hardware designs, and CHERI is a leading hardware/software system that provides native spatial safety and a foundation for temporal memory safety. Cornucopia Reloaded, the current state-of-the-art CHERI temporal safety solution, provides use-after-reallocation safety instead of stronger use-after-free safety, and is not able to enforce initialisation safety. We show that a new 'poison' capability format can be used to enforce strict use-after-free and initialisation safety, and also to communicate memory state to the microarchitecture for efficient cache management of quarantined memory. We enable elegant delegation of memory poisoning privilege using capability bounds to allow nested allocators to enforce safety on their consumers without disturbing upstream allocators. PoisonCap can replace the Cornucopia shadow bitmap, and also automatically zeros memory on reallocation, or optionally traps on read-before-write to enforce initialisation safety. As a result, it incurs no fundamental overhead relative to a Cornucopia baseline that zeros before reallocation, strengthening CHERI temporal safety without performance overhead.

</details>


### [245] [FPGA-Accelerated Lock Management and Transaction Processing: Architecture, Optimization, and Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13398)
*Shien Zhu,Gustavo Alonso*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种硬件加速的数据库锁管理和事务处理系统，通过专用的锁代理和事务代理消除DRAM访问开销，在TPC-C基准测试上实现了高达51倍的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线事务处理(OLTP)业务不断增长，但基于CPU的OLTP锁服务效率低下。主要原因是大多数锁处于冷状态，锁代理必须频繁访问内存以检索锁详情来决定是否授予锁，这导致了严重的性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出了两个核心硬件组件：(1)低延迟锁代理，针对锁获取和释放请求进行优化，集成锁表以消除DRAM访问开销；(2)可扩展的事务代理，执行完整的事务生命周期。同时进行了架构设计、优化和设计空间探索。

Result: 在TPC-C基准测试中，相比CPU基线实现了高达51倍的事务吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 专用硬件加速的锁管理和事务处理系统能够有效解决CPU-based OLTP中的内存访问瓶颈问题，显著提升事务处理性能，证明了硬件加速在数据库系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Online Transaction Processing (OLTP) is a classic application with a growing business. CPU-based OLTP has low lock serving efficiency. The main reason is that most locks are cold, and the lock agent must issue frequent memory accesses to retrieve the lock details to determine whether to grant it. This motivates us to propose dedicated hardware-based lock agents with integrated lock tables to remove the DRAM access overhead.
  In this paper, we propose hardware-accelerated lock management and transaction processing for database systems. First, we propose a low-latency lock agent optimized for both lock acquiring and releasing requests. Second, we design a scalable transaction agent that executes the full transaction lifecycle. We present the architecture, optimizations, and design-space exploration of the proposed lock management and transaction processing system. The experiment results show up to 51X higher transaction throughput over the CPU baseline on the TPC-C benchmark.

</details>


### [246] [Reward-Weighted On-Policy Distillation with an Open Property-Equivalence Verifier for NL-to-SVA Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13501)
*Qingyun Zou,Yingze Li,Tianen Liu,Bingsheng He,Weng-Fai Wong*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出RWOPD方法，通过基于属性等价性的策略蒸馏，显著提升了LLM生成SystemVerilog断言的性能，刷新了SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于监督微调（SFT）的NL2SVA方法优化的是token级别的模仿而非属性等价性，导致模型在有界延迟和活性等规范上泛化能力差，仅能处理有限的模板。

Method: 提出Reward-Weighted On-Policy Distillation (RWOPD)。该方法对学生的采样输出进行SymbiYosys+Z3属性等价性检查（PEC）评分，并在验证通过的样本上应用基于验证器奖励权重的教师模型前向KL散度梯度，从而在保持密集监督的同时将学习目标锚定于属性等价行为。

Result: 将CodeV-SVA-14B蒸馏至Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct，在NL2SVA-Human和NL2SVA-Machine数据集的pass@1、pass@5和pass@10指标上均达到新的SOTA，超越了先前的专用模型及671B通用基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过将监督信号建立在形式化验证的属性等价性之上，RWOPD有效弥合了有界延迟和活性规范的泛化差距，为代码生成任务中结合形式化验证的模型优化提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLM-based generation of SystemVerilog Assertions (SVA) is often reported as nearing saturation, with the strongest specialized model reaching ${\sim}76\%$ accuracy on NL2SVA-Human. We show that this aggregate hides a temporal gap: models that appear strong overall still collapse to a few implication templates on bounded-delay and liveness specifications. The core issue is that the dominant recipe, supervised fine-tuning on NL/SVA pairs, optimizes token-level mimicry rather than the \emph{property equivalence} that defines SVA correctness. We introduce \emph{Reward-Weighted On-Policy Distillation} (RWOPD), an on-policy distillation method that samples student rollouts, scores them with an open SymbiYosys+Z3 Property-Equivalence Checker (PEC), and applies a verifier-reward-weighted forward-KL gradient from a frozen 14B teacher on verifier-passable rollouts. This keeps the supervision dense at every response token while grounding both selection and loss weight in property-equivalent behavior. RWOPD distills CodeV-SVA-14B into a Qwen2.5-Coder-7B-Instruct student that sets a new state of the art on NL2SVA-Human and NL2SVA-Machine across pass@1, pass@5, and pass@10, surpassing both specialized prior SOTA models and 671B general-purpose baselines.

</details>


### [247] [Efficient Implementation of an Adaptive Transformer Accelerator for Massive MIMO Outdoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13507)
*Ilayda Yaman,Sijia Cheng,Ove Edfors,Liang Liu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向5G大规模MIMO的自适应Transformer定位系统硬件加速器，通过利用波束-延迟信道的稀疏性实现了低于2.11ms的实时定位，在FPGA上实现约2倍加速且精度损失低于10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G大规模MIMO系统需要亚10毫秒级的实时定位能力，但Transformer模型的高计算复杂度难以满足实时性要求；信道传播特性中存在的稀疏性为硬件加速提供了优化空间，需要设计能够自适应利用这种稀疏性的硬件架构。

Method: 提出基于传播感知稀疏性的自适应Transformer硬件加速器：1) 利用波束-延迟信道表示的稀疏性，设计行级跳过机制去除低能量波束分量；2) 采用混合数据流架构结合输入/输出驻留执行模式；3) 设计异构向量处理引擎，包含并行处理单元和加法树；4) 引入轻量级运行时模型切换机制，通过单层感知机路由器的时序滤波输出实现专用模型间的稳定选择。

Result: 在Xilinx Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA上实现，基于真实大规模MIMO测量数据评估：峰值行稀疏度达65%，计算加速约2倍；相比浮点基线模型，平均定位精度损失低于10%；定位精度优于1.15m，推理延迟0.51-2.11ms，吞吐量达1961位置/秒。

Conclusion: 传播感知稀疏性、混合数据流执行和高效运行时模型切换机制能够实现可扩展、低延迟的自适应Transformer定位硬件加速，满足5G实时系统需求。

Abstract: We present the implementation of an adaptive Transformer-based localization system for 5G massive MIMO targeting sub-10ms real-time positioning. The design exploits propagation characteristics, where beam-delay channel representations exhibit sparsity, enabling a row-wise skipping mechanism that removes low-energy beam components with minimal control overhead. The contribution is focused on hardware realization of the model using a mixed dataflow architecture, combining input- and output-stationary execution, mapped onto a heterogeneous vector processing engine with parallel processing elements and adder trees for efficient matrix computation. Environment-dependent processing is supported through a lightweight runtime model-switching mechanism, where temporally filtered outputs of a single-layer perceptron router enable stable selection between specialized models with reduced latency. Implemented on a Xilinx Zynq UltraScale+ FPGA and evaluated on real-world massive MIMO measurements, the design achieves up to 65% row sparsity, yielding peak computational speedups of approximately 2x while limiting the average localization accuracy degradation to below 10%, relative to the floating-point baseline model. The accelerator attains below 1.15m localization accuracy across scenarios, with inference latency of 0.51-2.11ms and throughput of up to 1961 positions/s. These results demonstrate that propagation-aware sparsity, mixed dataflow execution, and efficient runtime model switching enable a scalable and low-latency hardware realization of adaptive Transformer-based localization for real-time 5G systems.

</details>


### [248] [Memristor Technologies for Dynamic Vision Sensors: A Critical Assessment and Research Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13699)
*Mohamad Yazan Sadoun,Edris Zaman Farsa,Sarah Sharif,Yaser Mike Banad*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本综述评估了事件驱动视觉传感器与存内模拟计算结合的Edge-AI部署潜力，发现六个应用领域中有一半仍处于理论预测阶段，现有硬件技术成熟度较低，并指出端到端DVS-忆阻器集成系统是该领域的关键开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Edge-AI部署受限于数据移动能耗瓶颈，事件驱动视觉传感器与存内模拟计算的结合有望将能效提升数个数量级，但缺乏区分已制造演示与预测系统的框架，且应用领域的实际进展不明晰。

Method: 采用证据分级综述方法，基于三范式架构分类法对六个应用领域（机器人、自动驾驶、AR/VR、监控、医学成像、物联网）进行调研，对比当前数字神经形态替代方案，评估技术成熟度等级。

Result: 六个应用领域中有一半完全依赖理论预测；现有硬件技术成熟度等级仅为2-5级；识别出端到端集成DVS-忆阻器系统为该领域的开放挑战，并提出了可测试的精度和功耗目标。

Conclusion: 事件驱动视觉传感器与存内模拟计算技术虽各自成熟，但集成系统仍处于早期阶段；实现端到端DVS-忆阻器系统是推动该领域发展的关键，需达成明确的精度与功耗目标以验证其优越性。

Abstract: Edge-AI deployment is bottlenecked by data-movement energy; pairing event-driven vision sensors with in-memory analog compute could lift that ceiling by orders of magnitude. Both technologies are individually mature; the framework distinguishing fabricated demonstrations from projected systems is missing. Of six application domains surveyed (robotics, autonomous vehicles, AR/VR, surveillance, medical imaging, IoT), half rest entirely on projection, and existing hardware sits at Technology Readiness Levels 2-5. This evidence-graded review applies a three-paradigm architectural taxonomy and benchmarks the gap against current digital neuromorphic alternatives. It identifies an end-to-end integrated DVS-memristor system as the field's open challenge, with testable accuracy and power targets.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [DelAC: A Multi-agent Reinforcement Learning of Team-Symmetric Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12555)
*Duan-Shin Lee,Yu-Hsiu Hung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文证明了团队对称博弈始终存在团队对称纳什均衡，并提出了一种基于Actor-Critic的多智能体强化学习算法，仿真表明其性能优于现有算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究团队对称博弈（$m\ge 2$个团队，团队内玩家身份对称且具有共同收益函数），旨在解决此类博弈中纳什均衡的存在性、计算及学习问题。

Method: 首先从理论上证明团队对称纳什均衡的存在性，然后将其建模为线性互补问题（LCP）并求解，最后提出一种基于Actor-Critic架构的多智能体强化学习算法。

Result: 证明了团队对称博弈总是存在团队对称纳什均衡；成功开发了求解该均衡的线性互补问题方法；仿真实验表明所提出的多智能体强化学习算法性能显著优于多种现有算法。

Conclusion: 团队对称博弈具有良好的理论性质（均衡存在性），可以通过线性互补方法进行有效计算，且所提出的强化学习算法为解决此类问题提供了高效的实践工具。

Abstract: In this paper we study team-symmetric games with $m\ge 2$ teams. Players within a team have symmetric identity and have a common payoff function. We show that team-symmetric games always have a team-symmetric Nash equilibrium. We develop and solve a linear complementarity problem of team-symmetric Nash equilibria. We propose an actor-critic based multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm for team-symmetric games. Through simulations, we show that this multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm performs much better than many existing algorithms.

</details>


### [250] [Synthesizing the Expert: A Validated Multimodal Dataset for Trustworthy AI-Assisted Swimming Coaching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12799)
*Ahmad Al-Kabbany,Esraa Kassem*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对游泳领域的结构化RAG系统合成框架，通过多智能体LLM架构从多模态知识库中生成高质量的问答数据集，以解决真实数据稀缺和标注成本高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI在游泳领域的应用日益广泛，但构建可靠的RAG系统面临真实数据稀缺、运动员生物识别数据的伦理限制以及专家标注成本高昂等挑战，亟需一种能够生成高质量合成数据的解决方案。

Method: 提出一种生成式框架，利用多智能体LLM架构，从生理数据、生理文献、运动学传感器数据和非结构化领域知识四个维度构建的多模态知识库中，合成高质量的"问题-上下文-答案"三元组数据集，并通过12条生理学合理性规则进行验证。

Result: 成功构建了包含1,864个经过验证的问答三元组的数据集（从1,914个候选草案中筛选得出），为游泳领域的AI应用提供了结构化的合成基准真值。

Conclusion: 该研究为水上运动领域的可信AI建立了基础基准，有望提高自动化教练系统的可靠性，并为Meta-Agent开发和运动员画像等未来研究方向铺平道路，有效弥合了原始数据工程与实践体育科学应用之间的差距。

Abstract: This research is primarily concerned with the critical problem of synthesizing a structured Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system for advanced AI applications in the domain of swimming. As the integration of Artificial Intelligence in sports science matures, its applications in swimming have become increasingly diverse, spanning from real-time technical coaching and talent scouting to comprehensive performance profiling and the dynamic personalization of training periodization. Within this landscape, RAG-based systems represent a pivotal advancement in Large Language Model (LLM) enhanced swimming analysis, as they allow for the grounding of generative outputs in authoritative domain knowledge, thereby ensuring the credibility of AI-generated advice, contextually and technically. Despite this potential, building robust RAG systems using only real-world aquatic data presents significant challenges, including ethical constraints regarding athlete biometrics, and the high cost of manual expert labeling. To address these barriers, we propose a novel generative framework that leverages a multimodal knowledge base gathered across four dimensions: physiological data, physiological literature, kinematic sensor data, and unstructured domain expertise. Our proposed framework utilizes a multi-agent LLM architecture to synthesize a high-fidelity dataset of 1,864 validated "Question-Context-Answer" triplets-drawn from 1,914 drafts evaluated against 12 physiological soundness rules. By providing a structured, synthetic ground truth, this work establishes a foundational benchmark for trustworthy AI in aquatics. The outcomes of this research promise to enhance the reliability of automated coaching and open a plethora of future directions in "Meta-Agent" development and athletic profiling, ultimately bridging the gap between raw data engineering and practical sports science application.

</details>


### [251] [Mechanism Plausibility in Generative Agent-Based Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12824)
*Patrick Zhao,David Huu Pham,Nicholas Vincent*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出了“机制合理性量表”，将模型生成充分性与机制合理性分离，为评估LLM-ABMs的解释能力提供四层级框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM已被用于ABM和社会模拟，但能力、预测与解释之间存在混淆；需要区分模型是否仅能重现现象，还是能解释现象如何产生。

Method: 整合LLM-ABM研究与当代科学哲学文献，操作化“合理性”概念，提出四层级评估框架，区分生成充分性与机制合理性。

Result: 构建了机制合理性量表，明确模型生成充分性（重现现象）与机制合理性（现象产生方式）的区分，并阐明预测模型与解释模型的不同作用。

Conclusion: 该量表帮助研究者更清晰地评估模型是否具备解释能力，为LLM-ABM研究的评估与定位提供理论依据。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate high-level diverse phenomena without explicitly programmed rules. This capability has led to their adoption within different agent-based models (ABMs) and social simulations. Recently, research has aim to test whether they are capable of generating different phenomena of interest, for example, human behavior on social media platforms or performance in game-theoretic scenarios.
  However, capability, prediction, and explanation are different -- drawing from the philosophy of science and mechanisms literature, \textit{explanation} requires showing, to some degree, how a phenomenon is produced by related organized entities and activities. For modelers, describing the characteristics of an experiment or whether a simulation provides progress in capability (or explanation), can be difficult without being grounded in potentially distant research areas.
  We integrate recent work on LLM-ABMs with contemporary philosophy of science literature and use it to operationalize a definition of `plausibility' in a four-level scale. Our scale separates the evaluation of a model's generative sufficiency (ability to reproduce a phenomenon) from its mechanistic plausibility (how the phenomenon could be produced), and clarifies the distinct roles of different models, such as predictive and explanatory ones. We introduce this as the Mechanism Plausibility Scale.

</details>


### [252] [ChipMATE: Multi-Agent Training via Reinforcement Learning for Enhanced RTL Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12857)
*Zhongkai Yu,Yichen Lin,Chenyang Zhou,Yuwei Zhang,Kun Zhou,Junxia Cui,Haotian Ye,Zhengding Hu,Zaifeng Pan,Ruiyi Wang,Yujie Zhao,Hejia Zhang,Jingbo Shang,Jishen Zhao,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出ChipMATE，首个自训练的多智能体RTL生成框架，通过Verilog与Python参考模型智能体的相互验证，在不依赖黄金测试平台的情况下，以4B和9B模型在VerilogEval V2上达到75.0%和80.1%的pass@1，超越所有现有自训练模型及1600B参数的DeepSeek V4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于API的RTL代码生成智能系统与工业实践存在根本性错位：假设生成时存在黄金测试平台、依赖闭源API与芯片厂商的物理隔离安全要求不兼容、无法在厂商专有RTL代码库上训练致使内部数据闲置；而近期自训练模型虽解决部署约束，但仍属忽略验证环节的单轮生成器。

Method: 受工业实践中独立编写的RTL模块与参考模型交叉比对以确保正确性的启发，构建Verilog智能体与Python参考模型智能体配对，使其在无黄金预言机的情况下相互验证输出；设计基于回溯的推理工作流以防错误跨轮次传播，并设计两阶段训练流水线：先单独训练各智能体以饱和其代码生成能力，再联合训练以提升协作效能；配套构建混合数据生成框架以产出64.4K高质量参考模型训练样本。

Result: ChipMATE在VerilogEval V2上以4B和9B基座模型分别达到75.0%和80.1%的pass@1，优于所有现有自训练模型，甚至超越1600B参数的DeepSeek V4；代码与模型权重已公开于https://github.com/zhongkaiyu/ChipMATE。

Conclusion: ChipMATE通过多智能体相互验证与两阶段训练，有效弥合了RTL生成研究与工业实践间的鸿沟，证明了自训练小模型在合理架构下可超越超大规模闭源模型，为在安全隔离环境中利用专有数据提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Existing API-based agentic systems for RTL code generation are fundamentally misaligned with industrial practice: they assume a golden testbench is available at generation time, rely on closed-source APIs incompatible with chip vendors' air-gapped security requirements, and cannot be trained on vendors' proprietary RTL codebases, leaving valuable internal data unused. Recent self-trained models address the deployment constraint but remain single-turn generators that overlook the critical role of verification in real industrial flows.
  To bridge these gaps, we present ChipMATE, the first self-trained multi-agent framework for RTL generation. Inspired by industrial practice where correctness emerges from cross-comparison between independently written RTL modules and reference models, ChipMATE pairs a Verilog agent with a Python reference-model agent that mutually verify each other's outputs without any golden oracle. We design a backtrack-based inference workflow to prevent error propagation across turns, and a two-stage training pipeline that first trains each agent individually to saturate its code-generation capability, then trains the team jointly to collaborate effectively. To support the training, we further build a hybrid data-generation framework that produces 64.4K high-quality reference model training samples. ChipMATE achieves 75.0\% and 80.1\% pass@1 on VerilogEval V2 with 4B and 9B base models, outperforming all existing self-trained models and even DeepSeek V4 with 1600B parameters. Our code and model weights are publicly available in https://github.com/zhongkaiyu/ChipMATE.

</details>


### [253] [SHM-Agents: A Generalist-Specialist Integrated Agent System for Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12916)
*Yuequan Bao,Xing Li,Huabin Sun,Dawei Liu,Yuxuan Tian,Haiyang Hu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了SHM-Agents系统，将大语言模型的推理规划能力与专业算法的问题解决能力相结合，通过自然语言实现了结构健康监测任务的端到端执行，有效降低了技术门槛并提升了系统的通用性与灵活性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构健康监测领域的专业算法虽然有效，但在实际工程应用中面临实施门槛高、互操作性有限以及训练流程复杂等挑战，亟需一种能够简化部署流程并提升系统通用性的解决方案。

Method: 提出了SHM-Agents通用-专用智能体系统，该系统整合了大语言模型的推理与规划能力以及专业算法的问题解决优势。系统采用模块化设计，支持深度学习预训练以简化部署流程，能够通过自然语言接口实现单一及组合SHM任务的端到端执行。

Result: 在某大跨度斜拉桥上的实验表明，SHM-Agents能够准确且高效地执行多种SHM任务，涵盖数据异常诊断与恢复、信号处理、统计分析、模态识别、损伤识别、有限元模型修正、车辆荷载建模、响应计算、可靠性评估、疲劳估算以及桥梁知识问答等。

Conclusion: SHM-Agents成功实现了大语言模型与专业算法的协同集成，显著降低了SHM任务的实施难度，提升了系统的互操作性与扩展性，为工程领域的结构健康监测提供了一种高效、灵活且易于部署的智能化解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence is increasingly used to simplify complex tasks. In engineering applications of structural health monitoring (SHM), existing specialized algorithms, while effective, often face high implementation barriers, limited interoperability and complex training procedures. To overcome these challenges, this paper proposes SHM-Agents, a generalist-specialist agent system that integrates the reasoning and planning abilities of large language models with the problem-solving strengths of specialized algorithms. SHM-Agents enables end-to-end execution of single and combined SHM tasks via natural language, supports deep learning pre-training to simplify deployment and allows flexible expansion through a modular design. Experiments on a long-span cable-stayed bridge show that SHM-Agents can accurately and efficiently perform diverse SHM tasks, including data anomaly diagnosis and recovery, signal processing, statistical analysis, modal identification, damage identification, finite element model updating, vehicle load modeling, response calculation, reliability assessment, fatigue estimation and bridge knowledge Q\&A.

</details>


### [254] [Embodied Multi-Agent Coordination by Aligning World Models Through Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12920)
*Vardhan Dongre,Dilek Hakkani-Tür*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文扩展了PARTNR协作机器人基准，引入自然语言对话通道，并提出世界模型对齐评估框架。实验发现对话虽然显著减少了动作冲突（40-83个百分点），但却降低了任务成功率，揭示了当前LLM智能体在通过对话实现真实世界模型对齐方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观察环境中，具身智能体之间的有效协作不仅需要在共享环境中行动，更需要基于各自世界模型演变的沟通。虽然理论上沟通可以弥合部分观察带来的协调困难，但当前基于LLM的智能体是否真正实现了有效的沟通能力尚不清楚，特别是对话是否导致了真实的世界模型对齐而非表面协调。

Method: 1. 扩展PARTNR基准，为两个具有部分可观察性的智能体添加自然语言对话通道；2. 提出世界模型对齐的评估框架，基于每智能体的世界图定义三个指标：观察收敛性（私有世界模型是否随时间对齐？）、信息新颖性（消息是否传达了合作伙伴缺少的信息？）和信念敏感消息传递（智能体是否建模了合作伙伴的知识？）。

Result: 在三个LLM上的实验表明：1. 对话减少了40至83个百分点的动作冲突；2. 但相对于无声协调，对话反而降低了任务成功率；3. 通过所提出的指标，成功刻画了表面协调与真实世界模型对齐之间的差距，并确定了当前模型在该谱系上的位置。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体虽然能够通过对话减少行为冲突，但未能实现真正的世界模型对齐，在沟通有效性方面存在显著缺陷。研究揭示了表面协调与深度协作之间的差距，为未来改进智能体协作能力指明了方向。

Abstract: Effective collaboration between embodied agents requires more than acting in a shared environment; it demands communication grounded in each agent's evolving understanding of the world. When agents can only partially observe their surroundings, coordination without communication is provably hard, but communication can, in principle, bridge this gap by allowing agents to share observations and align their world models. In this work, we examine whether LLM-based embodied agents actually realize the ability to communicate. We extend PARTNR, a benchmark for collaborative household robotics, with a natural-language dialogue channel that enables two agents with partial observability to communicate during task execution. To evaluate whether dialogue leads to genuine world-model alignment rather than superficial coordination, we propose a framework for measuring world-model alignment defined over per-agent world graphs: observation convergence (do private world models align over time?), information novelty (do messages convey what the partner lacks?), and belief-sensitive messaging (do agents model what their partner knows?). Our experiments across three LLMs reveal that dialogue reduces action conflicts 40 to 83 percentage points but degrades task success relative to silent coordination. Using our metrics, we characterize the gap between superficial coordination and genuine world-model alignment, and identify where current models fall on this spectrum.

</details>


### [255] [Conveyor Parcel Routing with Order-Contiguous Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13035)
*Takuro Kato,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了DOPP算法，用于解决仓库物流中包裹输送的在线多智能体路径规划问题，同时满足订单连续性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仓库物流场景中，从自动化存储系统输出的包裹需要通过输送网络路由到工作站。除了避免碰撞外，实际运营还有一个关键需求：订单连续到达，即同一订单的包裹必须在到达序列中连续到达，以减少下游重新分拣的工作量。现有方法无法有效处理这一约束。

Method: 提出双排序优先规划算法(DOPP)，这是一个完整的多项式时间算法，采用三层结构：(i)搜索订单级到达序列，(ii)细化智能体级优先级，(iii)通过优先规划合成可行解。该算法将问题形式化为在线订单连续多智能体路径规划(online MAPF-OC)，其中智能体（即包裹）随时间出现并在交付时退出。

Result: 在包括源自实际仓库的各种输送网络布局上进行的实验表明，DOPP具有良好的实际可扩展性，能够在紧凑的时间预算内生成高质量的计划方案。

Conclusion: DOPP算法能够有效解决具有订单连续性约束的在线多智能体路径规划问题，为仓库物流中的包裹路由提供了实用的解决方案，在保证解的质量的同时满足实时性要求。

Abstract: In warehouse logistics, parcels released from the outfeed of an automated storage system must be routed through conveyor networks to workstations. Beyond collision avoidance, practical operations impose an additional requirement of order-contiguous arrivals: at each delivery point, parcels belonging to the same order must arrive as a consecutive block in the arrival sequence to reduce downstream re-sorting effort. We formalize this problem as online multi-agent path finding with order-contiguity (online MAPF-OC), where agents (i.e., parcels) appear over time and exit upon delivery. To efficiently solve online MAPF-OC, we propose Dual-Ordering Prioritized Planning (DOPP), a complete polynomial-time algorithm with a three-level structure that (i) searches order-level arrival sequences, (ii) refines agent-level priorities, and (iii) synthesizes feasible solutions via prioritized planning. Experiments on various conveyor-network layouts, including those derived from actual warehouses, demonstrate DOPP's practical scalability and ability to generate high-quality plans within tight time budgets.

</details>


### [256] [Counterfactual Reasoning for Causal Responsibility Attribution in Probabilistic Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13077)
*Chunyan Mu,Muhammad Najib*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Shapley值的反向反事实责任分配方法，用于并发随机多人博弈中的多智能体系统，并建立了支持验证和策略推理的形式化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 责任分配（确定智能体对结果的责任程度）是多智能体系统设计与分析中的基本挑战，现有方法缺乏系统化的量化机制。

Method: 将多智能体系统建模为并发随机多人博弈，引入反向反事实责任概念量化智能体对策略结果的责任，并采用Shapley值进行责任分配，以满足公平性和一致性等关键属性。基于此，提出支持验证和策略推理的形式化框架，并采用纳什均衡计算权衡责任与期望奖励的稳定策略。

Result: 证明了基于Shapley值的责任分配方法满足公平性和一致性等关键属性，建立了支持验证和策略推理的形式化框架，并通过纳什均衡演示了智能体如何在责任与期望奖励之间权衡以获得稳定策略。

Conclusion: 该研究为多智能体系统中的责任分配提供了理论基础和实用框架，有助于设计更具问责性的多智能体系统。

Abstract: Responsibility allocation -- determining the extent to which agents are accountable for outcomes -- is a fundamental challenge in the design and analysis of multi-agent systems. In this work, we model such systems as concurrent stochastic multi-player games and introduce a notion of retrospective (backward) counterfactual responsibility, which quantifies an agent's accountability for outcomes resulting from a given strategy profile. To allocate responsibility among agents, we utilise the Shapley value and formally show that this method satisfies key desirable properties, including fairness and consistency. Building on this foundation, we propose a formal framework that supports both verification and strategic reasoning in responsibility-aware multi-agent systems. Furthermore, by adopting Nash equilibrium as the solution concept, we demonstrate how to compute stable strategy profiles in which agents trade off responsibility against expected reward.

</details>


### [257] [A Multi-Agent Orchestration Framework for Venture Capital Due Diligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13110)
*Grigorios Alexandrou,Katerina Pramatari*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向风险投资尽职调查与市场分析的自动化多智能体框架，通过事件驱动架构、实时网络检索与LLM协同，重点解决了从希腊商业登记处自动提取官方财务数据的问题，并通过结构化回退机制有效抑制了金融场景下的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险投资领域的尽职调查和市场分析通常涉及大量非结构化数据的收集与整理，流程繁琐且效率低下。同时，在金融语境下，大型语言模型（LLM）容易产生幻觉，生成未经验证的数据，这可能导致严重的决策风险。因此，迫切需要一种能够自动整合数据并确保信息准确性的自动化框架。

Method: 该系统采用事件驱动的编排架构，结合大型语言模型（LLM）与实时网络检索技术。核心方法包括：1. 设计程序化提取管道，逆向工程希腊商业登记处（Γ.E.MH.）的前后端通信，查询动态端点以检索官方财务文件；2. 使用布局感知的OCR提取器解析这些文件；3. 引入结构化回退机制，当数据缺失时显式标记，而非由模型生成未经验证的数值，从而从架构层面抑制幻觉。

Result: 成功构建了一个全自动化的多智能体系统，能够将非结构化数据合成为结构化的投资情报。该系统实现了从官方渠道自动获取并解析财务数据，有效解决了特定数据源的结构化提取难题。结构化回退机制确保了数据的可追溯性与真实性，避免了模型编造财务数据的风险。

Conclusion: 该框架证明了结合LLM与专用数据提取管道在金融自动化分析中的可行性，通过显式的数据验证与回退机制，显著提升了自动化投资分析的可信度与实用性。所有工作流资源的公开亦为相关研究的复现提供了便利。

Abstract: We present a fully automated multi-agent framework for corporate due diligence and market analysis in venture capital. The system runs on an event-driven orchestration architecture, combining Large Language Models (LLMs) with real-time web retrieval to synthesize unstructured data into structured investment intelligence. A central technical contribution is a programmatic extraction pipeline that reverse-engineers the frontend-to-backend communication of the Greek Business Registry ($Γ$.E.MH.), querying dynamic endpoints to retrieve official financial filings that are then parsed using a layout-aware OCR extractor. A structural fallback mechanism explicitly flags data absence rather than generating unverified figures, directly targeting hallucination in financial contexts. All workflow artifacts are publicly available to support replication.

</details>


### [258] [When Does Hierarchy Help? Benchmarking Agent Coordination in Event-Driven Industrial Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13172)
*Ziqi Wang,Yuhao Yang,Zhiwei Ling,Wenzhuo Qian,Hailiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了DESBench基准，用于评估分层事件驱动调度中的智能体协调，揭示了四种协调范式（集中式、分层式、异构式和整体式）在复杂动态环境中的结构性权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体系统基准主要评估弱耦合环境下的任务完成情况，缺乏对具有层次结构和耦合约束的共享动态系统中协调机制的研究，导致不同协调范式的适用条件这一重要问题未被充分探索。

Method: 构建了基于工业调度的共享离散事件驱动环境的DESBench基准，捕捉多时间尺度决策、部分可观测性和动态耦合约束特征，定义了有效性、约束对齐、协调效率和鲁棒性等评估指标，并针对集中式、分层式、异构式和整体式四种代表性协调范式进行受控评估。

Result: 发现了明确的协调权衡：集中式协调鲁棒且通信高效但可扩展性差；分层式协调通过分解提高效率但存在跨层对齐问题；异构式协调灵活但通信开销大；整体式协调约束满足好但失去全局鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 协调设计从根本上塑造了复杂环境中智能体系统的行为，揭示了仅靠结果指标无法捕捉的结构性权衡，强调了未来多智能体系统研究中需要更具适应性、原则性和动态性的协调机制。

Abstract: Recent advances in agent and multi-agent systems have shown strong performance on tool use, reasoning, and collaborative tasks. However, existing benchmarks mostly evaluate task completion in weakly coupled environments, and provide limited support for studying coordination in shared, dynamically evolving systems with hierarchy and coupled constraints. This leaves an important question underexplored: when do different coordination paradigms succeed or fail? We introduce Distributed Event-driven Scheduling Benchmark (DESBench), a benchmark for evaluating agent coordination in hierarchical event-driven scheduling. Built on a shared discrete-event driven environment in industrial scheduling, our benchmark captures multi-timescale decision making, partial observability, and dynamically coupled constraints. We define tasks and metrics that evaluate effectiveness, constraint alignment, coordination efficiency, and robustness, and focus on four representative coordination paradigms: centralized, hierarchical, heterarchical, and holonic. These paradigms correspond to distinct mechanisms of information flow, decision authority, and conflict resolution. Our controlled evaluations reveal clear coordination trade-offs: centralized coordination is robust and communication-efficient but scales poorly with difficulty; hierarchical coordination improves efficiency through decomposition but suffers from cross-level misalignment; heterarchical coordination is flexible but communication-heavy; and holonic coordination satisfies constraints well but loses global robustness. These findings demonstrate that coordination design fundamentally shapes agent system behavior in complex environments, revealing structural trade-offs that cannot be captured by outcome metrics alone and underscoring the imperative for more adaptive, principled, and dynamic coordination mechanisms in future MAS research.

</details>


### [259] [Constitutional Governance in Metric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13362)
*Ehud Shapiro,Nimrod Talmon*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个在度量空间中的宪法治理框架，将聚合、协商、修正和共识等阶段整合到一个多项式时间的过程中，解决了现有社会选择理论中各阶段孤立且关键聚合问题NP难的困境，为数字社区和组织提供了完整的民主治理解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算社会选择和算法决策理论虽然提供了丰富的聚合理论，但缺乏端到端的多项式时间过程来实现平等主义自治。现有研究将聚合、协商、修正和共识等阶段孤立处理，且关键的度量空间聚合器通常是NP难的，这阻碍了实际的自治应用。

Method: 提出度量空间中的宪法治理框架：宪法为每个可修正组件分配度量空间、聚合规则和绝对多数阈值；成员提交理想元素（投票和个人提案）；成员可提交获得绝对多数公共支持的公开提案（来源于联盟协商、优化或AI调解）；宪法规则根据得分在提案和现状之间做出选择，相同规则也可用于修改宪法本身。以广义中位数作为工作规则进行实例化。

Result: 建立了框架层面的理论保证；证明不存在误报能弱占优真诚投票；研究了最佳峰值与无约束最优之间的妥协差距——在一维情况下为零，在一般情况下有界，通过简单启发式方法在模拟中缩小；在七个典型场景中实例化框架；在附录中提出均值作为功利主义替代方案。

Conclusion: 通过统一度量空间聚合、现实感知社会选择、绝对多数修正、宪法共识、协商联盟形成和AI调解，该工作为数字社区和组织的宪法民主治理提供了一个全面的解决方案，实现了从理论到实践的完整治理过程。

Abstract: Computational social choice and algorithmic decision theory offer rich aggregation theory but no end-to-end, polynomial-time process for egalitarian self-governance: prior work treats aggregation, deliberation, amendment, and consensus in isolation, and key metric-space aggregators are NP-hard. We propose constitutional governance in metric spaces, integrating these stages into one polynomial-time process. The constitution assigns, per amendable component, a metric space, aggregation rule, and supermajority threshold. Each member submits an ideal element -- both vote and personal proposal. Any member may then submit a public proposal carrying supermajority public support under the revealed votes -- sourced from coalition deliberation, optimization, or AI mediation. The constitutional rule scores proposals against the status quo, adopting the supported proposal of positive maximal score (else retaining the status quo); the same rule, possibly with a higher threshold, amends the constitution itself. We develop the generalised median as the worked rule, establish framework-level guarantees, prove no misreport weakly dominates sincere voting, and study the compromise gap between best peak and unconstrained optimum -- zero in one dimension, bounded in general, narrowed in simulation by a simple heuristic. We instantiate the framework on seven canonical settings; the mean appears as a utilitarian alternative in the appendix. By unifying metric-space aggregation, reality-aware social choice, supermajority amendment, constitutional consensus, deliberative coalition formation, and AI mediation, this work delivers a comprehensive solution to the constitutional democratic governance of digital communities and organisations.

</details>


### [260] [EconAI: Dynamic Persona Evolution and Memory-Aware Agents in Evolving Economic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13762)
*Annie Liu,Zane Cao,Lang Chen,Zongxin Xu,Zigan Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了EconAI框架，这是首个在统一框架下模拟宏观/微观经济环境及交互的LLM驱动系统。通过引入经济情绪指数、记忆加权和动态决策机制，该框架解决了传统方法无法平衡短期优化与长期战略规划的问题，实现了更拟人化的经济智能体决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型（LLM）的经济模拟框架难以捕捉短期优化与长期战略规划之间的相互作用。传统方法依赖静态数据驱动预测，无法纳入受经济情绪、市场波动和个人目标影响的适应性行为，导致模拟不够真实和全面。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的EconAI框架，核心机制包括：1) 经济情绪索引（ESI）以量化经济信念；2) 记忆加权机制以调整历史数据的影响；3) 动态决策机制以链接工作与消费行为。该框架使智能体能够根据市场信号和长期目标动态调整行动。

Result: 实证评估表明，EconAI提高了经济响应的稳定性，更好地复现了现实世界的就业-消费周期，并增强了整体决策的鲁棒性。实验证明该框架能够实现更接近人类的决策过程。

Conclusion: EconAI框架的成功开发标志着向更现实、更具适应性的经济智能体模拟迈出了关键一步，为未来经济模拟研究提供了新的方向和工具。

Abstract: The integration of large language models (LLMs) in economic simulations has significantly enhanced agent-based modeling, yet existing frameworks struggle to capture the interplay between short-term optimization and long-term strategic planning. Conventional approaches rely on static data-driven predictions, failing to incorporate adaptive behaviors influenced by economic sentiment, market volatility, and individual goals. To address these limitations, we introduce a novel EconAI framework, incorporating economic sentiment indexing (ESI), memory weighting, and dynamic decision-making mechanisms. By quantifying economic belief, adjusting historical data influence, and linking work-consumption behaviors, EconAI achieves a more human-like decision process, where agents adapt their actions based on both market signals and long-term objectives. It is the first LLM-powered simulation system that can simulate the macro/microeconomic environment and interactions in a unified framework. Empirical evaluations show that EconAI improves stability in economic responses, better replicates real-world employment-consumption cycles, and enhances overall decision robustness. This advancement marks a crucial step towards more realistic, adaptive economic agent simulations.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [261] [Efficient and Portable Support for Overdecomposition on Distributed Memory GPGPU Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12734)
*Aditya Bhosale,Anant Jain,Shourya Goel,Ritvik Rao,Peddoju Sateesh Kumar,Laxmikant Kale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对过分解技术在GPGPU环境下面临的开销挑战，开发了新技术和软件，证明了该技术可以在不同GPU厂商和互连网络组合上高效支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过分解技术在并行编程中已被广泛应用，但在GPGPU成为主流计算组件后，该范式面临实际和感知上的挑战，特别是过分区导致的多对象分配到同一GPU设备所带来的更高开销问题，以及可移植性问题。

Method: 开发了新的技术和软件，以解决GPU环境下过分解的开销问题，并确保跨不同GPU厂商类型和互连网络的兼容性。

Result: 成功展示了过分解技术可以在不同GPU厂商类型和互连网络的组合上高效且富有成效地得到支持，解决了开销和可移植性问题。

Conclusion: 过分解技术不仅适用于传统并行编程环境，经过适当的技术优化后，同样可以在异构GPU计算环境中高效运行，具有重要的实用价值和推广前景。

Abstract: Overdecomposition has emerged as a powerful and sometimes essential technique in parallel programming. Many application domains or frameworks, including those based on adaptive mesh refinements, or tree codes use it. Charm++ is a parallel programming system which has demonstrated the utility of overdecomposition for many applications and in multiple contexts. However, the emergence of GPGPUs as a dominant compute component has created some real and perceived challenges for this paradigm, especially regarding the higher overhead brought about by overpartitioning -- having multiple objects assigned to the same GPGPU device. We address this issue as well as the issue of portability by developing techniques and software that demonstrate that overdecomposition can be efficiently and productively supported on combinations of GPU vendor types, and interconnection networks.

</details>


### [262] [The Distributed Complexity Landscape on Trees Depends on the Knowledge About the Network Size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12787)
*Alkida Balliu,Sebastian Brandt,Fabian Kuhn,Dennis Olivetti,Timothé Picavet,Gustav Schmid*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了LOCAL模型中节点对网络规模n的了解程度如何影响局部可检查标记问题(LCLs)的理论。结果表明：若节点不知道n或仅知道n的多项式上界，即使在树上，LCL的理论也会发生显著变化——随机性在更多情况下有帮助，某些问题具有非自然的复杂度，某些下界甚至依赖于Ω的定义方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算研究者在LOCAL模型下研究分布式图问题时，对节点是否知道网络规模n的假设存在差异：有的假设节点知道精确的n，有的仅知道n的多项式上界，有的完全不知道。已有关于LCL问题的复杂性结果（如自动加速定理）从根本上依赖于节点知道n这一假设。本文旨在探明这种假设差异是仅具有技术性影响，还是会从根本上改变LCL的理论。

Method: 本文理论性地分析了当节点对网络规模n不知情或仅知道多项式上界时，LCL问题的复杂性景观如何变化。研究聚焦于有界度树这一特殊图类，将其与节点已知n情况下的已知结果进行对比分析，考察问题分类、复杂度类别及随机性与确定性算法的关系。

Result: 在节点不知道n或仅知道n的多项式上界的情况下：(1)LCL问题的基本分类保持不变；(2)复杂性景观变得更加复杂：随机性在更多问题中能提供帮助；(3)某些问题具有非常不自然的复杂度；(4)某些问题的下界依赖于Ω符号的具体定义方式。这与节点已知n情况下（随机算法可自动加速为确定性算法）形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 节点是否知道网络规模n这一假设并非仅具技术性意义，而是从根本上影响LCL理论。即使在结构简单的有界度树上，当节点不知道n时，LCL的复杂性景观比已知n情况复杂得多。这一发现对理解分布式算法中知识假设的重要性具有重要意义。

Abstract: One of the central models in distributed computing is Linial's LOCAL model [SIAM J. Comp. 1992]. Over time, researchers have studied distributed graph problems in the LOCAL model under slightly different assumptions, such as whether nodes know the exact network size $n$, only a polynomial upper bound on $n$, or nothing at all. We ask whether these differences are merely technical or fundamentally affect the theory of Locally Checkable Labelings (LCLs), one of the most studied problem classes.
  LCLs are graph problems whose valid solutions can be characterized by a finite set of allowed constant-radius neighborhoods. Since their introduction by Naor and Stockmeyer [FOCS 1995], they have become central in distributed computing, and the last decade has seen major progress in understanding their complexity. For example, Chang, Kopelowitz, and Pettie [FOCS 2016] showed that the randomized complexity of any LCL on $n$-node graphs is at least its deterministic complexity on $\sqrt{\log n}$-node graphs. Later, Chang and Pettie [FOCS 2017] showed that any randomized $n^{o(1)}$-round algorithm for LCLs on bounded-degree trees can be turned into a deterministic $O(\log n)$-round algorithm. Then, Balliu et al. [STOC 2018] showed that such automatic speedups are impossible for general bounded-degree graphs. However, these results fundamentally rely on nodes knowing $n$. How much does this assumption affect the theory of LCLs?
  Our work shows that if nodes are oblivious to $n$, or know only a polynomial upper bound on it, then even on trees, the theory of LCLs changes significantly. While the fundamental classification of problems remains the same, we show the landscape becomes much more complex: for example, for LCLs, randomness helps in more cases; some problems have very unnatural complexities; and some have a lower bound that depends on which definition of $Ω$ we use!

</details>


### [263] [Communication Efficient Byzantine Agreement with Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12935)
*Muhammad Ayaz Dzulfikar,Seth Gilbert*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文显著降低了带有分类预测的拜占庭协议算法的通信复杂度：非认证算法达到$\tilde{O}(n^{2.5})$，认证算法达到最优$O(n^2\kappa)$，打破了先前$\Omega(n^3)$比特的通信下界，且轮复杂度对任意预测错误数均最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有带分类预测的拜占庭协议算法存在通信开销过高（$\Omega(n^3)$比特）或容错能力有限的问题，且似乎必须共享大量预测位才能容忍较多错误预测。本文旨在打破这一认知，证明高通信量和预测共享并非必要。

Method: 提出新的带分类预测的拜占庭协议算法：非认证算法实现$\tilde{O}(n^{2.5})$通信复杂度；结合认证机制，设计出通信复杂度为$O(n^2\kappa)$的认证算法。两者均针对任意预测错误数量保持最优轮复杂度。

Result: 成功将通信复杂度从$\Omega(n^3)$比特大幅降低，非认证算法达到$\tilde{O}(n^{2.5})$，认证算法达到理论最优的$O(n^2\kappa)$，同时保持对任意预测错误数的最优轮复杂度。

Conclusion: 证明了高通信复杂度和大量预测共享并非带预测拜占庭协议的内禀属性，通过算法设计可在显著降低通信开销的同时保持最优轮复杂度，为该领域提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In Byzantine agreement with predictions each process begins with an input value and some (unreliable) prediction bits. Recently, it has been shown that with \emph{classification predictions} -- where the predictions predict each process to be honest or faulty -- Byzantine agreement can be completed more quickly than without predictions, circumventing the traditional $Ω(f)$ round lower bound. However, existing algorithms either handle limited prediction errors or send too many messages. Moreover, they all exchange $Ω(n^3)$ bits -- enough to allow the processes to approximately agree on the classifications. In fact, it almost seemed necessary to share a significant number of prediction bits if one wanted to tolerate a high number of incorrect predictions.
  In this paper, we show that this high level of communication (and sharing of predictions) is not inherent by developing an unauthenticated algorithm with $\tilde{O}(n^{2.5})$ communication complexity. Furthermore, with authentication, we give an algorithm with optimal $O(n^2κ)$ communication complexity (where $κ$ is a security parameter). All of our results have optimal round complexity for any number of errors in the predictions.

</details>


### [264] [Comparing the Performance of Heterogeneous Conjugate Gradient and Cholesky Solvers on Various Hardware Using SYCL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13209)
*Tim Thüring,Alexander Strack,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于SYCL的多厂商异构CG方法和Cholesky分解实现，通过同时利用CPU和GPU，在大规模矩阵求解中相比纯GPU实现最高提速32%，且跨NVIDIA、AMD、Intel平台均表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在系统辨识等实际应用中，大规模对称正定矩阵线性系统求解需求普遍，现有GPU单设备方案未能充分利用现代HPC系统中强大的CPU资源，限制了计算性能的进一步提升。

Method: 基于SYCL标准，设计并实现了多厂商支持的异构CG迭代求解器和Cholesky直接分解器，实现CPU与GPU协同计算；与传统纯GPU同构方案进行系统性性能对比实验。

Result: 在大规模矩阵测试中，异构CG方法比纯GPU实现提速高达32%，Cholesky分解提速高达29%；跨NVIDIA、AMD、Intel平台，异构Cholesky分解均实现至少12%的性能提升。

Conclusion: 异构协同计算策略能有效挖掘现代HPC系统的硬件潜能，在跨厂商异构平台下显著提升大规模对称正定矩阵求解性能，证明了所提方法的实用性和可移植性。

Abstract: Many important real-world applications, such as System Identification with Gaussian Processes, involve solving linear systems with symmetric positive-definite matrices.
