The iterative CG method and direct solvers based on the Cholesky decomposition are two popular methods that can be applied in this case. Since often very large systems have to be solved when dealing with such real-world scenarios, GPUs are commonly used to accelerate the computations. However, homogeneous approaches that only leverage the GPU in the system do not take full advantage of the often powerful CPUs located in modern HPC systems. In this work, we present multi-vendor, heterogeneous implementations of the CG method and the Cholesky decomposition that leverage the CPU and GPU of a heterogeneous system simultaneously using SYCL. Furthermore, we compare their runtime behavior to traditional, homogeneous approaches. The results show that for large matrices, our heterogeneous implementation is up to 32 percent faster for the CG method and up to 29 percent faster for the Cholesky decomposition compared to the corresponding GPU-only implementations. In addition, for large matrices, our heterogeneous implementation of the Cholesky decomposition can achieve at least 12 percent faster runtimes across several systems with GPUs from NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel.

</details>


### [265] [PipeSD: An Efficient Cloud-Edge Collaborative Pipeline Inference Framework with Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13319)
*Yunhe Han,Yunqi Gao,Bing Hu,Mahdi Boloursaz Mashhadi,Yitong Duan,Pei Xiao,Yanfeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PipeSD提出了一个云边协同推测解码框架，通过流水线调度和双重阈值触发机制，实现了1.16x-2.16x的加速和14.3%-25.3%的能耗降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的云边协同推测解码框架存在两个主要限制：（1）顺序的token生成和通信导致资源利用率低；（2）非自回归验证（NAV）触发机制不够灵活，会导致过早验证或昂贵的回滚操作。这些问题限制了推理效率的提升。

Method: 提出PipeSD框架，包含两个核心创新：1）基于动态规划优化的token批次流水线调度机制，实现token生成与通信的重叠；2）双重阈值NAV触发机制配合轻量级贝叶斯优化自动调优器，提高验证灵活性。系统基于llama-cpp-python、PyTorch和FastAPI实现。

Result: 在真实云边测试平台上，使用两组草稿-目标模型对在四种场景下进行评估。PipeSD在所有场景下均优于最新基线方法，实现了1.16x-2.16x的加速，能耗降低14.3%-25.3%。

Conclusion: PipeSD成功解决了现有云边协同推测解码框架的资源利用率和验证灵活性问题，为LLM推理加速提供了一个高效的解决方案，在性能和能效方面都取得了显著提升。

Abstract: Speculative decoding can significantly accelerate LLM inference, especially given that its cloud-edge collaborative deployment offers cloud workload offloading, offline robustness, and privacy enhancement. However, existing collaborative inference frameworks with speculative decoding are constrained by (i) sequential token generation and communication with low resource utilization, and (ii) inflexible cloud non-autoregressive verification (NAV) triggering that induces premature verification or costly rollbacks. In this paper, we propose PipeSD, an efficient cloud-edge collaborative pipeline inference framework with speculative decoding. PipeSD overlaps token generation and communication by a token-batch pipeline scheduling mechanism optimized by dynamic programming, and improves verification flexibility through a dual-threshold NAV triggering mechanism with a lightweight Bayesian optimization autotuner. We implement PipeSD using llama-cpp-python, PyTorch, and FastAPI, and evaluate it on a real-world cloud-edge testbed with two draft-target model pairs across four scenarios. Results show that PipeSD consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving 1.16x-2.16x speedup and reducing energy consumption by 14.3%-25.3%.

</details>


### [266] [TurboGR: An Accelerated Training System for Large-Scale Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13433)
*Huichao Chai,Zhixin Wu,Xuemiao Li,Shiqing Fan,Hengfeng Wang,Maojun Peng,Lu Xu,Yaoyuan Wang,Yibo Jin,Wei Guo,Yongxiang Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向昇腾NPU的生成式推荐训练系统\model，通过算子融合、负载均衡、分布式通信优化和负采样优化，解决了稀疏不规则计算与NPU密集计算架构不匹配的问题，实现了54.71%的MFU和近线性扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐（GR）作为一种新兴范式，展现出随模型规模和数据量增加而系统性提升的 Scaling-law 特性。然而，在昇腾NPU上大规模部署GR面临系统级挑战：缺乏针对不规则（Jagged）算子的高性能实现，以及稀疏原语与NPU面向密集计算的设计之间存在架构失配。

Method: 提出三个核心创新：(i) 昇腾亲和的不规则算子加速，通过融合算子消除填充冗余，利用动态负载均衡将设备间不平衡度从47%降至2.4%；(ii) 分布式通信优化，包括分层稀疏并行、具有收敛保证的半异步训练，以及维持94% NPU利用率的细粒度流水线编排；(iii) 负采样优化，通过异步卸载、感知不规则性的FP16量化和批次内logit共享，在不增加嵌入查找的情况下扩展有效负样本空间。

Result: 在KuaiRand-27K数据集上验证，\model支持高达0.2B参数规模的训练，实现了54.71%的模型算力利用率（MFU），并具备0.97的近线性扩展性。

Conclusion: \model成功解决了生成式推荐在昇腾NPU上部署的系统瓶颈，证明了通过硬件亲和的系统优化，可以在非传统密集计算架构上高效训练稀疏、不规则的推荐模型。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising paradigm that replaces fragmented, scenario-specific architectures with unified Transformer-based models, exhibiting scaling-law behavior where recommendation quality improves systematically with increased model capacity and training data. However, deploying GR at scale on Ascend NPUs faces fundamental system-level challenges. These challenges are further exacerbated on Ascend NPUs due to the absence of high-performance implementations for jagged operators and the architectural mismatch between irregular sparse primitives and NPU's dense-computation-optimized design. In this paper, we present \model, an Ascend-affinity training system for generative recommendation that systematically addresses these bottlenecks through three core innovations: (i) Ascend-affinity jagged acceleration, including fusion operators that eliminate padding redundancy and dynamic load balancing that reduces inter-device imbalance from 47\% to 2.4\%; (ii) distributed communication optimization, comprising hierarchical sparse parallelism, semi-asynchronous training with proven convergence guarantees, and fine-grained pipeline orchestration that sustains 94\% NPU utilization; and (iii) negative sampling optimization via asynchronous offloading, jaggedness-aware FP16 quantization, and intra-batch logit sharing that expand the effective negative space without additional embedding lookups. Evaluated on the KuaiRand-27K dataset, \model supports training at up to 0.2B parameters and achieves 54.71\% MFU with near-linear scalability (0.97).

</details>


### [267] [Sustainable Graph Analytics Workload Scheduling with Evolutionary Reinforcement Learning in Edge-Cloud Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13489)
*P. Ramicetty,H. Moore,S. Qi,A. Islam,M. Ghose,D. Milojicic,C. Bash,S. Pasricha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出MERSEM框架，结合进化搜索与强化学习，优化边缘-云环境下的图工作负载分配与调度，实现SLA违规降低45%、碳排放降低12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能系统（如智慧城市、物联网安全等）中图分析工作负载复杂度的增加，在异构边缘-云环境中执行这些任务导致了更高的能耗和碳排放，亟需可持续的系统管理方案。

Method: 提出MERSEM多目标进化强化学习框架，集成进化搜索进行全局解探索，并结合强化学习进行自适应局部优化，综合考虑动态碳强度、资源异构性和工作负载特性，联合最小化SLA违规和碳排放。

Result: 实验结果表明，MERSEM相较于现有最优方法，SLA违规最高降低45%，碳排放最高降低12%。

Conclusion: MERSEM有效解决了边缘-云环境下图工作负载的可持续管理问题，在保证服务质量的同时显著降低了碳排放，为绿色计算提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Graph analytics powers modern intelligent systems such as smart cities, cyber-physical infrastructure, IoT security, and large-scale social networks. As these workloads scale in complexity, their execution in heterogeneous edge-cloud environments results in higher energy use and carbon emission footprint. To address this challenge, we propose MERSEM, a multi-objective evolutionary reinforcement learning framework for sustainable edge-cloud system management. MERSEM integrates evolutionary search with reinforcement learning (RL) to solve the problem of graph workload allocation and scheduling. The evolutionary component explores diverse global solutions, while the RL agent refines decisions through adaptive local optimization. The framework is designed to jointly minimize service-level agreement (SLA) violations and carbon emissions by considering dynamic carbon intensity, resource heterogeneity, and workload characteristics. Experimental results demonstrate that MERSEM outperforms the state-of-the-art with up to 45% SLA violation reductions and up to 12% carbon emission reductions.

</details>


### [268] [MARLIN: Multi-Agent Game-Theoretic Reinforcement Learning for Sustainable LLM Inference in Cloud Datacenters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13496)
*H. Moore,S. Qi,D. Milojicic,C. Bash,S. Pasricha*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出MARLIN框架，通过多智能体博弈论强化学习协同优化LLM推理服务的时间、碳排放、水资源和能耗，相比现有方法分别降低至少18% TTFT、33%碳排放、43%水资源和11%能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型推理请求占生命周期总能耗高达90%，远超训练能耗。随着推理请求量激增，云平台面临碳排放和水资源消耗等环境足迹持续增长的问题，亟需提升LLM推理服务的可持续性。

Method: 提出MARLIN（多智能体博弈论强化学习框架），在云数据中心环境下协同优化LLM推理的首令牌时间（TTFT）、碳排放、水资源使用和能耗成本四个目标。

Result: 与最先进的LLM推理管理框架相比，MARLIN实现TTFT降低至少18%、碳排放降低33%、水资源使用降低43%、能耗成本降低11%。

Conclusion: MARLIN框架有效平衡了LLM推理服务的性能与可持续性目标，为云数据中心的绿色AI服务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have become increasingly prevalent in cloud-based platforms, propelled by the introduction of AI-based consumer and enterprise services. LLM inference requests in particular account for up to 90% of total LLM lifecycle energy use, dwarfing training energy costs. The rising volume of LLM inference requests is increasing environmental footprints, particularly carbon emissions and water consumption. To improve sustainability for LLM inference serving in cloud datacenter environments, we propose a novel multi-agent game-theoretic reinforcement learning framework called MARLIN to co-optimize time-to-first token (TTFT), carbon emissions, water usage, and energy costs associated with LLM inference. MARLIN demonstrates a reduction of at least 18% in TTFT, 33% in carbon emissions, 43% in water usage, and 11% in energy costs compared to state-of-the-art LLM inference management frameworks.

</details>


### [269] [KVServe: Service-Aware KV Cache Compression for Communication-Efficient Disaggregated LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13734)
*Zedong Liu,Xinyang Ma,Dejun Luo,Hairui Zhao,Bing Lu,Wenjing Huang,Yida Gu,Xingchen Liu,Zheng Wei,Jinyang Liu,Dingwen Tao,Guangming Tan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了KVServe，首个面向 disaggregated LLM serving 的服务感知自适应 KV 通信压缩框架，通过统一压缩策略空间、贝叶斯离线搜索和在线控制器，显著降低了端到端延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 disaggregated LLM serving（如 PD 分离和 KV 状态解耦）中，KV 成为跨网络和存储边界的显式负载，成为端到端瓶颈。现有 KV 压缩方法通常采用静态运行时配置，无法适应生产环境中随时间变化的工作负载混合、带宽和 SLO/质量预算，导致固定选择次优甚至增加延迟。

Method: KVServe 提出三项核心技术：(1) 将 KV 压缩统一为模块化策略空间，支持新组件和跨方法重组；(2) 引入 Bayesian Profiling Engine，高效搜索策略空间并蒸馏出 3D Pareto 候选集，将离线搜索开销降低 50 倍；(3) 部署 Service-Aware Online Controller，结合分析延迟模型和轻量级 bandit 算法，在约束条件下选择配置并修正离线到在线的失配。

Result: KVServe 集成到 vLLM 中，在跨数据集、模型、GPU 和网络的评估中，在 PD-separated serving 场景实现高达 9.13 倍 JCT 加速，在 KV-disaggregated serving 场景实现高达 32.8 倍 TTFT 降低。

Conclusion: KVServe 有效解决了 disaggregated LLM serving 中 KV 通信瓶颈问题，通过服务感知的自适应压缩策略，在保证服务质量的同时显著提升了系统性能，为生产环境中的 LLM 推理服务提供了有效的优化方案。

Abstract: LLMs are widely adopted in production, pushing inference systems to their limits. Disaggregated LLM serving (e.g., PD separation and KV state disaggregation) improves scalability and cost efficiency, but it also turns KV into an explicit payload crossing network and storage boundaries, making KV a dominant end-to-end bottleneck. Existing KV compression are typically static runtime configurations, despite production service context varies over time in workload mix, bandwidth, and SLO/quality budgets. As a result, a fixed choice can be suboptimal or even increase latency. We present \emph{KVServe}, the first service-aware and adaptive KV communication compression framework for disaggregated LLM serving: KVServe (1) unifies KV compression into a modular strategy space with new components and cross-method recomposition; (2) introduces Bayesian Profiling Engine that efficiently searches this space and distills a 3D Pareto candidate set, reducing $50\times$ offline search overhead; and (3) deploys a Service-Aware Online Controller that combines an analytical latency model with a lightweight bandit to select profiles under constraints and correct offline-to-online mismatch. Integrated into vLLM and evaluated across datasets, models, GPUs and networks, KVServe achieves up to $9.13\times$ JCT speedup in PD-separated serving and up to $32.8\times$ TTFT reduction in KV-disaggregated serving.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [DQN-Driven Adaptive Neighbor Discovery for Directional Aerial Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12552)
*Md Asif Ishrak Sarder,Murat Yuksel,Elizabeth Bentley*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度Q网络（DQN）的自适应收发器选择协议，用于解决空中定向天线网络中网络连通性与隐私保护之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向天线系统在空中网络中具有高增益、传输距离远和安全性强等优势，但其波束对准要求使得在移动环境中发现和到达邻居节点变得困难。同时，隐私问题在无线网络中同样至关重要。然而，在探测过程中如何确保全网连通性的同时保持有限的暴露，这一问题尚未被探索。

Method: 提出了一种基于深度Q网络（DQN）框架的自适应收发器选择协议。每个节点作为一个独立的DQN代理，与环境交互以学习如何平衡连通性与隐私的权衡。由于定向节点仅基于本地观察进行操作，采用了一种加权机制，通过自适应调整探测模式，引导节点优先考虑高可达性或隐私保护。

Result: 实验结果表明，DQN框架优于随机和Q学习基线。当权重偏向邻居发现时，探测效率和可达性更高；当权重偏向隐私时，虽然可达性降低，但能确保有限的暴露，最终获得更高的目标值。

Conclusion: 所提出的DQN框架能够有效平衡定向天线网络中的连通性与隐私保护，通过自适应调整探测模式，在不同权重设置下实现了性能优化。

Abstract: Directional antenna systems are gaining substantial traction for aerial networks due to their higher gain, extended transmission range, and enhanced security. However, the requirement of beam alignment makes the task of finding and reaching neighbors challenging, particularly in a mobile setting. For wireless networks, privacy concerns play an equally critical role. However, the problem of ensuring network-wide connectivity while maintaining limited exposure when probing around is still unexplored. We address this trade-off by proposing an adaptive transceiver selection protocol based on the Deep Q-Network (DQN) framework. Each node acts as an independent DQN agent and interacts with the environment to learn how to balance the trade-off. Since the directional nodes operate only based on local observations, we adopt a weighted mechanism that guides them in prioritizing either high reachability or privacy by adaptively tuning the probing patterns. Results show that DQN framework surpasses the Random and Q-Learning baselines. Weights favoring discovery provide higher probing efficiency and reachability, while weights prioritizing privacy ensure limited exposure at the cost of low reachability, eventually attaining higher objective value.

</details>


### [271] [Decentralized Multi-Channel MANET Power Optimization Using Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12612)
*Tomer Alter,Nir Shlezinger,Michael Segal*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MANET-GNN，一种基于图神经网络的分布式算法，用于解决多信道移动自组织网络中的功率分配问题，实现了高效、可扩展且泛化能力强的实时通信优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着移动自组织网络（MANETs）需求的增长，亟需在严格的资源约束下进行节点与信道间发射功率分配的分布式机制。然而，现有的基于优化的方法未能充分考虑每个链路包含多个信道（如多频带信令）的实际场景，这促使作者利用机器学习在分布式优化中的最新进展来寻求解决方案。

Method: 作者基于约束优化问题建模，提出了一种受消息传递机制启发的专用图神经网络（GNN）架构——MANET-GNN。该方法通过无监督学习过程进行训练，能够显式利用网络拓扑结构，且对带有噪声的信道状态信息具有鲁棒性，从而实现分布式的功率分配。

Result: 数值评估结果表明，MANET-GNN能够随着节点数量和频带数量的增加高效扩展，在不同网络拓扑和信道条件下具有良好的泛化能力，并支持适用于实时部署的近瞬时推理，在多种MANET场景中实现了高吞吐量的多信道通信。

Conclusion: MANET-GNN成功地将图神经网络引入多信道MANETs的功率分配任务中，克服了传统方法的局限性。该方法不仅在性能上表现优异，还具备良好的可扩展性、泛化性和实时性，为去中心化的网络资源管理提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: The increasing demand for mobile ad hoc networks (MANETs) calls for decentralized mechanisms that can allocate transmit power across nodes and channels under stringent resource constraints. Existing optimization-based approaches, however, do not account for expected settings where each link includes multiple channels (e.g., multi-band signaling). Motivated by recent advances in machine learning for distributed optimization, we propose MANET-GNN, a graph neural network (GNN)-based algorithm for decentralized power allocation in multi-channel MANETs. MANET-GNN explicitly exploits the network topology, scales efficiently with the number of nodes and frequency bands, generalizes across topologies and channel conditions, and enables near-instantaneous inference suitable for real-time deployment. Our design builds on a constrained optimization formulation and employs a dedicated GNN architecture inspired by message passing, trained via an unsupervised procedure that is robust to noisy channel state information. Numerical evaluations demonstrate that MANET-GNN achieves high-throughput multi-channel communication across diverse MANET scenarios.

</details>


### [272] [Toward Communication-Efficient Space Data Centers: Bottlenecks, Architectures, and New Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12681)
*Minghao Sun,Zehui Chen,Jinbo Hou,Kezhi Wang,Xiaoli Chu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对空间数据中心（SDC）通信受限问题，提出利用语义通信降低上行链路压力，并验证了多层异构SDC架构在能源与热约束下的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面数据中心功耗密度快速增长，引发能源供应、散热和空间扩展挑战；将计算基础设施部署于太空可利用持续太阳能和辐射冷却优势。然而，地面数据中心内部为拍比特级数据交换，而星地链路仅为吉比特级容量，通信能力成为空间数据中心的关键瓶颈。

Method: 系统分析空间数据中心架构中的通信约束，提出将语义通信作为关键使能范式，传输紧凑的、任务相关的语义表征而非原始数据，从而显著降低上行链路压力；评估由中继卫星与轨道计算节点构成的多层异构SDC框架，在耦合能源与热约束下运行。

Result: 通过语义通信方法有效缓解上行链路压力；所提出的多层异构空间数据中心框架在能源与热约束耦合条件下，验证了通信高效的轨道AI基础设施的可行性。

Conclusion: 语义通信是缓解空间数据中心通信瓶颈的关键使能技术；多层异构架构在能源与热约束下可实现可行的轨道AI基础设施；文章最后展望了可扩展部署面临的开放研究挑战。

Abstract: The rapid growth of foundation model training and large-scale AI services has driven ground data centers toward unprecedented power densities, intensifying challenges in energy supply, cooling, and spatial scalability. Space Data Centers (SDCs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for hosting energy-intensive computing infrastructures in orbit, leveraging continuous solar energy and radiative cooling advantages. However, unlike ground facilities primarily constrained by power and site availability, SDCs are fundamentally limited by communication capability. The gap between petabit-scale internal data exchange in ground data centers and the gigabit-scale capacity of ground-space links forms a critical bottleneck. This article systematically analyzes communication constraints in SDC architectures and explores semantic communication as a key enabling paradigm. By transmitting compact, task-relevant semantic representations instead of raw data, uplink pressure can be substantially reduced. The feasibility of communication-efficient orbital AI infrastructures is demonstrated through the evaluation of a multi-layer heterogeneous SDC framework consisting of relay satellites and orbital computing nodes operating under coupled energy and thermal constraints. The article further outlines open research challenges toward scalable deployment.

</details>


### [273] [Large Language Models for Agentic NetOps and AIOps: Architectures, Evaluation, and Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12729)
*Muhammad Bilal,Jon Crowcroft,Ruizhi Wang,Xiaolong Xu,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文综述了大语言模型在网络运维和智能运维中的应用，指出操作可靠性主要依赖于模型周围的机制而非模型本身，强调了基于工作流的评估方法和安全治理的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在NetOps和AIOps领域的广泛应用，包括事件调查、根因分析、配置合成和有限自愈等任务，传统的静态问答评估方式已无法满足需求。由于运维决策会产生即时影响，需要确保代理操作的可靠性和安全性，因此亟需建立新的评估框架和安全治理机制。

Method: 本文采用文献综述的方法，围绕自主性层级、工具范围、证据追踪和保障合约这四个维度组织相关文献。其中保障合约定义了代理可观察、提议和执行的内容，以及执行前必须通过的检查。同时提出了以工作流为中心的评估方法，包括追踪质量、有界工具使用、安全提议生成、沙盒环境重放和具备回滚感知的金丝雀试验。

Result: 研究发现跨遥测查询推荐、诊断、根因分析、配置合成、变更规划和有限自愈等工作存在一致模式：操作可靠性主要不来自模型本身，而是依赖于模型周围的机制。缺乏适当评估措施的系统可能看似健壮但实际上过于脆弱。当代理接近运维控制面时，安全、隐私和治理风险会变得尤为严重。

Conclusion: 智能NetOps和AIOps的进步取决于将自主性视为一个受约束的运维控制问题，其输出必须可靠、可审计且可安全部署。需要超越静态问答评估，采用基于工作流的评估方法，并重视代理操作带来的安全、隐私和治理挑战。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly being used to support network operations (NetOps) and artificial intelligence for IT operations (AIOps), including incident investigation, root-cause analysis, configuration synthesis, and limited self-healing. In both NetOps and AIOps, this shift is changing how tasks are managed. Agent-based operations work as workflows, from gathering evidence to taking action, following permissions, policies, and checks, and providing rollback options when necessary. This is crucial because operational decisions can have instant impacts. To make the argument concrete, we organise the relevant literature around the hierarchy of autonomy, tool scope, evidence traces, and assurance contracts. These contracts define what an agent may observe, propose, and execute. They also define the checks that must pass before any action is allowed. A consistent pattern appears across work on telemetry query recommendation, diagnosis, root-cause analysis, configuration synthesis, change planning, and limited self-healing. Operational reliability does not come chiefly from the model itself. It depends on the machinery around the model. We also argue that evaluation should go beyond static question answering. Agentic NetOps and AIOps systems require workflow-centred evaluation, including trace quality, bounded tool use, safe proposal generation, replay in sandboxed environments, and canary trials with rollback-aware scoring. Without these measures, a system may appear robust yet remain too fragile. Finally, we examine security, privacy, and governance risks that become acute when agents sit close to operational control surfaces. Taken together, the survey concludes that progress in intelligent NetOps and AIOps will depend on treating autonomy as a constrained operational control problem, whose outputs must be reliable, auditable, and securely deployable.

</details>


### [274] [Bridge: Optimizing Collective Communication Schedules in Reconfigurable Networks with Reusable Subrings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12766)
*Anton Juerss,Stefan Schmid*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出Bridge，一种针对光电路交换网络集合通信的重配置策略，通过复用光链路显著降低All-to-All和AllReduce等操作的完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光电路交换网络可运行时重配置拓扑，但每次重配置存在不可忽略的延迟，且需暂停通信。现有策略仅优化当前步骤，未考虑链路在未来步骤的复用，导致重配置收益受限。核心问题在于判断重配置是否值得其代价。

Method: 利用Bruck通信模式的结构支持高效稀疏重配置。核心思想是直接连接即时通信伙伴以减少传播与传输延迟，并通过连接的子环保持对未来对等节点的高效可达性，使光链路可在多个后续步骤中复用，将重配置收益分摊到多个步骤。

Result: 在毫秒级重配置延迟下，Bridge将All-to-All完成时间相比静态基线降低3倍至10倍；对于AllReduce，Bridge一致优于现有重配置策略，最高提升1.5倍，且在中小规模工作负载上比带宽最优的Ring算法快1.5倍至6.6倍。

Conclusion: Bridge通过跨步骤复用光链路有效解决了重配置代价与收益的权衡问题，显著提升了AI/ML和HPC应用中关键集合通信原语的性能，证明了稀疏重配置策略的有效性。

Abstract: Optical circuit-switched networks have emerged as an appealing alternative to electrical fabrics as they can reconfigure the network topology at runtime, reducing communication cost and improving bandwidth utilization. Yet exploiting optical reconfigurable networks for collective communication comes with a fundamental trade-off: each reconfiguration incurs non-negligible delay, communication must pause while the fabric reconfigures, and the benefit of a new topology depends on future traffic. The central question is therefore when reconfiguration is worth its cost. While prior work has demonstrated the benefits of reconfiguration, existing strategies use optical links only to optimize the current step, without reusing them for future steps.
  In this paper, we present Bridge, a reconfiguration strategy for important collective communication primitives used in AI/ML and HPC applications, namely All-to-All, AllReduce, Reduce-Scatter, and AllGather. Bridge exploits the structure of Bruck's communication pattern to support efficient sparse reconfiguration. The key idea is to reduce propagation and transmission delay by directly connecting immediate communication partners and preserve efficient reachability to future peers through connected subrings. As a result, optical links can be reused across multiple subsequent steps, allowing the benefit of reconfiguration to amortize beyond a single step. Our evaluation shows that Bridge reduces All-to-All completion time by typically $3\times$ to $10\times$ over static baselines even with millisecond-scale reconfiguration delays. For AllReduce, Bridge uniformly outperforms existing reconfiguration strategies, delivers up to $1.5\times$ speedup, and exceeds the bandwidth-optimal Ring algorithm by $1.5\times$ to $6.6\times$ on low to moderate-sized workloads.

</details>


### [275] [NeuroRisk: Physics-Informed Neural Optimization for Risk-Aware Traffic Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12862)
*Yingming Mao,Ximeng Liu,Jingyi Cheng,Xiyuan Liu,Jiashuai Liu,Yike Liu,Zhen Yao,Yuzhou Zhou,Siyuan Feng,Qiaozhu Zhai,Shizhen Zhao*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出NeuroRisk，一种基于深度展开优化的风险感知流量工程方法，通过利用Sort-and-Select结构，在满足可用性约束的同时实现了比传统求解器快2-5个数量级的计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产广域网中，相关性故障是导致可用性损失的主要原因，迫使运营商预留较大的安全裕度，导致容量利用率低下。在严格的可用性目标下实现高利用率需要在数十到数百个概率故障场景下进行风险感知的流量工程，但在运营时间尺度上解决该问题仍然难以实现。现有的风险感知公式要么为了效率限制场景选择，要么产生过高的分解成本。而现有的深度学习方法主要针对最大链路利用率，在显式容量约束和场景相关风险下会失效。

Method: 提出NeuroRisk，一种物理信息深度展开优化器。核心创新包括：1) 发现现有风险感知公式可以统一在嵌入式Sort-and-Select结构下；2) 通过门控边缘局部预留机制强制可行性约束；3) 通过排列不变、梯度对齐的线索表示场景集合。该方法充分利用Sort-and-Select结构，平衡了表达能力和计算效率。

Result: 在生产级广域网上的评估显示：1) 相对于求解器，NeuroRisk在风险目标上实现了很小的最优性差距；2) 计算速度比传统求解器快2-5个数量级(10²-10⁵×)；3) 在标称吞吐量方面优于神经基线方法。

Conclusion: NeuroRisk成功解决了生产广域网中风险感知流量工程的计算挑战，通过深度展开优化和物理信息约束的结合，在保证解的质量的同时实现了显著的加速，为在运营时间尺度上实施风险感知TE提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In production Wide-Area Networks (WANs), correlated failures dominate availability losses, forcing operators to reserve large safety margins that leave substantial capacity underutilized. Achieving high utilization under strict availability targets therefore requires risk-aware Traffic Engineering (TE) over dozens to hundreds of probabilistic failure scenarios-yet solving this problem at operational timescales remains elusive. We demonstrate that existing risk-aware formulations can be unified under an embedded Sort-and-Select structure, exposing a fundamental trade-off between expressiveness and tractability: classical optimizers either restrict scenario selection for efficiency or incur prohibitive decomposition costs. While deep learning appears promising, prior Deep TE methods mainly target maximum link utilization and rely on scaling-based feasibility, which fundamentally breaks under explicit capacity constraints and scenario-dependent risk. We present NeuroRisk, a physics-informed deep unrolled optimizer that exploits the structure of Sort-and-Select. NeuroRisk enforces feasibility via gated edge-local reservations and represents scenario sets through permutation-invariant, gradient-aligned cues. Evaluations on production-style WANs show that NeuroRisk achieves small optimality gaps relative to the solver with orders of magnitude speedup $(10^2- 10^5 \times)$ on risk objectives, while outperforming neural baselines on nominal throughput.

</details>


### [276] [A Persistence-Aware Framework for Age Violation Control in Wireless Status Update Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13002)
*Haoyuan Pan,Chen Chen,Shiyong Zhou,Kun Chen,Tse-Tin Chan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于连续年龄违例率向量（C-AVR）的持久性感知可靠性框架，并开发了分位数回归对偶双深度Q网络（QR-D3QN）进行优化。该方法能够有效捕捉年龄信息违例的时间持久性，在安全敏感的无线应用中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的信息年龄（AoI）指标（如平均AoI和年龄违例率AVR）仅刻画时间平均新鲜度或违例频率，无法显式捕捉连续年龄违例的时间持久性。而在安全敏感的无线应用中，连续违例的持续时间可能至关重要，因此需要新的可靠性评估框架。

Method: 1. 提出基于连续年龄违例率向量（C-AVR）的持久性感知可靠性框架，其分量量化不同长度连续时间窗口内的AoI阈值违例；2. 通过灵活的加权方案统一从平均持久性到尾部敏感性能的可靠性目标；3. 开发基于分位数回归对偶双深度Q网络（QR-D3QN）的分布强化学习方法，通过建模基于分位数的回报分布而非标量期望回报，为稀有但持久的违例序列提供更丰富的价值估计信号。

Result: 仿真结果表明：1. QR-D3QN在广泛的加权方案和系统设置下始终优于基于期望的基线方法；2. 在尾部敏感的持久性目标下，性能提升尤为显著；3. 分量分析显示分布价值学习在多个持久性尺度上显著提升可靠性，特别是对于长连续违例序列。

Conclusion: 提出的C-AVR框架为持久性感知可靠性评估提供了有效基础，分布强化学习方法能够有效处理连续违例的时间相关性问题，在安全敏感的无线应用场景中具有重要的实用价值。

Abstract: Timely and reliable status updates are essential for emerging QoS-sensitive wireless applications. Common age of information (AoI)-based metrics, such as average AoI and age violation rate (AVR), characterize time-averaged freshness or violation frequency but do not explicitly capture the temporal persistence of consecutive age violations, which can be critical in safety-sensitive wireless applications. We develop a persistence-aware reliability framework based on the consecutive age violation rate (C-AVR) vector, whose components quantify AoI threshold violations over consecutive time windows of different lengths. Through flexible weighting schemes, the proposed framework unifies reliability objectives ranging from average persistence to tail-sensitive performance. Optimizing weighted C-AVR objectives is challenging because consecutive violations are temporally correlated, leading to sparse learning signals. To address this issue, we develop a distributional reinforcement learning approach based on a quantile regression dueling double deep Q-network (QR-D3QN). By modeling a quantile-based return distribution rather than only a scalar expected return, QR-D3QN provides richer value-estimation signals for rare but prolonged violation sequences under stochastic packet arrivals, unreliable channels, and transmission cost constraints. Simulation results show that QR-D3QN consistently outperforms expectation-based baselines across a wide range of weighting schemes and system settings, with particularly significant gains under tail-sensitive persistence objectives. Component-wise analysis further shows that distributional value learning substantially improves reliability across multiple persistence scales, especially for long consecutive violation sequences. Overall, our results establish the proposed C-AVR framework as an effective foundation for persistence-aware reliability evaluation.

</details>


### [277] [Toward Practical Age-of-Information Scheduling in 5G Cellular](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13012)
*Zhuoyi Zhao,Igor Kadota*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对5G网络中gNB无法直接观测目标端信息年龄（AoI）的限制，提出了一种低复杂度的AoI感知调度方案。该方案包含一个能够推断UE侧时间戳和目标端AoI的估计器，以及基于此的Max-Weight调度策略（MW-LC），在仿真中表现出接近理想估计器的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际5G网络中，gNB需要在严格的时隙级运行时约束下进行上行链路调度决策，但无法直接观测目标端的信息年龄（AoI），这限制了时间敏感型传输的调度效率。现有研究缺乏针对这种有限可观测性场景的低复杂度解决方案。

Method: 1. 设计了一个低复杂度估计器，能够从gNB可见的观测数据中推断UE侧数据包时间戳和目标端AoI；2. 基于这些估计值，提出了Max-Weight调度策略（MW-LC）；3. 在标准兼容的5G仿真器NetSim中实现并验证了该策略；4. 使用MATLAB仿真对比了 richer estimator-based 方案。

Result: 1. 在NetSim仿真中，MW-LC策略相比基准5G调度策略表现出更好的性能；2. MATLAB仿真表明，所提出的低复杂度估计器和MW-LC策略能够达到接近文献中基于更丰富估计器的AoI策略的性能水平；3. 该估计器具有独立价值，可应用于5G调度之外的AoI感知算法。

Conclusion: 本文成功开发了适用于有限可观测性5G蜂窝网络的低复杂度AoI感知调度方案，证明了在无法直接观测目标端AoI的实际约束下，通过合理的估计方法仍能实现高效的年龄感知调度。所提出的估计器具有普适性，可推广至更广泛的AoI感知算法领域。

Abstract: We consider a 5G cellular network where a gNB schedules time-sensitive uplink transmissions from multiple UEs and forwards received packets to remote destinations. In practical 5G networks, the gNB does not directly observe the destination-side Age of Information (AoI) and must make scheduling decisions under stringent slot-level runtime constraints. In this paper, we develop a low-complexity AoI-aware scheduling policy for 5G cellular under limited observability. We first design a low-complexity estimator that infers UE-side packet timestamps and destination-side AoI from gNB-visible observations. Based on these estimates, we propose and implement a Max-Weight policy (MW-LC) in NetSim, a 5G emulator with a standards-compatible protocol stack, to showcase its performance against baseline 5G scheduling policies. Furthermore, we use MATLAB simulations to show that the LC estimator and MW-LC achieve performance close to a richer estimator-based AoI policy from the literature. The estimator may be of independent interest to the community, enabling AoI-aware algorithms beyond 5G scheduling.

</details>


### [278] [Content Caching Methods in Named Data Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13104)
*Pankaj Chaudhary,Neminath Hubballi,Sameer G. Kulkarni*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于信息中心网络（ICN）特别是命名数据网络（NDN）中缓存算法的综述，系统性地回顾了现有缓存技术，分析了其优缺点，并总结了性能评估指标和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICN/NDN作为一种新兴的网络架构，其核心特性是在网络层面进行内容缓存以提升内容分发性能。尽管已有大量缓存技术被提出，但缺乏系统性的梳理和分类，因此需要对该领域的缓存算法进行全面综述。

Method: 首先提出ICN缓存技术的分类体系，然后详细讨论各种缓存方法及其工作原理，同时总结每种方法的优缺点，最后归纳文献中常用的缓存性能评估指标。

Result: 提供了ICN/NDN缓存算法的结构化综述，建立了缓存技术分类法，详细分析了各种缓存方法的工作原理、优势与不足，并总结了性能评估指标。

Conclusion: 本综述为ICN/NDN缓存技术的研究提供了系统参考，指出了当前研究的不足，并提出了该领域未来可能的研究方向。

Abstract: Information Centric Networking (ICN) is a new network architecture (Internet) that focuses on content rather than the end-hosts. Named Data Networking (NDN) is a specific implementation of ICN, which relies on the use of named data and a request-response model for content distribution. These Internet architectures are known for their ability to cache content at the network level. Many caching techniques have been designed as part of various ICN/NDN projects. Caching techniques help improve the content delivery performance by storing content in the router to meet future demand. In this survey, we provide a structured review of caching algorithms designed for ICN, with a particular emphasis on NDN. We first present a taxonomy of caching techniques, followed by a detailed discussion of the various methods. Alongside their working principles, we also summarize their advantages and disadvantages. Finally, we discuss the performance metrics commonly used in the literature to evaluate caching methods and outline directions for future research in this area.

</details>


### [279] [Intelligence Delivery Network: Toward an Internet Architecture for the AI Age](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13235)
*Hanling Wang,Qing Li,Dan Zhao,Yuhong Song,Xingchi Chen,Teng Gao,Peiyuan Zong,Zhuyun Qi,Yue Yu,Yong Jiang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了智能交付网络（IDN），一种将AI能力作为可交付网络服务的互联网架构，旨在解决当前云中心化AI服务部署范式存在的延迟、流量、资源利用和隐私等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI服务的云中心化部署范式虽然运营便利，但存在延迟和抖动高、广域网流量大、分布式异构计算资源利用率低，以及日益增长的隐私和治理问题，无法满足用户对智能服务的需求。

Method: 提出智能交付网络（IDN）架构。其核心思想是根据需求局部性、资源可用性和策略约束，在云、区域、边缘和本地环境中定位、选择、重用和验证智能。该架构集成了能力抽象、计算资源整合、需求驱动部署、服务路由、状态感知缓存和信任管理等核心机制。

Result: 阐述了IDN的系统假设，定义了其核心架构机制，并讨论了各机制如何协同支持分布式AI服务。

Conclusion: IDN为AI时代的互联网架构提供了一条切实可行的路径，使AI能力更易于获取、高效、可信，并能响应多样化的应用需求。

Abstract: The rapid emergence of AI-powered applications is reshaping the role of the Internet. Users increasingly rely on the network to obtain intelligence services derived from large foundation models, rather than merely to reach remote endpoints or retrieve specific content. Today's dominant deployment paradigm for AI services remains cloud-centric, where user requests are transmitted to remote data centers for centralized inference. Although operationally convenient, this paradigm suffers from latency and jitter, heavy wide-area traffic, limited utilization of distributed heterogeneous compute resources, and growing privacy and governance concerns. In this paper, we propose the Intelligence Delivery Network (IDN), an Internet architecture that treats AI capabilities as deliverable network services. The key idea is to position, select, reuse, and verify intelligence across cloud, regional, edge, and local environments according to demand locality, resource availability, and policy constraints. We present the system assumptions of IDN, define its core architectural mechanisms, and discuss how capability abstraction, compute resource integration, demand-driven deployment, service routing, state-aware caching, and trust management can jointly support distributed AI services. We believe that IDN provides a practical path toward an Internet architecture for the AI age, making AI capabilities more accessible, efficient, trustworthy, and responsive to diverse application needs.

</details>


### [280] [Swarm Network-as-a-Service (SNaaS)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13341)
*Balsam Alkouz,Osama Amin,Basem Shihada*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Swarm Network-as-a-Service (SNaaS)框架，利用无人机群提供按需连接服务，通过服务组合和自适应策略显著降低了延迟和SLA违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的按需连接场景日益需要具有严格服务级别保证的网络解决方案，现有方案难以满足大规模、动态变化的连接需求。

Method: 提出SNaaS服务导向框架，将无人机与设备、无人机之间的交互建模为可组合服务；设计SDN架构实现提供者-消费者-注册中心三元组；提出三种组合策略（直接、集群、并行）并基于排队论开发启发式策略选择算法；设计执行模块持续监控队列稳定性和SLA延迟并自适应重构无人机群。

Result: 基于真实空对地测量数据的实验表明，该框架在延迟、SLA违规次数和适应性方面均优于固定组合方案，且在负载和无人机规模增加时表现更稳定。

Conclusion: SNaaS框架能够有效提供大规模按需连接服务，自适应组合策略显著提升了服务质量，为未来动态网络场景提供了可行解决方案。

Abstract: Emerging on-demand connectivity scenarios increasingly require networking solutions with stringent service-level guarantees. We propose Swarm Network-as-a-Service (SNaaS), a service-oriented framework that leverages fleets of drones to provide on-demand connectivity at scale. SNaaS explicitly models drone-to-device and drone-to-drone interactions as composable services, enabling consumers to request connectivity through Service-Level Agreements (SLAs). We formalize atomic and composite SNaaS services, present an SDN-inspired architecture that integrates the service-oriented triad of provider, consumer, and registry. We introduce a composition framework that orchestrates drones into end-to-end services. Within this framework, we define and analyze three composition strategies, i.e., direct, clustered, and parallel, and propose a queuing-theory-based heuristic for selecting the most suitable strategy under varying load conditions. A dedicated enforcement module continuously monitors queue stability and SLA latency, adaptively reconfiguring the swarm when violations occur. Experiments using real air-to-ground measurements show that the framework consistently outperforms fixed compositions, achieving lower latency, fewer SLA violations, and smoother adaptation as load and swarm size increase.

</details>
